<div align="center">

# 🦷 Sinus 3D Viewer Pro
### Browser-Based CT/CBCT DICOM Visualization with Smooth Multiplanar Reconstruction and Interactive 3D Rendering

**A privacy-conscious, read-only medical imaging viewer for exploring sinus CT and cone-beam CT studies using axial, coronal, sagittal and volumetric views.**

![Python](https://img.shields.io/badge/Python-3.11+-3776AB?logo=python&logoColor=white)

> [!IMPORTANT]
> **Research and visualization software, not a certified diagnostic medical device.** It must not be used as the sole basis for clinical decisions. Patient-identifying DICOM files, access links, credentials, and unredacted screenshots must never be committed to a public repository.

## 📖 Project Overview

**Sinus 3D Viewer Pro** is a web-based medical-image visualization project designed to make large CT/CBCT DICOM series easier to inspect through a browser. It combines a Python medical-imaging backend with a web user interface and a working **Google Colab one-cell launcher**. The viewer supports three orthogonal *multiplanar reconstruction* (MPR) planes—**axial, coronal and sagittal**—alongside an interactive **3D volume** view.

The uploaded project contains **two related execution paths**:

1. **Colab viewer (demonstrated working workflow):** a one-cell notebook scans an authorized DICOM folder, prepares temporary visualization artifacts, launches a FastAPI server, serves browser-resident MPR and interactive VTK.js rendering, and creates a temporary HTTPS link.
2. **Modular full-stack application (source architecture):** a React/TypeScript/Vite frontend and FastAPI backend with modules for dataset browsing, volume services, security, DICOM decoding, viewing controls and measurements. Some modules are intentionally empty placeholders in the supplied source; this track must not be described as fully production-complete.

### The central idea

Instead of requesting a new image from the server every time the mouse wheel moves, an optimized browser-resident MPR strategy can deliver volume data once and perform slice navigation directly inside the browser. This reduces dependence on round-trip network latency during scrolling while retaining a separate, optimized 3D rendering path.

## 🎯 Problem Statement

High-resolution sinus CT/CBCT studies often contain hundreds of DICOM slices. Reviewing them can be inconvenient when:
- users need to install specialized desktop software;
- remote viewers request individual slices continuously, resulting in choppy mouse-wheel scrolling;
- large volumes demand substantial memory, transfer time and preprocessing;
- low-quality interpolation makes anatomy appear blurry;
- axial, coronal, sagittal and 3D views are separated across interfaces;
- medical data must be handled without changing the original studies.

### Project objectives

| Objective | Project approach |
|---|---|
| Explore real DICOM studies | Scan and select a consistent image series from an authorized folder |
| Preserve source data | Read-only input workflow; generated files belong in temporary/runtime locations |
| Three-plane inspection | Axial, coronal and sagittal reconstruction |
| Volume understanding | Interactive VTK.js 3D rendering |
| Smooth interaction | Browser-resident MPR / local slice navigation |
| Efficient transfer | Compressed volume delivery and an optimized 3D representation |
| Accessible demo | Colab launcher + temporary HTTPS access |
| Safer session handling | Generated session credentials and password-protected access in the Colab path |

## ✨ Features

### Implemented in the Colab viewer path
- **Smart DICOM scan and series selection** for a patient/study folder.
- **Read-only source handling**; derived artifacts are generated in a separate runtime directory.
- **Three orthogonal MPR views**: axial, coronal, sagittal.
- **Interactive 3D volume** with VTK.js.
- **Mouse wheel navigation**, with a browser-side MPR optimization to reduce per-slice server requests.
- **Window/level conversion and rendering controls** to inspect intensity ranges.
- **Physical aspect-ratio awareness** using voxel-spacing metadata.
- **Previews**, volume metadata and serialized visualization data.
- **Compressed browser-volume delivery**, improving transfer efficiency relative to an uncompressed payload.
- **Temporary FastAPI server + Cloudflare tunnel** with session/password handling.
- **Single-cell restart workflow** after Colab runtime setup; the temporary URL is session-bound.

### Present in the modular codebase
The React app and FastAPI code include implementations or modules for dataset browsing, previews, MPR canvases and viewports, crosshairs, slice sliders, window-level controls, orientation labels, measurements, scale bars, API clients, DICOM scanning/decoding, volume building, caching and security. **Not all of these components have been validated together in an end-to-end deployment.** Some associated routes, tests, docs and scaffolding files are empty in the uploaded archive.

## 🏗️ System Architecture

```mermaid
flowchart TD
    A["Authorized patient CT / CBCT DICOM folder"] --> B["Read-only DICOM scanner"]
    B --> C["Group, validate and select image series"]
    C --> D["Read pixel data + DICOM geometry"]
    D --> E["Construct NumPy volume and spacing metadata"]
    E --> F["Temporary runtime artifacts / previews"]
    F --> G["FastAPI HTTP server"]

    G --> H["Compressed browser-volume endpoint"]
    G --> I["Optimized 3D data endpoint"]
    H --> J["Browser-side MPR engine"]
    I --> K["VTK.js volume renderer"]

    J --> L["Axial viewport"]
    J --> M["Coronal viewport"]
    J --> N["Sagittal viewport"]
    K --> O["Interactive 3D viewport"]

    P["Generated password / session"] --> Q["Temporary HTTPS tunnel"]
    Q --> G

    style A fill:#e9f4ff,stroke:#417aaa,color:#163c60
    style F fill:#fff4e5,stroke:#bf862c,color:#5e4010
    style J fill:#eaf8ed,stroke:#36804b,color:#21562e
    style K fill:#eaf8ed,stroke:#36804b,color:#21562e
```

### How a DICOM Study Becomes a Browser-Rendered Volume

```mermaid
sequenceDiagram
    autonumber

    participant U as User
    participant N as Colab Notebook
    participant D as DICOM Dataset
    participant S as FastAPI Server
    participant B as Web Browser

    U->>N: Enter patient folder path
    N->>D: Scan available DICOM files
    D-->>N: Return image slices and metadata

    N->>N: Validate and organize DICOM series
    N->>N: Decode image slices
    N->>N: Reconstruct 3D volume
    N->>N: Generate optimized runtime files

    N->>S: Start FastAPI backend
    S-->>N: Server running

    N-->>U: Generate secure viewer URL
    U->>B: Open viewer URL
    B->>S: Request authentication
    S-->>B: Authentication successful

    B->>S: Request volume metadata
    S-->>B: Return metadata

    B->>S: Request compressed volume
    S-->>B: Deliver optimized volume

    B->>B: Initialize MPR rendering engine
    B->>B: Initialize VTK.js 3D renderer

    loop Slice Navigation
        U->>B: Scroll mouse wheel
        B->>B: Update active slice locally
        B->>B: Render selected MPR plane
    end

    U->>B: Rotate or zoom 3D volume
    B->>B: Update interactive 3D visualization
```
```

### The four-view workspace

```mermaid
flowchart LR
    V["Selected 3D voxel volume"] --> AX["Axial (XY): top ↔ bottom"]
    V --> CO["Coronal (XZ): front ↔ back"]
    V --> SA["Sagittal (YZ): left ↔ right"]
    V --> VR["3D volume: interactive spatial overview"]
```

> Orientation and radiological display conventions must be verified against DICOM image-position, image-orientation and patient-coordinate tags; the simplified labels above explain the intent, not diagnostic orientation guarantees.

## 🧠 Processing Pipeline — Detailed Explanation

**1. Discover DICOM slices.** The scanner examines the selected folder and reads study/series metadata. Files belonging to a consistent acquisition are grouped so unrelated series are not silently combined.

**2. Reconstruct the volume.** DICOM frames are decoded and organized into an ordered 3D numeric array. Slice order, spacing and image geometry matter: treating a volume as a random collection of JPEGs would destroy its spatial meaning.

**3. Prepare preview and rendering data.** A volume representation and previews are written to temporary Colab locations. The 3D renderer uses an optimized representation to limit interactive rendering costs. These runtime outputs are separate from the original DICOM inputs.

**4. Serve data through the API.** FastAPI supplies the viewer HTML/assets, metadata and the volume payload. The later optimized Colab path supports compressed delivery to the browser.

**5. Render orthogonal slices locally.** Given volume `V[z, y, x]`, an axial plane uses `V[z, :, :]`; a coronal plane uses `V[:, y, :]`; and a sagittal plane uses `V[:, :, x]`. Proper spatial scaling uses the voxel spacing associated with each axis.

**6. Apply display transforms.** Window and level map a selected voxel-intensity range into displayed grayscale values. Browser-side control and suitable interpolation help preserve anatomical readability; image appearance still depends on the original scan, acquisition protocol and decoding quality.

**7. Render the 3D volume.** VTK.js displays a volume representation for interactive camera rotation, zooming and spatial exploration. Surface/volume appearance depends on transfer-function configuration and image density.

**8. End the session safely.** The public access link is temporary. The notebook runtime may disconnect or expire, at which point the server and tunnel must be relaunched. Do not expose credentials in logs or screenshots.

## 🚀 Running the Project

### Option A — Google Colab (recommended for the demonstrated viewer)

1. Open `notebooks/sinus_viewer.ipynb` in Google Colab.
2. Connect a runtime and mount your Google Drive if prompted/needed.
3. Run the **one-cell launcher**.
4. When prompted, paste the **full path to a patient directory containing authorized DICOM data**, e.g.:

   ```text
   /content/drive/MyDrive/<your-private-dataset>/<patient-folder>
   ```

5. Allow the notebook to scan the folder, select/prepare the DICOM series and start the temporary server.
6. Open the generated HTTPS viewer URL and enter the generated password.
7. Navigate axial/coronal/sagittal slices with the mouse wheel, and interact with the 3D viewer.

**Runtime behavior:** A Colab disconnect invalidates the session; reconnect and rerun the launcher. The notebook generates files under a temporary `/content/sinus_viewer_runtime` location and should not modify source DICOM files. Confirm that your Google Drive is mounted after runtime restart.

**Privacy warning:** Colab processes files on a hosted runtime, and the browser viewer is exposed through a temporary tunnel. Use only data that you are authorized to process under applicable privacy rules. De-identify test samples and restrict access.

### Option B — Modular full-stack source (development track, not fully verified)

The uploaded React source declares Node `>=22.12.0` and npm `>=10.0.0`. Backend requirements recommend Python 3.11. Some integrations/routes remain scaffolding, so this path is **for development and verification**, not a promise of an immediately functional production viewer.

Frontend:
```bash
cd frontend
npm ci
npm run dev
```

Backend (separate terminal, from the repository root):
```bash
python -m venv .venv
# Windows PowerShell:
.venv\Scripts\Activate.ps1
# Linux/macOS:
# source .venv/bin/activate
pip install -r backend/requirements.txt
uvicorn backend.app.main:app --reload
```

> Backend configuration, permitted dataset roots, session settings, frontend API base URL, and optional auth credentials must be reviewed before using live data. The default startup and routing require local integration validation; do not publicly expose this development server.

## 🛠️ Technology Stack

| Layer | Technology | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| Notebook runtime | Google Colab / Python | Fast startup, dataset access, preprocessing |
| Medical image I/O | pydicom, SimpleITK (declared in backend) | DICOM metadata/pixel decoding and volume workflows |
| Numeric processing | NumPy, SciPy | 3D arrays and image processing |
| Backend | FastAPI, Uvicorn | Metadata, streaming, temporary browser endpoints |
| Frontend (modular) | React 19, TypeScript, Vite 6 | Browser UI / state / controls |
| 3D | VTK.js | Interactive volume rendering |
| Imaging framework (modular dependency) | Cornerstone3D | Web medical-imaging integration |
| Networking (Colab) | Cloudflare Tunnel | Temporary remote browser access |
| Styling | Tailwind CSS | Modular UI styles |

## ⚡ Performance Design

### Why slice-by-slice network rendering feels slow
When every wheel event triggers a fresh server request, perceived latency accumulates from network travel, image conversion and decoding. Quickly moving across many slices can produce visually discontinuous updates.

### What was optimized
- **Browser-resident volume**: reduce per-scroll server communication after initial loading.
- **Compressed data transport**: reduce transfer size where the volume representation compresses effectively.
- **Optimized 3D representation**: preserve interactive orbiting without forcing full-resolution CPU/GPU costs in every view.
- **Independent rendering paths**: MPR can prioritize navigable, sharp slices while 3D emphasizes responsiveness.
- **Voxel-spacing-aware display**: prevent simple geometry distortion caused by non-isotropic acquisition spacing.

Tradeoffs remain: loading large browser volumes consumes RAM, downsampling may sacrifice 3D detail, compressed payloads have decoding costs, and actual frame rates vary by hardware and scan size.

## 📊 Results and Current Status

### Observed qualitative outcomes

| Area | Outcome | Evidence level |
|---|---|---|
| 3D visualization | Interactive 3D viewer reported working well in the user's test session | User-observed, qualitative |
| Downloaded/browser-resident MPR | Smooth slice scrolling reported as a major improvement over the earlier network-driven 2D viewer | User-observed, qualitative |
| Earlier MPR iteration | Scrolling and clarity were reported as poor, prompting cache/browser-side redesign | User-observed, qualitative |
| One-cell execution | Consolidated launcher exists in the uploaded notebook | Source verified |
| Source-data protection | Explicit read-only source design and temporary runtime outputs | Code/design verified, no independent filesystem audit |
| Modular web app | Major source modules exist; several files are empty | Source inspected, end-to-end integration unverified |

### Benchmarks not yet supplied

No reproducible **FPS, average scrolling latency, initial volume load time, dataset-size comparison, diagnostic-quality benchmark, or GPU/RAM measurement** was provided in the archive. Do not label unmeasured improvements as “60 FPS”, “10× faster”, or “zero latency.”

Suggested future benchmark protocol:
1. Select a **de-identified** CT/CBCT series and record slice count, volume dimensions, voxel spacing and input size.
2. Measure preprocessing time, compressed/uncompressed payload size, and initial loading time.
3. Measure median / p95 scrolling response and animation smoothness over a fixed navigation script.
4. Record peak browser memory, CPU/GPU usage and 3D frame rate.
5. Repeat across 2–3 hardware/browser combinations and report the same test cases and methodology.

## 🔐 Data Safety and Privacy

- **Never** include real patient DICOM folders, patient identifiers, study UIDs tied to patients, logs with identifiers, or private configuration values in a public repository.
- The working Colab flow is designed not to edit, move, rename or delete original DICOM files; confirm all code changes preserve this invariant.
- Keep generated volumes, previews and caches in isolated runtime directories.
- Do not publish `.env`, session tokens, generated passwords, temporary tunnel URLs or Drive paths.
- Public tunnels are not a substitute for institutional access controls, encryption policy, auditing or regulatory review.
- Only use anonymized/de-identified demonstration images for documentation.
- Clear downloaded data from shared/browser devices when appropriate.

## ⚠️ Limitations

- **Not clinically validated** as a diagnostic viewer.
- Current results are qualitative; quantitative benchmark evidence is pending.
- Browser-side volume loading can be memory-intensive for large acquisitions.
- Visualization quality depends on DICOM codecs, voxel spacing, scanner parameters and rendering settings.
- The hosted Colab runtime and temporary public tunnel can disconnect.
- The modular React/FastAPI source includes unimplemented scaffolding and should be integrated/tested before presenting it as production-ready.
- Loading the entire DICOM input from hosted Colab can be inappropriate for confidential medical datasets without approved data governance.

### sinus-viewer/frontend/postcss.config.js
```
/**
 * ============================================================
 * SINUS VIEWER — POSTCSS CONFIGURATION
 * ============================================================
 *
 * Application : Sinus Viewer
 * Frontend    : React 19 + TypeScript + Vite 6
 * CSS Engine  : Tailwind CSS v3 + PostCSS
 *
 * PURPOSE:
 * - Generate Tailwind utility classes
 * - Add browser-compatible CSS vendor prefixes
 * - Process production CSS through Vite
 *
 * DEPLOYMENT:
 * - No local dependency installation
 * - CSS compilation happens in Google Colab
 *
 * ============================================================
 */

import tailwindcss from "tailwindcss";
import autoprefixer from "autoprefixer";

/**
 * PostCSS configuration.
 *
 * Plugin execution order:
 *
 * 1. Tailwind CSS
 *    Expands @tailwind directives and utility classes.
 *
 * 2. Autoprefixer
 *    Adds necessary vendor prefixes based on
 *    supported browser targets.
 *
 * Vite performs production CSS bundling and minification
 * when the frontend production build is executed.
 */

const config = {
  plugins: [
    tailwindcss({
      config: "./tailwind.config.ts",
    }),

    autoprefixer(),
  ],
};

export default config;
```

### sinus-viewer/frontend/tailwind.config.ts
```
/**

 * ============================================================
 *
 * Professional Medical Imaging Workstation
 * Theme: Dark Radiology Interface
 * Tailwind CSS: v3
 *
 * This file defines design tokens only.
 * Actual component layouts are implemented separately.
 * ============================================================

  darkMode: ["class"],

  content: [
    "./index.html",
    "./src/**/*.{js,ts,jsx,tsx}",
  ],

        lg: "2rem",
        xl: "2.5rem",

        "2xl": "1440px",

    },

      // ------------------------------------------------------
      // COLOR SYSTEM
      // ------------------------------------------------------

      colors: {
        border: "hsl(var(--border) / <alpha-value>)",
        input: "hsl(var(--input) / <alpha-value>)",
        ring: "hsl(var(--ring) / <alpha-value>)",

        },

        },

        primary: {
          DEFAULT: "hsl(var(--primary) / <alpha-value>)",
          foreground: "hsl(var(--primary-foreground) / <alpha-value>)",
        },

        secondary: {
          DEFAULT: "hsl(var(--secondary) / <alpha-value>)",
          foreground: "hsl(var(--secondary-foreground) / <alpha-value>)",
        },

        muted: {
          DEFAULT: "hsl(var(--muted) / <alpha-value>)",

          foreground: "hsl(var(--accent-foreground) / <alpha-value>)",
        },

        destructive: {
          DEFAULT: "hsl(var(--destructive) / <alpha-value>)",

        },

 * IMPORTANT:

### sinus-viewer/frontend/vite.config.ts
```
/**
 * ============================================================
 * SINUS VIEWER — VITE CONFIGURATION
 * ============================================================
 *
 * Frontend:
 *   React 19 + TypeScript + Vite 6
 *
 * Medical Rendering:
 *   Cornerstone3D + vtk.js
 *
 * Deployment:
 *   Development: Vite
 *   Production: FastAPI serves the compiled frontend
 *   Hosting: Google Colab + authenticated external tunnel
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 *   No software dependencies need to be installed locally.
 *   All npm installation and building happens in Colab.
 * ============================================================
 */

import { defineConfig, loadEnv, type UserConfig } from "vite";
import react from "@vitejs/plugin-react";
import { fileURLToPath, URL } from "node:url";

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// APPLICATION CONSTANTS
// ------------------------------------------------------------

const APP_NAME = "Sinus Viewer";
const FRONTEND_PORT = 5173;
const DEFAULT_BACKEND_URL = "http://127.0.0.1:8000";

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// PATH ALIAS HELPER
// ------------------------------------------------------------

function srcPath(relativePath: string): string {
  return fileURLToPath(
    new URL(`./src/${relativePath}`, import.meta.url)
  );
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// VITE CONFIGURATION
// ------------------------------------------------------------

export default defineConfig(({ mode }): UserConfig => {
  const env = loadEnv(mode, process.cwd(), "VITE_");

  const backendTarget =
    env.VITE_DEV_BACKEND_URL || DEFAULT_BACKEND_URL;

  const config: UserConfig = {
    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // APPLICATION BASE
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    base: "/",

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // REACT PLUGIN
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    plugins: [
      react({
        jsxRuntime: "automatic",
      }),
    ],

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // SOURCE PATH ALIASES
    // Keep synchronized with tsconfig.json
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    resolve: {
      alias: {
        "@": srcPath(""),
        "@components": srcPath("components"),
        "@pages": srcPath("pages"),
        "@viewer": srcPath("viewer"),
        "@services": srcPath("services"),
        "@store": srcPath("store"),
        "@types": srcPath("types"),
      },
      dedupe: [
        "react",
        "react-dom",
      ],
    },

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // DEVELOPMENT SERVER
    // Not exposed directly through the public tunnel.
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    server: {
      host: "127.0.0.1",
      port: FRONTEND_PORT,
      strictPort: true,

      open: false,

      // Allow the backend to receive API requests in dev.
      proxy: {
        "/api": {
          target: backendTarget,
          changeOrigin: false,
          secure: true,
          ws: false,
        },
      },

      // Restrict the development file server.
      fs: {
        strict: true,
        deny: [
          ".env",
          ".env.*",
          "*.pem",
          "*.key",
          "*.p12",
          "*.dcm",
          "*.dicom",
          "*.nii",
          "*.nii.gz",
          "**/.git/**",
        ],
      },
    },

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // PRODUCTION BUILD
    // FastAPI will serve the resulting dist directory.
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    build: {
      target: "es2022",

      outDir: "dist",

      assetsDir: "assets",

      emptyOutDir: true,

      sourcemap: false,

      minify: "esbuild",

      cssCodeSplit: true,

      modulePreload: {
        polyfill: true,
      },

      // Preserve worker compatibility for medical decoding.
      assetsInlineLimit: 0,

      chunkSizeWarningLimit: 1500,

      reportCompressedSize: true,
    },

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // WEB WORKERS
    // Used by decoding and medical rendering libraries.
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    worker: {
      format: "es",
    },

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // DEPENDENCY OPTIMIZATION
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    optimizeDeps: {
      include: [
        "react",
        "react-dom",
        "react-router-dom",
        "zustand",
      ],
    },

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // GLOBAL BUILD CONSTANTS
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    define: {
      __APP_NAME__: JSON.stringify(APP_NAME),
      __APP_VERSION__: JSON.stringify("0.1.0"),
    },

    // --------------------------------------------------------
    // BUILD LOGGING
    // --------------------------------------------------------

    clearScreen: false,

    logLevel: "info",
  };

  return config;
});

### sinus-viewer/backend/app/main.py
```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — FASTAPI MAIN APPLICATION
=======================================

Central backend application entry point.

Responsibilities:
- Load and validate application configuration.
- Construct FastAPI with a controlled lifespan.
- Initialize authentication and imaging services.
- Register the complete application service bundle.
- Register all previously implemented API routers.
- Activate the central security middleware.
- Normalize API errors without leaking patient data.
- Release service references at shutdown.
- Preserve original DICOM dataset read-only.

Deployment assumptions:
- One Uvicorn worker for process-local sessions and cache.
- Google Colab/Linux installs backend dependencies.
- Public browser access only through an appropriately
  secured HTTPS reverse proxy / tunnel.
- No anonymous access to medical image endpoints.
- No automatic dataset scan at startup.
- No writes to original Google Drive DICOM directories.

This file does NOT:
- Mount Google Drive.
- Install Python dependencies.
- Start a public tunnel.
- Build the React frontend.
- Create or modify original dataset files.

Python 3.10+
FastAPI / Pydantic v2
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from contextlib import asynccontextmanager
from typing import AsyncIterator

from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException, Request
from fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
from starlette.responses import Response

from .api.dependencies import (
    ApplicationServices,
    register_application_services,
    unregister_application_services,
)
from .api.routes import (
    auth,
    datasets,
    health,
    previews,
    streaming,
    volumes,
)
from .api.schemas import ERROR_MESSAGES, PublicErrorCode
from .core.config import AppSettings, get_settings
from .security.auth import (
    AuthenticationService,
    create_authentication_service,
)
from .security.middleware import register_security_middleware
from .services.cache_service import (
    VolumeCacheService,
    create_volume_cache,
)
from .services.dataset_service import (
    DatasetService,
    create_dataset_service,
)
from .services.preview_service import (
    PreviewService,
    create_preview_service,
)
from .services.streaming_service import (
    VolumeStreamingService,
    create_streaming_service,
)
from .services.volume_service import (
    VolumeService,
    create_volume_service,
)


# ============================================================
# 01 — APPLICATION CONSTANTS
# ============================================================

APP_TITLE = "Sinus Viewer"
APP_VERSION = "0.1.0"

API_PREFIX = "/api"

# Source code contains no dataset-specific patient details.
# Avoid exposing Swagger/OpenAPI schemas anonymously.
ENABLE_PUBLIC_API_DOCS = False

SAFE_RESPONSE_HEADERS = {
    "Cache-Control": "no-store, private",
    "Pragma": "no-cache",
    "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
}


# ============================================================
# 02 — SAFE ERROR CODES
# ============================================================

HTTP_ERROR_CODES: dict[int, PublicErrorCode] = {
    400: PublicErrorCode.INVALID_REQUEST,
    401: PublicErrorCode.AUTH_REQUIRED,
    403: PublicErrorCode.ACCESS_DENIED,
    404: PublicErrorCode.INVALID_REQUEST,
    405: PublicErrorCode.INVALID_REQUEST,
    409: PublicErrorCode.PROCESSING_FAILED,
    413: PublicErrorCode.REQUEST_TOO_LARGE,
    416: PublicErrorCode.RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE,
    422: PublicErrorCode.INVALID_REQUEST,
    429: PublicErrorCode.RATE_LIMITED,
    500: PublicErrorCode.PROCESSING_FAILED,
    503: PublicErrorCode.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
    507: PublicErrorCode.OUT_OF_MEMORY,
}


# ============================================================
# 03 — ERROR RESPONSE FACTORY
# ============================================================

def create_safe_error_response(
    *,
    status_code: int,
    code: PublicErrorCode,
    request: Request,
    additional_headers: dict[str, str] | None = None,
) -> JSONResponse:
    """
    Produce a consistent, non-disclosing API error.

    Only allowlisted error codes and messages are exposed.
    No internal exception text, DICOM paths or patient data.
    """

    request_id = getattr(
        request.state,
        "request_id",
        None,
    )

    headers = dict(SAFE_RESPONSE_HEADERS)

    if additional_headers:
        # Only known HTTP headers are forwarded from the
        # original controlled HTTPException.
        permitted = {
            "retry-after",
            "www-authenticate",
            "content-range",
        }

        for name, value in additional_headers.items():
            if (
                name.lower() in permitted
                and isinstance(value, str)
                and "\r" not in value
                and "\n" not in value
            ):
                headers[name] = value

    if (
        isinstance(request_id, str)
        and len(request_id) == 32
        and all(c in "0123456789abcdef" for c in request_id)
    ):
        headers["X-Request-ID"] = request_id
    else:
        request_id = None

    content = {
        "success": False,
        "error": {
            "code": code.value,
            "message": ERROR_MESSAGES[code],
            "retryable": status_code in (429, 503),
            "request_id": request_id,
        },
    }

    return JSONResponse(
        status_code=status_code,
        content=content,
        headers=headers,
    )


# ============================================================
# 04 — SAFE HTTP ERROR HANDLER
# ============================================================

async def http_exception_handler(
    request: Request,
    exc: HTTPException,
) -> Response:
    """
    Normalize HTTP errors into the centralized API schema.

    Route-supplied detail dictionaries are not copied
    blindly to the browser.
    """

    status_code = int(exc.status_code)

    code = HTTP_ERROR_CODES.get(
        status_code,
        PublicErrorCode.PROCESSING_FAILED,
    )

    detail = exc.detail

    if isinstance(detail, dict):
        candidate = detail.get("code")

        if isinstance(candidate, str):
            try:
                selected = PublicErrorCode(candidate)
            except ValueError:
                selected = None

            if selected is not None:
                code = selected

    return create_safe_error_response(
        status_code=status_code,
        code=code,
        request=request,
        additional_headers=exc.headers,
    )


# ============================================================
# 05 — REQUEST VALIDATION HANDLER
# ============================================================

async def request_validation_exception_handler(
    request: Request,
    exc: RequestValidationError,
) -> Response:
    """
    Return a generic 422 without echoing submitted values.

    FastAPI's default validation error may include request
    fields or rejected input. That detail is intentionally
    omitted for this medical-data API.
    """

    return create_safe_error_response(
        status_code=422,
        code=PublicErrorCode.INVALID_REQUEST,
        request=request,
    )


# ============================================================
# 06 — UNHANDLED ERROR HANDLER
# ============================================================

async def unexpected_exception_handler(
    request: Request,
    exc: Exception,
) -> Response:
    """
    Safe fallback for unexpected application exceptions.

    Do not log str(exc), traceback locals or request bodies:
    they may contain patient data or credentials.
    """

    return create_safe_error_response(
        status_code=500,
        code=PublicErrorCode.PROCESSING_FAILED,
        request=request,
    )


# ============================================================
# 07 — SERVICE INITIALIZATION
# ============================================================

def initialize_application_services(
    settings: AppSettings,
) -> ApplicationServices:
    """
    Initialize backend services in dependency order.

    Existing original DICOM files are neither opened nor
    modified during routine service construction.

    Authentication configuration fails closed when required
    environment credentials are unavailable.
    """

    authentication: AuthenticationService | None = None
    datasets: DatasetService | None = None
    cache: VolumeCacheService | None = None
    volumes: VolumeService | None = None
    previews: PreviewService | None = None
    streaming: VolumeStreamingService | None = None

    try:
        authentication = create_authentication_service(
            settings
        )

        datasets = create_dataset_service(
            settings
        )

        cache = create_volume_cache(
            settings
        )

        volumes = create_volume_service(
            dataset_service=datasets,
            cache_service=cache,
            settings=settings,
        )

        previews = create_preview_service(
            dataset_service=datasets,
            settings=settings,
        )

        streaming = create_streaming_service(
            volume_service=volumes,
            settings=settings,
        )

        bundle = ApplicationServices(
            authentication=authentication,
            datasets=datasets,
            cache=cache,
            volumes=volumes,
            previews=previews,
            streaming=streaming,
            settings=settings,
        )

        bundle.validate()

        return bundle

    except Exception:
        # Cleanup partial construction without hiding
        # the original startup failure.
        for service in (
            streaming,
            previews,
            volumes,
            cache,
            datasets,
            authentication,
        ):
            if service is not None:
                try:
                    service.close()
                except Exception:
                    pass

        raise


# ============================================================
# 08 — SERVICE SHUTDOWN
# ============================================================

def shutdown_application_services(
    services: ApplicationServices,
) -> None:
    """
    Close services in reverse dependency order.

    This detaches references held by process-local caches
    and dataset registries. Active worker requests must
    be drained by the ASGI server before shutdown.

    No original DICOM source files are deleted or modified.
    """

    for service in (
        services.streaming,
        services.previews,
        services.volumes,
        services.cache,
        services.datasets,
        services.authentication,
    ):
        try:
            service.close()
        except Exception:
            # Continue closing remaining services.
            # Do not expose internal details.
            pass


# ============================================================
# 09 — FASTAPI LIFESPAN
# ============================================================

@asynccontextmanager
async def application_lifespan(
    app: FastAPI,
) -> AsyncIterator[None]:
    """
    Create services on startup and release on shutdown.

    Only one process-local service bundle is registered
    per application instance.
    """

    settings = app.state.bootstrap_settings

    services = initialize_application_services(
        settings
    )

    try:
        register_application_services(
            app,
            services,
        )

        yield

    finally:
        shutdown_application_services(services)

        unregister_application_services(app)


# ============================================================
# 10 — REGISTER API ROUTERS
# ============================================================

def register_api_routes(
    application: FastAPI,
) -> None:
    """
    Register all implemented backend routers.

    Each router retains its existing /api prefix.
    No duplicate prefixes are applied here.
    """

    application.include_router(auth.router)
    application.include_router(datasets.router)
    application.include_router(volumes.router)
    application.include_router(streaming.router)
    application.include_router(previews.router)
    application.include_router(health.router)


# ============================================================
# 11 — REGISTER SAFE ERROR HANDLERS
# ============================================================

def register_error_handlers(
    application: FastAPI,
) -> None:
    application.add_exception_handler(
        HTTPException,
        http_exception_handler,
    )

    application.add_exception_handler(
        RequestValidationError,
        request_validation_exception_handler,
    )

    application.add_exception_handler(
        Exception,
        unexpected_exception_handler,
    )


# ============================================================
# 12 — APPLICATION FACTORY
# ============================================================

def create_app(
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
) -> FastAPI:
    """
    Build the Sinus Viewer FastAPI application.

    The startup lifespan initializes credentials, services
    and authenticated application state.

    OpenAPI/Swagger docs are disabled on the public server.
    """

    active = (
        settings
        if settings is not None
        else get_settings()
    )

    application = FastAPI(
        title=APP_TITLE,
        version=APP_VERSION,
        docs_url=None if not ENABLE_PUBLIC_API_DOCS else "/docs",
        redoc_url=None,
        openapi_url=None if not ENABLE_PUBLIC_API_DOCS else "/openapi.json",
        lifespan=application_lifespan,
    )

    application.state.bootstrap_settings = active

    register_error_handlers(application)
    register_api_routes(application)

    # Registration occurs at application-construction time
    # before Starlette builds the ASGI middleware stack.
    register_security_middleware(
        application,
        active,
    )

    return application


# ============================================================
# 13 — ASGI APPLICATION
# ============================================================

app = create_app()


# ============================================================
# 14 — STATIC CAPABILITY REPORT
# ============================================================

def get_main_application_capabilities() -> dict:
    return {
        "application": APP_TITLE,
        "version": APP_VERSION,
        "api_prefix": API_PREFIX,
        "authentication": True,
        "security_middleware": True,
        "service_registry": True,
        "lifespan_management": True,
        "dataset_routes": True,
        "volume_routes": True,
        "streaming_routes": True,
        "preview_routes": True,
        "health_routes": True,
        "public_api_docs": ENABLE_PUBLIC_API_DOCS,
        "read_only_original_dataset": True,
        "frontend_build_included": False,
        "tunnel_launcher_included": False,
    }


# ============================================================
# END OF SINUS VIEWER MAIN APPLICATION
# ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/frontend/src/vite-env.d.ts
```
/// <reference types="vite/client" />

/**
 * ============================================================
 * SINUS VIEWER — VITE ENVIRONMENT TYPE DECLARATIONS
 * ============================================================
 *
 * Application: Sinus Viewer
 * Frontend: React + TypeScript + Vite
 *
 * PURPOSE:
 * 1. Type-check public frontend environment variables.
 * 2. Declare build-time application constants.
 * 3. Provide static asset and worker module types.
 *
 * SECURITY:
 * Every VITE_* environment variable may be exposed to the
 * browser bundle. Never place patient data, passwords,
 * API secrets, private keys, or access tokens in VITE_*.
 *
 * No local dependencies are required to write this file.
 * ============================================================
 */

/**
 * Public environment variables made available by Vite.
 *
 * These are deployment settings, not secrets.
 */
interface ImportMetaEnv {
  /**
   * Optional public application title.
   */
  readonly VITE_APP_NAME?: string;

  /**
   * Optional public application version.
   */
  readonly VITE_APP_VERSION?: string;

  /**
   * Browser API base path.
   *
   * Production default: /api
   *
   * Prefer same-origin relative paths rather than
   * exposing Colab's internal backend address.
   */
  readonly VITE_API_BASE_URL?: string;

  /**
   * Development-only Vite proxy destination.
   *
   * Used by vite.config.ts during development.
   * Must not contain authentication credentials.
   */
  readonly VITE_DEV_BACKEND_URL?: string;

  /**
   * Optional public deployment label.
   *
   * Examples:
   * development
   * staging
   * production
   */
  readonly VITE_DEPLOYMENT_ENV?: string;

  /**
   * Optional public feature flags.
   *
   * Parse and validate these values at runtime.
   * Vite environment values are strings.
   */
  readonly VITE_ENABLE_3D?: string;
  readonly VITE_ENABLE_MEASUREMENTS?: string;
  readonly VITE_ENABLE_DEBUG_UI?: string;
}

/**
 * Extend Vite's import.meta typing.
 */
interface ImportMeta {
  readonly env: ImportMetaEnv;
}

/**
 * Application constants injected through vite.config.ts.
 *
 * These identifiers are replaced during the Vite build.
 */
declare const __APP_NAME__: string;
declare const __APP_VERSION__: string;

/**
 * Static SVG imports.
 */
declare module "*.svg" {
  const source: string;
  export default source;
}

/**
 * Worker imports using the Vite ?worker query.
 */
declare module "*?worker" {
  const WorkerConstructor: {
    new (): Worker;
  };

  export default WorkerConstructor;
}

/**
 * URL-style worker imports.
 */
declare module "*?worker&url" {
  const workerUrl: string;
  export default workerUrl;
}

/**
 * Raw text imports supported by Vite.
 */
declare module "*?raw" {
  const rawContent: string;
  export default rawContent;
}
```

### sinus-viewer/backend/app/api/dependencies.py
```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — FASTAPI SERVICE DEPENDENCIES
===========================================

Central application service access for authenticated routes.

Responsibilities:
- Register shared backend services during FastAPI startup.
- Verify the complete required service registry.
- Resolve service instances from application state.
- Require trusted authentication for protected services.
- Provide typed dependencies for API route modules.
- Prevent accidental per-request service construction.
- Expose minimal, non-sensitive service readiness data.
- Support controlled shutdown through the lifespan manager.

Security contract:
- Authentication derives identity from the server session.
- Never accept owner_id from a browser request parameter.
- Dataset and volume methods still authorize each lookup.
- Services must be registered before requests are served.
- No fallback to a new, unauthenticated service instance.
- No source paths, DICOM identifiers or credentials in errors.
- The health dependency exposes service readiness only.
- Shutdown ordering is managed by the application lifespan.

This module performs no patient dataset file operations
and creates no filesystem artifacts.

Python 3.10+
FastAPI / Pydantic v2
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Annotated, Any, TypeVar

from fastapi import Depends, FastAPI, Request

from ..core.config import AppSettings
from ..core.exceptions import ConfigurationFailure
from ..security.auth import AuthenticationService
from ..security.session import (
    AuthenticatedContext,
    get_authentication_service,
    require_authenticated_context,
)
from ..services.cache_service import VolumeCacheService
from ..services.dataset_service import DatasetService
from ..services.preview_service import PreviewService
from ..services.streaming_service import VolumeStreamingService
from ..services.volume_service import VolumeService


# ============================================================
# 01 — APPLICATION STATE NAMES
# ============================================================

AUTH_STATE = "auth_service"
DATASET_STATE = "dataset_service"
CACHE_STATE = "cache_service"
VOLUME_STATE = "volume_service"
PREVIEW_STATE = "preview_service"
STREAMING_STATE = "streaming_service"
SETTINGS_STATE = "settings"

REGISTRY_FLAG = "sinus_services_registered"

REQUIRED_SERVICE_NAMES = (
    AUTH_STATE,
    DATASET_STATE,
    CACHE_STATE,
    VOLUME_STATE,
    PREVIEW_STATE,
    STREAMING_STATE,
)

SERVICE_TYPES: tuple[tuple[str, type], ...] = (
    (AUTH_STATE, AuthenticationService),
    (DATASET_STATE, DatasetService),
    (CACHE_STATE, VolumeCacheService),
    (VOLUME_STATE, VolumeService),
    (PREVIEW_STATE, PreviewService),
    (STREAMING_STATE, VolumeStreamingService),
)

T = TypeVar("T")


# ============================================================
# 02 — CONTROLLED REGISTRY ERRORS
# ============================================================

class ServiceRegistryError(RuntimeError):
    """
    Internal service registration error.

    No environment secrets, patient metadata or
    filesystem paths are attached.
    """

    public_code = "CONFIG_ERROR"

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(self.public_code)


class ServiceAlreadyRegistered(ServiceRegistryError):
    pass


class ServiceNotAvailable(ServiceRegistryError):
    pass


# ============================================================
# 03 — IMMUTABLE SERVICE BUNDLE
# ============================================================

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ApplicationServices:
    """
    References to the six process-local services.

    Service instances themselves manage mutable state.
    This container does not copy volumes or patient data.
    """

    authentication: AuthenticationService
    datasets: DatasetService
    cache: VolumeCacheService
    volumes: VolumeService
    previews: PreviewService
    streaming: VolumeStreamingService
    settings: AppSettings

    def validate(self) -> None:
        """
        Enforce exact expected service categories.

        Existing service instances must be supplied by
        the application's trusted startup code.
        """

        expected = (
            (self.authentication, AuthenticationService),
            (self.datasets, DatasetService),
            (self.cache, VolumeCacheService),
            (self.volumes, VolumeService),
            (self.previews, PreviewService),
            (self.streaming, VolumeStreamingService),
            (self.settings, AppSettings),
        )

        for instance, expected_type in expected:
            if not isinstance(instance, expected_type):
                raise ServiceRegistryError()


# ============================================================
# 04 — REGISTER APPLICATION SERVICES
# ============================================================

def register_application_services(
    app: FastAPI,
    services: ApplicationServices,
) -> None:
    """
    Register all services atomically during application
    construction/lifespan initialization.

    Registration must happen before serving HTTP requests.

    The service bundle is built by trusted startup code
    after configuration and credential validation.
    """

    if not isinstance(app, FastAPI):
        raise TypeError("FastAPI application required.")

    if not isinstance(services, ApplicationServices):
        raise ServiceRegistryError()

    services.validate()

    state = app.state

    if getattr(state, REGISTRY_FLAG, False):
        raise ServiceAlreadyRegistered()

    assignments = (
        (AUTH_STATE, services.authentication),
        (DATASET_STATE, services.datasets),
        (CACHE_STATE, services.cache),
        (VOLUME_STATE, services.volumes),
        (PREVIEW_STATE, services.previews),
        (STREAMING_STATE, services.streaming),
        (SETTINGS_STATE, services.settings),
    )

    # Reject partial or conflicting registration.
    # This intentionally detects services previously
    # registered by other startup helpers.
    for name, _ in assignments:
        if getattr(state, name, None) is not None:
            raise ServiceAlreadyRegistered()

    for name, instance in assignments:
        setattr(state, name, instance)

    setattr(state, REGISTRY_FLAG, True)


# ============================================================
# 05 — REGISTRY INSPECTION
# ============================================================

def validate_service_registry(
    app: FastAPI,
) -> ApplicationServices:
    """
    Verify that every required service exists and has
    the expected type.

    Does not read source files or disclose service secrets.
    """

    state = app.state

    if not getattr(state, REGISTRY_FLAG, False):
        raise ServiceNotAvailable()

    for name, expected_type in SERVICE_TYPES:
        instance = getattr(state, name, None)

        if not isinstance(instance, expected_type):
            raise ServiceNotAvailable()

    settings = getattr(
        state,
        SETTINGS_STATE,
        None,
    )

    if not isinstance(settings, AppSettings):
        raise ServiceNotAvailable()

    return ApplicationServices(
        authentication=getattr(state, AUTH_STATE),
        datasets=getattr(state, DATASET_STATE),
        cache=getattr(state, CACHE_STATE),
        volumes=getattr(state, VOLUME_STATE),
        previews=getattr(state, PREVIEW_STATE),
        streaming=getattr(state, STREAMING_STATE),
        settings=settings,
    )


# ============================================================
# 06 — INTERNAL SERVICE LOOKUP
# ============================================================

def _get_registered_service(
    request: Request,
    state_name: str,
    expected_type: type[T],
) -> T:
    """
    Resolve one service with fail-closed checks.

    No new service is constructed for a request.
    """

    if not getattr(
        request.app.state,
        REGISTRY_FLAG,
        False,
    ):
        raise ConfigurationFailure()

    result = getattr(
        request.app.state,
        state_name,
        None,
    )

    if not isinstance(result, expected_type):
        raise ConfigurationFailure()

    return result


# ============================================================
# 07 — INTERNAL / PUBLIC SERVICE ACCESS
# ============================================================

def get_app_settings(
    request: Request,
) -> AppSettings:
    """
    Resolve application settings from trusted startup.

    Never return this object directly in an HTTP response.
    It may contain private configuration values.
    """

    return _get_registered_service(
        request,
        SETTINGS_STATE,
        AppSettings,
    )


def get_public_health_status(
    request: Request,
) -> dict[str, str]:
    """
    Minimal unauthenticated readiness status.

    Does not expose hardware, dataset selection, settings
    or internal service implementation details.
    """

    try:
        validate_service_registry(request.app)
        return {
            "status": "ok",
            "service": "sinus-viewer",
        }
    except ServiceRegistryError:
        return {
            "status": "degraded",
            "service": "sinus-viewer",
        }


# ============================================================
# 08 — PROTECTED DATASET DEPENDENCY
# ============================================================

def require_dataset_service(
    request: Request,
    auth: Annotated[
        AuthenticatedContext,
        Depends(require_authenticated_context),
    ],
) -> DatasetService:
    """
    DatasetService is accessible only after authentication.

    Ownership still must be passed to each dataset method:
        owner_id=auth.owner_id
    """

    return _get_registered_service(
        request,
        DATASET_STATE,
        DatasetService,
    )


# ============================================================
# 09 — PROTECTED CACHE DEPENDENCY
# ============================================================

def require_cache_service(
    request: Request,
    auth: Annotated[
        AuthenticatedContext,
        Depends(require_authenticated_context),
    ],
) -> VolumeCacheService:
    """
    Internal authenticated cache access.

    Never expose administrative clear/invalidate
    functions as unrestricted browser endpoints.
    """

    return _get_registered_service(
        request,
        CACHE_STATE,
        VolumeCacheService,
    )


# ============================================================
# 10 — PROTECTED VOLUME DEPENDENCY
# ============================================================

def require_volume_service(
    request: Request,
    auth: Annotated[
        AuthenticatedContext,
        Depends(require_authenticated_context),
    ],
) -> VolumeService:
    """
    Authenticated access to volume management.

    The route must use auth.owner_id when requesting
    any patient-related imaging content.
    """

    return _get_registered_service(
        request,
        VOLUME_STATE,
        VolumeService,
    )


# ============================================================
# 11 — PROTECTED PREVIEW DEPENDENCY
# ============================================================

def require_preview_service(
    request: Request,
    auth: Annotated[
        AuthenticatedContext,
        Depends(require_authenticated_context),
    ],
) -> PreviewService:
    """
    Authenticated access to low-memory preview generation.
    """

    return _get_registered_service(
        request,
        PREVIEW_STATE,
        PreviewService,
    )


# ============================================================
# 12 — PROTECTED STREAMING DEPENDENCY
# ============================================================

def require_streaming_service(
    request: Request,
    auth: Annotated[
        AuthenticatedContext,
        Depends(require_authenticated_context),
    ],
) -> VolumeStreamingService:
    """
    Authenticated access to bounded volume streaming.

    Pixel data must be requested with auth.owner_id.
    """

    return _get_registered_service(
        request,
        STREAMING_STATE,
        VolumeStreamingService,
    )


# ============================================================
# 13 — AUTHENTICATED API CONTEXT
# ============================================================

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AuthorizedDatasetContext:
    """
    Bound owner identity plus authorized dataset service.

    This object is for internal route handlers only,
    not JSON serialization.
    """

    owner_id: str
    datasets: DatasetService

    def list_datasets(self) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        return self.datasets.list_datasets(
            owner_id=self.owner_id,
        )


def require_authorized_dataset_context(
    auth: Annotated[
        AuthenticatedContext,
        Depends(require_authenticated_context),
    ],
    datasets: Annotated[
        DatasetService,
        Depends(require_dataset_service),
    ],
) -> AuthorizedDatasetContext:
    """
    Bind a verified owner to DatasetService.

    Reduces the chance of routes accidentally accepting
    owner IDs supplied by the browser.
    """

    return AuthorizedDatasetContext(
        owner_id=auth.owner_id,
        datasets=datasets,
    )


# ============================================================
# 14 — SERVICE BUNDLE FOR TRUSTED INTERNAL OPERATIONS
# ============================================================

def get_internal_service_bundle(
    app: FastAPI,
) -> ApplicationServices:
    """
    For application lifespan and trusted internal code only.

    Do not expose this function as a public API dependency.
    It includes administrative-capable service references.
    """

    return validate_service_registry(app)


# ============================================================
# 15 — REGISTRY CLEANUP
# ============================================================

def unregister_application_services(
    app: FastAPI,
) -> None:
    """
    Detach service references after controlled shutdown.

    This function does NOT close services itself.

    Shutdown order should be:
        1. Stop accepting new HTTP requests.
        2. Drain active request/worker tasks.
        3. Close streaming and preview services.
        4. Close volume, cache and dataset services.
        5. Close authentication service.
        6. Unregister the service bundle.

    Only trusted application lifespan code may call this.
    """

    state = app.state

    if not getattr(state, REGISTRY_FLAG, False):
        return

    for name in (
        *REQUIRED_SERVICE_NAMES,
        SETTINGS_STATE,
    ):
        if hasattr(state, name):
            delattr(state, name)

    setattr(state, REGISTRY_FLAG, False)


# ============================================================
# 16 — CAPABILITIES
# ============================================================

def get_dependency_capabilities() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Static, non-sensitive integration feature declaration.
    """

    return {
        "service_registry": True,
        "process_local_services": True,
        "authentication_required": True,
        "trusted_owner_context": True,
        "default_anonymous_service_access": False,
        "request_service_creation": False,
        "patient_data_serialization": False,
        "read_only_dataset": True,
    }


# ============================================================
# END OF FASTAPI DEPENDENCY INJECTION
# ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/backend/app/api/schemas.py
```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — CENTRALIZED FASTAPI SCHEMAS
==========================================

Strict Pydantic v2 contracts for authenticated APIs.

Covers:
- Login / logout / session status
- Dataset selection and indexing
- Patient and series discovery
- Series validation and safe issues
- Volume geometry and preview levels
- Byte-range streaming descriptors
- Service health and sanitized errors

SECURITY:
- Do not serialize pydicom Dataset objects directly.
- Do not return original paths, DICOM UIDs, PatientID,
  PatientName, StudyDate or raw image metadata.
- All identifiers exposed here are opaque server handles.
- These models do not implement authentication themselves.
- API routes must authenticate and authorize before
  constructing responses.
- Volume bytes are transferred separately, never in JSON.

Pydantic v2 / Python 3.10+
No filesystem operations.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import re
from enum import Enum
from typing import Annotated, Literal

from pydantic import (
    BaseModel,
    ConfigDict,
    Field,
    StringConstraints,
    field_validator,
    model_validator,
)


# ============================================================
# 01 — COMMON CONSTRAINTS
# ============================================================

HANDLE_PATTERN = r"^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128}$"
REQUEST_ID_PATTERN = r"^[a-f0-9]{32}$"

OpaqueHandle = Annotated[
    str,
    StringConstraints(
        min_length=1,
        max_length=128,
        pattern=HANDLE_PATTERN,
        strict=True,
    ),
]

RequestId = Annotated[
    str,
    StringConstraints(
        pattern=REQUEST_ID_PATTERN,
        strict=True,
    ),
]

PositiveCount = Annotated[
    int,
    Field(strict=True, ge=0),
]

PositiveSize = Annotated[
    int,
    Field(strict=True, gt=0),
]

FiniteNumber = Annotated[
    float,
    Field(allow_inf_nan=False),
]

PositiveFiniteNumber = Annotated[
    float,
    Field(gt=0, allow_inf_nan=False),
]

Vector3 = tuple[FiniteNumber, FiniteNumber, FiniteNumber]
Direction9 = tuple[
    FiniteNumber, FiniteNumber, FiniteNumber,
    FiniteNumber, FiniteNumber, FiniteNumber,
    FiniteNumber, FiniteNumber, FiniteNumber,
]

Shape3 = tuple[PositiveSize, PositiveSize, PositiveSize]
Shape2 = tuple[PositiveSize, PositiveSize]


class StrictAPIModel(BaseModel):
    """
    Every JSON API model rejects undeclared fields.

    Never pass an entire internal object through
    model_dump() or attribute reflection into these schemas.
    """

    model_config = ConfigDict(
        extra="forbid",
        validate_assignment=True,
        use_enum_values=False,
    )


# ============================================================
# 02 — SAFE ERROR CONTRACT
# ============================================================

class PublicErrorCode(str, Enum):
    AUTH_REQUIRED = "AUTH_REQUIRED"
    AUTH_FAILED = "AUTH_FAILED"
    SESSION_EXPIRED = "SESSION_EXPIRED"
    ACCESS_DENIED = "ACCESS_DENIED"
    RATE_LIMITED = "RATE_LIMITED"
    INVALID_REQUEST = "INVALID_REQUEST"
    REQUEST_TOO_LARGE = "REQUEST_TOO_LARGE"
    PATH_REJECTED = "PATH_REJECTED"
    DATASET_NOT_FOUND = "DATASET_NOT_FOUND"
    PATIENT_NOT_FOUND = "PATIENT_NOT_FOUND"
    SERIES_NOT_FOUND = "SERIES_NOT_FOUND"
    SERIES_INVALID = "SERIES_INVALID"
    SOURCE_CHANGED = "SOURCE_CHANGED"
    DECODER_ERROR = "DECODER_ERROR"
    OUT_OF_MEMORY = "OUT_OF_MEMORY"
    RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE = "RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE"
    OPERATION_CANCELLED = "OPERATION_CANCELLED"
    SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE = "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE"
    PROCESSING_FAILED = "PROCESSING_FAILED"
    CONFIG_ERROR = "CONFIG_ERROR"
    HTTPS_REQUIRED = "HTTPS_REQUIRED"


ERROR_MESSAGES: dict[PublicErrorCode, str] = {
    PublicErrorCode.AUTH_REQUIRED: "Authentication is required.",
    PublicErrorCode.AUTH_FAILED: "Authentication failed.",
    PublicErrorCode.SESSION_EXPIRED: "Session expired.",
    PublicErrorCode.ACCESS_DENIED: "Access denied.",
    PublicErrorCode.RATE_LIMITED: "Too many requests.",
    PublicErrorCode.INVALID_REQUEST: "Invalid request.",
    PublicErrorCode.REQUEST_TOO_LARGE: "Request too large.",
    PublicErrorCode.PATH_REJECTED: "Dataset path rejected.",
    PublicErrorCode.DATASET_NOT_FOUND: "Dataset not found.",
    PublicErrorCode.PATIENT_NOT_FOUND: "Patient not found.",
    PublicErrorCode.SERIES_NOT_FOUND: "Series not found.",
    PublicErrorCode.SERIES_INVALID: "Series is not volume-ready.",
    PublicErrorCode.SOURCE_CHANGED: "Source data changed.",
    PublicErrorCode.DECODER_ERROR: "Image decoding failed.",
    PublicErrorCode.OUT_OF_MEMORY: "Memory limit exceeded.",
    PublicErrorCode.RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE: "Invalid byte range.",
    PublicErrorCode.OPERATION_CANCELLED: "Operation cancelled.",
    PublicErrorCode.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE: "Service unavailable.",
    PublicErrorCode.PROCESSING_FAILED: "Processing failed.",
    PublicErrorCode.CONFIG_ERROR: "Service configuration error.",
    PublicErrorCode.HTTPS_REQUIRED: "Secure transport required.",
}


class APIErrorDetail(StrictAPIModel):
    code: PublicErrorCode
    message: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=160)
    retryable: bool = False
    request_id: RequestId | None = None

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def enforce_safe_message(self) -> "APIErrorDetail":
        if self.message != ERROR_MESSAGES[self.code]:
            raise ValueError("Error message is not allowlisted.")
        return self


class APIErrorResponse(StrictAPIModel):
    success: Literal[False] = False
    error: APIErrorDetail


def make_error_response(
    code: PublicErrorCode,
    *,
    request_id: str | None = None,
    retryable: bool = False,
) -> APIErrorResponse:
    return APIErrorResponse(
        error=APIErrorDetail(
            code=code,
            message=ERROR_MESSAGES[code],
            retryable=retryable,
            request_id=request_id,
        )
    )


# ============================================================
# 03 — AUTHENTICATION
# ============================================================

class LoginRequest(StrictAPIModel):
    username: str = Field(
        min_length=1,
        max_length=128,
    )
    password: str = Field(
        min_length=1,
        max_length=1024,
        repr=False,
    )


class SessionStatus(StrictAPIModel):
    authenticated: Literal[True] = True
    expires_at: FiniteNumber = Field(gt=0)


class LoginResponse(StrictAPIModel):
    success: Literal[True] = True
    session: SessionStatus


class LogoutResponse(StrictAPIModel):
    success: Literal[True] = True
    authenticated: Literal[False] = False


# ============================================================
# 04 — DATASET SESSION
# ============================================================

class DatasetSelectRequest(StrictAPIModel):
    """
    Absolute path selection must be independently validated
    by DatasetService and the centralized path guard.

    This schema only restricts request size and syntax.
    """

    dataset_path: str = Field(
        min_length=1,
        max_length=2048,
    )

    @field_validator("dataset_path")
    @classmethod
    def reject_unsafe_text(cls, value: str) -> str:
        if (
            "\x00" in value
            or "\r" in value
            or "\n" in value
        ):
            raise ValueError("Invalid dataset path.")
        return value


class DatasetStatus(str, Enum):
    READY = "ready"
    SCANNING = "scanning"
    CLOSED = "closed"


class DatasetSummary(StrictAPIModel):
    dataset_session_id: OpaqueHandle
    state: DatasetStatus
    generation: PositiveSize
    patient_count: PositiveCount
    series_count: PositiveCount
    dicom_files: PositiveCount


class DatasetResponse(StrictAPIModel):
    success: Literal[True] = True
    dataset: DatasetSummary


class DatasetListResponse(StrictAPIModel):
    success: Literal[True] = True
    datasets: list[DatasetSummary] = Field(max_length=100)


class DatasetCloseResponse(StrictAPIModel):
    success: Literal[True] = True
    closed: Literal[True] = True


# ============================================================
# 05 — PATIENT AND SERIES BROWSER
# ============================================================

class PatientSummary(StrictAPIModel):
    """
    No real patient names or IDs.

    UI can display a generic Patient 1, Patient 2 label
    based on ordering, without storing identifying labels.
    """

    patient_handle: OpaqueHandle
    series_count: PositiveCount
    volume_ready_series: PositiveCount

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def valid_ready_count(self) -> "PatientSummary":
        if self.volume_ready_series > self.series_count:
            raise ValueError("Invalid series counts.")
        return self


class PatientListResponse(StrictAPIModel):
    success: Literal[True] = True
    dataset_session_id: OpaqueHandle
    patients: list[PatientSummary] = Field(
        max_length=100000
    )


class SeriesSummary(StrictAPIModel):
    series_handle: OpaqueHandle
    patient_handle: OpaqueHandle
    modality: str | None = Field(
        default=None,
        max_length=16,
        pattern=r"^[A-Za-z0-9_-]*$",
    )
    slice_count: PositiveCount
    rows: PositiveCount
    columns: PositiveCount
    volume_ready: bool


class SeriesListResponse(StrictAPIModel):
    success: Literal[True] = True
    dataset_session_id: OpaqueHandle
    patient_handle: OpaqueHandle
    series: list[SeriesSummary] = Field(
        max_length=100000
    )


# ============================================================
# 06 — SERIES VALIDATION
# ============================================================

class ValidationSeverity(str, Enum):
    INFO = "info"
    WARNING = "warning"
    ERROR = "error"


class SeriesIssue(StrictAPIModel):
    """
    Only approved generic codes are exposed.

    No DICOM metadata, paths or vendor exception strings.
    """

    code: str = Field(
        min_length=1,
        max_length=64,
        pattern=r"^[A-Z0-9_]+$",
    )
    severity: ValidationSeverity


class SeriesValidationResponse(StrictAPIModel):
    success: Literal[True] = True
    series_handle: OpaqueHandle
    valid: bool
    volume_ready: bool
    file_count: PositiveCount
    frame_count: PositiveCount
    issues: list[SeriesIssue] = Field(
        default_factory=list,
        max_length=256,
    )


# ============================================================
# 07 — MEDICAL GEOMETRY
# ============================================================

class VolumeGeometrySchema(StrictAPIModel):
    shape_zyx: Shape3
    spacing_xyz_mm: tuple[
        PositiveFiniteNumber,
        PositiveFiniteNumber,
        PositiveFiniteNumber,
    ]
    origin_lps: Vector3
    direction_lps: Direction9
    coordinate_system: Literal["LPS"] = "LPS"

    @field_validator("direction_lps")
    @classmethod
    def validate_direction(cls, value: Direction9) -> Direction9:
        matrix = (
            value[0:3],
            value[3:6],
            value[6:9],
        )

        for axis in matrix:
            squared_norm = sum(component * component for component in axis)
            if not math.isclose(squared_norm, 1.0, abs_tol=1e-3):
                raise ValueError("Invalid direction matrix.")

        for a, b in ((0, 1), (0, 2), (1, 2)):
            dot = sum(
                matrix[a][i] * matrix[b][i]
                for i in range(3)
            )
            if not math.isclose(dot, 0.0, abs_tol=1e-3):
                raise ValueError("Invalid direction matrix.")

        return value


# ============================================================
# 08 — FULL / PREVIEW VOLUME LEVELS
# ============================================================

class VolumeLevelSchema(StrictAPIModel):
    level: int = Field(strict=True, ge=0, le=64)
    is_preview: bool
    shape_zyx: Shape3
    byte_size: PositiveSize
    dtype: Literal["float32"] = "float32"
    axis_order: Literal["zyx"] = "zyx"
    byte_order: Literal["little"] = "little"
    geometry: VolumeGeometrySchema

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def check_level_consistency(self) -> "VolumeLevelSchema":
        if self.is_preview != (self.level > 0):
            raise ValueError("Invalid preview level.")
        expected_bytes = math.prod(self.shape_zyx) * 4
        if self.byte_size != expected_bytes:
            raise ValueError("Invalid volume byte size.")
        if self.geometry.shape_zyx != self.shape_zyx:
            raise ValueError("Inconsistent geometry.")
        return self


class VolumeDescriptor(StrictAPIModel):
    format: Literal["sinus-viewer-volume-v1"] = (
        "sinus-viewer-volume-v1"
    )
    coordinate_system: Literal["LPS"] = "LPS"
    levels: list[VolumeLevelSchema] = Field(
        min_length=1,
        max_length=65,
    )

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_level_order(self) -> "VolumeDescriptor":
        identifiers = [entry.level for entry in self.levels]
        if len(identifiers) != len(set(identifiers)):
            raise ValueError("Duplicate volume levels.")
        if 0 not in identifiers:
            raise ValueError("Full-resolution level is missing.")
        return self


class VolumeResponse(StrictAPIModel):
    success: Literal[True] = True
    series_handle: OpaqueHandle
    volume: VolumeDescriptor


# ============================================================
# 09 — NAVIGATION PREVIEWS
# ============================================================

class PreviewPlaneName(str, Enum):
    AXIAL = "axial"
    CORONAL = "coronal"
    SAGITTAL = "sagittal"


class PreviewImageSchema(StrictAPIModel):
    plane: PreviewPlaneName
    shape_yx: Shape2
    dtype: Literal["uint8"] = "uint8"
    byte_size: PositiveSize
    sampled_slice_count: PositiveSize
    representative_slice_index: PositiveCount
    window_low: FiniteNumber
    window_high: FiniteNumber
    diagnostic_quality: Literal[False] = False

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_preview(self) -> "PreviewImageSchema":
        if self.byte_size != math.prod(self.shape_yx):
            raise ValueError("Invalid preview byte size.")
        if self.window_high <= self.window_low:
            raise ValueError("Invalid preview window.")
        return self


class PreviewDescriptor(StrictAPIModel):
    axial: PreviewImageSchema
    coronal: PreviewImageSchema
    sagittal: PreviewImageSchema
    diagnostic_quality: Literal[False] = False

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def verify_planes(self) -> "PreviewDescriptor":
        if (
            self.axial.plane != PreviewPlaneName.AXIAL
            or self.coronal.plane != PreviewPlaneName.CORONAL
            or self.sagittal.plane != PreviewPlaneName.SAGITTAL
        ):
            raise ValueError("Invalid preview plane mapping.")
        return self


class PreviewResponse(StrictAPIModel):
    success: Literal[True] = True
    series_handle: OpaqueHandle
    previews: PreviewDescriptor


# ============================================================
# 10 — STREAMING CONTRACTS
# ============================================================

class StreamRangeRequest(StrictAPIModel):
    level: int = Field(strict=True, ge=0, le=64)
    offset: int = Field(strict=True, ge=0)
    length: int = Field(
        strict=True,
        ge=1,
        le=64 * 1024 * 1024,
    )


class StreamDescriptor(StrictAPIModel):
    level: int = Field(strict=True, ge=0, le=64)
    total_bytes: PositiveSize
    content_length: PositiveSize
    chunk_size_bytes: PositiveSize
    status_code: Literal[200, 206]
    is_partial: bool
    content_type: Literal["application/octet-stream"] = (
        "application/octet-stream"
    )
    encoding: Literal["sinus-viewer-f32-zyx-v1"] = (
        "sinus-viewer-f32-zyx-v1"
    )

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_stream_contract(self) -> "StreamDescriptor":
        if self.content_length > self.total_bytes:
            raise ValueError("Invalid stream length.")
        if self.is_partial != (self.status_code == 206):
            raise ValueError("Invalid streaming status.")
        if self.chunk_size_bytes > 4 * 1024 * 1024:
            raise ValueError("Stream chunk is too large.")
        return self


class StreamResponse(StrictAPIModel):
    success: Literal[True] = True
    stream: StreamDescriptor


# ============================================================
# 11 — HEALTH RESPONSE
# ============================================================

class HealthStatus(str, Enum):
    OK = "ok"
    DEGRADED = "degraded"


class HealthResponse(StrictAPIModel):
    """
    Suitable for a minimal public health endpoint.

    No hardware inventory, paths, patient information,
    credentials or configuration are exposed.
    """

    status: HealthStatus
    service: Literal["sinus-viewer"] = "sinus-viewer"


# ============================================================
# 12 — SCHEMA CAPABILITIES
# ============================================================

def get_api_schema_capabilities() -> dict:
    return {
        "schema_version": "1",
        "pydantic_version": "2",
        "strict_extra_fields": True,
        "opaque_handles": True,
        "patient_identifiers_exposed": False,
        "raw_dicom_paths_exposed": False,
        "volume_binary_separate": True,
        "coordinate_system": "LPS",
        "volume_axis_order": "zyx",
        "volume_dtype": "float32",
    }
