```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — CENTRAL CONFIGURATION ENGINE
============================================

Responsibilities:
- Load config/settings.yaml safely.
- Validate important application settings.
- Resolve project-relative paths.
- Enforce configuration-level dataset protection.
- Keep runtime cache outside the original dataset.
- Provide typed configuration to backend modules.
- Read authentication secrets from environment variables.
- Never modify original dataset files.

No package installation or code execution is required locally.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
from functools import lru_cache
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal

import yaml
from pydantic import (
    BaseModel,
    ConfigDict,
    Field,
    ValidationError,
    field_validator,
    model_validator,
)


# ============================================================
# 01 — PROJECT CONSTANTS
# ============================================================

PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]

DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH = PROJECT_ROOT / "config" / "settings.yaml"

SUPPORTED_ENVIRONMENTS = {
    "development",
    "staging",
    "production",
}

SUPPORTED_HARDWARE_MODES = {
    "auto",
    "cpu",
    "gpu",
}


# ============================================================
# 02 — CONFIGURATION EXCEPTIONS
# ============================================================

class ConfigurationError(RuntimeError):
    """Raised when configuration cannot be safely loaded."""


class ConfigurationSecurityError(ConfigurationError):
    """Raised when a configuration violates a safety rule."""


# ============================================================
# 03 — BASE MODEL
# ============================================================

class ConfigSection(BaseModel):
    """
    Shared configuration model.

    Unknown fields are rejected to catch configuration typos.
    Models are frozen to prevent direct field reassignment.
    """

    model_config = ConfigDict(
        extra="forbid",
        frozen=True,
        validate_default=True,
    )


# ============================================================
# 04 — APPLICATION CONFIGURATION
# ============================================================

class ViewerConfig(ConfigSection):
    title: str = "Sinus Viewer"
    default_theme: Literal["dark", "light"] = "dark"
    default_layout: Literal["2x2", "1x3", "single"] = "2x2"
    default_page: str = "datasets"

    enable_3d: bool = True
    enable_measurements: bool = True
    enable_crosshairs: bool = True
    enable_keyboard_shortcuts: bool = True

    show_dicom_metadata: bool = True
    show_patient_identifiers: bool = False


class ApplicationConfig(ConfigSection):
    name: str = "Sinus Viewer"
    version: str = "0.1.0"
    environment: Literal[
        "development",
        "staging",
        "production",
    ] = "development"

    debug: bool = False
    timezone: str = "UTC"

    viewer: ViewerConfig = Field(default_factory=ViewerConfig)


# ============================================================
# 05 — SERVER CONFIGURATION
# ============================================================

class CorsConfig(ConfigSection):
    enabled: bool = False
    allowed_origins: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
    allow_credentials: bool = False


class ServerLimits(ConfigSection):
    max_concurrent_volume_loads: int = Field(default=1, ge=1)
    max_concurrent_metadata_scans: int = Field(default=2, ge=1)
    max_request_body_mb: int = Field(default=16, ge=1)


class ServerConfig(ConfigSection):
    host: str = "127.0.0.1"
    port: int = Field(default=8000, ge=1, le=65535)
    workers: int = Field(default=1, ge=1)
    reload: bool = False

    api_prefix: str = "/api"
    docs_enabled: bool = False

    request_timeout_seconds: int = Field(default=120, ge=1)

    cors: CorsConfig = Field(default_factory=CorsConfig)
    limits: ServerLimits = Field(default_factory=ServerLimits)

    @field_validator("api_prefix")
    @classmethod
    def validate_api_prefix(cls, value: str) -> str:
        if not value.startswith("/") or value == "/":
            raise ValueError(
                "API prefix must be an absolute URL path."
            )

        if ".." in value or "//" in value:
            raise ValueError("Invalid API prefix.")

        return value.rstrip("/")

# ============================================================
# 13 — MAIN APPLICATION SETTINGS MODEL
# ============================================================

class AppSettings(ConfigSection):
    """
    Main validated configuration model.

    Every top-level section corresponds to settings.yaml.
    """

    application: ApplicationConfig
    server: ServerConfig
    storage: StorageConfig
    dicom: DicomConfig
    hardware: HardwareConfig
    performance: PerformanceConfig
    security: SecurityConfig
    deployment: DeploymentConfig
    logging: LoggingConfig
    errors: ErrorConfig

    @model_validator(mode="after")
    def validate_security_invariants(self) -> "AppSettings":
        """
        Reject configurations violating required safety rules.
        """

        dataset = self.storage.dataset
        access = self.security.access
        sessions = self.security.sessions

        if dataset.access_mode != "read_only":
            raise ValueError(
                "Original DICOM datasets must remain read-only."
            )

        if dataset.allow_arbitrary_filesystem_paths:
            raise ValueError(
                "Arbitrary filesystem access is forbidden."
            )

        if dataset.follow_symlinks:
            raise ValueError(
                "Dataset symlink traversal must remain disabled."
            )

        if not self.storage.cleanup.never_cleanup_dataset_paths:
            raise ValueError(
                "Dataset cleanup protection must remain enabled."
            )

        if not self.security.require_authentication:
            raise ValueError(
                "Authentication cannot be disabled."
            )

        if self.security.allow_anonymous_access:
            raise ValueError(
                "Anonymous access is forbidden."
            )

        if self.security.allow_public_dataset_browsing:
            raise ValueError(
                "Public dataset browsing is forbidden."
            )

        if not access.restrict_paths_to_dataset_root:
            raise ValueError(
                "Dataset path restrictions cannot be disabled."
            )

        if not access.reject_path_traversal:
            raise ValueError(
                "Path traversal protection cannot be disabled."
            )

        if not access.reject_symlinks:
            raise ValueError(
                "Symlink protection cannot be disabled."
            )

        if not access.require_authenticated_api:
            raise ValueError(
                "All patient data APIs require authentication."
            )

        if not sessions.enabled:
            raise ValueError("Sessions must remain enabled.")

        if not sessions.cookie_http_only:
            raise ValueError("HttpOnly cookies are required.")

        if not sessions.cookie_secure:
            raise ValueError("Secure cookies are required.")

        if self.deployment.dependencies.install_locally:
            raise ValueError(
                "Local dependency installation is disabled."
            )

        if self.deployment.external_access.enabled:
            if not self.deployment.external_access.require_https:
                raise ValueError(
                    "External access must require HTTPS."
                )

        if self.server.host not in {
            "127.0.0.1",
            "localhost",
            "::1",
        }:
            raise ValueError(
                "Backend must bind to loopback only."
            )

        if self.server.cors.enabled:
            raise ValueError(
                "Cross-origin API access is disabled in this deployment."
            )

        if self.logging.include_tracebacks:
            raise ValueError(
                "Production-safe logging cannot include tracebacks."
            )

        sensitive = self.security.logging

        if any((
            sensitive.log_patient_names,
            sensitive.log_patient_ids,
            sensitive.log_dicom_metadata,
            sensitive.log_request_bodies,
        )):
            raise ValueError(
                "Sensitive patient information logging is forbidden."
            )

        if not sensitive.redact_query_parameters:
            raise ValueError(
                "Query parameter redaction must remain enabled."
            )

        if self.errors.expose_internal_tracebacks_to_browser:
            raise ValueError(
                "Internal tracebacks must not be exposed."
            )

        self._validate_storage_paths()

        return self

    def _validate_storage_paths(self) -> None:
        """
        Validate storage isolation without creating directories.

        The dataset root must not contain runtime cache paths.
        """

        dataset_root = self.storage.dataset.root_path
        runtime = self.storage.runtime

        if not dataset_root.is_absolute():
            raise ValueError(
                "Dataset root path must be absolute."
            )

        if not runtime.root_path.is_absolute():
            raise ValueError(
                "Runtime root path must be absolute."
            )

        source = dataset_root.resolve(strict=False)
        output = runtime.root_path.resolve(strict=False)

        if source == output:
            raise ValueError(
                "Runtime and dataset roots cannot be identical."
            )

        if output.is_relative_to(source):
            raise ValueError(
                "Runtime directory cannot be inside the dataset root."
            )

        if source.is_relative_to(output):
            raise ValueError(
                "Dataset root cannot be inside runtime storage."
            )

        for name, path in (
            ("cache", runtime.cache_path),
            ("temp", runtime.temp_path),
            ("logs", runtime.logs_path),
        ):
            if not path.is_absolute():
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Runtime {name} path must be absolute."
                )

            resolved = path.resolve(strict=False)

            if resolved == output:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Runtime {name} must be a subdirectory."
                )

            if not resolved.is_relative_to(output):
                raise ValueError(
                    f"Runtime {name} must remain inside runtime root."
                )

    @property
    def dataset_root(self) -> Path:
        """Configured dataset root; does not scan it."""
        return self.storage.dataset.root_path

    @property
    def runtime_root(self) -> Path:
        """Configured runtime root; does not create it."""
        return self.storage.runtime.root_path

    @property
    def is_production(self) -> bool:
        return self.application.environment == "production"


# ============================================================
# 14 — SAFE YAML LOADING
# ============================================================

def resolve_config_path(
    config_path: str | Path | None = None,
) -> Path:
    """
    Resolve a configuration path.

    Only the settings.yaml file under the project's config
    directory is accepted by the standard application loader.
    """

    allowed = DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH.resolve(strict=False)

    if config_path is None:
        return allowed

    candidate = Path(config_path).expanduser()

    if not candidate.is_absolute():
        candidate = PROJECT_ROOT / candidate

    candidate = candidate.resolve(strict=False)

    if candidate != allowed:
        raise ConfigurationSecurityError(
            "Custom configuration locations are not permitted."
        )

    return candidate


def load_yaml_config(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Read YAML safely.

    This function never writes to the source file.
    """

    if not path.is_file():
        raise ConfigurationError(
            f"Configuration file not found: {path}"
        )

    try:
        with path.open(
            "r",
            encoding="utf-8",
        ) as handle:
            raw = yaml.safe_load(handle)

    except (OSError, yaml.YAMLError) as exc:
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Unable to read application configuration."
        ) from exc

    if not isinstance(raw, dict):
        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Configuration must contain a YAML mapping."
        )

    return raw


# ============================================================
# 15 — ENVIRONMENT OVERRIDES
# ============================================================

def apply_environment_overrides(
    raw: dict[str, Any],
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Apply explicitly permitted environment overrides.

    Security settings cannot be weakened through environment
    variables.

    Secrets are never placed inside the serialized settings.
    """

    data = dict(raw)

    application = dict(data.get("application", {}))
    server = dict(data.get("server", {}))

    environment = os.getenv("SINUS_VIEWER_ENVIRONMENT")

    if environment:
        if environment not in SUPPORTED_ENVIRONMENTS:
            raise ConfigurationError(
                "Invalid SINUS_VIEWER_ENVIRONMENT value."
            )

        application["environment"] = environment

    port_value = os.getenv("SINUS_VIEWER_PORT")

    if port_value:
        try:
            port = int(port_value)
        except ValueError as exc:
            raise ConfigurationError(
                "SINUS_VIEWER_PORT must be an integer."
            ) from exc

        if not 1 <= port <= 65535:
            raise ConfigurationError(
                "SINUS_VIEWER_PORT is outside valid range."
            )

        server["port"] = port

    data["application"] = application
    data["server"] = server

    return data


# ============================================================
# 16 — LOAD AND VALIDATE SETTINGS
# ============================================================

def load_settings(
    config_path: str | Path | None = None,
) -> AppSettings:
    """
    Load the complete application configuration.

    This function:
    - Reads YAML.
    - Applies permitted environment overrides.
    - Validates schema and security invariants.

    It does NOT:
    - Install dependencies.
    - Mount Google Drive.
    - Scan patient folders.
    - Create directories.
    - Modify DICOM files.
    """

    path = resolve_config_path(config_path)

    raw = load_yaml_config(path)
    raw = apply_environment_overrides(raw)

    try:
        settings = AppSettings.model_validate(raw)

    except ValidationError as exc:
        # Avoid printing raw input values or settings content.
        problems = [
            ".".join(str(part) for part in error["loc"])
            for error in exc.errors()
        ]

        raise ConfigurationError(
            "Invalid configuration fields: "
            + ", ".join(problems)
        ) from exc

    return settings


# ============================================================
# 17 — CACHED CONFIGURATION ACCESS
# ============================================================

@lru_cache(maxsize=1)
def get_settings() -> AppSettings:
    """
    Return cached validated configuration.

    Suitable for FastAPI dependency injection.
    """

    return load_settings()


def clear_settings_cache() -> None:
    """
    Clear cached configuration.

    Intended for isolated tests and controlled reloads.
    """

    get_settings.cache_clear()


# ============================================================
# 18 — AUTHENTICATION ENVIRONMENT ACCESS
# ============================================================

def get_auth_environment(
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """
    Retrieve authentication material from environment variables.

    Never returns fallback/default credentials.

    Returns:
        (username, password_hash)

    Note:
        Password verification and session handling are
        implemented separately in security/auth.py.
    """

    active = settings or get_settings()

    credentials = active.security.credentials

    username = os.getenv(
        credentials.username_env,
        "",
    ).strip()

    password_hash = os.getenv(
        credentials.password_hash_env,
        "",
    ).strip()

    if not username or not password_hash:
        raise ConfigurationSecurityError(
            "Authentication credentials are not configured."
        )

    return username, password_hash


# ============================================================
# 19 — SAFE CONFIGURATION SUMMARY
# ============================================================

def get_safe_config_summary(
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Return non-sensitive application configuration metadata.

    Dataset paths and authentication secrets are excluded.
    """

    active = settings or get_settings()

    return {
        "application": active.application.name,
        "version": active.application.version,
        "environment": active.application.environment,
        "platform": active.deployment.platform,
        "authentication_required": (
            active.security.require_authentication
        ),
        "dataset_access_mode": (
            active.storage.dataset.access_mode
        ),
        "hardware_selection": (
            active.hardware.selection_mode
        ),
        "browser_rendering": (
            active.performance.rendering.preferred_backend
        ),
        "mpr_enabled": (
            active.performance.rendering.enable_mpr
        ),
        "volume_rendering_enabled": (
            active.performance.rendering.enable_volume_rendering
        ),
    }


# ============================================================
# END OF CONFIGURATION MODULE
# ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/backend/app/core/exceptions.py

```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — CENTRAL EXCEPTION HANDLING
==========================================

Provides:
- Stable application error codes.
- Typed domain exceptions.
- Safe user-facing error messages.
- Consistent JSON API error responses.
- Request correlation identifiers.
- FastAPI exception handler registration.
- PHI-safe operational logging.

Security:
- Never expose raw Python exceptions to browsers.
- Never expose original DICOM paths or patient metadata.
- Never log request URLs, bodies or DICOM identifiers.
- Never modify original dataset files.

Python 3.10+
FastAPI / Starlette / Pydantic v2
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import re
import uuid
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
from typing import Any

from fastapi import FastAPI, Request
from fastapi.exceptions import RequestValidationError
from fastapi.responses import JSONResponse
from starlette.exceptions import HTTPException as StarletteHTTPException

from .logging import get_logger


# ============================================================
# 01 — STABLE ERROR CODES
# ============================================================

class ErrorCode(str, Enum):
    INTERNAL_ERROR = "INTERNAL_ERROR"
    CONFIG_ERROR = "CONFIG_ERROR"

    INVALID_REQUEST = "INVALID_REQUEST"
    RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND = "RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND"
    METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED = "METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED"
    RATE_LIMITED = "RATE_LIMITED"

    AUTH_REQUIRED = "AUTH_REQUIRED"
    AUTH_FAILED = "AUTH_FAILED"
    ACCESS_DENIED = "ACCESS_DENIED"
    SESSION_EXPIRED = "SESSION_EXPIRED"

    PATH_REJECTED = "PATH_REJECTED"
    DATASET_NOT_FOUND = "DATASET_NOT_FOUND"
    DATASET_EMPTY = "DATASET_EMPTY"
    DATASET_UNAVAILABLE = "DATASET_UNAVAILABLE"

    PATIENT_NOT_FOUND = "PATIENT_NOT_FOUND"
    SERIES_NOT_FOUND = "SERIES_NOT_FOUND"
    SERIES_INVALID = "SERIES_INVALID"
    SERIES_AMBIGUOUS = "SERIES_AMBIGUOUS"

    DICOM_INVALID = "DICOM_INVALID"
    DICOM_UNSUPPORTED = "DICOM_UNSUPPORTED"
    DICOM_DECODING_FAILED = "DICOM_DECODING_FAILED"

    GEOMETRY_INVALID = "GEOMETRY_INVALID"
    VOLUME_TOO_LARGE = "VOLUME_TOO_LARGE"
    MEMORY_LIMIT = "MEMORY_LIMIT"

    PROCESSING_FAILED = "PROCESSING_FAILED"
    OPERATION_TIMEOUT = "OPERATION_TIMEOUT"
    SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE = "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE"


# ============================================================
# 02 — SAFE PUBLIC ERROR DEFINITIONS
# ============================================================

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ErrorDefinition:
    status_code: int
    message: str
    retryable: bool = False


ERROR_DEFINITIONS: dict[ErrorCode, ErrorDefinition] = {
    ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR: ErrorDefinition(
        500, "An unexpected server error occurred."
    ),
    ErrorCode.CONFIG_ERROR: ErrorDefinition(
        503, "The application is not configured correctly."
    ),

    ErrorCode.INVALID_REQUEST: ErrorDefinition(
        400, "The submitted request is invalid."
    ),
    ErrorCode.RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND: ErrorDefinition(
        404, "The requested resource was not found."
    ),
    ErrorCode.METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED: ErrorDefinition(
        405, "This operation is not supported."
    ),
    ErrorCode.RATE_LIMITED: ErrorDefinition(
        429, "Too many requests. Please try again later.", True
    ),

    ErrorCode.AUTH_REQUIRED: ErrorDefinition(
        401, "Authentication is required."
    ),
    ErrorCode.AUTH_FAILED: ErrorDefinition(
        401, "Authentication failed."
    ),
    ErrorCode.ACCESS_DENIED: ErrorDefinition(
        403, "Access to this resource is not permitted."
    ),
    ErrorCode.SESSION_EXPIRED: ErrorDefinition(
        401, "Your session has expired."
    ),

    ErrorCode.PATH_REJECTED: ErrorDefinition(
        403, "The selected folder is not permitted."
    ),
    ErrorCode.DATASET_NOT_FOUND: ErrorDefinition(
        404, "The selected dataset folder was not found."
    ),
    ErrorCode.DATASET_EMPTY: ErrorDefinition(
        422, "No supported patient scans were found."
    ),
    ErrorCode.DATASET_UNAVAILABLE: ErrorDefinition(
        503, "The dataset is temporarily unavailable.", True
    ),

    ErrorCode.PATIENT_NOT_FOUND: ErrorDefinition(
        404, "The selected patient was not found."
    ),
    ErrorCode.SERIES_NOT_FOUND: ErrorDefinition(
        404, "The selected scan series was not found."
    ),
    ErrorCode.SERIES_INVALID: ErrorDefinition(
        422, "This scan series cannot be reconstructed."
    ),
    ErrorCode.SERIES_AMBIGUOUS: ErrorDefinition(
        409, "Please select a specific scan series."
    ),

    ErrorCode.DICOM_INVALID: ErrorDefinition(
        422, "One or more DICOM files are invalid."
    ),
    ErrorCode.DICOM_UNSUPPORTED: ErrorDefinition(
        415, "This DICOM encoding is not supported."
    ),
    ErrorCode.DICOM_DECODING_FAILED: ErrorDefinition(
        422, "The scan image data could not be decoded."
    ),

    ErrorCode.GEOMETRY_INVALID: ErrorDefinition(
        422, "The scan geometry is incomplete or inconsistent."
    ),
    ErrorCode.VOLUME_TOO_LARGE: ErrorDefinition(
        413, "This scan exceeds the configured volume limit."
    ),
    ErrorCode.MEMORY_LIMIT: ErrorDefinition(
        503, "Insufficient memory to load this scan.", True
    ),

    ErrorCode.PROCESSING_FAILED: ErrorDefinition(
        500, "Scan processing failed."
    ),
    ErrorCode.OPERATION_TIMEOUT: ErrorDefinition(
        504, "The operation timed out.", True
    ),
    ErrorCode.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE: ErrorDefinition(
        503, "The service is temporarily unavailable.", True
    ),
}


# ============================================================
# 03 — APPLICATION EXCEPTION BASE CLASS
# ============================================================

class SinusViewerError(Exception):
    """
    Base class for expected application errors.

    Only standardized public messages are returned to users.
    Raw exception details are deliberately excluded.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        code: ErrorCode,
        *,
        request_id: str | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.code = code
        self.definition = ERROR_DEFINITIONS[code]
        self.request_id = request_id

        super().__init__(self.definition.message)

    @property
    def status_code(self) -> int:
        return self.definition.status_code

    @property
    def retryable(self) -> bool:
        return self.definition.retryable


# ============================================================
# 04 — DOMAIN-SPECIFIC EXCEPTIONS
# ============================================================

class ConfigurationFailure(SinusViewerError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(ErrorCode.CONFIG_ERROR)


class AuthenticationRequired(SinusViewerError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(ErrorCode.AUTH_REQUIRED)


class AuthenticationFailed(SinusViewerError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(ErrorCode.AUTH_FAILED)


class AccessDenied(SinusViewerError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(ErrorCode.ACCESS_DENIED)


class SessionExpired(SinusViewerError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(ErrorCode.SESSION_EXPIRED)


class PathRejected(SinusViewerError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(ErrorCode.PATH_REJECTED)


class DatasetNotFound(SinusViewerError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(ErrorCode.DATASET_NOT_FOUND)


class DatasetEmpty(SinusViewerError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(ErrorCode.DATASET_EMPTY)


class DatasetUnavailable(SinusViewerError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(ErrorCode.DATASET_UNAVAILABLE)


class PatientNotFound(SinusViewerError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(ErrorCode.PATIENT_NOT_FOUND)


class SeriesNotFound(SinusViewerError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(ErrorCode.SERIES_NOT_FOUND)


class SeriesInvalid(SinusViewerError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(ErrorCode.SERIES_INVALID)


class SeriesAmbiguous(SinusViewerError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(ErrorCode.SERIES_AMBIGUOUS)


class DicomInvalid(SinusViewerError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(ErrorCode.DICOM_INVALID)


class DicomUnsupported(SinusViewerError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(ErrorCode.DICOM_UNSUPPORTED)


class DicomDecodingFailed(SinusViewerError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(ErrorCode.DICOM_DECODING_FAILED)


class GeometryInvalid(SinusViewerError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(ErrorCode.GEOMETRY_INVALID)


class VolumeTooLarge(SinusViewerError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(ErrorCode.VOLUME_TOO_LARGE)


class MemoryLimitExceeded(SinusViewerError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(ErrorCode.MEMORY_LIMIT)


class ProcessingFailed(SinusViewerError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(ErrorCode.PROCESSING_FAILED)


class OperationTimeout(SinusViewerError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(ErrorCode.OPERATION_TIMEOUT)


class ServiceUnavailable(SinusViewerError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(ErrorCode.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE)


# ============================================================
# 05 — CORRELATION IDENTIFIERS
# ============================================================

REQUEST_ID_PATTERN = re.compile(r"^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{8,64}$")


def get_request_id(request: Request) -> str:
    """
    Return a server-controlled request identifier.

    Untrusted incoming request-id headers are not accepted.
    """

    existing = getattr(request.state, "request_id", None)

    if (
        isinstance(existing, str)
        and REQUEST_ID_PATTERN.fullmatch(existing)
    ):
        return existing

    generated = uuid.uuid4().hex
    request.state.request_id = generated

    return generated


# ============================================================
# 06 — SAFE ERROR RESPONSE FACTORY
# ============================================================

def create_error_response(
    code: ErrorCode,
    request_id: str,
) -> JSONResponse:
    """
    Produce the consistent frontend-facing error envelope.

    No internal paths, raw exceptions, traceback details,
    patient names or DICOM metadata are returned.
    """

    definition = ERROR_DEFINITIONS[code]

    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
        "success": False,
        "error": {
            "code": code.value,
            "message": definition.message,
            "retryable": definition.retryable,
            "request_id": request_id,
        },
    }

    headers = {
        "X-Request-ID": request_id,
        "Cache-Control": "no-store",
    }

    if definition.status_code == 401:
        headers["WWW-Authenticate"] = "Session"

    if definition.status_code == 429:
        headers["Retry-After"] = "30"

    return JSONResponse(
        status_code=definition.status_code,
        content=payload,
        headers=headers,
    )


# ============================================================
# 07 — SAFE OPERATIONAL ERROR LOGGING
# ============================================================

LOGGING_ERROR_MAP: dict[ErrorCode, str] = {
    ErrorCode.CONFIG_ERROR: "CONFIG_ERROR",
    ErrorCode.AUTH_FAILED: "AUTH_FAILED",
    ErrorCode.ACCESS_DENIED: "ACCESS_DENIED",
    ErrorCode.PATH_REJECTED: "PATH_REJECTED",
    ErrorCode.DICOM_INVALID: "DICOM_INVALID",
    ErrorCode.DICOM_UNSUPPORTED: "DICOM_UNSUPPORTED",
    ErrorCode.SERIES_INVALID: "SERIES_INVALID",
    ErrorCode.GEOMETRY_INVALID: "GEOMETRY_INVALID",
    ErrorCode.VOLUME_TOO_LARGE: "VOLUME_TOO_LARGE",
    ErrorCode.MEMORY_LIMIT: "OUT_OF_MEMORY",
    ErrorCode.DICOM_DECODING_FAILED: "DECODER_ERROR",
    ErrorCode.OPERATION_TIMEOUT: "TIMEOUT",
}


def _log_safe_error(
    code: ErrorCode,
    request_id: str,
) -> None:
    """
    Log an allowlisted operational category.

    Logging never includes full URLs or patient-derived text.
    """

    safe_code = LOGGING_ERROR_MAP.get(
        code,
        "INTERNAL_ERROR",
    )

    get_logger().error(
        "request_failed",
        error_code=safe_code,
        component="api",
        request_id=request_id,
    )


# ============================================================
# 08 — FASTAPI EXCEPTION HANDLERS
# ============================================================

async def handle_application_error(
    request: Request,
    exc: SinusViewerError,
) -> JSONResponse:
    request_id = get_request_id(request)

    _log_safe_error(exc.code, request_id)

    return create_error_response(
        code=exc.code,
        request_id=request_id,
    )


async def handle_request_validation_error(
    request: Request,
    exc: RequestValidationError,
) -> JSONResponse:
    # Do not serialize exc.errors() or exc.body:
    # request validation payloads may contain sensitive data.

    request_id = get_request_id(request)

    _log_safe_error(
        ErrorCode.INVALID_REQUEST,
        request_id,
    )

    return create_error_response(
        ErrorCode.INVALID_REQUEST,
        request_id,
    )


async def handle_http_error(
    request: Request,
    exc: StarletteHTTPException,
) -> JSONResponse:
    request_id = get_request_id(request)

    if exc.status_code == 401:
        code = ErrorCode.AUTH_REQUIRED
    elif exc.status_code == 403:
        code = ErrorCode.ACCESS_DENIED
    elif exc.status_code == 404:
        code = ErrorCode.RESOURCE_NOT_FOUND
    elif exc.status_code == 405:
        code = ErrorCode.METHOD_NOT_ALLOWED
    elif exc.status_code == 429:
        code = ErrorCode.RATE_LIMITED
    elif exc.status_code == 503:
        code = ErrorCode.SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE
    else:
        code = ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR

    _log_safe_error(code, request_id)

    return create_error_response(code, request_id)


async def handle_unexpected_error(
    request: Request,
    exc: Exception,
) -> JSONResponse:
    """
    Catch unexpected errors without leaking details.

    Raw exception strings and stack traces are not logged.
    """

    request_id = get_request_id(request)

    _log_safe_error(
        ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR,
        request_id,
    )

    return create_error_response(
        ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR,
        request_id,
    )


# ============================================================
# 09 — REGISTER ALL HANDLERS
# ============================================================

def register_exception_handlers(app: FastAPI) -> None:
    """
    Register centralized handlers during FastAPI startup.

    Calling this function does not scan or modify datasets.
    """

    app.add_exception_handler(
        SinusViewerError,
        handle_application_error,
    )

    app.add_exception_handler(
        RequestValidationError,
        handle_request_validation_error,
    )

    app.add_exception_handler(
        StarletteHTTPException,
        handle_http_error,
    )

    app.add_exception_handler(
        Exception,
        handle_unexpected_error,
    )


# ============================================================
# END OF EXCEPTION ENGINE
# ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/backend/app/core/hardware.py
```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — HARDWARE & RESOURCE MANAGEMENT
=============================================

Responsibilities:
- Detect CPU and system RAM.
- Detect available NVIDIA GPUs using nvidia-smi.
- Inspect GPU memory and CUDA availability.
- Select a safe processing device.
- Estimate DICOM volume memory requirements.
- Prevent oversized allocations before volume loading.
- Recommend full-resolution or preview-only processing.
- Provide sanitized hardware information to the backend.

IMPORTANT:
- GPU presence does not imply GPU acceleration is implemented.
- GPU processing requires an actual CUDA-compatible pipeline.
- Browser WebGL rendering is independent of Colab GPU.
- No hardware packages are installed by this module.
- No DICOM files are opened or modified.
- No filesystem writes during import or detection.

Python: 3.10+
Dependencies: psutil, Pydantic configuration module
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
from dataclasses import asdict, dataclass
from enum import Enum
from typing import Any, Literal

import psutil

from .config import AppSettings, get_settings


# ============================================================
# 01 — CONSTANTS
# ============================================================

GIB = 1024 ** 3
MIB = 1024 ** 2

NVIDIA_SMI_TIMEOUT = 5

SUPPORTED_GPU_IDENTIFIERS = {
    "NVIDIA_T4": ("t4", "tesla t4"),
    "NVIDIA_L4": ("l4", "nvidia l4"),
    "NVIDIA_A100": ("a100",),
}

SUPPORTED_PIXEL_DTYPES = {
    "uint8": 1,
    "int8": 1,
    "uint16": 2,
    "int16": 2,
    "uint32": 4,
    "int32": 4,
    "float32": 4,
    "float64": 8,
}


# ============================================================
# 02 — ENUMERATIONS
# ============================================================

class DeviceType(str, Enum):
    CPU = "cpu"
    GPU = "gpu"


class VolumeLoadMode(str, Enum):
    FULL = "full"
    PREVIEW = "preview"
    REJECT = "reject"


# ============================================================
# 03 — IMMUTABLE HARDWARE DATA MODELS
# ============================================================

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CPUInfo:
    logical_cores: int
    physical_cores: int | None
    total_ram_bytes: int
    available_ram_bytes: int
    used_ram_percent: float


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class GPUInfo:
    index: int
    name: str
    total_vram_bytes: int
    free_vram_bytes: int
    used_vram_bytes: int
    recognized_target: str | None
    supported_by_config: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class HardwareSnapshot:
    cpu: CPUInfo
    gpus: tuple[GPUInfo, ...]
    nvidia_smi_available: bool
    tpu_enabled: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DeviceSelection:
    device: DeviceType
    gpu_index: int | None
    reason: str
    acceleration_ready: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VolumeMemoryEstimate:
    width: int
    height: int
    depth: int
    dtype: str
    voxel_count: int
    raw_bytes: int
    estimated_peak_bytes: int
    estimated_peak_gib: float


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VolumeLoadDecision:
    mode: VolumeLoadMode
    reason: str
    estimate: VolumeMemoryEstimate
    available_memory_bytes: int
    permitted_memory_bytes: int


# ============================================================
# 04 — CPU AND RAM DETECTION
# ============================================================

def detect_cpu() -> CPUInfo:
    """
    Return the current CPU and system RAM snapshot.

    Read-only system inspection.
    """

    memory = psutil.virtual_memory()

    logical = psutil.cpu_count(logical=True) or 1
    physical = psutil.cpu_count(logical=False)

    return CPUInfo(
        logical_cores=max(1, logical),
        physical_cores=physical,
        total_ram_bytes=int(memory.total),
        available_ram_bytes=int(memory.available),
        used_ram_percent=float(memory.percent),
    )


# ============================================================
# 05 — NVIDIA GPU IDENTIFICATION
# ============================================================

def recognize_gpu(name: str) -> str | None:
    """
    Recognize known GPU families.

    An unrecognized NVIDIA GPU may still be usable by
    compatible libraries, but is not automatically approved.
    """

    normalized = name.casefold().strip()

    for target, patterns in SUPPORTED_GPU_IDENTIFIERS.items():
        if any(pattern in normalized for pattern in patterns):
            return target

    return None


def _nvidia_smi_path() -> str | None:
    """Find nvidia-smi without installing additional software."""

    return shutil.which("nvidia-smi")


def detect_nvidia_gpus(
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
) -> tuple[GPUInfo, ...]:
    """
    Inspect NVIDIA GPU memory via nvidia-smi.

    Fail safely if nvidia-smi is unavailable, times out,
    or produces unexpected output.

    No PyTorch/CuPy import and no GPU memory allocation.
    """

    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()

    if not active.hardware.gpu.enabled:
        return ()

    command = _nvidia_smi_path()

    if command is None:
        return ()

    args = [
        command,
        "--query-gpu=index,name,memory.total,memory.free,memory.used",
        "--format=csv,noheader,nounits",
    ]

    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            args,
            capture_output=True,
            text=True,
            timeout=NVIDIA_SMI_TIMEOUT,
            check=False,
            shell=False,
        )

    except (
        OSError,
        subprocess.TimeoutExpired,
        UnicodeError,
    ):
        return ()

    if result.returncode != 0:
        return ()

    supported_targets = set(
        active.hardware.gpu.supported_targets
    )

    detected: list[GPUInfo] = []

    for line in result.stdout.splitlines():
        parts = [part.strip() for part in line.split(",")]

        if len(parts) != 5:
            continue

        try:
            index = int(parts[0])
            name = parts[1]

            total_mb = int(parts[2])
            free_mb = int(parts[3])
            used_mb = int(parts[4])

        except (ValueError, IndexError):
            continue

        if (
            index < 0
            or total_mb <= 0
            or free_mb < 0
            or used_mb < 0
            or free_mb > total_mb
        ):
            continue

        recognized = recognize_gpu(name)

        detected.append(
            GPUInfo(
                index=index,
                name=name,
                total_vram_bytes=total_mb * MIB,
                free_vram_bytes=free_mb * MIB,
                used_vram_bytes=used_mb * MIB,
                recognized_target=recognized,
                supported_by_config=(
                    recognized in supported_targets
                ),
            )
        )

    return tuple(
        sorted(detected, key=lambda gpu: gpu.index)
    )


# ============================================================
# 06 — COMPLETE HARDWARE SNAPSHOT
# ============================================================

def detect_hardware(
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
) -> HardwareSnapshot:
    """
    Detect current backend compute resources.

    This function does not initialize CUDA contexts.
    """

    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()

    return HardwareSnapshot(
        cpu=detect_cpu(),
        gpus=detect_nvidia_gpus(active),
        nvidia_smi_available=(
            _nvidia_smi_path() is not None
        ),
        tpu_enabled=active.hardware.tpu.enabled,
    )


# ============================================================
# 07 — DEVICE SELECTION
# ============================================================

def select_processing_device(
    snapshot: HardwareSnapshot,
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
    *,
    gpu_acceleration_available: bool = False,
) -> DeviceSelection:
    """
    Select processing hardware without claiming unsupported CUDA.

    gpu_acceleration_available must only be True after the
    actual GPU processing implementation has passed its own
    initialization and capability checks.

    Selecting a GPU here does not transfer data to CUDA.
    """

    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()

    hardware = active.hardware
    mode = hardware.selection_mode

    if mode == "cpu":
        return DeviceSelection(
            device=DeviceType.CPU,
            gpu_index=None,
            reason="cpu_explicitly_selected",
            acceleration_ready=False,
        )

    candidates = [
        gpu for gpu in snapshot.gpus
        if gpu.supported_by_config
        and gpu.free_vram_bytes >= (
            hardware.gpu.minimum_free_vram_gb * GIB
        )
    ]

    candidates.sort(
        key=lambda gpu: gpu.free_vram_bytes,
        reverse=True,
    )

    gpu_ready = (
        hardware.gpu.enabled
        and hardware.gpu.detect_cuda
        and hardware.gpu.enable_optional_gpu_preprocessing
        and gpu_acceleration_available
    )

    if gpu_ready and candidates:
        selected = candidates[0]

        return DeviceSelection(
            device=DeviceType.GPU,
            gpu_index=selected.index,
            reason="gpu_available_and_processing_supported",
            acceleration_ready=True,
        )

    if not hardware.cpu.enabled:
        raise RuntimeError(
            "No usable processing device is available."
        )

    return DeviceSelection(
        device=DeviceType.CPU,
        gpu_index=None,
        reason=(
            "gpu_processing_unavailable_cpu_fallback"
            if mode == "gpu"
            else "safe_cpu_processing"
        ),
        acceleration_ready=False,
    )


# ============================================================
# 08 — DICOM VOLUME MEMORY ESTIMATION
# ============================================================

def estimate_volume_memory(
    *,
    width: int,
    height: int,
    depth: int,
    dtype: str = "int16",
    working_memory_multiplier: float = 3.0,
) -> VolumeMemoryEstimate:
    """
    Estimate raw and peak volume memory requirements.

    The multiplier accounts approximately for temporary
    decode, resampling and volume-processing buffers.

    It is a conservative planning estimate, not a guarantee
    of actual peak memory usage.
    """

    if any(
        not isinstance(value, int)
        or isinstance(value, bool)
        or value <= 0
        for value in (width, height, depth)
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            "Volume dimensions must be positive integers."
        )

    if dtype not in SUPPORTED_PIXEL_DTYPES:
        raise ValueError(
            "Unsupported volume pixel dtype."
        )

    if (
        not math.isfinite(working_memory_multiplier)
        or working_memory_multiplier < 1.0
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            "Invalid working memory multiplier."
        )

    voxels = width * height * depth

    raw_bytes = voxels * SUPPORTED_PIXEL_DTYPES[dtype]

    peak_bytes = math.ceil(
        raw_bytes * working_memory_multiplier
    )

    return VolumeMemoryEstimate(
        width=width,
        height=height,
        depth=depth,
        dtype=dtype,
        voxel_count=voxels,
        raw_bytes=raw_bytes,
        estimated_peak_bytes=peak_bytes,
        estimated_peak_gib=round(
            peak_bytes / GIB, 3
        ),
    )


# ============================================================
# 09 — ALLOCATION SAFETY CHECK
# ============================================================

def evaluate_volume_load(
    estimate: VolumeMemoryEstimate,
    snapshot: HardwareSnapshot,
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
) -> VolumeLoadDecision:
    """
    Evaluate whether a volume can be loaded safely.

    Checks:
    - Configured maximum voxel count.
    - Configured raw volume size.
    - Available RAM after safety reservation.
    - Maximum permitted RAM utilization.

    Does not load or allocate the volume.
    """

    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()

    volume_config = active.dicom.volume
    memory_config = active.performance.memory

    available = snapshot.cpu.available_ram_bytes

    reserve = int(
        max(
            active.hardware.cpu.reserve_system_memory_gb,
            memory_config.minimum_free_ram_gb,
        ) * GIB
    )

    memory_fraction = (
        memory_config.max_ram_utilization_percent / 100.0
    )

    total_budget = int(
        snapshot.cpu.total_ram_bytes * memory_fraction
    )

    currently_used = max(
        0,
        snapshot.cpu.total_ram_bytes - available,
    )

    remaining_budget = max(
        0,
        total_budget - currently_used,
    )

    available_after_reserve = max(
        0,
        available - reserve,
    )

    permitted = min(
        remaining_budget,
        available_after_reserve,
    )

    exceeds_voxel_limit = (
        estimate.voxel_count > volume_config.max_voxels
    )

    exceeds_raw_size_limit = (
        estimate.raw_bytes >
        volume_config.max_uncompressed_volume_mb * MIB
    )

    exceeds_memory_budget = (
        memory_config.enable_memory_guard
        and estimate.estimated_peak_bytes > permitted
    )

    if not (
        exceeds_voxel_limit
        or exceeds_raw_size_limit
        or exceeds_memory_budget
    ):
        return VolumeLoadDecision(
            mode=VolumeLoadMode.FULL,
            reason="full_volume_within_limits",
            estimate=estimate,
            available_memory_bytes=available,
            permitted_memory_bytes=permitted,
        )

    if volume_config.preview.enabled:
        return VolumeLoadDecision(
            mode=VolumeLoadMode.PREVIEW,
            reason="full_volume_exceeds_limits",
            estimate=estimate,
            available_memory_bytes=available,
            permitted_memory_bytes=permitted,
        )

    return VolumeLoadDecision(
        mode=VolumeLoadMode.REJECT,
        reason="volume_exceeds_limits_preview_disabled",
        estimate=estimate,
        available_memory_bytes=available,
        permitted_memory_bytes=permitted,
    )


# ============================================================
# 10 — PREVIEW DIMENSION PLANNING
# ============================================================

def suggest_preview_dimensions(
    *,
    width: int,
    height: int,
    depth: int,
    max_dimension: int = 384,
) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    """
    Calculate aspect-ratio-preserving preview dimensions.

    Dimensions are returned as (width, height, depth).

    No pixel data is read or modified.

    The volume builder must also verify physical voxel
    spacing and orientation before any actual resampling.
    """

    dimensions = (width, height, depth)

    if any(
        not isinstance(value, int)
        or isinstance(value, bool)
        or value <= 0
        for value in dimensions
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            "Invalid preview volume dimensions."
        )

    if max_dimension < 1:
        raise ValueError(
            "max_dimension must be positive."
        )

    largest = max(dimensions)

    if largest <= max_dimension:
        return dimensions

    scale = max_dimension / largest

    return tuple(
        max(1, round(value * scale))
        for value in dimensions
    )


# ============================================================
# 11 — SAFE HARDWARE SUMMARY
# ============================================================

def get_hardware_summary(
    snapshot: HardwareSnapshot,
    selection: DeviceSelection,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Return operational hardware details.

    No patient information, dataset paths, machine usernames
    or authentication credentials are included.
    """

    return {
        "cpu": {
            "logical_cores": snapshot.cpu.logical_cores,
            "physical_cores": snapshot.cpu.physical_cores,
            "total_ram_gib": round(
                snapshot.cpu.total_ram_bytes / GIB, 2
            ),
            "available_ram_gib": round(
                snapshot.cpu.available_ram_bytes / GIB, 2
            ),
            "used_ram_percent": snapshot.cpu.used_ram_percent,
        },
        "gpu": [
            {
                "index": gpu.index,
                "name": gpu.name,
                "total_vram_gib": round(
                    gpu.total_vram_bytes / GIB, 2
                ),
                "free_vram_gib": round(
                    gpu.free_vram_bytes / GIB, 2
                ),
                "supported": gpu.supported_by_config,
            }
            for gpu in snapshot.gpus
        ],
        "selection": {
            "device": selection.device.value,
            "gpu_index": selection.gpu_index,
            "reason": selection.reason,
            "acceleration_ready": selection.acceleration_ready,
        },
        "browser_gpu": {
            "checked_by_backend": False,
            "note": "WebGL capability is checked in the browser.",
        },
    }


# ============================================================
# 12 — MAIN DETECTION HELPER
# ============================================================

def initialize_hardware(
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
    *,
    gpu_acceleration_available: bool = False,
) -> tuple[HardwareSnapshot, DeviceSelection]:
    """
    Detect hardware and select a processing device.

    This function does not:
    - Install drivers.
    - Initialize PyTorch or CUDA.
    - Allocate GPU memory.
    - Read DICOM files.
    - Modify original patient datasets.
    """

    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()

    snapshot = detect_hardware(active)

    selection = select_processing_device(
        snapshot,
        active,
        gpu_acceleration_available=gpu_acceleration_available,
    )

    return snapshot, selection


# ============================================================
# END OF HARDWARE ENGINE
# ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/backend/app/core/logging.py
```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — SECURE STRUCTURED LOGGING
=========================================

Goals:
- Standard Python logging for FastAPI and backend modules.
- JSON-formatted operational logs.
- Safe allowlisted metadata only.
- No patient identifiers, DICOM metadata or raw file paths.
- Optional rotating logs outside the dataset.
- No filesystem changes during import.
- No installation or execution required on local Windows.

IMPORTANT:
Automatic redaction cannot guarantee removal of all PHI from
arbitrary free-form text. Never pass patient-derived values,
request bodies, full paths or raw exception strings to logs.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import re
import sys
import threading
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from logging.handlers import RotatingFileHandler
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Mapping

from .config import AppSettings, get_settings


# ============================================================
# 01 — CONSTANTS
# ============================================================

LOGGER_NAMESPACE = "sinus_viewer"

SAFE_CONTEXT_KEYS = frozenset({
    "event",
    "component",
    "operation",
    "status",
    "error_code",
    "request_id",
    "duration_ms",
    "elapsed_ms",
    "count",
    "series_count",
    "slice_count",
    "volume_count",
    "bytes_processed",
    "cache_hit",
    "retry_count",
    "hardware_type",
    "device_type",
    "memory_mb",
    "volume_mb",
})

NUMERIC_KEYS = frozenset({
    "duration_ms",
    "elapsed_ms",
    "count",
    "series_count",
    "slice_count",
    "volume_count",
    "bytes_processed",
    "retry_count",
    "memory_mb",
    "volume_mb",
})

BOOLEAN_KEYS = frozenset({
    "cache_hit",
})

SAFE_TOKEN_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-zA-Z0-9_.:-]{1,64}$")
SAFE_REQUEST_ID_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-zA-Z0-9_-]{1,64}$")

SAFE_EVENT_NAMES = frozenset({
    "startup",
    "shutdown",
    "health_check",
    "dataset_scan_started",
    "dataset_scan_completed",
    "dataset_scan_failed",
    "patient_index_completed",
    "series_discovery_completed",
    "series_load_started",
    "series_load_completed",
    "series_load_failed",
    "volume_build_started",
    "volume_build_completed",
    "volume_build_failed",
    "cache_lookup",
    "cache_eviction",
    "authentication_success",
    "authentication_failure",
    "request_completed",
    "request_failed",
    "hardware_detected",
    "unexpected_error",
})

SAFE_ERROR_CODES = frozenset({
    "CONFIG_ERROR",
    "AUTH_FAILED",
    "ACCESS_DENIED",
    "PATH_REJECTED",
    "DICOM_INVALID",
    "DICOM_UNSUPPORTED",
    "SERIES_INVALID",
    "GEOMETRY_INVALID",
    "VOLUME_TOO_LARGE",
    "OUT_OF_MEMORY",
    "DECODER_ERROR",
    "TIMEOUT",
    "INTERNAL_ERROR",
})

_SAFE_LEVELS = {
    "DEBUG": logging.DEBUG,
    "INFO": logging.INFO,
    "WARNING": logging.WARNING,
    "ERROR": logging.ERROR,
    "CRITICAL": logging.CRITICAL,
}

_CONFIGURATION_LOCK = threading.RLock()


# ============================================================
# 02 — SAFE CONTEXT SANITIZATION
# ============================================================

def _safe_token(value: Any) -> str | None:
    """
    Accept only simple machine-generated operational tokens.

    Do not use for names, patient IDs or file paths.
    """
    if not isinstance(value, str):
        return None

    if not SAFE_TOKEN_RE.fullmatch(value):
        return None

    return value


def _sanitize_context(
    context: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Filter additional log fields through an explicit allowlist.

    Unrecognized keys and unsafe values are dropped.
    """
    if not context:
        return {}

    result: dict[str, Any] = {}

    for key, value in context.items():
        if key not in SAFE_CONTEXT_KEYS:
            continue

        if key in NUMERIC_KEYS:
            if isinstance(value, (int, float)) and not isinstance(value, bool):
                if abs(value) <= 1e15:
                    result[key] = value
            continue

        if key in BOOLEAN_KEYS:
            if isinstance(value, bool):
                result[key] = value
            continue

        if key == "event":
            if value in SAFE_EVENT_NAMES:
                result[key] = value
            continue

        if key == "error_code":
            if value in SAFE_ERROR_CODES:
                result[key] = value
            continue

        if key == "request_id":
            if isinstance(value, str) and SAFE_REQUEST_ID_RE.fullmatch(value):
                result[key] = value
            continue

        token = _safe_token(value)
        if token is not None:
            result[key] = token

    return result


# ============================================================
# 03 — SECURE FORMATTER
# ============================================================

class SecureJSONFormatter(logging.Formatter):
    """
    Convert log records into JSON.

    Raw record messages are intentionally excluded.
    Only controlled operational event identifiers and safe
    structured context are serialized.

    Tracebacks, stack information and arbitrary record
    attributes are never serialized.
    """

    def format(self, record: logging.LogRecord) -> str:
        event = getattr(record, "safe_event", "unexpected_error")

        if event not in SAFE_EVENT_NAMES:
            event = "unexpected_error"

        context = _sanitize_context(
            getattr(record, "safe_context", None)
        )

        output: dict[str, Any] = {
            "timestamp": datetime.fromtimestamp(
                record.created,
                tz=timezone.utc,
            ).isoformat(),
            "level": record.levelname,
            "logger": LOGGER_NAMESPACE,
            "event": event,
        }

        context.pop("event", None)
        output.update(context)

        return json.dumps(
            output,
            ensure_ascii=True,
            separators=(",", ":"),
            allow_nan=False,
        )


class SecureTextFormatter(SecureJSONFormatter):
    """
    Compact text representation of sanitized JSON records.
    """

    def format(self, record: logging.LogRecord) -> str:
        payload = json.loads(super().format(record))

        timestamp = payload.pop("timestamp")
        level = payload.pop("level")
        event = payload.pop("event")

        payload.pop("logger", None)

        details = " ".join(
            f"{key}={value}"
            for key, value in payload.items()
        )

        return (
            f"{timestamp} [{level}] {event}"
            + (f" {details}" if details else "")
        )


# ============================================================
# 04 — SAFE LOGGER ADAPTER
# ============================================================

class SecureLogger:
    """
    Controlled interface for application event logging.

    Do not pass raw exceptions or patient-derived data.
    Use fixed error codes instead.
    """

    def __init__(self, logger: logging.Logger):
        self._logger = logger

    def log(
        self,
        level: int,
        event: str,
        **context: Any,
    ) -> None:
        safe_event = (
            event if event in SAFE_EVENT_NAMES
            else "unexpected_error"
        )

        safe_context = _sanitize_context(context)

        self._logger.log(
            level,
            safe_event,
            extra={
                "safe_event": safe_event,
                "safe_context": safe_context,
            },
            exc_info=False,
            stack_info=False,
        )

    def debug(self, event: str, **context: Any) -> None:
        self.log(logging.DEBUG, event, **context)

    def info(self, event: str, **context: Any) -> None:
        self.log(logging.INFO, event, **context)

    def warning(self, event: str, **context: Any) -> None:
        self.log(logging.WARNING, event, **context)

    def error(self, event: str, **context: Any) -> None:
        self.log(logging.ERROR, event, **context)

    def critical(self, event: str, **context: Any) -> None:
        self.log(logging.CRITICAL, event, **context)


# ============================================================
# 05 — LOG PATH VALIDATION
# ============================================================

def _validate_log_directory(
    settings: AppSettings,
) -> Path:
    """
    Ensure log output remains inside the dedicated runtime.

    Dataset source and runtime destinations must remain
    separate. Path guards will additionally secure all
    patient-file API operations.
    """

    dataset_root = settings.storage.dataset.root_path.resolve(
        strict=False
    )

    runtime_root = settings.storage.runtime.root_path.resolve(
        strict=False
    )

    log_directory = settings.storage.runtime.logs_path.resolve(
        strict=False
    )

    if (
        log_directory == dataset_root
        or log_directory.is_relative_to(dataset_root)
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            "Logging inside the dataset is forbidden."
        )

    if (
        log_directory == runtime_root
        or not log_directory.is_relative_to(runtime_root)
    ):
        raise ValueError(
            "Log directory must be inside runtime storage."
        )

    return log_directory


# ============================================================
# 06 — LOGGING SETUP
# ============================================================

def configure_logging(
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
    *,
    enable_file_logging: bool | None = None,
) -> SecureLogger:
    """
    Configure application logging once per explicit startup.

    No directories are created during module import.

    File output is optional and stored outside the dataset.
    """

    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()

    log_config = active.logging

    level = _SAFE_LEVELS[log_config.level]

    logger = logging.getLogger(LOGGER_NAMESPACE)

    formatter: logging.Formatter

    if log_config.format == "structured":
        formatter = SecureJSONFormatter()
    else:
        formatter = SecureTextFormatter()

    use_files = (
        log_config.file_enabled
        if enable_file_logging is None
        else enable_file_logging
    )

    with _CONFIGURATION_LOCK:
        handlers: list[logging.Handler] = []

        if log_config.console:
            console = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout)
            console.setFormatter(formatter)
            console.setLevel(level)
            handlers.append(console)

        if use_files:
            log_directory = _validate_log_directory(active)

            # Application logs only. Never create dataset folders.
            log_directory.mkdir(
                parents=True,
                exist_ok=True,
            )

            file_handler = RotatingFileHandler(
                filename=log_directory / "sinus_viewer.log",
                mode="a",
                maxBytes=log_config.rotate_mb * 1024 * 1024,
                backupCount=max(
                    1,
                    min(log_config.retention_days, 30),
                ),
                encoding="utf-8",
                delay=True,
            )

            file_handler.setFormatter(formatter)
            file_handler.setLevel(level)
            handlers.append(file_handler)

        logger.disabled = False
        logger.setLevel(level)

        # Avoid propagation into uncontrolled root/Uvicorn
        # handlers that might serialize raw event arguments.
        logger.propagate = False

        previous_handlers = list(logger.handlers)

        for handler in previous_handlers:
            logger.removeHandler(handler)

        for handler in handlers:
            logger.addHandler(handler)

        for handler in previous_handlers:
            handler.close()

    return SecureLogger(logger)


# ============================================================
# 07 — LOGGER ACCESS
# ============================================================

def get_logger() -> SecureLogger:
    """
    Obtain the dedicated application logger.

    This does not initialize logging or create directories.
    Startup code must call configure_logging() explicitly.
    """

    logger = logging.getLogger(LOGGER_NAMESPACE)
    return SecureLogger(logger)


# ============================================================
# 08 — SAFE ERROR LOGGING
# ============================================================

def log_error(
    *,
    error_code: str,
    component: str = "backend",
    request_id: str | None = None,
) -> None:
    """
    Log only a standardized error category.

    Do not provide exception messages, file paths,
    DICOM metadata or patient identifiers.
    """

    if error_code not in SAFE_ERROR_CODES:
        error_code = "INTERNAL_ERROR"

    context: dict[str, Any] = {
        "error_code": error_code,
        "component": component,
    }

    if request_id is not None:
        context["request_id"] = request_id

    get_logger().error(
        "unexpected_error",
        **context,
    )


# ============================================================
# END OF LOGGING MODULE
# ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/backend/app/dicom/decoder.py
```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — DICOM PIXEL DECODER
==================================

Purpose:
- Decode conventional single-frame monochrome DICOM.
- Support uncompressed and plugin-supported compression.
- Check transfer syntax and pixel metadata.
- Validate output dimensions and numeric values.
- Apply modality rescale slope/intercept when requested.
- Enforce per-image memory limits.
- Return safe, structured errors.
- Preserve original DICOM files strictly read-only.

Notes:
- Uses pydicom 3.x pixel decoding.
- Plugins are installed later in Google Colab.
- This module does not build 3D volumes.
- Enhanced multiframe is deliberately unsupported here.
- Rescaled CBCT values are not necessarily calibrated HU.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
import pydicom
from pydicom.dataset import Dataset
from pydicom.errors import InvalidDicomError
from pydicom.uid import UID

from ..core.config import AppSettings, get_settings
from .metadata import (
    DicomMetadata,
    validate_dicom_path,
)


# ============================================================
# 01 — CONSTANTS
# ============================================================

MIB = 1024 * 1024

SUPPORTED_PHOTOMETRIC = frozenset({
    "MONOCHROME1",
    "MONOCHROME2",
})

SUPPORTED_BITS_ALLOCATED = frozenset({
    8,
    16,
    32,
})

# Well-known uncompressed transfer syntaxes.
UNCOMPRESSED_UIDS = frozenset({
    "1.2.840.10008.1.2",
    "1.2.840.10008.1.2.1",
    "1.2.840.10008.1.2.2",
})

# Common compressed transfer syntaxes.
# Actual availability is determined by installed plugins.
KNOWN_COMPRESSED_UIDS = frozenset({
    "1.2.840.10008.1.2.5",
    "1.2.840.10008.1.2.4.50",
    "1.2.840.10008.1.2.4.51",
    "1.2.840.10008.1.2.4.57",
    "1.2.840.10008.1.2.4.70",
    "1.2.840.10008.1.2.4.80",
    "1.2.840.10008.1.2.4.81",
    "1.2.840.10008.1.2.4.90",
    "1.2.840.10008.1.2.4.91",
    "1.2.840.10008.1.2.4.201",
    "1.2.840.10008.1.2.4.202",
    "1.2.840.10008.1.2.4.203",
})

MAX_DECODED_SLICE_MB = 256
MAX_DICOM_FILE_MB = 2048


# ============================================================
# 02 — CONTROLLED ERROR CODES
# ============================================================

class DecoderErrorCode(str, Enum):
    INVALID_DICOM = "DICOM_INVALID"
    UNSUPPORTED_SYNTAX = "DICOM_UNSUPPORTED"
    DECODER_UNAVAILABLE = "DECODER_UNAVAILABLE"
    DECODING_FAILED = "DECODER_ERROR"
    INVALID_PIXELS = "INVALID_PIXEL_DATA"
    SOURCE_CHANGED = "SOURCE_CHANGED"
    MEMORY_LIMIT = "OUT_OF_MEMORY"
    PATH_REJECTED = "PATH_REJECTED"


class DicomDecoderError(RuntimeError):
    """
    Safe decoder exception.

    Do not expose raw vendor metadata, original file paths
    or low-level library exception strings.
    """

    def __init__(self, code: DecoderErrorCode) -> None:
        self.code = code
        super().__init__(code.value)


# ============================================================
# 03 — OUTPUT DATA MODELS
# ============================================================

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class TransferSyntaxInfo:
    uid: str
    name: str
    compressed: bool
    recognized: bool

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "compressed": self.compressed,
            "recognized": self.recognized,
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DecodeResult:
    pixels: np.ndarray = field(repr=False)

    rows: int
    columns: int
    dtype: str

    compressed_source: bool
    photometric_interpretation: str
    modality_rescale_applied: bool

    @property
    def size_bytes(self) -> int:
        return int(self.pixels.nbytes)

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "rows": self.rows,
            "columns": self.columns,
            "dtype": self.dtype,
            "size_bytes": self.size_bytes,
            "compressed_source": self.compressed_source,
            "modality_rescale_applied": (
                self.modality_rescale_applied
            ),
        }


# ============================================================
# 04 — TRANSFER SYNTAX INSPECTION
# ============================================================

def inspect_transfer_syntax(
    dataset: Dataset,
) -> TransferSyntaxInfo:
    """
    Inspect file transfer syntax.

    Recognition does not guarantee decoder availability.
    """

    file_meta = getattr(dataset, "file_meta", None)

    if file_meta is None:
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.INVALID_DICOM
        )

    value = getattr(
        file_meta,
        "TransferSyntaxUID",
        None,
    )

    if value is None:
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.INVALID_DICOM
        )

    try:
        uid = UID(str(value))
        compressed = bool(uid.is_compressed)
    except (TypeError, ValueError, AttributeError):
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.INVALID_DICOM
        ) from None

    recognized = (
        str(uid) in UNCOMPRESSED_UIDS
        or str(uid) in KNOWN_COMPRESSED_UIDS
    )

    return TransferSyntaxInfo(
        uid=str(uid),
        name=str(uid.name),
        compressed=compressed,
        recognized=recognized,
    )


# ============================================================
# 05 — PIXEL HEADER VALIDATION
# ============================================================

def validate_pixel_header(dataset: Dataset) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """
    Restrict this decoder to conventional monochrome images.
    """

    try:
        rows = int(dataset.Rows)
        columns = int(dataset.Columns)

        samples = int(dataset.SamplesPerPixel)
        bits = int(dataset.BitsAllocated)
        stored = int(dataset.BitsStored)
        high_bit = int(dataset.HighBit)
        representation = int(dataset.PixelRepresentation)

        frames = int(
            getattr(dataset, "NumberOfFrames", 1)
        )

        photometric = str(
            dataset.PhotometricInterpretation
        )

    except (
        AttributeError,
        TypeError,
        ValueError,
        OverflowError,
    ):
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.INVALID_DICOM
        ) from None

    if rows <= 0 or columns <= 0:
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.INVALID_PIXELS
        )

    if frames != 1 or samples != 1:
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.UNSUPPORTED_SYNTAX
        )

    if photometric not in SUPPORTED_PHOTOMETRIC:
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.UNSUPPORTED_SYNTAX
        )

    if (
        bits not in SUPPORTED_BITS_ALLOCATED
        or stored < 1
        or stored > bits
        or high_bit != stored - 1
        or representation not in (0, 1)
    ):
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.INVALID_PIXELS
        )

    return rows, columns


# ============================================================
# 06 — SOURCE HEADER COMPARISON
# ============================================================

def verify_expected_metadata(
    dataset: Dataset,
    expected: DicomMetadata,
) -> None:
    """
    Detect changes since the initial metadata scan.

    Identity and geometry verification in series_reader.py
    remains mandatory before full volume reconstruction.
    """

    comparisons = (
        ("StudyInstanceUID", expected.study_uid),
        ("SeriesInstanceUID", expected.series_uid),
        ("SOPInstanceUID", expected.sop_instance_uid),
    )

    for attribute, original in comparisons:
        if original is None:
            continue

        current = getattr(dataset, attribute, None)

        if current is None or str(current) != original:
            raise DicomDecoderError(
                DecoderErrorCode.SOURCE_CHANGED
            )

    try:
        rows = int(dataset.Rows)
        columns = int(dataset.Columns)
    except (AttributeError, ValueError, TypeError):
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.SOURCE_CHANGED
        ) from None

    if (
        rows != expected.geometry.rows
        or columns != expected.geometry.columns
    ):
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.SOURCE_CHANGED
        )

    current_syntax = inspect_transfer_syntax(dataset)

    if (
        expected.pixels.transfer_syntax_uid is not None
        and current_syntax.uid
        != expected.pixels.transfer_syntax_uid
    ):
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.SOURCE_CHANGED
        )


# ============================================================
# 07 — DECODED MEMORY ESTIMATION
# ============================================================

def estimate_slice_memory(
    rows: int,
    columns: int,
    *,
    output_float32: bool = True,
) -> int:
    """
    Conservative planning estimate.

    Includes estimated decoded data, float conversion,
    and intermediate buffers. Not a peak-RAM guarantee.
    """

    if (
        isinstance(rows, bool)
        or isinstance(columns, bool)
        or not isinstance(rows, int)
        or not isinstance(columns, int)
        or rows <= 0
        or columns <= 0
    ):
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.INVALID_PIXELS
        )

    bytes_per_pixel = 4 if output_float32 else 4

    return rows * columns * bytes_per_pixel * 4


# ============================================================
# 08 — ACTUAL PIXEL DECODING
# ============================================================

def decode_dataset_pixels(
    dataset: Dataset,
    *,
    apply_modality_rescale: bool = True,
    max_decoded_slice_mb: int = MAX_DECODED_SLICE_MB,
) -> DecodeResult:
    """
    Decode pixels from an already loaded DICOM Dataset.

    Uses pydicom's installed decoder plugins.

    No Dataset.decompress() or Dataset.save_as() is used.
    The input dataset is not intentionally modified.

    Pixel decoding may internally allocate additional buffers.
    """

    syntax = inspect_transfer_syntax(dataset)

    if not syntax.recognized:
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.UNSUPPORTED_SYNTAX
        )

    rows, columns = validate_pixel_header(dataset)

    if max_decoded_slice_mb < 1:
        raise ValueError(
            "max_decoded_slice_mb must be positive."
        )

    estimated_bytes = estimate_slice_memory(
        rows,
        columns,
        output_float32=True,
    )

    if estimated_bytes > max_decoded_slice_mb * MIB:
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.MEMORY_LIMIT
        )

    if "PixelData" not in dataset:
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.INVALID_PIXELS
        )

    try:
        # Plugin selection is delegated to pydicom.
        # This supports uncompressed data and codecs
        # actually available in the Colab environment.
        decoded = dataset.pixel_array

    except MemoryError:
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.MEMORY_LIMIT
        ) from None

    except (RuntimeError, ImportError, NotImplementedError):
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.DECODER_UNAVAILABLE
        ) from None

    except (
        ValueError,
        TypeError,
        AttributeError,
        OSError,
    ):
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.DECODING_FAILED
        ) from None

    if (
        not isinstance(decoded, np.ndarray)
        or decoded.ndim != 2
        or decoded.shape != (rows, columns)
        or not np.issubdtype(decoded.dtype, np.number)
        or np.iscomplexobj(decoded)
    ):
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.INVALID_PIXELS
        )

    try:
        output = np.array(
            decoded,
            dtype=np.float32,
            copy=True,
        )

        if apply_modality_rescale:
            slope = float(
                getattr(dataset, "RescaleSlope", 1.0)
            )
            intercept = float(
                getattr(dataset, "RescaleIntercept", 0.0)
            )

            if (
                not math.isfinite(slope)
                or not math.isfinite(intercept)
            ):
                raise DicomDecoderError(
                    DecoderErrorCode.INVALID_PIXELS
                )

            # Equivalent linear rescale:
            # value = stored_value * slope + intercept
            output *= slope
            output += intercept

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(output)):
            raise DicomDecoderError(
                DecoderErrorCode.INVALID_PIXELS
            )

        output.setflags(write=False)

    except MemoryError:
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.MEMORY_LIMIT
        ) from None

    return DecodeResult(
        pixels=output,
        rows=rows,
        columns=columns,
        dtype=str(output.dtype),
        compressed_source=syntax.compressed,
        photometric_interpretation=str(
            dataset.PhotometricInterpretation
        ),
        modality_rescale_applied=apply_modality_rescale,
    )


# ============================================================
# 09 — READ AND DECODE FROM A DICOM FILE
# ============================================================

def decode_dicom_file(
    path: str | Path,
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
    *,
    expected_metadata: DicomMetadata | None = None,
    apply_modality_rescale: bool = True,
) -> DecodeResult:
    """
    Read one conventional DICOM image and decode pixels.

    Original file is opened only in binary read mode.
    Path validation restricts access to authorized datasets.

    No source modification, file creation or deletion.
    """

    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()

    try:
        safe_path = validate_dicom_path(
            path,
            active.storage.dataset.root_path,
        )

    except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError):
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.PATH_REJECTED
        ) from None

    try:
        file_size = safe_path.stat().st_size

        if (
            file_size <= 0
            or file_size > MAX_DICOM_FILE_MB * MIB
        ):
            raise DicomDecoderError(
                DecoderErrorCode.MEMORY_LIMIT
            )

        with safe_path.open("rb") as stream:
            dataset = pydicom.dcmread(
                stream,
                force=False,
            )

        if expected_metadata is not None:
            verify_expected_metadata(
                dataset,
                expected_metadata,
            )

        if (
            not active.dicom.decoding.enable_compressed_dicom
            and inspect_transfer_syntax(dataset).compressed
        ):
```
