            raise DicomDecoderError(
                DecoderErrorCode.UNSUPPORTED_SYNTAX
            )

        return decode_dataset_pixels(
            dataset,
            apply_modality_rescale=apply_modality_rescale,
        )

    except DicomDecoderError:
        raise

    except MemoryError:
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.MEMORY_LIMIT
        ) from None

    except (
        InvalidDicomError,
        OSError,
        EOFError,
        ValueError,
        TypeError,
    ):
        raise DicomDecoderError(
            DecoderErrorCode.INVALID_DICOM
        ) from None


# ============================================================
# 10 — SAFE RESULT WRAPPER
# ============================================================

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DecodeAttempt:
    success: bool
    result: DecodeResult | None = field(
        default=None,
        repr=False,
    )
    error_code: str | None = None

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "success": self.success,
            "error_code": self.error_code,
            "image": (
                self.result.safe_summary()
                if self.result else None
            ),
        }


def try_decode_dicom_file(
    path: str | Path,
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
    *,
    expected_metadata: DicomMetadata | None = None,
) -> DecodeAttempt:
    """
    Return a controlled outcome for expected decoder errors.

    Never fall back to fabricated or arbitrary pixel data.
    """

    try:
        result = decode_dicom_file(
            path,
            settings,
            expected_metadata=expected_metadata,
        )

        return DecodeAttempt(
            success=True,
            result=result,
        )

    except DicomDecoderError as exc:
        return DecodeAttempt(
            success=False,
            error_code=exc.code.value,
        )


# ============================================================
# 11 — DECODER CAPABILITIES
# ============================================================

def get_decoder_capabilities() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Describe implemented decoder contracts.

    This is not a live check of installed codec plugins.
    """

    return {
        "engine": "pydicom",
        "single_frame": True,
        "multiframe": False,
        "monochrome": True,
        "color": False,
        "output_dtype": "float32",
        "compressed_requires_plugins": True,
        "supported_pixel_bits": sorted(
            SUPPORTED_BITS_ALLOCATED
        ),
        "known_compressed_syntaxes": len(
            KNOWN_COMPRESSED_UIDS
        ),
        "modality_rescale": True,
        "read_only_source": True,
    }


# ============================================================
# END OF DICOM PIXEL DECODER
# ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/backend/app/dicom/geometry.py
```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — 3D GEOMETRY ENGINE
=================================

Medical image geometry, coordinate transformations and
physical measurement utilities.

Conventions:
    NumPy volume array order: (Z, Y, X)
    Voxel index order:       (X, Y, Z)
    DICOM physical space:    LPS
    Optional display space:  RAS
    Physical distance:       millimetres

DICOM:
    ImageOrientationPatient[0:3] is increasing column
    index direction (image row direction).
    ImageOrientationPatient[3:6] is increasing row
    index direction (image column direction).

    PixelSpacing[0] = row spacing (Y index)
    PixelSpacing[1] = column spacing (X index)

Limitations:
    - Affine regular grids only.
    - Nonuniform, tilted or sheared stacks require separate
      geometry handling or validated resampling.
    - No diagnostic orientation claims without testing.

No files are read, written or modified by this module.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Sequence

import numpy as np

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from .series_reader import SeriesVolume


# ============================================================
# 01 — CONSTANTS
# ============================================================

ORTHOGONAL_TOLERANCE = 1e-4
DETERMINANT_TOLERANCE = 1e-8

LPS_TO_RAS_MATRIX = np.diag(
    [-1.0, -1.0, 1.0]
).astype(np.float64)


# ============================================================
# 02 — ERRORS
# ============================================================

class GeometryError(ValueError):
    """Controlled geometry validation error."""


# ============================================================
# 03 — COORDINATE SYSTEMS
# ============================================================

class CoordinateSystem(str, Enum):
    LPS = "LPS"
    RAS = "RAS"


class AnatomicalPlane(str, Enum):
    AXIAL = "axial"
    CORONAL = "coronal"
    SAGITTAL = "sagittal"


# ============================================================
# 04 — VALIDATED VECTOR HELPERS
# ============================================================

def _vector3(
    values: Sequence[float],
) -> np.ndarray:
    try:
        result = np.asarray(
            values,
            dtype=np.float64,
        )
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        raise GeometryError(
            "Invalid coordinate vector."
        ) from None

    if result.shape != (3,):
        raise GeometryError(
            "Expected a three-dimensional vector."
        )

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(result)):
        raise GeometryError(
            "Non-finite coordinate value."
        )

    return result


def _positive_spacing(
    values: Sequence[float],
) -> np.ndarray:
    result = _vector3(values)

    if np.any(result <= 0):
        raise GeometryError(
            "Voxel spacing must be positive."
        )

    return result


def _direction_matrix(
    values: Sequence[float],
) -> np.ndarray:
    try:
        matrix = np.asarray(
            values,
            dtype=np.float64,
        ).reshape(3, 3)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        raise GeometryError(
            "Invalid direction matrix."
        ) from None

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(matrix)):
        raise GeometryError(
            "Direction matrix contains invalid values."
        )

    gram = matrix.T @ matrix

    if not np.allclose(
        gram,
        np.eye(3),
        atol=ORTHOGONAL_TOLERANCE,
        rtol=0,
    ):
        raise GeometryError(
            "Direction axes are not orthonormal."
        )

    determinant = float(np.linalg.det(matrix))

    if not math.isfinite(determinant):
        raise GeometryError(
            "Invalid direction determinant."
        )

    if abs(determinant - 1.0) > ORTHOGONAL_TOLERANCE:
        raise GeometryError(
            "Direction matrix must be right-handed."
        )

    if abs(determinant) < DETERMINANT_TOLERANCE:
        raise GeometryError(
            "Singular direction matrix."
        )

    return matrix


# ============================================================
# 05 — IMMUTABLE VOLUME GEOMETRY
# ============================================================

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VolumeGeometry:
    """
    Regular 3D grid in DICOM LPS coordinates.

    shape_zyx:
        Array shape in (Z, Y, X).

    spacing_xyz_mm:
        Physical spacing for increasing X, Y, Z voxel indices.

    direction_lps:
        Row-major 3x3 matrix.
        Its columns are X, Y and Z voxel axis directions.

    origin_lps:
        Centre of voxel (X=0, Y=0, Z=0).
    """

    shape_zyx: tuple[int, int, int]
    spacing_xyz_mm: tuple[float, float, float]
    origin_lps: tuple[float, float, float]
    direction_lps: tuple[float, ...]

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if (
            len(self.shape_zyx) != 3
            or any(
                not isinstance(v, int)
                or isinstance(v, bool)
                or v <= 0
                for v in self.shape_zyx
            )
        ):
            raise GeometryError(
                "Volume dimensions must be positive integers."
            )

        _positive_spacing(self.spacing_xyz_mm)
        _vector3(self.origin_lps)
        _direction_matrix(self.direction_lps)

    @property
    def dimensions_xyz(self) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
        z, y, x = self.shape_zyx
        return (x, y, z)

    @property
    def matrix_lps(self) -> np.ndarray:
        return _direction_matrix(
            self.direction_lps
        )

    @property
    def affine_lps(self) -> np.ndarray:
        """
        Map (X, Y, Z, 1) voxel indices to LPS coordinates.
        """

        affine = np.eye(4, dtype=np.float64)

        affine[:3, :3] = (
            self.matrix_lps
            @ np.diag(self.spacing_xyz_mm)
        )

        affine[:3, 3] = _vector3(
            self.origin_lps
        )

        return affine

    @property
    def affine_ras(self) -> np.ndarray:
        transform = np.eye(4, dtype=np.float64)
        transform[:3, :3] = LPS_TO_RAS_MATRIX

        return transform @ self.affine_lps

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "shape_zyx": list(self.shape_zyx),
            "dimensions_xyz": list(self.dimensions_xyz),
            "spacing_xyz_mm": list(self.spacing_xyz_mm),
            "coordinate_system": "LPS",
            "geometry_valid": True,
        }


# ============================================================
# 06 — CREATE FROM EXISTING SERIES VOLUME
# ============================================================

def geometry_from_series_volume(
    volume: SeriesVolume,
) -> VolumeGeometry:
    """
    Convert SeriesVolume metadata to VolumeGeometry.

    SeriesVolume spacing is stored in (Z, Y, X) order.
    """

    spacing_z, spacing_y, spacing_x = (
        volume.spacing_zyx_mm
    )

    return VolumeGeometry(
        shape_zyx=volume.shape_zyx,
        spacing_xyz_mm=(
            spacing_x,
            spacing_y,
            spacing_z,
        ),
        origin_lps=volume.origin_lps,
        direction_lps=volume.direction_lps,
    )


# ============================================================
# 07 — VOXEL TO PHYSICAL SPACE
# ============================================================

def voxel_to_lps(
    geometry: VolumeGeometry,
    index_xyz: Sequence[float],
) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
    """
    Convert fractional voxel indices (X,Y,Z) to LPS mm.
    """

    index = _vector3(index_xyz)

    point = (
        geometry.matrix_lps
        @ (
            index
            * np.asarray(
                geometry.spacing_xyz_mm,
                dtype=np.float64,
            )
        )
        + _vector3(geometry.origin_lps)
    )

    return tuple(float(value) for value in point)


# ============================================================
# 08 — PHYSICAL SPACE TO VOXEL
# ============================================================

def lps_to_voxel(
    geometry: VolumeGeometry,
    point_lps: Sequence[float],
) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
    """
    Convert LPS millimetres to fractional (X,Y,Z) indices.

    Values outside the volume are returned without clipping.
    """

    displacement = (
        _vector3(point_lps)
        - _vector3(geometry.origin_lps)
    )

    projected = (
        geometry.matrix_lps.T
        @ displacement
    )

    index = projected / np.asarray(
        geometry.spacing_xyz_mm,
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    return tuple(float(value) for value in index)


# ============================================================
# 09 — LPS AND RAS TRANSFORMATIONS
# ============================================================

def lps_to_ras(
    point_lps: Sequence[float],
) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
    point = LPS_TO_RAS_MATRIX @ _vector3(point_lps)
    return tuple(float(v) for v in point)


def ras_to_lps(
    point_ras: Sequence[float],
) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
    # The conversion is its own inverse.
    point = LPS_TO_RAS_MATRIX @ _vector3(point_ras)
    return tuple(float(v) for v in point)


def voxel_to_ras(
    geometry: VolumeGeometry,
    index_xyz: Sequence[float],
) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
    return lps_to_ras(
        voxel_to_lps(geometry, index_xyz)
    )


def ras_to_voxel(
    geometry: VolumeGeometry,
    point_ras: Sequence[float],
) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
    return lps_to_voxel(
        geometry,
        ras_to_lps(point_ras),
    )


# ============================================================
# 10 — VOXEL INDEX RANGE CHECK
# ============================================================

def is_voxel_inside(
    geometry: VolumeGeometry,
    index_xyz: Sequence[float],
    *,
    tolerance: float = 1e-6,
) -> bool:
    index = _vector3(index_xyz)

    if (
        not math.isfinite(tolerance)
        or tolerance < 0
    ):
        raise GeometryError(
            "Invalid coordinate tolerance."
        )

    return all(
        -tolerance <= index[axis] <= (
            geometry.dimensions_xyz[axis] - 1
            + tolerance
        )
        for axis in range(3)
    )


def clamp_voxel_index(
    geometry: VolumeGeometry,
    index_xyz: Sequence[float],
) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
    index = _vector3(index_xyz)
    dimensions = geometry.dimensions_xyz

    result = np.clip(
        index,
        [0.0, 0.0, 0.0],
        [
            float(dimensions[0] - 1),
            float(dimensions[1] - 1),
            float(dimensions[2] - 1),
        ],
    )

    return tuple(float(v) for v in result)


# ============================================================
# 11 — VOLUME CENTRE
# ============================================================

def volume_center_voxel(
    geometry: VolumeGeometry,
) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
    return tuple(
        (dimension - 1) / 2.0
        for dimension in geometry.dimensions_xyz
    )


def volume_center_lps(
    geometry: VolumeGeometry,
) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
    return voxel_to_lps(
        geometry,
        volume_center_voxel(geometry),
    )


# ============================================================
# 12 — PHYSICAL BOUNDS
# ============================================================

def volume_bounds_lps(
    geometry: VolumeGeometry,
) -> tuple[
    tuple[float, float],
    tuple[float, float],
    tuple[float, float],
]:
    """
    Axis-aligned LPS bounds of voxel centres.

    This is not the outer voxel-cell surface extent.
    """

    x, y, z = geometry.dimensions_xyz

    corners = np.asarray([
        voxel_to_lps(geometry, (ix, iy, iz))
        for ix in (0, x - 1)
        for iy in (0, y - 1)
        for iz in (0, z - 1)
    ])

    return tuple(
        (
            float(corners[:, axis].min()),
            float(corners[:, axis].max()),
        )
        for axis in range(3)
    )


# ============================================================
# 13 — DISTANCE MEASUREMENTS
# ============================================================

def physical_distance_mm(
    first_point: Sequence[float],
    second_point: Sequence[float],
) -> float:
    """
    Euclidean distance between two physical points in mm.
    """

    a = _vector3(first_point)
    b = _vector3(second_point)

    return float(np.linalg.norm(a - b))


def voxel_distance_mm(
    geometry: VolumeGeometry,
    first_index_xyz: Sequence[float],
    second_index_xyz: Sequence[float],
) -> float:
    return physical_distance_mm(
        voxel_to_lps(geometry, first_index_xyz),
        voxel_to_lps(geometry, second_index_xyz),
    )


# ============================================================
# 14 — ANATOMICAL PLANES
# ============================================================

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PlaneDefinition:
    name: AnatomicalPlane
    normal_lps: tuple[float, float, float]
    horizontal_lps: tuple[float, float, float]
    vertical_lps: tuple[float, float, float]


def get_anatomical_plane(
    plane: AnatomicalPlane,
) -> PlaneDefinition:
    """
    Canonical patient-axis anatomical planes.

    These describe plane orientation, not final screen
    radiological display convention or camera flipping.
    """

    if plane == AnatomicalPlane.AXIAL:
        return PlaneDefinition(
            name=plane,
            normal_lps=(0.0, 0.0, 1.0),
            horizontal_lps=(1.0, 0.0, 0.0),
            vertical_lps=(0.0, 1.0, 0.0),
        )

    if plane == AnatomicalPlane.CORONAL:
        return PlaneDefinition(
            name=plane,
            normal_lps=(0.0, 1.0, 0.0),
            horizontal_lps=(1.0, 0.0, 0.0),
            vertical_lps=(0.0, 0.0, 1.0),
        )

    if plane == AnatomicalPlane.SAGITTAL:
        return PlaneDefinition(
            name=plane,
            normal_lps=(1.0, 0.0, 0.0),
            horizontal_lps=(0.0, 1.0, 0.0),
            vertical_lps=(0.0, 0.0, 1.0),
        )

    raise GeometryError(
        "Unsupported anatomical plane."
    )


# ============================================================
# 15 — CROSSHAIR COORDINATE HELPERS
# ============================================================

def crosshair_from_voxel(
    geometry: VolumeGeometry,
    index_xyz: Sequence[float],
) -> dict:
    """
    Shared crosshair location for synchronized viewports.

    UI may use the LPS or RAS point depending on its
    configured world-coordinate convention.
    """

    if not is_voxel_inside(geometry, index_xyz):
        raise GeometryError(
            "Crosshair position is outside the volume."
        )

    voxel = _vector3(index_xyz)
    point_lps = voxel_to_lps(geometry, voxel)

    return {
        "voxel_xyz": [float(v) for v in voxel],
        "world_lps": list(point_lps),
        "world_ras": list(lps_to_ras(point_lps)),
    }


def crosshair_from_lps(
    geometry: VolumeGeometry,
    point_lps: Sequence[float],
) -> dict:
    """
    Create synchronized crosshair state from LPS coordinates.
    """

    index = lps_to_voxel(
        geometry,
        point_lps,
    )

    return crosshair_from_voxel(
        geometry,
        index,
    )


# ============================================================
# 16 — 4x4 MATRIX SERIALIZATION
# ============================================================

def serialize_affine(
    geometry: VolumeGeometry,
    coordinate_system: CoordinateSystem = CoordinateSystem.LPS,
) -> list[list[float]]:
    """
    Serialize a 4x4 index-to-world affine as nested rows.

    Frontend must explicitly convert row-major serialized
    matrices if its library expects column-major storage.
    """

    if coordinate_system == CoordinateSystem.LPS:
        affine = geometry.affine_lps

    elif coordinate_system == CoordinateSystem.RAS:
        affine = geometry.affine_ras

    else:
        raise GeometryError(
            "Unsupported coordinate system."
        )

    return affine.astype(float).tolist()


# ============================================================
# 17 — GEOMETRY REPORT
# ============================================================

def build_geometry_report(
    geometry: VolumeGeometry,
) -> dict:
    """
    Technical geometry metadata for authenticated APIs.

    No patient names, source paths or identifiers included.
    """

    return {
        **geometry.safe_summary(),
        "origin_lps": list(geometry.origin_lps),
        "direction_lps": list(geometry.direction_lps),
        "center_lps": list(volume_center_lps(geometry)),
        "bounds_lps": [
            list(bounds)
            for bounds in volume_bounds_lps(geometry)
        ],
        "affine_lps": serialize_affine(
            geometry,
            CoordinateSystem.LPS,
        ),
        "affine_ras": serialize_affine(
            geometry,
            CoordinateSystem.RAS,
        ),
    }


# ============================================================
# END OF GEOMETRY ENGINE
# ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/backend/app/dicom/metadata.py
```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — DICOM METADATA ENGINE
====================================

Purpose:
- Read medical image metadata without decoding pixels.
- Identify studies, series and individual instances.
- Extract geometry information needed for MPR.
- Identify compressed transfer syntaxes.
- Support conventional and multiframe DICOM metadata.
- Return structured results and controlled failures.

Dataset protection:
- Strictly read-only operations.
- No renaming, modification, deletion or writing.
- Reject paths outside the authorized dataset root.
- Reject symlinks along the source path.
- Never place patient identifiers in logs or errors.

Important:
- This module does NOT build 3D volumes.
- This module does NOT establish clinically valid geometry.
- Enhanced multiframe per-frame geometry is handled later.

Python 3.10+
pydicom 3.x
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import os
import stat
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Literal

import pydicom
from pydicom.dataset import Dataset
from pydicom.errors import InvalidDicomError
from pydicom.uid import UID

from ..core.config import AppSettings, get_settings


# ============================================================
# 01 — CONSTANTS
# ============================================================

SUPPORTED_IMAGE_MODALITIES = frozenset({
    "CT",
    "CBCT",
    "DX",
    "CR",
    "OT",
    "XA",
})

DICOM_EXTENSIONS = frozenset({
    ".dcm",
    ".dicom",
    ".ima",
    "",
})

MAX_METADATA_FILE_BYTES = 1024 ** 3 * 4

METADATA_TAGS = [
    "PatientID",
    "PatientName",
    "StudyInstanceUID",
    "SeriesInstanceUID",
    "SOPInstanceUID",
    "SOPClassUID",
    "FrameOfReferenceUID",
    "Modality",
    "Manufacturer",
    "ManufacturerModelName",
    "StudyDescription",
    "SeriesDescription",
    "StudyDate",
    "StudyTime",
    "SeriesNumber",
    "InstanceNumber",
    "Rows",
    "Columns",
    "NumberOfFrames",
    "SamplesPerPixel",
    "PhotometricInterpretation",
    "BitsAllocated",
    "BitsStored",
    "HighBit",
    "PixelRepresentation",
    "PixelSpacing",
    "ImagerPixelSpacing",
    "SliceThickness",
    "SpacingBetweenSlices",
    "ImagePositionPatient",
    "ImageOrientationPatient",
    "RescaleSlope",
    "RescaleIntercept",
    "RescaleType",
    "ImageType",
    "VolumetricProperties",
    "BodyPartExamined",
    "AcquisitionNumber",
    "SliceLocation",
]

MetadataStatus = Literal[
    "ok",
    "invalid",
    "unreadable",
    "unsupported",
]


# ============================================================
# 02 — CONTROLLED ERRORS
# ============================================================

class MetadataError(Exception):
    """Base metadata processing error."""

    public_code = "DICOM_INVALID"

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__("DICOM metadata could not be processed.")


class MetadataPathError(MetadataError):
    public_code = "PATH_REJECTED"


class MetadataReadError(MetadataError):
    public_code = "DICOM_INVALID"


class MetadataUnsupportedError(MetadataError):
    public_code = "DICOM_UNSUPPORTED"


# ============================================================
# 03 — STRUCTURED METADATA MODELS
# ============================================================

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DicomGeometry:
    rows: int | None = None
    columns: int | None = None

    pixel_spacing: tuple[float, float] | None = None
    imager_pixel_spacing: tuple[float, float] | None = None

    image_position: tuple[float, float, float] | None = None

    image_orientation: (
        tuple[float, float, float, float, float, float] | None
    ) = None

    slice_thickness: float | None = None
    spacing_between_slices: float | None = None

    geometry_complete: bool = False


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DicomPixelInfo:
    frames: int = 1
    samples_per_pixel: int | None = None
    photometric_interpretation: str | None = None

    bits_allocated: int | None = None
    bits_stored: int | None = None
    high_bit: int | None = None
    pixel_representation: int | None = None

    transfer_syntax_uid: str | None = None
    is_compressed: bool | None = None

    rescale_slope: float | None = None
    rescale_intercept: float | None = None
    rescale_type: str | None = None

    @property
    def is_multiframe(self) -> bool:
        return self.frames > 1


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DicomMetadata:
    # Internal indexing identifiers.
    # Do not serialize this object directly into public API
    # responses, logs or debugging output.

    source_path: Path = field(repr=False)

    patient_id: str | None = field(default=None, repr=False)
    patient_name: str | None = field(default=None, repr=False)

    study_uid: str | None = field(default=None, repr=False)
    series_uid: str | None = field(default=None, repr=False)
    sop_instance_uid: str | None = field(
        default=None,
        repr=False,
    )
    sop_class_uid: str | None = field(
        default=None,
        repr=False,
    )
    frame_of_reference_uid: str | None = field(
        default=None,
        repr=False,
    )

    modality: str | None = None
    manufacturer: str | None = field(default=None, repr=False)
    manufacturer_model: str | None = field(
        default=None,
        repr=False,
    )

    study_description: str | None = field(
        default=None,
        repr=False,
    )
    series_description: str | None = field(
        default=None,
        repr=False,
    )
    study_date: str | None = field(default=None, repr=False)

    series_number: int | None = None
    instance_number: int | None = None

    geometry: DicomGeometry = field(
        default_factory=DicomGeometry
    )

    pixels: DicomPixelInfo = field(
        default_factory=DicomPixelInfo
    )

    file_size_bytes: int = 0

    @property
    def is_volume_candidate(self) -> bool:
        return (
            self.geometry.rows is not None
            and self.geometry.columns is not None
            and self.geometry.rows > 0
            and self.geometry.columns > 0
            and self.modality in SUPPORTED_IMAGE_MODALITIES
        )

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Non-identifying technical summary only.

        Do not add identifiers, source paths, descriptions
        or manufacturer free text to this response.
        """

        return {
            "modality": self.modality,
            "rows": self.geometry.rows,
            "columns": self.geometry.columns,
            "frames": self.pixels.frames,
            "multiframe": self.pixels.is_multiframe,
            "geometry_complete": (
                self.geometry.geometry_complete
            ),
            "bits_allocated": self.pixels.bits_allocated,
            "compressed": self.pixels.is_compressed,
            "volume_candidate": self.is_volume_candidate,
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MetadataResult:
    status: MetadataStatus
    metadata: DicomMetadata | None = field(
        default=None,
        repr=False,
    )
    error_code: str | None = None

    @property
    def success(self) -> bool:
        return self.status == "ok" and self.metadata is not None


# ============================================================
# 04 — BASIC FIELD CONVERSION
# ============================================================

def _text(value: Any) -> str | None:
    if value is None:
        return None

    result = str(value).strip()

    return result or None


def _integer(value: Any) -> int | None:
    if value is None:
        return None

    try:
        result = int(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
        return None

    return result


def _float(value: Any) -> float | None:
    if value is None:
        return None

    try:
        result = float(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
        return None

    return result if math.isfinite(result) else None


def _vector(
    value: Any,
    length: int,
) -> tuple[float, ...] | None:
    if value is None:
        return None

    try:
        values = list(value)
    except TypeError:
        return None

    if len(values) != length:
        return None

    converted = tuple(_float(v) for v in values)

    if any(v is None for v in converted):
        return None

    return converted


def _positive_vector(
    value: Any,
    length: int,
) -> tuple[float, ...] | None:
    converted = _vector(value, length)

    if converted is None or any(v <= 0 for v in converted):
        return None

    return converted


# ============================================================
# 05 — SOURCE PATH PROTECTION
# ============================================================

def validate_dicom_path(
    path: str | Path,
    dataset_root: str | Path,
) -> Path:
    """
    Restrict reading to files inside the configured dataset.

    Reject:
    - Paths outside dataset root.
    - Symlinked directory components.
    - Symlinked DICOM files.
    - Missing and non-regular files.

    The later path_guard module will centralize filesystem
    access and handle additional race-condition protections.
    """

    root = Path(dataset_root).expanduser().absolute()
    candidate = Path(path).expanduser().absolute()

    if candidate == root or not candidate.is_relative_to(root):
        raise MetadataPathError()

    # Check the directory chain for symlinks.
    current = root

    try:
        if current.is_symlink():
            raise MetadataPathError()

        relative_parts = candidate.relative_to(root).parts

        for part in relative_parts:
            current = current / part

            if current.is_symlink():
                raise MetadataPathError()

        resolved_root = root.resolve(strict=True)
        resolved_file = candidate.resolve(strict=True)

        if not resolved_file.is_relative_to(resolved_root):
            raise MetadataPathError()

        file_stat = resolved_file.stat()

        if not stat.S_ISREG(file_stat.st_mode):
            raise MetadataPathError()

    except MetadataPathError:
        raise
    except (OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError):
        raise MetadataPathError() from None

    return resolved_file


# ============================================================
# 06 — TRANSFER SYNTAX IDENTIFICATION
# ============================================================

def read_transfer_syntax(
    dataset: Dataset,
) -> tuple[str | None, bool | None]:
    """
    Return (transfer_syntax_uid, compressed).

    Missing transfer syntax is reported as unknown.
    """

    file_meta = getattr(dataset, "file_meta", None)

    if file_meta is None:
        return None, None

    value = getattr(
        file_meta,
        "TransferSyntaxUID",
        None,
    )

    if value is None:
        return None, None

    try:
        uid = UID(str(value))

        return str(uid), bool(uid.is_compressed)
    except (TypeError, ValueError, AttributeError):
        return None, None


# ============================================================
# 07 — GEOMETRY EXTRACTION
# ============================================================

def extract_geometry(dataset: Dataset) -> DicomGeometry:
    rows = _integer(getattr(dataset, "Rows", None))
    columns = _integer(getattr(dataset, "Columns", None))

    spacing = _positive_vector(
        getattr(dataset, "PixelSpacing", None),
        2,
    )

    imager_spacing = _positive_vector(
        getattr(dataset, "ImagerPixelSpacing", None),
        2,
    )

    position = _vector(
        getattr(dataset, "ImagePositionPatient", None),
        3,
    )

    orientation = _vector(
        getattr(dataset, "ImageOrientationPatient", None),
        6,
    )

    thickness = _float(
        getattr(dataset, "SliceThickness", None)
    )

    between_slices = _float(
        getattr(dataset, "SpacingBetweenSlices", None)
    )

    if thickness is not None and thickness <= 0:
        thickness = None

    valid_dimensions = (
        rows is not None
        and columns is not None
        and rows > 0
        and columns > 0
    )

    # Presence is necessary but not sufficient for valid MPR.
    complete = (
        valid_dimensions
        and spacing is not None
        and position is not None
        and orientation is not None
    )

    return DicomGeometry(
        rows=rows,
        columns=columns,
        pixel_spacing=spacing,
        imager_pixel_spacing=imager_spacing,
        image_position=position,
        image_orientation=orientation,
        slice_thickness=thickness,
        spacing_between_slices=between_slices,
        geometry_complete=bool(complete),
    )


# ============================================================
# 08 — PIXEL HEADER EXTRACTION
# ============================================================

def extract_pixel_info(dataset: Dataset) -> DicomPixelInfo:
    transfer_uid, compressed = read_transfer_syntax(dataset)

    frames = _integer(
        getattr(dataset, "NumberOfFrames", None)
    )

    if frames is None or frames < 1:
        frames = 1

    return DicomPixelInfo(
        frames=frames,
        samples_per_pixel=_integer(
            getattr(dataset, "SamplesPerPixel", None)
        ),
        photometric_interpretation=_text(
            getattr(dataset, "PhotometricInterpretation", None)
        ),
        bits_allocated=_integer(
            getattr(dataset, "BitsAllocated", None)
        ),
        bits_stored=_integer(
            getattr(dataset, "BitsStored", None)
        ),
        high_bit=_integer(
            getattr(dataset, "HighBit", None)
        ),
        pixel_representation=_integer(
            getattr(dataset, "PixelRepresentation", None)
        ),
        transfer_syntax_uid=transfer_uid,
        is_compressed=compressed,
        rescale_slope=_float(
            getattr(dataset, "RescaleSlope", None)
        ),
        rescale_intercept=_float(
            getattr(dataset, "RescaleIntercept", None)
        ),
        rescale_type=_text(
            getattr(dataset, "RescaleType", None)
        ),
    )


# ============================================================
# 09 — CONVERT DATASET TO STRUCTURED METADATA
# ============================================================

def extract_metadata(
    dataset: Dataset,
    *,
    source_path: Path,
    file_size_bytes: int,
) -> DicomMetadata:
    """
    Convert a pydicom Dataset to internal structured data.

    Never log or automatically serialize the result.
    """

    return DicomMetadata(
        source_path=source_path,
        patient_id=_text(
            getattr(dataset, "PatientID", None)
        ),
        patient_name=_text(
            getattr(dataset, "PatientName", None)
        ),
        study_uid=_text(
            getattr(dataset, "StudyInstanceUID", None)
        ),
        series_uid=_text(
            getattr(dataset, "SeriesInstanceUID", None)
        ),
        sop_instance_uid=_text(
            getattr(dataset, "SOPInstanceUID", None)
        ),
        sop_class_uid=_text(
            getattr(dataset, "SOPClassUID", None)
        ),
        frame_of_reference_uid=_text(
            getattr(dataset, "FrameOfReferenceUID", None)
        ),
        modality=_text(
            getattr(dataset, "Modality", None)
        ),
        manufacturer=_text(
            getattr(dataset, "Manufacturer", None)
        ),
        manufacturer_model=_text(
            getattr(dataset, "ManufacturerModelName", None)
        ),
        study_description=_text(
            getattr(dataset, "StudyDescription", None)
        ),
        series_description=_text(
            getattr(dataset, "SeriesDescription", None)
        ),
        study_date=_text(
            getattr(dataset, "StudyDate", None)
        ),
        series_number=_integer(
            getattr(dataset, "SeriesNumber", None)
        ),
        instance_number=_integer(
            getattr(dataset, "InstanceNumber", None)
        ),
        geometry=extract_geometry(dataset),
        pixels=extract_pixel_info(dataset),
        file_size_bytes=file_size_bytes,
    )


# ============================================================
# 10 — READ DICOM METADATA
# ============================================================

def read_dicom_metadata(
    path: str | Path,
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
    *,
    allow_non_part10: bool = False,
) -> DicomMetadata:
    """
    Read DICOM headers without decoding pixel data.

    Strict default:
    - Standard Part-10 files only.
    - Non-Part-10 reading requires explicit opt-in.
    - No arbitrary filesystem paths.
    - No writing or source modification.

    Non-Part-10 parsing may be useful for vendor exports,
    but its success does not prove the file is valid DICOM.
    """

    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()

    root = active.storage.dataset.root_path

    safe_path = validate_dicom_path(path, root)

    try:
        file_size = safe_path.stat().st_size

        if file_size <= 0:
            raise MetadataReadError()

        if file_size > MAX_METADATA_FILE_BYTES:
            raise MetadataUnsupportedError()

        # Open source in read-binary mode only.
        # No pixel arrays are accessed.
        with safe_path.open("rb") as stream:
            dataset = pydicom.dcmread(
                stream,
                stop_before_pixels=True,
                force=allow_non_part10,
                specific_tags=METADATA_TAGS,
            )

        # Reject arbitrary binary files that accidentally
        # parse when force=True was explicitly requested.
        if not (
            getattr(dataset, "SOPClassUID", None)
            or getattr(dataset, "Modality", None)
            or getattr(dataset, "Rows", None)
        ):
            raise MetadataReadError()

        metadata = extract_metadata(
            dataset,
            source_path=safe_path,
            file_size_bytes=file_size,
        )

        return metadata

    except (MetadataReadError, MetadataUnsupportedError):
        raise

    except (
        InvalidDicomError,
        OSError,
        EOFError,
        ValueError,
        TypeError,
    ):
        # Never put raw exception strings or file paths
        # into application errors.
        raise MetadataReadError() from None


# ============================================================
# 11 — CONTROLLED RESULT WRAPPER
# ============================================================

def inspect_dicom_metadata(
    path: str | Path,
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
    *,
    allow_non_part10: bool = False,
) -> MetadataResult:
    """
    Safe wrapper for bulk patient-folder scanning.

    Returns an error code rather than throwing expected
    metadata and filesystem exceptions.
    """

    try:
        metadata = read_dicom_metadata(
            path,
            settings=settings,
            allow_non_part10=allow_non_part10,
        )

        return MetadataResult(
            status="ok",
            metadata=metadata,
        )

    except MetadataPathError:
        return MetadataResult(
            status="unreadable",
            error_code="PATH_REJECTED",
        )

    except MetadataUnsupportedError:
        return MetadataResult(
            status="unsupported",
            error_code="DICOM_UNSUPPORTED",
        )

    except MetadataReadError:
        return MetadataResult(
            status="invalid",
            error_code="DICOM_INVALID",
        )


# ============================================================
# 12 — INTERNAL SERIES GROUPING KEYS
# ============================================================

def get_series_group_key(
    metadata: DicomMetadata,
) -> tuple[str | None, str | None]:
    """
    Return internal Study/Series UID grouping keys.

    Missing UIDs remain None. Do not use fabricated
    identifiers for clinical series grouping.

    Never expose these identifiers without authorization.
    """

    return metadata.study_uid, metadata.series_uid


def get_instance_sort_hint(
    metadata: DicomMetadata,
) -> int | None:
    """
    Return InstanceNumber as a diagnostic hint only.

    Correct 3D ordering must use DICOM geometry:
    ImagePositionPatient and ImageOrientationPatient.

    InstanceNumber must never be the sole ordering rule.
    """

    return metadata.instance_number


# ============================================================
# 13 — PUBLIC-SAFE TECHNICAL SUMMARY
# ============================================================

def build_safe_metadata_summary(
    metadata: DicomMetadata,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Return only selected technical imaging attributes.

    This result deliberately excludes:
    - PatientName and PatientID
    - Study, Series and SOP UIDs
    - File paths and filenames
    - Study dates and free-text descriptions
    - Manufacturer identifiers

    The API must still require authentication.
    """

    return metadata.safe_summary()


# ============================================================
# END OF DICOM METADATA ENGINE
# ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/backend/app/dicom/scanner.py
```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — READ-ONLY DICOM DATASET SCANNER
==============================================

Purpose:
- Discover patient folders inside a selected dataset.
- Recursively inspect supported DICOM files.
- Read metadata without decoding pixel arrays.
- Group scans by StudyInstanceUID and SeriesInstanceUID.
- Report missing, invalid and unsupported files.
- Preserve multiple studies and series per patient.
- Generate stable internal handles for navigation.
- Never modify source datasets.

Patient layout:
    dataset/
        Patient_001/
            scan1.dcm
            scan2.dcm
        Patient_002/
            DICOM/
                image001.dcm

Security:
- Dataset path must remain inside authorized root.
- Symlink directories and files are rejected.
- Patient identities and paths remain internal.
- Public-safe reports omit identifiers and filenames.
- No filesystem writing takes place.

This is a synchronous scanning foundation.
Background indexing and API pagination will be implemented
in the services and API layers.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import os
import stat
import threading
from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Callable, Iterator, Sequence

from ..core.config import AppSettings, get_settings
from .metadata import (
    DicomMetadata,
    inspect_dicom_metadata,
)
from .validator import (
    SeriesValidationReport,
    validate_series,
)


# ============================================================
# 01 — CONSTANTS
# ============================================================

SUPPORTED_DEFAULT_EXTENSIONS = frozenset({
    ".dcm",
    ".dicom",
    ".ima",
    "",
})

IGNORED_FILENAMES = frozenset({
    "DICOMDIR",
    "DICOMRM",
    "desktop.ini",
    "Thumbs.db",
})

ERROR_CATEGORIES = frozenset({
    "PATH_REJECTED",
    "DICOM_INVALID",
    "DICOM_UNSUPPORTED",
    "DIRECTORY_UNREADABLE",
    "SCAN_CANCELLED",
})

DEFAULT_MAX_CANDIDATE_FILES = 250000

ProgressCallback = Callable[[int, int], None]


# ============================================================
# 02 — SCANNER EXCEPTIONS
# ============================================================

class ScannerError(Exception):
    """Base scanner exception without sensitive path details."""

    def __init__(self, message: str = "Dataset scanning failed."):
        super().__init__(message)


class ScannerPathError(ScannerError):
    """Invalid or unauthorized dataset directory."""


class ScannerCancelled(ScannerError):
    """Scanning was cancelled by the caller."""


class ScannerLimitExceeded(ScannerError):
    """Configured scanner resource limit exceeded."""


# ============================================================
# 03 — RESULT DATA MODELS
# ============================================================

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ScanStatistics:
    patient_count: int
    series_count: int
    dicom_files: int
    invalid_files: int
    unsupported_files: int
    rejected_files: int
    unreadable_directories: int
    inspected_candidates: int
    total_frames: int
    cancelled: bool = False

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "patient_count": self.patient_count,
            "series_count": self.series_count,
            "dicom_files": self.dicom_files,
            "invalid_files": self.invalid_files,
            "unsupported_files": self.unsupported_files,
            "rejected_files": self.rejected_files,
            "unreadable_directories": self.unreadable_directories,
            "inspected_candidates": self.inspected_candidates,
            "total_frames": self.total_frames,
            "cancelled": self.cancelled,
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ScanSeries:
    handle: str
    patient_handle: str

    study_uid: str | None = field(repr=False)
    series_uid: str | None = field(repr=False)

    instances: tuple[DicomMetadata, ...] = field(
        repr=False,
        default_factory=tuple,
    )

    validation: SeriesValidationReport | None = field(
        repr=False,
        default=None,
    )

    @property
    def file_count(self) -> int:
        return len(self.instances)

    @property
    def frame_count(self) -> int:
        return sum(
            max(1, item.pixels.frames)
            for item in self.instances
        )

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "handle": self.handle,
            "patient_handle": self.patient_handle,
            "file_count": self.file_count,
            "frame_count": self.frame_count,
            "modalities": sorted({
                item.modality
                for item in self.instances
                if item.modality
            }),
            "volume_ready": (
                self.validation.volume_ready
                if self.validation else False
            ),
            "validation": (
                self.validation.safe_summary()
                if self.validation else None
            ),
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ScanPatient:
    handle: str
    folder_name: str = field(repr=False)
    folder_path: Path = field(repr=False)

    series: tuple[ScanSeries, ...] = ()

    @property
    def file_count(self) -> int:
        return sum(s.file_count for s in self.series)

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "handle": self.handle,
            "series_count": len(self.series),
            "file_count": self.file_count,
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class DatasetScanResult:
    dataset_path: Path = field(repr=False)
    patients: tuple[ScanPatient, ...] = ()
    statistics: ScanStatistics | None = None

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "patients": [
                patient.safe_summary()
                for patient in self.patients
            ],
            "statistics": (
                self.statistics.safe_summary()
                if self.statistics else None
            ),
        }

    def get_patient(
        self,
        handle: str,
    ) -> ScanPatient | None:
        return next(
            (p for p in self.patients if p.handle == handle),
            None,
        )

    def get_series(
        self,
        handle: str,
    ) -> ScanSeries | None:
        for patient in self.patients:
            for series in patient.series:
                if series.handle == handle:
                    return series
        return None


# ============================================================
# 04 — INTERNAL OPAQUE HANDLES
# ============================================================

def make_handle(
    kind: str,
    *parts: str,
) -> str:
    """
    Generate an internal deterministic lookup handle.

    This is NOT authentication, anonymization or an access
    token. API authorization must be enforced separately.
    """

    digest = hashlib.sha256()

    for part in (kind, *parts):
        encoded = part.encode("utf-8", errors="surrogatepass")
        digest.update(len(encoded).to_bytes(8, "big"))
        digest.update(encoded)

    return f"{kind}_{digest.hexdigest()[:24]}"


# ============================================================
# 05 — DATASET PATH VALIDATION
# ============================================================

def validate_dataset_directory(
    path: str | Path,
    settings: AppSettings,
) -> Path:
    """
    Validate a directory under the authorized dataset root.

    Reject symlinks in every component between dataset root
    and the selected directory.
    """

    root = settings.storage.dataset.root_path.expanduser().absolute()
    candidate = Path(path).expanduser()

    if not candidate.is_absolute():
        candidate = root / candidate

    candidate = candidate.absolute()

    if not candidate.is_relative_to(root):
        raise ScannerPathError("Dataset path is not permitted.")

    try:
        current = root

        if current.is_symlink():
            raise ScannerPathError("Symlink paths are not permitted.")

        for component in candidate.relative_to(root).parts:
            current = current / component

            if current.is_symlink():
                raise ScannerPathError(
                    "Symlink paths are not permitted."
                )

        resolved_root = root.resolve(strict=True)
        resolved = candidate.resolve(strict=True)

        if not resolved.is_relative_to(resolved_root):
            raise ScannerPathError("Dataset path is not permitted.")

        if not resolved.is_dir():
            raise ScannerPathError("Dataset directory not found.")

    except ScannerPathError:
        raise
    except (OSError, RuntimeError, ValueError):
        raise ScannerPathError(
            "Dataset directory is unavailable."
        ) from None

    return resolved


# ============================================================
# 06 — FILE ELIGIBILITY
# ============================================================

def is_dicom_candidate(
    filename: str,
    settings: AppSettings,
) -> bool:
    """
    Identify candidate file names only.

    True does not prove the file contains valid DICOM data.
    """

    discovery = settings.dicom.discovery

    if filename in IGNORED_FILENAMES:
        return False

    if discovery.ignore_hidden_files and filename.startswith("."):
        return False

    extension = Path(filename).suffix.lower()

    allowed = set(discovery.supported_extensions)

    if extension:
        return extension in allowed

    return discovery.detect_extensionless_dicom


# ============================================================
# 07 — READ-ONLY DIRECTORY WALKER
# ============================================================

def iter_candidate_files(
    patient_directory: Path,
    settings: AppSettings,
    *,
    cancel_event: threading.Event | None = None,
    on_directory_error: Callable[[], None] | None = None,
) -> Iterator[Path]:
    """
    Iterative directory traversal.

    Avoids recursive Python call-stack growth.
    Does not follow symlinks.
    Does not write to source folders.
    """

    discovery = settings.dicom.discovery

    stack: list[tuple[Path, int]] = [
        (patient_directory, 0)
    ]

    while stack:
        if cancel_event is not None and cancel_event.is_set():
            raise ScannerCancelled("Dataset scan cancelled.")

        directory, depth = stack.pop()

        try:
            with os.scandir(directory) as iterator:
                entries = sorted(
                    list(iterator),
                    key=lambda e: e.name.casefold(),
                )

        except OSError:
            if on_directory_error:
                on_directory_error()
            continue

        for entry in entries:
            if cancel_event is not None and cancel_event.is_set():
                raise ScannerCancelled("Dataset scan cancelled.")

            name = entry.name

            if (
                discovery.ignore_hidden_files
                and name.startswith(".")
            ):
                continue

            try:
                if entry.is_symlink():
                    continue

                if entry.is_dir(follow_symlinks=False):
                    if (
                        discovery.recursive
                        and depth < discovery.max_directory_depth
                    ):
                        stack.append(
                            (Path(entry.path), depth + 1)
                        )

                    continue

                if not entry.is_file(follow_symlinks=False):
                    continue

                if is_dicom_candidate(name, settings):
                    yield Path(entry.path)

            except OSError:
                if on_directory_error:
                    on_directory_error()
                continue


# ============================================================
# 08 — PATIENT FOLDER DISCOVERY
# ============================================================

def discover_patient_directories(
    dataset_path: str | Path,
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
) -> tuple[Path, ...]:
    """
    Discover immediate child directories as patient folders.

    A dataset containing DICOMs directly is treated as one
    scan collection.

    Nested subfolders inside patients are scanned recursively.
    """

    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()

    dataset = validate_dataset_directory(
        dataset_path,
        active,
    )

    folders: list[Path] = []

    try:
        with os.scandir(dataset) as iterator:
            entries = sorted(
                list(iterator),
                key=lambda e: e.name.casefold(),
            )

        for entry in entries:
            if (
                active.dicom.discovery.ignore_hidden_files
                and entry.name.startswith(".")
            ):
                continue

            if entry.is_symlink():
                continue

            if entry.is_dir(follow_symlinks=False):
                folders.append(Path(entry.path))

    except OSError:
        raise ScannerPathError(
            "Unable to enumerate patient folders."
        ) from None

    if folders:
        return tuple(folders)

    # Support a single directly selected patient folder.
    return (dataset,)


# ============================================================
# 09 — SERIES GROUPING
# ============================================================

def group_patient_series(
    patient_handle: str,
    metadata_items: Sequence[DicomMetadata],
    *,
    settings: AppSettings,
) -> tuple[ScanSeries, ...]:
    """
    Group by Study UID + Series UID.

    Never automatically merge files with missing identifiers:
    they are isolated for later inspection.
    """

    groups: dict[
        tuple[str, ...],
        list[DicomMetadata],
    ] = defaultdict(list)

    for metadata in metadata_items:
        if metadata.study_uid and metadata.series_uid:
            key = (
                "identified",
                metadata.study_uid,
                metadata.series_uid,
            )
        else:
            key = (
                "unidentified",
                str(metadata.source_path),
            )

        groups[key].append(metadata)

    result: list[ScanSeries] = []

    for key in sorted(groups):
        instances = tuple(groups[key])

        study_uid = instances[0].study_uid
        series_uid = instances[0].series_uid

        handle = make_handle(
            "series",
            patient_handle,
            *key,
        )

        report = validate_series(instances, settings)

        result.append(
            ScanSeries(
                handle=handle,
                patient_handle=patient_handle,
                study_uid=study_uid,
                series_uid=series_uid,
                instances=instances,
                validation=report,
            )
        )

    return tuple(result)


# ============================================================
# 10 — MAIN DATASET SCANNER
# ============================================================

def scan_dataset(
    dataset_path: str | Path,
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
    *,
    cancel_event: threading.Event | None = None,
    progress_callback: ProgressCallback | None = None,
    max_candidate_files: int = DEFAULT_MAX_CANDIDATE_FILES,
) -> DatasetScanResult:
    """
    Scan the selected dataset and build a patient/series index.

    Read-only:
    - No file creation.
    - No file modification.
    - No renaming or deletion.
    - No pixel decoding.

    NOTE:
    This initial synchronous implementation retains metadata
    for all accepted files in memory. Very large datasets
    may require a streaming/indexed storage strategy.
    """

    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()

    if max_candidate_files < 1:
        raise ValueError("max_candidate_files must be positive.")

    dataset = validate_dataset_directory(
        dataset_path,
        active,
    )

    patient_directories = discover_patient_directories(
        dataset,
        active,
    )

    patients: list[ScanPatient] = []

    valid_files = 0
    invalid_files = 0
    unsupported_files = 0
    rejected_files = 0
    unreadable_directories = 0
    candidates = 0
    frame_total = 0

    def register_directory_error() -> None:
        nonlocal unreadable_directories
        unreadable_directories += 1

    for patient_directory in patient_directories:
        if cancel_event is not None and cancel_event.is_set():
            raise ScannerCancelled("Dataset scan cancelled.")

        # Recheck selected patient directory at the boundary.
        safe_patient_directory = validate_dataset_directory(
            patient_directory,
            active,
        )

        patient_handle = make_handle(
            "patient",
            str(dataset),
            str(safe_patient_directory),
        )

        metadata_items: list[DicomMetadata] = []

        for file_path in iter_candidate_files(
            safe_patient_directory,
            active,
            cancel_event=cancel_event,
            on_directory_error=register_directory_error,
        ):
            if candidates >= max_candidate_files:
                raise ScannerLimitExceeded(
                    "Dataset scan exceeded the file limit."
                )

            candidates += 1

            inspected = inspect_dicom_metadata(
                file_path,
                active,
                allow_non_part10=False,
            )

            if inspected.success and inspected.metadata is not None:
                metadata_items.append(inspected.metadata)
                valid_files += 1
                frame_total += inspected.metadata.pixels.frames

            elif inspected.status == "unsupported":
                unsupported_files += 1

            elif inspected.error_code == "PATH_REJECTED":
                rejected_files += 1

            else:
                invalid_files += 1

            if (
                progress_callback is not None
                and candidates % 100 == 0
            ):
                progress_callback(candidates, valid_files)

        series = group_patient_series(
            patient_handle,
            metadata_items,
            settings=active,
        )

        if series:
            patients.append(
                ScanPatient(
                    handle=patient_handle,
                    folder_name=safe_patient_directory.name,
                    folder_path=safe_patient_directory,
                    series=series,
                )
            )

    patients.sort(
        key=lambda patient: patient.folder_name.casefold()
    )

    series_total = sum(
        len(patient.series)
        for patient in patients
    )

    statistics = ScanStatistics(
        patient_count=len(patients),
        series_count=series_total,
        dicom_files=valid_files,
        invalid_files=invalid_files,
        unsupported_files=unsupported_files,
        rejected_files=rejected_files,
        unreadable_directories=unreadable_directories,
        inspected_candidates=candidates,
        total_frames=frame_total,
        cancelled=False,
    )

    if progress_callback is not None:
        progress_callback(candidates, valid_files)

    return DatasetScanResult(
        dataset_path=dataset,
        patients=tuple(patients),
        statistics=statistics,
    )


# ============================================================
# 11 — PATIENT LIST FOR AUTHORIZED API LAYER
# ============================================================

def build_patient_list(
    scan_result: DatasetScanResult,
) -> list[dict]:
    """
    Generate technical patient list records.

    Patient names and folder paths are intentionally omitted.
    Later authenticated APIs may provide permitted display
    labels according to the privacy policy.
    """

    return [
        {
            "handle": patient.handle,
            "series_count": len(patient.series),
            "file_count": patient.file_count,
            "volume_ready_series": sum(
                bool(
                    series.validation
                    and series.validation.volume_ready
                )
                for series in patient.series
            ),
        }
        for patient in scan_result.patients
    ]


# ============================================================
# 12 — SERIES LIST FOR AUTHORIZED API LAYER
# ============================================================

def build_series_list(
    patient: ScanPatient,
) -> list[dict]:
    """
    Return selected technical series information.

    Actual DICOM pixel endpoints must check authentication
    and verify that the requested series belongs to the
    authorized dataset index.
    """

    return [
        series.safe_summary()
        for series in patient.series
    ]


# ============================================================
# END OF DICOM SCANNER
# ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/backend/app/dicom/series_reader.py
```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — DICOM SERIES READER
==================================

Reads a validated conventional single-frame DICOM series
into a geometry-ordered NumPy volume.

Features:
- Strict read-only DICOM access
- Geometry-based slice ordering
- Series and instance identity checks
- Compressed DICOM decoding through pydicom plugins
- Memory/resource preflight checks
- Modality rescale slope/intercept support
- Cancellation and progress callbacks
- Safe error categories without patient information
- No dataset writes, moves, renames or deletions

Limitations:
- Conventional, single-frame monochrome images only
- Enhanced multiframe data requires a separate reader
- Unsupported tilted/sheared/nonuniform stacks are rejected
- Diagnostic correctness requires integration testing

All dependencies are installed later in Google Colab.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import threading
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Callable, Sequence

import numpy as np
import pydicom
from pydicom.errors import InvalidDicomError

from ..core.config import AppSettings, get_settings
from ..core.hardware import (
    HardwareSnapshot,
    VolumeLoadMode,
    detect_hardware,
    estimate_volume_memory,
    evaluate_volume_load,
)
from .metadata import (
    DicomMetadata,
    validate_dicom_path,
)
from .validator import (
    SeriesGeometrySummary,
    SeriesValidationReport,
    require_valid_series,
    validate_series,
)


# ============================================================
# 01 — CONSTANTS AND TYPES
# ============================================================

OUTPUT_DTYPE = np.dtype("float32")
OUTPUT_BYTES_PER_VOXEL = 4

ReaderProgress = Callable[[int, int], None]


class ReaderErrorCode(str, Enum):
    INVALID_SERIES = "SERIES_INVALID"
    INVALID_GEOMETRY = "GEOMETRY_INVALID"
    INVALID_DICOM = "DICOM_INVALID"
    DECODER_FAILED = "DECODER_ERROR"
    UNSUPPORTED = "DICOM_UNSUPPORTED"
    MEMORY_LIMIT = "OUT_OF_MEMORY"
    CANCELLED = "SCAN_CANCELLED"
    SOURCE_CHANGED = "SOURCE_CHANGED"


# ============================================================
# 02 — CONTROLLED EXCEPTIONS
# ============================================================

class SeriesReaderError(RuntimeError):
    """
    Safe reader exception.

    Raw file paths, patient identifiers and decoder
    exception messages must never be embedded.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        code: ReaderErrorCode,
    ) -> None:
        self.code = code
        super().__init__(code.value)


class SeriesValidationFailure(SeriesReaderError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(
            ReaderErrorCode.INVALID_SERIES
        )


class SeriesDecodeFailure(SeriesReaderError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(
            ReaderErrorCode.DECODER_FAILED
        )


class SeriesMemoryFailure(SeriesReaderError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(
            ReaderErrorCode.MEMORY_LIMIT
        )


class SeriesReadCancelled(SeriesReaderError):
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(
            ReaderErrorCode.CANCELLED
        )


# ============================================================
# 03 — IMMUTABLE RESULT MODELS
# ============================================================

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SeriesReadPlan:
    ordered_instances: tuple[DicomMetadata, ...] = field(
        repr=False
    )

    validation: SeriesValidationReport = field(
        repr=False
    )

    geometry: SeriesGeometrySummary = field(
        repr=False
    )

    shape_zyx: tuple[int, int, int]
    dtype: str
    estimated_raw_bytes: int
    estimated_peak_bytes: int
    estimated_peak_gib: float

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "shape_zyx": list(self.shape_zyx),
            "dtype": self.dtype,
            "slice_count": self.shape_zyx[0],
            "estimated_raw_bytes": self.estimated_raw_bytes,
            "estimated_peak_gib": self.estimated_peak_gib,
            "volume_ready": self.validation.volume_ready,
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SeriesVolume:
    """
    Loaded image data.

    Array shape is (Z, Y, X).
    Spatial orientation remains in DICOM LPS coordinates.

    The array is marked non-writable after construction.
    This protects in-memory pixels, not filesystem access.
    """

    voxels: np.ndarray = field(repr=False)

    geometry: SeriesGeometrySummary = field(repr=False)

    shape_zyx: tuple[int, int, int]
    dtype: str
    spacing_zyx_mm: tuple[float, float, float]

    origin_lps: tuple[float, float, float]
    direction_lps: tuple[float, ...]

    modality_values_applied: bool

    @property
    def size_bytes(self) -> int:
        return int(self.voxels.nbytes)

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "shape_zyx": list(self.shape_zyx),
            "dtype": self.dtype,
            "size_bytes": self.size_bytes,
            "spacing_zyx_mm": list(self.spacing_zyx_mm),
            "modality_values_applied": (
                self.modality_values_applied
            ),
        }


# ============================================================
# 04 — CANCELLATION
# ============================================================

def check_cancelled(
    cancel_event: threading.Event | None,
) -> None:
    if (
        cancel_event is not None
        and cancel_event.is_set()
    ):
        raise SeriesReadCancelled()


# ============================================================
# 05 — PREPARE GEOMETRY-ORDERED SERIES
# ============================================================

def prepare_series_read(
    instances: Sequence[DicomMetadata],
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
) -> SeriesReadPlan:
    """
    Validate and order instances without reading pixels.

    The validator determines slice order using physical
    positions projected onto the orientation-derived normal.
    """

    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()

    images = tuple(instances)

    if not images:
        raise SeriesValidationFailure()

    validation = validate_series(
        images,
        active,
    )

    if not validation.volume_ready:
        raise SeriesValidationFailure()

    try:
        geometry = require_valid_series(validation)
    except ValueError:
        raise SeriesValidationFailure() from None

    # Enhanced multiframe data is not supported here.
    if any(image.pixels.frames != 1 for image in images):
        raise SeriesReaderError(
            ReaderErrorCode.UNSUPPORTED
        )

    ordered = tuple(
        images[index]
        for index in geometry.sorted_indices
    )

    # Float32 output is used so modality rescale transforms
    # can be applied consistently, including per-slice values.
    depth = len(ordered)
    height = geometry.rows
    width = geometry.columns

    estimate = estimate_volume_memory(
        width=width,
        height=height,
        depth=depth,
        dtype="float32",
        working_memory_multiplier=3.0,
    )

    return SeriesReadPlan(
        ordered_instances=ordered,
        validation=validation,
        geometry=geometry,
        shape_zyx=(depth, height, width),
        dtype="float32",
        estimated_raw_bytes=estimate.raw_bytes,
        estimated_peak_bytes=estimate.estimated_peak_bytes,
        estimated_peak_gib=estimate.estimated_peak_gib,
    )


# ============================================================
# 06 — MEMORY PRECHECK
# ============================================================

def check_volume_memory(
    plan: SeriesReadPlan,
    snapshot: HardwareSnapshot,
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
) -> None:
    """
    Block loading before allocation if the full volume
    exceeds configured size or current RAM limits.

    Preview-only mode is reported to the caller.
    It is not silently substituted for full resolution.
    """

    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()

    depth, height, width = plan.shape_zyx

    estimate = estimate_volume_memory(
        width=width,
        height=height,
        depth=depth,
        dtype="float32",
        working_memory_multiplier=3.0,
    )

    decision = evaluate_volume_load(
        estimate,
        snapshot,
        active,
    )

    if decision.mode != VolumeLoadMode.FULL:
        raise SeriesMemoryFailure()


# ============================================================
# 07 — SOURCE INTEGRITY
# ============================================================

def verify_instance_identity(
    dataset: pydicom.dataset.Dataset,
    metadata: DicomMetadata,
) -> None:
    """
    Verify selected DICOM identity and dimensions.

    This detects obvious changes between metadata indexing
    and pixel decoding. It is not a cryptographic file
    integrity verification.
    """

    comparisons = (
        (
            "StudyInstanceUID",
            metadata.study_uid,
        ),
        (
            "SeriesInstanceUID",
            metadata.series_uid,
        ),
        (
            "SOPInstanceUID",
            metadata.sop_instance_uid,
        ),
    )

    for attribute, expected in comparisons:
        actual = getattr(dataset, attribute, None)

        if expected is not None:
            if actual is None or str(actual) != expected:
                raise SeriesReaderError(
                    ReaderErrorCode.SOURCE_CHANGED
                )

    geometry = metadata.geometry

    if (
        int(getattr(dataset, "Rows", -1))
        != geometry.rows
        or int(getattr(dataset, "Columns", -1))
        != geometry.columns
    ):
        raise SeriesReaderError(
            ReaderErrorCode.SOURCE_CHANGED
        )

    if int(getattr(dataset, "NumberOfFrames", 1)) != 1:
        raise SeriesReaderError(
            ReaderErrorCode.UNSUPPORTED
        )


# ============================================================
# 08 — SPATIAL HEADER RECHECK
# ============================================================

def verify_instance_geometry(
    dataset: pydicom.dataset.Dataset,
    metadata: DicomMetadata,
) -> None:
    """
    Compare spatial tags against the indexed metadata.

    The dedicated geometry module will provide more advanced
    physical-coordinate transformations later.
    """

    expected = metadata.geometry

    fields = (
        (
            "ImagePositionPatient",
            expected.image_position,
        ),
        (
            "ImageOrientationPatient",
            expected.image_orientation,
        ),
        (
            "PixelSpacing",
            expected.pixel_spacing,
        ),
    )

    for attribute, reference in fields:
        actual = getattr(dataset, attribute, None)

        if reference is None or actual is None:
            raise SeriesReaderError(
                ReaderErrorCode.INVALID_GEOMETRY
            )

        try:
            values = tuple(float(value) for value in actual)
        except (TypeError, ValueError, OverflowError):
            raise SeriesReaderError(
                ReaderErrorCode.INVALID_GEOMETRY
            ) from None

        if (
            len(values) != len(reference)
            or not all(
                math.isfinite(a)
                and math.isclose(
                    a,
                    b,
                    rel_tol=1e-5,
                    abs_tol=1e-4,
                )
                for a, b in zip(values, reference)
            )
        ):
            raise SeriesReaderError(
                ReaderErrorCode.SOURCE_CHANGED
            )


# ============================================================
# 09 — DECODE ONE SLICE
# ============================================================

def decode_single_slice(
    metadata: DicomMetadata,
    settings: AppSettings,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Read and decode one image in read-only mode.

    The DICOM dataset is never saved or modified.
    """

    safe_path = validate_dicom_path(
        metadata.source_path,
        settings.storage.dataset.root_path,
    )

    try:
        with safe_path.open("rb") as stream:
            dataset = pydicom.dcmread(
                stream,
                force=False,
            )

        verify_instance_identity(
            dataset,
            metadata,
        )

        verify_instance_geometry(
            dataset,
            metadata,
        )

        # Confirm pixel-related attributes have not changed.
        pixel = metadata.pixels

        header_checks = (
            ("BitsAllocated", pixel.bits_allocated),
            ("BitsStored", pixel.bits_stored),
            ("PixelRepresentation", pixel.pixel_representation),
            ("SamplesPerPixel", pixel.samples_per_pixel),
        )

        for attribute, expected in header_checks:
            if expected is None:
                raise SeriesReaderError(
                    ReaderErrorCode.INVALID_DICOM
                )

            if int(getattr(dataset, attribute, -1)) != expected:
                raise SeriesReaderError(
                    ReaderErrorCode.SOURCE_CHANGED
                )

        photometric = str(
            getattr(
                dataset,
                "PhotometricInterpretation",
                "",
            )
        )

        if photometric != pixel.photometric_interpretation:
            raise SeriesReaderError(
                ReaderErrorCode.SOURCE_CHANGED
            )

        if photometric not in {
            "MONOCHROME1",
            "MONOCHROME2",
        }:
            raise SeriesReaderError(
                ReaderErrorCode.UNSUPPORTED
            )

        # pydicom delegates compressed pixel decoding to
        # installed compatible handlers/plugins.
        decoded = dataset.pixel_array

        if (
            decoded.ndim != 2
            or decoded.shape != (
                metadata.geometry.rows,
                metadata.geometry.columns,
            )
        ):
            raise SeriesReaderError(
                ReaderErrorCode.INVALID_DICOM
            )

        if not np.issubdtype(
            decoded.dtype,
            np.number,
        ):
            raise SeriesReaderError(
                ReaderErrorCode.UNSUPPORTED
            )

        slope = float(
            getattr(
                dataset,
                "RescaleSlope",
                1.0,
            )
        )

        intercept = float(
            getattr(
                dataset,
                "RescaleIntercept",
                0.0,
            )
        )

        if not (
            math.isfinite(slope)
            and math.isfinite(intercept)
        ):
            raise SeriesReaderError(
                ReaderErrorCode.INVALID_DICOM
            )

        # Convert to physical/modality-scaled values.
        # These are not automatically guaranteed to be HU
        # for all CBCT scanners.
        image = np.asarray(
            decoded,
            dtype=OUTPUT_DTYPE,
        )

        image = image * slope + intercept

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(image)):
            raise SeriesReaderError(
                ReaderErrorCode.INVALID_DICOM
            )

        return image

    except SeriesReaderError:
        raise

    except MemoryError:
        raise SeriesMemoryFailure() from None

    except (
        InvalidDicomError,
        OSError,
        ValueError,
        TypeError,
        RuntimeError,
        AttributeError,
        KeyError,
    ):
        raise SeriesDecodeFailure() from None


# ============================================================
# 10 — SPATIAL DIRECTION MATRIX
# ============================================================

def build_direction_lps(
    geometry: SeriesGeometrySummary,
) -> tuple[float, ...]:
    """
    Build a flattened 3x3 direction matrix in row-major form.

    Columns represent increasing X, Y, Z array indices
    in DICOM LPS physical coordinates.
    """

    orientation = geometry.orientation

    row_direction = np.asarray(
        orientation[:3],
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    column_direction = np.asarray(
        orientation[3:],
        dtype=np.float64,
    )

    normal = np.cross(
        row_direction,
        column_direction,
    )

    matrix = np.column_stack((
        row_direction,
        column_direction,
        normal,
    ))

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(matrix)):
        raise SeriesReaderError(
            ReaderErrorCode.INVALID_GEOMETRY
        )

    return tuple(
        float(value)
        for value in matrix.reshape(-1)
    )


# ============================================================
# 11 — LOAD COMPLETE SERIES
# ============================================================

def load_series_volume(
    instances: Sequence[DicomMetadata],
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
    *,
    hardware_snapshot: HardwareSnapshot | None = None,
    cancel_event: threading.Event | None = None,
    progress_callback: ReaderProgress | None = None,
) -> SeriesVolume:
    """
    Load one validated conventional DICOM series.

    Processing sequence:
      1. Validate metadata and physical geometry.
      2. Calculate correct spatial slice ordering.
      3. Evaluate memory budget.
      4. Allocate output volume.
      5. Decode slices in physical order.
      6. Apply modality rescale values.
      7. Attach LPS spatial geometry.
