```
    Source files are opened only in read-binary mode.

    No filesystem writes occur.
    """

    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()

    check_cancelled(cancel_event)

    plan = prepare_series_read(
        instances,
        active,
    )

    snapshot = (
        hardware_snapshot
        if hardware_snapshot is not None
        else detect_hardware(active)
    )

    check_volume_memory(
        plan,
        snapshot,
        active,
    )

    shape = plan.shape_zyx

    try:
        volume = np.empty(
            shape,
            dtype=OUTPUT_DTYPE,
        )
    except (MemoryError, ValueError):
        raise SeriesMemoryFailure() from None

    try:
        for index, metadata in enumerate(
            plan.ordered_instances
        ):
            check_cancelled(cancel_event)

            image = decode_single_slice(
                metadata,
                active,
            )

            volume[index, :, :] = image

            del image

            if progress_callback is not None:
                progress_callback(
                    index + 1,
                    shape[0],
                )

        check_cancelled(cancel_event)

        geometry = plan.geometry

        direction = build_direction_lps(
            geometry
        )

        spacing = (
            float(geometry.slice_spacing_mm),
            float(geometry.pixel_spacing[0]),
            float(geometry.pixel_spacing[1]),
        )

        if any(
            not math.isfinite(value) or value <= 0
            for value in spacing
        ):
            raise SeriesReaderError(
                ReaderErrorCode.INVALID_GEOMETRY
            )

        volume.setflags(write=False)

        return SeriesVolume(
            voxels=volume,
            geometry=geometry,
            shape_zyx=shape,
            dtype="float32",
            spacing_zyx_mm=spacing,
            origin_lps=geometry.first_position,
            direction_lps=direction,
            modality_values_applied=True,
        )

    except BaseException:
        # Release local output buffer on any failure.
        del volume
        raise


# ============================================================
# 12 — SAFE READER INFORMATION
# ============================================================

def get_series_reader_capabilities() -> dict:
    """
    Non-sensitive description of current reader support.
    """

    return {
        "reader": "conventional_single_frame",
        "supports_ct": True,
        "supports_cbct_when_dicom_compatible": True,
        "supports_multiframe": False,
        "supports_monochrome": True,
        "supports_color": False,
        "supports_compressed_dicom": (
            "when_decoder_plugin_available"
        ),
        "output_dtype": "float32",
        "output_axis_order": "zyx",
        "coordinate_system": "LPS",
        "supports_cancel": True,
        "original_files_read_only": True,
    }


# ============================================================
# END OF DICOM SERIES READER
# ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/backend/app/dicom/validator.py
```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — DICOM VALIDATION ENGINE
======================================

Responsibilities:
- Validate DICOM image metadata.
- Validate physical image geometry.
- Verify consistency within a scan series.
- Detect duplicate and irregular slice positions.
- Calculate geometry-based slice ordering.
- Estimate memory requirements.
- Reject unsafe 3D reconstruction candidates.
- Produce privacy-safe validation reports.

Important:
- No DICOM files are opened by this module.
- No original dataset files are modified.
- Validation does not decode pixel data.
- Enhanced multiframe geometry requires a separate reader.
- Validation PASS does not certify diagnostic correctness.

Python 3.10+
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
from typing import Iterable, Sequence

from .metadata import DicomMetadata, DicomGeometry
from ..core.config import AppSettings, get_settings


# ============================================================
# 01 — CONSTANTS
# ============================================================

ORIENTATION_TOLERANCE = 1e-4
SPACING_RELATIVE_TOLERANCE = 1e-3
MIN_POSITION_TOLERANCE_MM = 1e-4

SUPPORTED_SCALAR_PHOTOMETRIC = frozenset({
    "MONOCHROME1",
    "MONOCHROME2",
})

SUPPORTED_PIXEL_BITS = frozenset({
    8,
    16,
    32,
})


# ============================================================
# 02 — VALIDATION ENUMS
# ============================================================

class ValidationSeverity(str, Enum):
    INFO = "info"
    WARNING = "warning"
    ERROR = "error"


class ValidationCode(str, Enum):
    EMPTY_SERIES = "EMPTY_SERIES"
    INSUFFICIENT_SLICES = "INSUFFICIENT_SLICES"

    INVALID_DIMENSIONS = "INVALID_DIMENSIONS"
    MIXED_DIMENSIONS = "MIXED_DIMENSIONS"

    INVALID_PIXEL_FORMAT = "INVALID_PIXEL_FORMAT"
    MIXED_PIXEL_FORMAT = "MIXED_PIXEL_FORMAT"

    MISSING_SERIES_UID = "MISSING_SERIES_UID"
    MIXED_SERIES_UID = "MIXED_SERIES_UID"
    MIXED_STUDY_UID = "MIXED_STUDY_UID"
    MIXED_FRAME_OF_REFERENCE = "MIXED_FRAME_OF_REFERENCE"

    MISSING_GEOMETRY = "MISSING_GEOMETRY"
    INVALID_ORIENTATION = "INVALID_ORIENTATION"
    MIXED_ORIENTATION = "MIXED_ORIENTATION"
    INVALID_PIXEL_SPACING = "INVALID_PIXEL_SPACING"
    MIXED_PIXEL_SPACING = "MIXED_PIXEL_SPACING"

    DUPLICATE_SLICE_POSITION = "DUPLICATE_SLICE_POSITION"
    IRREGULAR_SLICE_SPACING = "IRREGULAR_SLICE_SPACING"
    INPLANE_POSITION_DRIFT = "INPLANE_POSITION_DRIFT"

    MULTIFRAME_GEOMETRY_REQUIRED = (
        "MULTIFRAME_GEOMETRY_REQUIRED"
    )

    VOLUME_LIMIT_EXCEEDED = "VOLUME_LIMIT_EXCEEDED"


# ============================================================
# 03 — IMMUTABLE REPORT MODELS
# ============================================================

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ValidationIssue:
    code: ValidationCode
    severity: ValidationSeverity
    message: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SeriesGeometrySummary:
    rows: int
    columns: int
    slice_count: int
    pixel_spacing: tuple[float, float]
    slice_spacing_mm: float
    orientation: tuple[float, ...]
    first_position: tuple[float, float, float]

    # Sorted source indices in the original input sequence.
    sorted_indices: tuple[int, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SeriesValidationReport:
    valid: bool
    volume_ready: bool
    file_count: int
    frame_count: int
    issues: tuple[ValidationIssue, ...]
    geometry: SeriesGeometrySummary | None

    @property
    def error_count(self) -> int:
        return sum(
            issue.severity == ValidationSeverity.ERROR
            for issue in self.issues
        )

    @property
    def warning_count(self) -> int:
        return sum(
            issue.severity == ValidationSeverity.WARNING
            for issue in self.issues
        )

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict:
        """
        Excludes filenames, paths, UIDs and patient identifiers.
        """

        return {
            "valid": self.valid,
            "volume_ready": self.volume_ready,
            "file_count": self.file_count,
            "frame_count": self.frame_count,
            "error_count": self.error_count,
            "warning_count": self.warning_count,
            "issues": [
                {
                    "code": issue.code.value,
                    "severity": issue.severity.value,
                    "message": issue.message,
                }
                for issue in self.issues
            ],
        }


# ============================================================
# 04 — VECTOR MATH HELPERS
# ============================================================

def dot(
    a: Sequence[float],
    b: Sequence[float],
) -> float:
    return sum(x * y for x, y in zip(a, b))


def subtract(
    a: Sequence[float],
    b: Sequence[float],
) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
    return (
        a[0] - b[0],
        a[1] - b[1],
        a[2] - b[2],
    )


def cross(
    a: Sequence[float],
    b: Sequence[float],
) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
    return (
        a[1] * b[2] - a[2] * b[1],
        a[2] * b[0] - a[0] * b[2],
        a[0] * b[1] - a[1] * b[0],
    )


def norm(values: Sequence[float]) -> float:
    return math.sqrt(dot(values, values))


def normalize(
    values: Sequence[float],
) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
    magnitude = norm(values)

    if not math.isfinite(magnitude) or magnitude <= 0:
        raise ValueError("Invalid direction vector.")

    return tuple(value / magnitude for value in values)


def are_close(
    a: float,
    b: float,
    *,
    absolute: float,
    relative: float = 0.0,
) -> bool:
    return math.isclose(
        a,
        b,
        abs_tol=absolute,
        rel_tol=relative,
    )


# ============================================================
# 05 — ISSUE HELPER
# ============================================================

def make_issue(
    code: ValidationCode,
    severity: ValidationSeverity = ValidationSeverity.ERROR,
) -> ValidationIssue:
    """
    Standardized issue descriptions.

    No raw metadata values are inserted into messages.
    """

    descriptions = {
        ValidationCode.EMPTY_SERIES:
            "No DICOM image instances were provided.",

        ValidationCode.INSUFFICIENT_SLICES:
            "Not enough slices are available for 3D reconstruction.",

        ValidationCode.INVALID_DIMENSIONS:
            "An image has invalid dimensions.",

        ValidationCode.MIXED_DIMENSIONS:
            "The scan contains inconsistent image dimensions.",

        ValidationCode.INVALID_PIXEL_FORMAT:
            "An image has unsupported pixel metadata.",

        ValidationCode.MIXED_PIXEL_FORMAT:
            "The scan contains inconsistent pixel formats.",

        ValidationCode.MISSING_SERIES_UID:
            "A required series identifier is missing.",

        ValidationCode.MIXED_SERIES_UID:
            "Images from different series were mixed.",

        ValidationCode.MIXED_STUDY_UID:
            "Images from different studies were mixed.",

        ValidationCode.MIXED_FRAME_OF_REFERENCE:
            "Images use different frames of reference.",

        ValidationCode.MISSING_GEOMETRY:
            "Required spatial metadata is missing.",

        ValidationCode.INVALID_ORIENTATION:
            "Image orientation vectors are invalid.",

        ValidationCode.MIXED_ORIENTATION:
            "The scan contains inconsistent orientations.",

        ValidationCode.INVALID_PIXEL_SPACING:
            "Pixel spacing is missing or invalid.",

        ValidationCode.MIXED_PIXEL_SPACING:
            "Pixel spacing differs across images.",

        ValidationCode.DUPLICATE_SLICE_POSITION:
            "Multiple slices have the same spatial position.",

        ValidationCode.IRREGULAR_SLICE_SPACING:
            "Slice spacing is not uniform.",

        ValidationCode.INPLANE_POSITION_DRIFT:
            "Slice origins shift unexpectedly within the image plane.",

        ValidationCode.MULTIFRAME_GEOMETRY_REQUIRED:
            "Multiframe geometry requires specialized validation.",

        ValidationCode.VOLUME_LIMIT_EXCEEDED:
            "The reconstructed volume exceeds configured limits.",
    }

    return ValidationIssue(
        code=code,
        severity=severity,
        message=descriptions[code],
    )


# ============================================================
# 06 — INDIVIDUAL IMAGE VALIDATION
# ============================================================

def validate_image_metadata(
    metadata: DicomMetadata,
) -> tuple[ValidationIssue, ...]:
    issues: list[ValidationIssue] = []

    geometry = metadata.geometry
    pixels = metadata.pixels

    if (
        geometry.rows is None
        or geometry.columns is None
        or geometry.rows <= 0
        or geometry.columns <= 0
    ):
        issues.append(
            make_issue(ValidationCode.INVALID_DIMENSIONS)
        )

    if (
        pixels.samples_per_pixel != 1
        or pixels.photometric_interpretation
        not in SUPPORTED_SCALAR_PHOTOMETRIC
        or pixels.bits_allocated not in SUPPORTED_PIXEL_BITS
        or pixels.bits_stored is None
        or pixels.bits_stored <= 0
        or (
            pixels.bits_allocated is not None
            and pixels.bits_stored > pixels.bits_allocated
        )
        or pixels.pixel_representation not in (0, 1)
    ):
        issues.append(
            make_issue(ValidationCode.INVALID_PIXEL_FORMAT)
        )

    if metadata.series_uid is None:
        issues.append(
            make_issue(ValidationCode.MISSING_SERIES_UID)
        )

    spacing = geometry.pixel_spacing

    if (
        spacing is None
        or len(spacing) != 2
        or any(
            not math.isfinite(value) or value <= 0
            for value in spacing
        )
    ):
        issues.append(
            make_issue(ValidationCode.INVALID_PIXEL_SPACING)
        )

    if (
        geometry.image_position is None
        or geometry.image_orientation is None
    ):
        issues.append(
            make_issue(ValidationCode.MISSING_GEOMETRY)
        )
    elif not valid_orientation(
        geometry.image_orientation
    ):
        issues.append(
            make_issue(ValidationCode.INVALID_ORIENTATION)
        )

    if metadata.pixels.frames > 1:
        issues.append(
            make_issue(
                ValidationCode.MULTIFRAME_GEOMETRY_REQUIRED
            )
        )

    return tuple(issues)


# ============================================================
# 07 — ORIENTATION VALIDATION
# ============================================================

def valid_orientation(
    orientation: Sequence[float],
) -> bool:
    if len(orientation) != 6:
        return False

    if not all(math.isfinite(v) for v in orientation):
        return False

    row = orientation[:3]
    column = orientation[3:]

    return (
        are_close(
            norm(row),
            1.0,
            absolute=ORIENTATION_TOLERANCE,
        )
        and are_close(
            norm(column),
            1.0,
            absolute=ORIENTATION_TOLERANCE,
        )
        and are_close(
            dot(row, column),
            0.0,
            absolute=ORIENTATION_TOLERANCE,
        )
    )


def slice_normal(
    orientation: Sequence[float],
) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
    if not valid_orientation(orientation):
        raise ValueError("Invalid image orientation.")

    return normalize(
        cross(
            orientation[:3],
            orientation[3:],
        )
    )


# ============================================================
# 08 — SERIES VALIDATION
# ============================================================

def validate_series(
    instances: Iterable[DicomMetadata],
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
) -> SeriesValidationReport:
    """
    Validate a conventional single-frame DICOM series.

    This routine never changes the input collection and does
    not load pixel data.
    """

    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()

    images = tuple(instances)
    issues: list[ValidationIssue] = []

    def add(
        code: ValidationCode,
        severity: ValidationSeverity = ValidationSeverity.ERROR,
    ) -> None:
        if not any(issue.code == code for issue in issues):
            issues.append(make_issue(code, severity))

    if not images:
        add(ValidationCode.EMPTY_SERIES)

        return SeriesValidationReport(
            valid=False,
            volume_ready=False,
            file_count=0,
            frame_count=0,
            issues=tuple(issues),
            geometry=None,
        )

    frame_count = sum(max(1, m.pixels.frames) for m in images)

    for image in images:
        for issue in validate_image_metadata(image):
            add(issue.code, issue.severity)

    if frame_count < active.dicom.series.minimum_slices_for_volume:
        add(ValidationCode.INSUFFICIENT_SLICES)

    study_uids = {m.study_uid for m in images}
    series_uids = {m.series_uid for m in images}
    frame_uids = {m.frame_of_reference_uid for m in images}

    if len(study_uids) > 1:
        add(ValidationCode.MIXED_STUDY_UID)

    if len(series_uids) > 1:
        add(ValidationCode.MIXED_SERIES_UID)

    if len(frame_uids) > 1:
        add(ValidationCode.MIXED_FRAME_OF_REFERENCE)

    rows = {m.geometry.rows for m in images}
    columns = {m.geometry.columns for m in images}

    if len(rows) > 1 or len(columns) > 1:
        add(ValidationCode.MIXED_DIMENSIONS)

    pixel_formats = {
        (
            m.pixels.samples_per_pixel,
            m.pixels.photometric_interpretation,
            m.pixels.bits_allocated,
            m.pixels.bits_stored,
            m.pixels.pixel_representation,
        )
        for m in images
    }

    if len(pixel_formats) > 1:
        add(ValidationCode.MIXED_PIXEL_FORMAT)

    if any(m.pixels.frames > 1 for m in images):
        return SeriesValidationReport(
            valid=False,
            volume_ready=False,
            file_count=len(images),
            frame_count=frame_count,
            issues=tuple(issues),
            geometry=None,
        )

    # Spatial validation requires every slice to have
    # consistent orientation, spacing and position.

    if any(
        not m.geometry.geometry_complete
        or not valid_orientation(
            m.geometry.image_orientation or ()
        )
        for m in images
    ):
        add(ValidationCode.MISSING_GEOMETRY)

        return SeriesValidationReport(
            valid=False,
            volume_ready=False,
            file_count=len(images),
            frame_count=frame_count,
            issues=tuple(issues),
            geometry=None,
        )

    reference = images[0].geometry

    reference_orientation = reference.image_orientation
    reference_spacing = reference.pixel_spacing

    assert reference_orientation is not None
    assert reference_spacing is not None

    for image in images[1:]:
        geometry = image.geometry

        if any(
            not are_close(
                a,
                b,
                absolute=ORIENTATION_TOLERANCE,
            )
            for a, b in zip(
                reference_orientation,
                geometry.image_orientation or (),
            )
        ):
            add(ValidationCode.MIXED_ORIENTATION)

        if any(
            not are_close(
                a,
                b,
                absolute=1e-5,
                relative=SPACING_RELATIVE_TOLERANCE,
            )
            for a, b in zip(
                reference_spacing,
                geometry.pixel_spacing or (),
            )
        ):
            add(ValidationCode.MIXED_PIXEL_SPACING)

    if any(
        issue.severity == ValidationSeverity.ERROR
        for issue in issues
    ):
        return SeriesValidationReport(
            valid=False,
            volume_ready=False,
            file_count=len(images),
            frame_count=frame_count,
            issues=tuple(issues),
            geometry=None,
        )

    normal = slice_normal(reference_orientation)

    # Project image origins onto the reference slice normal.
    projections = []

    for index, image in enumerate(images):
        position = image.geometry.image_position

        assert position is not None

        projections.append(
            (dot(position, normal), index, position)
        )

    projections.sort(key=lambda item: item[0])

    # Validate consecutive slice separations.
    differences = [
        projections[i + 1][0] - projections[i][0]
        for i in range(len(projections) - 1)
    ]

    tolerance = max(
        MIN_POSITION_TOLERANCE_MM,
        active.dicom.series.slice_spacing_tolerance_mm,
    )

    if any(delta <= tolerance for delta in differences):
        add(ValidationCode.DUPLICATE_SLICE_POSITION)

    positive_deltas = [
        delta for delta in differences
        if delta > tolerance
    ]

    slice_spacing = (
        sorted(positive_deltas)[len(positive_deltas) // 2]
        if positive_deltas
        else 0.0
    )

    if positive_deltas and any(
        abs(delta - slice_spacing) > tolerance
        for delta in positive_deltas
    ):
        add(ValidationCode.IRREGULAR_SLICE_SPACING)

    # Detect origins shifting within the image plane.
    # Oblique but parallel image stacks remain supported.
    first_position = projections[0][2]

    for _, _, position in projections[1:]:
        displacement = subtract(position, first_position)
        normal_component = dot(displacement, normal)

        in_plane = tuple(
            displacement[i] - normal_component * normal[i]
            for i in range(3)
        )

        if norm(in_plane) > tolerance:
            add(ValidationCode.INPLANE_POSITION_DRIFT)
            break

    # Estimated raw volume memory.
    bits = images[0].pixels.bits_allocated or 16
    bytes_per_voxel = max(1, math.ceil(bits / 8))

    voxel_count = (
        (reference.rows or 0)
        * (reference.columns or 0)
        * len(images)
    )

    raw_bytes = voxel_count * bytes_per_voxel

    if (
        voxel_count > active.dicom.volume.max_voxels
        or raw_bytes >
        active.dicom.volume.max_uncompressed_volume_mb
        * 1024 * 1024
    ):
        add(ValidationCode.VOLUME_LIMIT_EXCEEDED)

    valid = not any(
        issue.severity == ValidationSeverity.ERROR
        for issue in issues
    )

    geometry_summary = None

    if valid:
        geometry_summary = SeriesGeometrySummary(
            rows=reference.rows,
            columns=reference.columns,
            slice_count=len(images),
            pixel_spacing=reference_spacing,
            slice_spacing_mm=slice_spacing,
            orientation=reference_orientation,
            first_position=first_position,
            sorted_indices=tuple(
                item[1] for item in projections
            ),
        )

    return SeriesValidationReport(
        valid=valid,
        volume_ready=valid,
        file_count=len(images),
        frame_count=frame_count,
        issues=tuple(issues),
        geometry=geometry_summary,
    )


# ============================================================
# 09 — RECONSTRUCTION GATE
# ============================================================

def require_valid_series(
    report: SeriesValidationReport,
) -> SeriesGeometrySummary:
    """
    Prevent invalid series from reaching volume reconstruction.
    """

    if not report.volume_ready or report.geometry is None:
        raise ValueError(
            "DICOM series failed spatial validation."
        )

    return report.geometry


# ============================================================
# END OF VALIDATION ENGINE
# ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/backend/app/dicom/volume_builder.py
```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — 3D VOLUME BUILDER
================================

Build and prepare validated medical volumes for rendering.

Features:
- Existing DICOM series reader integration
- Validated LPS voxel geometry
- Full-resolution volume preservation
- Memory-bounded preview generation
- Physical spacing preservation during downsampling
- Progressive preview levels
- Cancellation and progress callbacks
- Authenticated API-ready technical descriptors
- Zero dataset writes

IMPORTANT:
Preview generation here requires a successfully loaded
full-resolution volume. A true low-memory preview decoder
will be implemented separately.

Preview downsampling uses voxel subsampling. It preserves
sample positions but does not provide anti-alias filtering;
therefore previews are for navigation, not diagnosis.

No files are created, edited, deleted, moved or renamed.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import threading
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Callable, Sequence

import numpy as np

from ..core.config import AppSettings, get_settings
from ..core.hardware import (
    HardwareSnapshot,
    detect_hardware,
)
from .geometry import (
    VolumeGeometry,
    build_geometry_report,
    geometry_from_series_volume,
)
from .metadata import DicomMetadata
from .series_reader import (
    SeriesVolume,
    load_series_volume,
)


# ============================================================
# 01 — TYPES AND CONSTANTS
# ============================================================

MIB = 1024 ** 2
GIB = 1024 ** 3

ProgressCallback = Callable[[int, int], None]

DEFAULT_PREVIEW_DIMENSIONS = (
    128,
    256,
    384,
)


# ============================================================
# 02 — CONTROLLED EXCEPTIONS
# ============================================================

class VolumeBuilderError(RuntimeError):
    """Base safe volume-building exception."""

    public_code = "PROCESSING_FAILED"

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(self.public_code)


class VolumeBuilderMemoryError(VolumeBuilderError):
    public_code = "OUT_OF_MEMORY"


class VolumeBuilderGeometryError(VolumeBuilderError):
    public_code = "GEOMETRY_INVALID"


class VolumeBuilderCancelled(VolumeBuilderError):
    public_code = "SCAN_CANCELLED"


# ============================================================
# 03 — DATA MODELS
# ============================================================

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PreparedVolume:
    """
    One prepared 3D volume.

    voxels shape: Z, Y, X
    geometry spacing: X, Y, Z

    Full-resolution voxels may reference the existing
    SeriesVolume array without making another copy.
    """

    voxels: np.ndarray = field(repr=False)
    geometry: VolumeGeometry

    level: int
    is_preview: bool
    source_shape_zyx: tuple[int, int, int]

    stride_zyx: tuple[int, int, int]

    @property
    def shape_zyx(self) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
        return tuple(int(x) for x in self.voxels.shape)

    @property
    def byte_size(self) -> int:
        return int(self.voxels.nbytes)

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "level": self.level,
            "is_preview": self.is_preview,
            "shape_zyx": list(self.shape_zyx),
            "source_shape_zyx": list(self.source_shape_zyx),
            "stride_zyx": list(self.stride_zyx),
            "dtype": str(self.voxels.dtype),
            "byte_size": self.byte_size,
            "geometry": self.geometry.safe_summary(),
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VolumePackage:
    """
    Full volume plus available preview levels.

    No patient identifiers or source DICOM paths are
    included in frontend descriptors.
    """

    full: PreparedVolume = field(repr=False)

    previews: tuple[PreparedVolume, ...] = field(
        default_factory=tuple,
        repr=False,
    )

    def all_levels(self) -> tuple[PreparedVolume, ...]:
        return self.previews + (self.full,)

    def get_level(self, level: int) -> PreparedVolume | None:
        for item in self.all_levels():
            if item.level == level:
                return item
        return None

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "full": self.full.safe_summary(),
            "previews": [
                preview.safe_summary()
                for preview in self.previews
            ],
            "available_levels": [
                volume.level
                for volume in self.all_levels()
            ],
        }


# ============================================================
# 04 — CANCELLATION
# ============================================================

def check_cancelled(
    cancel_event: threading.Event | None,
) -> None:
    if cancel_event is not None and cancel_event.is_set():
        raise VolumeBuilderCancelled()


# ============================================================
# 05 — VALIDATE EXISTING SERIES VOLUME
# ============================================================

def prepare_full_volume(
    volume: SeriesVolume,
) -> PreparedVolume:
    """
    Wrap an already-loaded DICOM volume without copying pixels.

    Checks dtype, dimensions, finite values and geometry.
    """

    voxels = volume.voxels

    if not isinstance(voxels, np.ndarray):
        raise VolumeBuilderGeometryError()

    if voxels.ndim != 3 or any(
        dimension <= 0 for dimension in voxels.shape
    ):
        raise VolumeBuilderGeometryError()

    if tuple(voxels.shape) != tuple(volume.shape_zyx):
        raise VolumeBuilderGeometryError()

    if voxels.dtype != np.dtype("float32"):
        raise VolumeBuilderGeometryError()

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(voxels)):
        raise VolumeBuilderGeometryError()

    try:
        geometry = geometry_from_series_volume(volume)
    except (ValueError, TypeError):
        raise VolumeBuilderGeometryError() from None

    if geometry.shape_zyx != tuple(voxels.shape):
        raise VolumeBuilderGeometryError()

    voxels.setflags(write=False)

    return PreparedVolume(
        voxels=voxels,
        geometry=geometry,
        level=0,
        is_preview=False,
        source_shape_zyx=tuple(voxels.shape),
        stride_zyx=(1, 1, 1),
    )


# ============================================================
# 06 — PREVIEW STRIDE CALCULATION
# ============================================================

def calculate_preview_stride(
    shape_zyx: Sequence[int],
    max_dimension: int,
) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    """
    Determine integer strides that keep every output axis
    within max_dimension.

    Integer-stride sampling preserves exact original
    physical voxel positions.
    """

    if (
        len(shape_zyx) != 3
        or any(
            not isinstance(size, int)
            or isinstance(size, bool)
            or size < 1
            for size in shape_zyx
        )
        or not isinstance(max_dimension, int)
        or isinstance(max_dimension, bool)
        or max_dimension < 1
    ):
        raise ValueError("Invalid preview dimensions.")

    return tuple(
        max(1, math.ceil(size / max_dimension))
        for size in shape_zyx
    )


def preview_shape(
    source_shape: Sequence[int],
    stride: Sequence[int],
) -> tuple[int, int, int]:
    if (
        len(source_shape) != 3
        or len(stride) != 3
        or any(
            not isinstance(s, int)
            or isinstance(s, bool)
            or s < 1
            for s in (*source_shape, *stride)
        )
    ):
        raise ValueError("Invalid preview shape or stride.")

    return tuple(
        (size - 1) // step + 1
        for size, step in zip(source_shape, stride)
    )


# ============================================================
# 07 — PREVIEW MEMORY CHECK
# ============================================================

def enforce_preview_memory_limit(
    shape_zyx: Sequence[int],
    dtype: np.dtype,
    settings: AppSettings,
) -> None:
    """
    Reject preview allocations exceeding available memory
    or configured limits.

    Not a guarantee of actual process peak memory usage.
    """

    voxels = math.prod(shape_zyx)
    allocation_bytes = voxels * dtype.itemsize

    if voxels > settings.dicom.volume.max_voxels:
        raise VolumeBuilderMemoryError()

    if (
        allocation_bytes
        > settings.dicom.volume.max_uncompressed_volume_mb
        * MIB
    ):
        raise VolumeBuilderMemoryError()

    import psutil

    available_bytes = psutil.virtual_memory().available

    reserve_bytes = int(
        max(
            settings.hardware.cpu.reserve_system_memory_gb,
            settings.performance.memory.minimum_free_ram_gb,
        ) * GIB
    )

    if settings.performance.memory.enable_memory_guard:
        if allocation_bytes * 2 > max(
            0,
            available_bytes - reserve_bytes,
        ):
            raise VolumeBuilderMemoryError()


# ============================================================
# 08 — PREVIEW GEOMETRY
# ============================================================

def calculate_preview_geometry(
    source: VolumeGeometry,
    stride_zyx: tuple[int, int, int],
    new_shape_zyx: tuple[int, int, int],
) -> VolumeGeometry:
    """
    Preserve physical positions when sampling source voxels.

    Sampling starts at source voxel (0,0,0), so origin and
    direction remain unchanged. Spacing grows by each stride.
    """

    stride_z, stride_y, stride_x = stride_zyx

    spacing_x, spacing_y, spacing_z = (
        source.spacing_xyz_mm
    )

    return VolumeGeometry(
        shape_zyx=new_shape_zyx,
        spacing_xyz_mm=(
            spacing_x * stride_x,
            spacing_y * stride_y,
            spacing_z * stride_z,
        ),
        origin_lps=source.origin_lps,
        direction_lps=source.direction_lps,
    )


# ============================================================
# 09 — GENERATE ONE PREVIEW
# ============================================================

def build_preview_volume(
    source: PreparedVolume,
    *,
    max_dimension: int,
    level: int,
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
    cancel_event: threading.Event | None = None,
) -> PreparedVolume:
    """
    Generate an independent contiguous float32 preview.

    Uses nearest-neighbour voxel subsampling.

    This is intended for navigation and rapid overview only.
    It is not a diagnostic-quality resampling method.
    """

    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()

    check_cancelled(cancel_event)

    if level <= 0:
        raise ValueError("Preview level must be positive.")

    stride = calculate_preview_stride(
        source.shape_zyx,
        max_dimension,
    )

    new_shape = preview_shape(
        source.shape_zyx,
        stride,
    )

    enforce_preview_memory_limit(
        new_shape,
        source.voxels.dtype,
        active,
    )

    z_step, y_step, x_step = stride

    try:
        # First create a view, then one contiguous copy.
        sampled = source.voxels[
            ::z_step,
            ::y_step,
            ::x_step,
        ]

        check_cancelled(cancel_event)

        preview_pixels = np.ascontiguousarray(sampled)

        # If stride is 1 in every direction, NumPy may
        # reuse the original buffer. Force separation.
        if np.shares_memory(
            preview_pixels,
            source.voxels,
        ):
            preview_pixels = preview_pixels.copy()

        preview_pixels.setflags(write=False)

        preview_geometry = calculate_preview_geometry(
            source.geometry,
            stride,
            tuple(preview_pixels.shape),
        )

    except MemoryError:
        raise VolumeBuilderMemoryError() from None

    check_cancelled(cancel_event)

    return PreparedVolume(
        voxels=preview_pixels,
        geometry=preview_geometry,
        level=level,
        is_preview=True,
        source_shape_zyx=source.source_shape_zyx,
        stride_zyx=tuple(
            a * b
            for a, b in zip(
                source.stride_zyx,
                stride,
            )
        ),
    )


# ============================================================
# 10 — PREVIEW PYRAMID
# ============================================================

def build_preview_pyramid(
    full: PreparedVolume,
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
    *,
    cancel_event: threading.Event | None = None,
) -> tuple[PreparedVolume, ...]:
    """
    Prepare low-resolution volume levels.

    Limits follow settings.yaml max preview dimension.
    Identical shapes are not duplicated.

    Preview memory is released with the containing package.
    """

    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()

    preview_config = active.dicom.volume.preview

    if not preview_config.enabled:
        return ()

    limits = sorted(set(
        min(dimension, preview_config.max_dimension)
        for dimension in DEFAULT_PREVIEW_DIMENSIONS
    ))

    previews: list[PreparedVolume] = []
    seen_shapes: set[tuple[int, int, int]] = set()

    for max_dimension in limits:
        check_cancelled(cancel_event)

        stride = calculate_preview_stride(
            full.shape_zyx,
            max_dimension,
        )

        shape = preview_shape(
            full.shape_zyx,
            stride,
        )

        # Do not duplicate the full-resolution volume.
        if shape == full.shape_zyx:
            continue

        if shape in seen_shapes:
            continue

        level = len(previews) + 1

        preview = build_preview_volume(
            full,
            max_dimension=max_dimension,
            level=level,
            settings=active,
            cancel_event=cancel_event,
        )

        previews.append(preview)
        seen_shapes.add(preview.shape_zyx)

    return tuple(previews)


# ============================================================
# 11 — BUILD PACKAGE FROM LOADED SERIES
# ============================================================

def package_loaded_volume(
    series_volume: SeriesVolume,
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
    *,
    cancel_event: threading.Event | None = None,
) -> VolumePackage:
    """
    Package an existing volume without reading DICOM files.
    """

    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()

    check_cancelled(cancel_event)

    full = prepare_full_volume(series_volume)

    previews = build_preview_pyramid(
        full,
        active,
        cancel_event=cancel_event,
    )

    check_cancelled(cancel_event)

    return VolumePackage(
        full=full,
        previews=previews,
    )


# ============================================================
# 12 — COMPLETE DICOM-TO-VOLUME PIPELINE
# ============================================================

def build_volume_package(
    instances: Sequence[DicomMetadata],
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
    *,
    hardware_snapshot: HardwareSnapshot | None = None,
    cancel_event: threading.Event | None = None,
    progress_callback: ProgressCallback | None = None,
) -> VolumePackage:
    """
    Read one validated conventional DICOM series and
    generate full-resolution plus available preview levels.

    The source reader enforces dataset path protection,
    geometric validation and full-volume memory checks.

    No DICOM source data is modified.
    """

    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()

    check_cancelled(cancel_event)

    snapshot = (
        hardware_snapshot
        if hardware_snapshot is not None
        else detect_hardware(active)
    )

    loaded = load_series_volume(
        instances,
        active,
        hardware_snapshot=snapshot,
        cancel_event=cancel_event,
        progress_callback=progress_callback,
    )

    check_cancelled(cancel_event)

    return package_loaded_volume(
        loaded,
        active,
        cancel_event=cancel_event,
    )


# ============================================================
# 13 — LEVEL DESCRIPTORS
# ============================================================

def describe_volume_level(
    volume: PreparedVolume,
) -> dict:
    """
    Technical descriptor for an authenticated API.

    No pixel buffer, patient identifiers or source paths.
    """

    return {
        **volume.safe_summary(),
        "geometry": build_geometry_report(
            volume.geometry
        ),
        "byte_order": "little",
        "axis_order": "zyx",
        "pixel_encoding": "float32",
        "compression": "none",
    }


def describe_volume_package(
    package: VolumePackage,
) -> dict:
    """
    Return available level descriptions.

    Actual volume bytes must be served via a separate
    authenticated, bounded streaming endpoint.
    """

    return {
        "format": "sinus-viewer-volume-v1",
        "coordinate_system": "LPS",
        "levels": [
            describe_volume_level(volume)
            for volume in package.all_levels()
        ],
    }


# ============================================================
# 14 — RAW PIXEL VIEW
# ============================================================

def get_volume_byte_view(
    volume: PreparedVolume,
) -> memoryview:
    """
    Return a read-only view over contiguous float32 bytes.

    The API layer must:
    - Authenticate and authorize every request.
    - Enforce bounded response sizes.
    - Set no-store cache headers.
    - Keep references alive while streaming.

    No network response is sent by this function.
    """

    data = volume.voxels

    if (
        data.dtype != np.dtype("float32")
        or data.ndim != 3
        or not data.flags.c_contiguous
    ):
        raise VolumeBuilderGeometryError()

    # A bytes view is read-only and does not modify pixels.
    return memoryview(data).cast("B").toreadonly()


# ============================================================
# 15 — CAPABILITY REPORT
# ============================================================

def get_volume_builder_capabilities() -> dict:
    return {
        "full_resolution": True,
        "preview_pyramid": True,
        "preview_method": "integer_stride_subsampling",
        "preview_diagnostic_quality": False,
        "preview_without_full_volume": False,
        "input_axis_order": "zyx",
        "pixel_encoding": "float32",
        "coordinate_system": "LPS",
        "geometry_preserved": True,
        "read_only_source": True,
        "gpu_preprocessing": False,
        "browser_streaming_endpoint": False,
    }


# ============================================================
# END OF VOLUME BUILDER
# ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/backend/app/security/__init__.py


### sinus-viewer/backend/app/security/auth.py
```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — SECURE AUTHENTICATION ENGINE
===========================================

Features:
- Argon2id password verification.
- Credentials loaded from environment variables.
- Cryptographically random session tokens.
- Only SHA-256 token digests retained in session storage.
- Idle and absolute session expiration.
- Session renewal by authenticated activity.
- Logout and global session revocation.
- Bounded in-memory session registry.
- Secure, HttpOnly, SameSite cookies.
- Random, per-session owner IDs for dataset isolation.
- No authentication credentials in logs or API errors.
- No filesystem operations or dataset modification.

SECURITY LIMITATIONS:
- This module does not implement HTTP login routes.
- Origin validation, CSRF protection, HTTPS enforcement,
  request throttling and proxy trust checks are required
  in the FastAPI security integration.
- Session state is process-local and is lost on restart.
- Multiple backend workers require shared session storage
  or sticky routing; this initial deployment uses one worker.
- In-memory logout revocation does not revoke any HTTP
  response that has already transmitted patient data.

Python 3.10+
Required dependency on Colab: argon2-cffi
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import hmac
import os
import re
import secrets
import threading
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

from argon2 import PasswordHasher
from argon2.exceptions import (
    InvalidHashError,
    VerificationError,
    VerifyMismatchError,
)
from fastapi import Response

from ..core.config import AppSettings, get_settings


# ============================================================
# 01 — CONSTANTS
# ============================================================

USERNAME_ENV = "SINUS_VIEWER_USERNAME"
PASSWORD_HASH_ENV = "SINUS_VIEWER_PASSWORD_HASH"

SECURE_COOKIE_NAME = "__Host-sinus_viewer_session"
DEVELOPMENT_COOKIE_NAME = "sinus_viewer_session"

MAX_USERNAME_LENGTH = 128
MAX_PASSWORD_LENGTH = 1024
MAX_SESSION_TOKEN_LENGTH = 128
MAX_ACTIVE_SESSIONS = 32

SESSION_TOKEN_PATTERN = re.compile(
    r"^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{32,128}$",
    re.ASCII,
)

ARGON2ID_PREFIX = "$argon2id$"


# ============================================================
# 02 — CONTROLLED AUTHENTICATION ERRORS
# ============================================================

class AuthError(RuntimeError):
    """Safe authentication exception."""

    public_code = "AUTH_FAILED"

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(self.public_code)


class AuthConfigurationError(AuthError):
    public_code = "CONFIG_ERROR"


class InvalidCredentials(AuthError):
    public_code = "AUTH_FAILED"


class AuthenticationRequired(AuthError):
    public_code = "AUTH_REQUIRED"


class SessionExpired(AuthError):
    public_code = "SESSION_EXPIRED"


class SessionCapacityExceeded(AuthError):
    public_code = "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE"


class AuthenticationServiceClosed(AuthError):
    public_code = "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE"


# ============================================================
# 03 — SAFE DATA MODELS
# ============================================================

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AuthIdentity:
    """
    Trusted identity returned after session validation.

    owner_id is an opaque server-generated value suitable
    for DatasetService ownership checks.

    Never construct this object from request parameters.
    """

    owner_id: str
    issued_at: float
    expires_at: float


@dataclass
class _SessionRecord:
    owner_id: str = field(repr=False)
    created_monotonic: float
    last_seen_monotonic: float
    absolute_deadline: float
    idle_deadline: float
    issued_wall_time: float


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AuthenticationStatistics:
    active_sessions: int
    maximum_sessions: int
    login_successes: int
    login_failures: int
    expired_sessions: int
    revoked_sessions: int
    closed: bool

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "active_sessions": self.active_sessions,
            "maximum_sessions": self.maximum_sessions,
            "login_successes": self.login_successes,
            "login_failures": self.login_failures,
            "expired_sessions": self.expired_sessions,
            "revoked_sessions": self.revoked_sessions,
            "closed": self.closed,
        }


# ============================================================
# 04 — ENVIRONMENT CREDENTIAL LOADING
# ============================================================

def load_auth_credentials(
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
) -> tuple[str, str]:
    """
    Load configured credentials without printing their values.

    Fails closed when credentials are absent or malformed.
    """

    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()

    credentials = active.security.credentials

    if credentials.source != "environment":
        raise AuthConfigurationError()

    username_key = credentials.username_env
    hash_key = credentials.password_hash_env

    if not username_key or not hash_key:
        raise AuthConfigurationError()

    username = os.environ.get(username_key)
    password_hash = os.environ.get(hash_key)

    if (
        not isinstance(username, str)
        or not username
        or len(username) > MAX_USERNAME_LENGTH
        or username.strip() != username
        or not isinstance(password_hash, str)
        or not password_hash.startswith(ARGON2ID_PREFIX)
        or len(password_hash) > 1024
    ):
        raise AuthConfigurationError()

    return username, password_hash


# ============================================================
# 05 — TOKEN HELPERS
# ============================================================

def generate_session_token() -> str:
    """
    Generate a high-entropy bearer session token.

    The plaintext token must only travel in a secure cookie.
    """

    return secrets.token_urlsafe(48)


def digest_session_token(token: str) -> str:
    """
    Hash a bearer token before using it as an internal key.
    """

    if (
        not isinstance(token, str)
        or not SESSION_TOKEN_PATTERN.fullmatch(token)
        or len(token) > MAX_SESSION_TOKEN_LENGTH
    ):
        raise AuthenticationRequired()

    return hashlib.sha256(
        token.encode("ascii")
    ).hexdigest()


def generate_owner_id() -> str:
    """
    Create an opaque per-login owner identifier.

    Do not use usernames, patient IDs or source paths.
    """

    return "owner_" + secrets.token_urlsafe(24)


# ============================================================
# 06 — PASSWORD VERIFICATION
# ============================================================

def verify_password(
    password: str,
    expected_hash: str,
    *,
    hasher: PasswordHasher | None = None,
) -> bool:
    """
    Verify Argon2id hash using argon2-cffi.

    Never compare plaintext passwords directly.
    Never emit password values or verifier exceptions.

    Rate limiting is required at the HTTP layer.
    """

    if (
        not isinstance(password, str)
        or not password
        or len(password) > MAX_PASSWORD_LENGTH
    ):
        return False

    if (
        not isinstance(expected_hash, str)
        or not expected_hash.startswith(ARGON2ID_PREFIX)
    ):
        raise AuthConfigurationError()

    verifier = hasher if hasher is not None else PasswordHasher()

    try:
        return bool(
            verifier.verify(expected_hash, password)
        )

    except VerifyMismatchError:
        return False

    except (VerificationError, InvalidHashError):
        return False


# ============================================================
# 07 — AUTHENTICATION SERVICE
# ============================================================

class AuthenticationService:
    """
    Thread-safe, process-local authentication manager.

    Session records are keyed by token digests, never
    by plaintext bearer tokens.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        settings: AppSettings | None = None,
        *,
        max_active_sessions: int = MAX_ACTIVE_SESSIONS,
    ) -> None:
        if (
            not isinstance(max_active_sessions, int)
            or isinstance(max_active_sessions, bool)
            or max_active_sessions < 1
        ):
            raise ValueError("Invalid session capacity.")

        self._settings = (
            settings if settings is not None
            else get_settings()
        )

        security = self._settings.security

        if (
            not security.require_authentication
            or security.allow_anonymous_access
            or not security.sessions.enabled
        ):
            raise AuthConfigurationError()

        self._username, self._password_hash = (
            load_auth_credentials(self._settings)
        )

        self._hasher = PasswordHasher()

        self._idle_seconds = (
            security.sessions.idle_timeout_minutes * 60
        )
        self._absolute_seconds = (
            security.sessions.absolute_timeout_hours * 3600
        )

        if (
            self._idle_seconds <= 0
            or self._absolute_seconds <= 0
        ):
            raise AuthConfigurationError()

        self._maximum_sessions = max_active_sessions

        self._sessions: dict[str, _SessionRecord] = {}
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

        self._closed = False

        self._login_successes = 0
        self._login_failures = 0
        self._expired_sessions = 0
        self._revoked_sessions = 0

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # INTERNAL STATE
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    def _require_open(self) -> None:
        if self._closed:
            raise AuthenticationServiceClosed()

    def _remove_expired(self, now: float) -> None:
        expired = [
            digest
            for digest, record in self._sessions.items()
            if (
                now >= record.idle_deadline
                or now >= record.absolute_deadline
            )
        ]

        for digest in expired:
            del self._sessions[digest]
            self._expired_sessions += 1

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # LOGIN
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    def login(
        self,
        username: str,
        password: str,
    ) -> str:
        """
        Authenticate credentials and return a bearer token.

        This is an internal service method, not an API
        response. The HTTP route must set a secure cookie
        and must not put the token into JSON or logs.
        """

        with self._lock:
            self._require_open()

        username_valid = (
            isinstance(username, str)
            and 0 < len(username) <= MAX_USERNAME_LENGTH
            and hmac.compare_digest(
                username.encode("utf-8"),
                self._username.encode("utf-8"),
            )
        )

        # Always verify the password hash for valid
        # bounded passwords, even if username is wrong.
        password_valid = verify_password(
            password,
            self._password_hash,
            hasher=self._hasher,
        )

        with self._lock:
            self._require_open()

            if not (username_valid and password_valid):
                self._login_failures += 1
                raise InvalidCredentials()

            now = time.monotonic()

            self._remove_expired(now)

            if len(self._sessions) >= self._maximum_sessions:
                raise SessionCapacityExceeded()

            token = generate_session_token()
            digest = digest_session_token(token)

            self._sessions[digest] = _SessionRecord(
                owner_id=generate_owner_id(),
                created_monotonic=now,
                last_seen_monotonic=now,
                absolute_deadline=now + self._absolute_seconds,
                idle_deadline=now + self._idle_seconds,
                issued_wall_time=time.time(),
            )

            self._login_successes += 1

            return token

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # AUTHENTICATE SESSION
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    def authenticate(
        self,
        token: str | None,
        *,
        update_activity: bool = True,
    ) -> AuthIdentity:
        """
        Validate an existing session and return trusted
        server-side identity information.
        """

        if token is None:
            raise AuthenticationRequired()

        digest = digest_session_token(token)

        with self._lock:
            self._require_open()

            now = time.monotonic()
            record = self._sessions.get(digest)

            if record is None:
                raise AuthenticationRequired()

            if (
                now >= record.idle_deadline
                or now >= record.absolute_deadline
            ):
                del self._sessions[digest]
                self._expired_sessions += 1
                raise SessionExpired()

            if update_activity:
                record.last_seen_monotonic = now
                record.idle_deadline = min(
                    now + self._idle_seconds,
                    record.absolute_deadline,
                )

            self._remove_expired(now)

            return AuthIdentity(
                owner_id=record.owner_id,
                issued_at=record.issued_wall_time,
                expires_at=(
                    time.time()
                    + max(
                        0.0,
                        record.absolute_deadline - now,
                    )
                ),
            )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # LOGOUT
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    def logout(self, token: str | None) -> bool:
        """
        Revoke one bearer token.

        Safe to call with a missing or already-revoked token.
        """

        if token is None:
            return False

        try:
            digest = digest_session_token(token)
        except AuthenticationRequired:
            return False

        with self._lock:
            self._require_open()

            removed = self._sessions.pop(
                digest,
                None,
            )

            if removed is not None:
                self._revoked_sessions += 1
                return True

            return False

    def revoke_all_sessions(self) -> int:
        """
        Revoke all sessions.

        Expose only through trusted administrative code.
        """

        with self._lock:
            self._require_open()

            count = len(self._sessions)

            self._sessions.clear()
            self._revoked_sessions += count

            return count

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # STATISTICS
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    def statistics(self) -> AuthenticationStatistics:
        with self._lock:
            self._remove_expired(time.monotonic())

            return AuthenticationStatistics(
                active_sessions=len(self._sessions),
                maximum_sessions=self._maximum_sessions,
                login_successes=self._login_successes,
                login_failures=self._login_failures,
                expired_sessions=self._expired_sessions,
                revoked_sessions=self._revoked_sessions,
                closed=self._closed,
            )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # SHUTDOWN
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    def close(self) -> None:
        """
        Revoke all in-memory sessions and prevent new ones.
        """

        with self._lock:
            if self._closed:
                return

            self._closed = True
            self._revoked_sessions += len(self._sessions)
            self._sessions.clear()


# ============================================================
# 08 — COOKIE POLICY
# ============================================================

def get_session_cookie_name(
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
) -> str:
    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()

    if active.security.sessions.cookie_secure:
        return SECURE_COOKIE_NAME

    return DEVELOPMENT_COOKIE_NAME


def set_session_cookie(
    response: Response,
    token: str,
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
) -> None:
    """
    Attach a bearer token to an HttpOnly session cookie.

    Only call after successful login and HTTPS/origin
    checks performed by the API security layer.
    """

    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()
    sessions = active.security.sessions

    digest_session_token(token)

    if not sessions.cookie_http_only:
        raise AuthConfigurationError()

    same_site = sessions.cookie_same_site.lower()

    if same_site not in {"strict", "lax", "none"}:
        raise AuthConfigurationError()

    if same_site == "none" and not sessions.cookie_secure:
        raise AuthConfigurationError()

    lifetime = int(
        sessions.absolute_timeout_hours * 3600
    )

    response.set_cookie(
        key=get_session_cookie_name(active),
        value=token,
        max_age=lifetime,
        path="/",
        secure=sessions.cookie_secure,
        httponly=True,
        samesite=same_site,
    )

    response.headers["Cache-Control"] = "no-store"
    response.headers["Pragma"] = "no-cache"


def clear_session_cookie(
    response: Response,
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
) -> None:
    """
    Remove browser cookie after server-side logout.

    The HTTP logout route must call logout() before this.
    """

    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()

    response.delete_cookie(
        key=get_session_cookie_name(active),
        path="/",
        secure=active.security.sessions.cookie_secure,
        httponly=True,
        samesite=active.security.sessions.cookie_same_site.lower(),
    )

    response.headers["Cache-Control"] = "no-store"
    response.headers["Pragma"] = "no-cache"


# ============================================================
# 09 — SERVICE FACTORY
# ============================================================

def create_authentication_service(
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
) -> AuthenticationService:
    """
    Construct one authentication service per backend process.

    Environment credentials must exist before startup.
    """

    return AuthenticationService(settings)


def get_authentication_summary(
    service: AuthenticationService,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Non-identifying operational statistics only.
    """

    return service.statistics().safe_summary()


# ============================================================
# END OF AUTHENTICATION ENGINE
# ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/backend/app/security/middleware.py
```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — CENTRAL HTTP SECURITY MIDDLEWARE
================================================

Provides:
- Default authentication for /api routes.
- Exact allowlist for public login and basic health checks.
- Origin / Fetch Metadata validation.
- Production HTTPS transport checks.
- Bounded request body buffering.
- Process-local login request throttling.
- Protective HTTP response headers.
- Server-generated request correlation IDs.
- Sanitized security failure responses.
- No raw request URL, cookie, token or patient-data logging.

Security boundaries:
- The authentication service is registered at app startup.
- Same-origin browser deployment is expected.
- Reverse proxies must terminate HTTPS and connect from
  an explicitly trusted loopback address.
- This is not a complete reverse-proxy trust framework.
- Rate limiting here is global and process-local.
- Do not deploy identifiable clinical patient data to a
  public Colab tunnel without an approved security design.
- No DICOM files are read, written, renamed or deleted.

Python 3.10+
FastAPI / Starlette
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hmac
import json
import threading
import time
import uuid
from collections import deque
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Callable

from fastapi import FastAPI, Request
from starlette.datastructures import Headers
from starlette.responses import JSONResponse
from starlette.types import (
    ASGIApp,
    Message,
    Receive,
    Scope,
    Send,
)

from ..core.config import AppSettings, get_settings
from ..core.exceptions import (
    AccessDenied,
    AuthenticationRequired,
    ConfigurationFailure,
    SessionExpired,
)
from .session import (
    authenticate_request,
    get_trusted_public_origin,
    validate_request_origin,
)


# ============================================================
# 01 — CONSTANTS
# ============================================================

API_PREFIX = "/api"

PUBLIC_API_PATHS = frozenset({
    "/api/auth/login",
    "/api/health",
})

LOGIN_PATH = "/api/auth/login"

SAFE_METHODS = frozenset({
    "GET",
    "HEAD",
    "OPTIONS",
})

BODY_METHODS = frozenset({
    "POST",
    "PUT",
    "PATCH",
    "DELETE",
})

LOGIN_RATE_WINDOW_SECONDS = 60
LOGIN_RATE_MAX_REQUESTS = 8

MAX_REQUEST_ID_LENGTH = 64

TRUSTED_LOOPBACK_HOSTS = frozenset({
    "127.0.0.1",
    "::1",
})

CONTENT_SECURITY_POLICY = (
    "default-src 'self'; "
    "base-uri 'self'; "
    "object-src 'none'; "
    "frame-ancestors 'none'; "
    "form-action 'self'; "
    "script-src 'self'; "
    "style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; "
    "img-src 'self' data: blob:; "
    "font-src 'self' data:; "
    "connect-src 'self' wss:; "
    "worker-src 'self' blob:"
)


# ============================================================
# 02 — CONTROLLED SECURITY FAILURES
# ============================================================

class SecurityMiddlewareError(RuntimeError):
    """
    Internal controlled security error.

    Never carry request content or patient information.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        code: str,
        status_code: int,
    ) -> None:
        self.code = code
        self.status_code = status_code
        super().__init__(code)


# ============================================================
# 03 — RATE LIMITER
# ============================================================

class LoginRateLimiter:
    """
    Thread-safe, process-local global login throttle.

    It intentionally does not store IP addresses or usernames.

    Limitation:
        The global limit can also affect legitimate users
        during high traffic or deliberate request flooding.
        A trusted gateway should provide stronger per-client
        rate limiting for production deployment.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        maximum_requests: int = LOGIN_RATE_MAX_REQUESTS,
        window_seconds: int = LOGIN_RATE_WINDOW_SECONDS,
    ) -> None:

        if (
            not isinstance(maximum_requests, int)
            or isinstance(maximum_requests, bool)
            or maximum_requests < 1
            or not isinstance(window_seconds, int)
            or isinstance(window_seconds, bool)
            or window_seconds < 1
        ):
            raise ValueError("Invalid rate-limit configuration.")

        self._maximum = maximum_requests
        self._window = window_seconds

        self._attempts: deque[float] = deque()
        self._lock = threading.Lock()

    @property
    def window_seconds(self) -> int:
        return self._window

    def allow_request(self) -> bool:
        """
        Count login requests in a rolling monotonic window.

        No filesystem or network operations.
        """

        now = time.monotonic()
        threshold = now - self._window

        with self._lock:
            while (
                self._attempts
                and self._attempts[0] <= threshold
            ):
                self._attempts.popleft()

            if len(self._attempts) >= self._maximum:
                return False

            self._attempts.append(now)

            return True


# ============================================================
# 04 — SECURITY RESPONSE HEADERS
# ============================================================

def get_security_headers(
    *,
    api_response: bool,
    secure_transport: bool,
) -> dict[str, str]:
    """
    Produce standard protective response headers.

    No user-controlled values are interpolated.
    """

    headers = {
        "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
        "Referrer-Policy": "no-referrer",
        "X-Frame-Options": "DENY",
        "Content-Security-Policy": CONTENT_SECURITY_POLICY,
        "Permissions-Policy": (
            "camera=(), microphone=(), "
            "geolocation=(), payment=()"
        ),
        "Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy": "same-origin",
    }

    if api_response:
        headers["Cache-Control"] = (
            "no-store, private"
        )
        headers["Pragma"] = "no-cache"

    if secure_transport:
        headers["Strict-Transport-Security"] = (
            "max-age=31536000"
        )

    return headers


# ============================================================
# 05 — SAFE ERROR RESPONSE
# ============================================================

def make_security_error_response(
    code: str,
    status_code: int,
    request_id: str,
    *,
    secure_transport: bool,
) -> JSONResponse:
    """
    Return standardized, non-disclosing security errors.
    """

    messages = {
        "AUTH_REQUIRED": "Authentication is required.",
        "SESSION_EXPIRED": "Your session has expired.",
        "ACCESS_DENIED": "Access denied.",
        "RATE_LIMITED": "Too many requests. Try again later.",
        "REQUEST_TOO_LARGE": "Request exceeds the size limit.",
        "HTTPS_REQUIRED": "A secure connection is required.",
        "CONFIG_ERROR": "Service configuration is unavailable.",
        "INVALID_REQUEST": "The request is invalid.",
    }

    if code not in messages:
        code = "ACCESS_DENIED"
        status_code = 403

    headers = get_security_headers(
        api_response=True,
        secure_transport=secure_transport,
    )

    headers["X-Request-ID"] = request_id

    if status_code == 429:
        headers["Retry-After"] = str(
            LOGIN_RATE_WINDOW_SECONDS
        )

    return JSONResponse(
        status_code=status_code,
        content={
            "success": False,
            "error": {
                "code": code,
                "message": messages[code],
                "retryable": status_code in (429, 503),
                "request_id": request_id,
            },
        },
        headers=headers,
    )


# ============================================================
# 06 — TRANSPORT SECURITY
# ============================================================

def _is_loopback_client(scope: Scope) -> bool:
    client = scope.get("client")

    if not client:
        return False

    return client[0] in TRUSTED_LOOPBACK_HOSTS


def is_secure_request(scope: Scope) -> bool:
    """
    Determine whether incoming HTTP transport is secure.

    Direct HTTPS is accepted.

    For a local reverse proxy, one X-Forwarded-Proto value
    may be accepted only from loopback. This assumes the
    loopback listener is not accessible to untrusted users
    and the trusted proxy overwrites forwarded headers.
    """

    if scope.get("scheme") == "https":
        return True

    if not _is_loopback_client(scope):
        return False

    headers = Headers(scope=scope)
    forwarded = headers.getlist("x-forwarded-proto")

    return (
        len(forwarded) == 1
        and forwarded[0].strip().lower() == "https"
    )


def enforce_transport_security(
    scope: Scope,
) -> None:
    """
    Reject non-HTTPS requests unless explicitly using
    an authorized localhost development origin.
    """

    if is_secure_request(scope):
        return

    try:
        trusted_origin = get_trusted_public_origin()
    except ConfigurationFailure:
        raise SecurityMiddlewareError(
            "HTTPS_REQUIRED",
            403,
        ) from None

    local_development = (
        trusted_origin.startswith("http://localhost")
        or trusted_origin.startswith("http://127.0.0.1")
        or trusted_origin.startswith("http://[::1]")
    )

    if not (
        local_development
        and _is_loopback_client(scope)
    ):
        raise SecurityMiddlewareError(
            "HTTPS_REQUIRED",
            403,
        )


# ============================================================
# 07 — REQUEST BODY LIMIT
# ============================================================

async def read_bounded_body(
    receive: Receive,
    *,
    maximum_bytes: int,
) -> bytes:
    """
    Buffer a bounded request body before sending it to app.

    This protects endpoints even when Content-Length is
    absent or misleading.

    Memory usage is bounded by configured max request size.
    """

    if maximum_bytes < 1:
        raise ValueError("Invalid maximum request size.")

    chunks: list[bytes] = []
    total = 0

    while True:
        message = await receive()

        message_type = message["type"]

        if message_type == "http.disconnect":
            raise SecurityMiddlewareError(
                "INVALID_REQUEST",
                400,
            )

        if message_type != "http.request":
            raise SecurityMiddlewareError(
                "INVALID_REQUEST",
                400,
            )

        chunk = message.get("body", b"")

        if not isinstance(chunk, bytes):
            raise SecurityMiddlewareError(
                "INVALID_REQUEST",
                400,
            )

        total += len(chunk)

        if total > maximum_bytes:
            raise SecurityMiddlewareError(
                "REQUEST_TOO_LARGE",
                413,
            )

        chunks.append(chunk)

        if not message.get("more_body", False):
            break

    return b"".join(chunks)


# ============================================================
# 08 — MIDDLEWARE STATISTICS
# ============================================================

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MiddlewareStatistics:
    requests: int
    rejected: int
    login_rate_limited: int

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "requests": self.requests,
            "rejected": self.rejected,
            "login_rate_limited": self.login_rate_limited,
        }


# ============================================================
# 09 — ASGI SECURITY MIDDLEWARE
# ============================================================

class SinusSecurityMiddleware:
    """
    Pure ASGI middleware for FastAPI.

    Security checks execute before protected route handlers.

    Login and health endpoints are explicitly exempted
    from session authentication, not from security headers
    or transport checks.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        app: ASGIApp,
        settings: AppSettings | None = None,
    ) -> None:

        self.app = app

        self.settings = (
            settings if settings is not None
            else get_settings()
        )

        self.max_request_bytes = (
            self.settings.server.limits.max_request_body_mb
            * 1024 * 1024
        )

        self.login_limiter = LoginRateLimiter()

        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        self._requests = 0
        self._rejected = 0
        self._login_rate_limited = 0

    def statistics(self) -> MiddlewareStatistics:
        with self._lock:
            return MiddlewareStatistics(
                requests=self._requests,
                rejected=self._rejected,
                login_rate_limited=self._login_rate_limited,
            )

    async def __call__(
        self,
        scope: Scope,
        receive: Receive,
        send: Send,
    ) -> None:

        if scope["type"] != "http":
            await self.app(scope, receive, send)
            return

        request_id = uuid.uuid4().hex

        scope.setdefault("state", {})
        scope["state"]["request_id"] = request_id

        path = scope.get("path", "")
        method = scope.get("method", "GET").upper()

        api_request = (
            path == API_PREFIX
            or path.startswith(API_PREFIX + "/")
        )

        secure = is_secure_request(scope)

        with self._lock:
            self._requests += 1

        async def send_secure(message: Message) -> None:
            if message["type"] == "http.response.start":
                existing = list(
                    message.get("headers", [])
                )

                security_headers = get_security_headers(
                    api_response=api_request,
                    secure_transport=secure,
                )

                security_headers["X-Request-ID"] = request_id

                # Replace matching header names rather than
                # producing inconsistent duplicate policies.
                replacement_names = {
                    key.lower().encode("latin-1")
                    for key in security_headers
                }

                existing = [
                    (key, value)
                    for key, value in existing
                    if key.lower() not in replacement_names
                ]

                existing.extend(
                    (
                        key.lower().encode("latin-1"),
                        value.encode("latin-1"),
                    )
                    for key, value in security_headers.items()
                )

                message["headers"] = existing

            await send(message)

        try:
            if api_request:
                enforce_transport_security(scope)

                request = Request(scope, receive=receive)

                # Origin checks protect both login and
                # authenticated unsafe API operations.
                validate_request_origin(request)

                if path == LOGIN_PATH:
                    if method != "POST":
                        raise SecurityMiddlewareError(
                            "ACCESS_DENIED",
                            403,
                        )

                    if not self.login_limiter.allow_request():
                        with self._lock:
                            self._login_rate_limited += 1

                        raise SecurityMiddlewareError(
                            "RATE_LIMITED",
                            429,
                        )

                elif path not in PUBLIC_API_PATHS:
                    # This sets a verified, server-derived
                    # authenticated context on request.state.
                    authenticate_request(request)

            # Protect all API methods with request bodies.
            if api_request and method in BODY_METHODS:
                headers = Headers(scope=scope)

                values = headers.getlist("content-length")

                if len(values) > 1:
                    raise SecurityMiddlewareError(
                        "INVALID_REQUEST",
                        400,
                    )

                if values:
                    try:
                        declared = int(values[0])
                    except ValueError:
                        raise SecurityMiddlewareError(
                            "INVALID_REQUEST",
                            400,
                        ) from None

                    if declared < 0:
                        raise SecurityMiddlewareError(
                            "INVALID_REQUEST",
                            400,
                        )

                    if declared > self.max_request_bytes:
                        raise SecurityMiddlewareError(
                            "REQUEST_TOO_LARGE",
                            413,
                        )

                body = await read_bounded_body(
                    receive,
                    maximum_bytes=self.max_request_bytes,
                )

                body_sent = False

                async def buffered_receive() -> Message:
                    nonlocal body_sent

                    if not body_sent:
                        body_sent = True

                        return {
                            "type": "http.request",
                            "body": body,
                            "more_body": False,
                        }

                    return await receive()

                await self.app(
                    scope,
                    buffered_receive,
                    send_secure,
                )

            else:
                await self.app(
                    scope,
                    receive,
                    send_secure,
                )

        except SecurityMiddlewareError as exc:
            with self._lock:
                self._rejected += 1

            response = make_security_error_response(
                exc.code,
                exc.status_code,
                request_id,
                secure_transport=secure,
            )

            await response(scope, receive, send_secure)

        except AuthenticationRequired:
            with self._lock:
                self._rejected += 1

            response = make_security_error_response(
                "AUTH_REQUIRED",
                401,
                request_id,
                secure_transport=secure,
            )

            await response(scope, receive, send_secure)

        except SessionExpired:
            with self._lock:
                self._rejected += 1

            response = make_security_error_response(
                "SESSION_EXPIRED",
```
