### sinus-viewer/backend/app/services/preview_service.py
```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — LOW-MEMORY DICOM PREVIEW SERVICE
==============================================

Builds lightweight, non-diagnostic navigation previews
without allocating a complete 3D volume.

Features:
- Authorized dataset and series selection
- Validated conventional DICOM slice geometry
- Individual source-slice decoding
- Axial, coronal and sagittal sampled previews
- Bounded preview dimensions and sampled slice counts
- Cancellation and progress reporting
- Monochrome display windowing
- Immutable 8-bit preview arrays
- Privacy-safe technical metadata
- No filesystem writes

IMPORTANT:
- Original DICOM data is strictly read-only.
- No volume-sized array is allocated.
- One decoded full-resolution slice is held at a time.
- Preview quality is not suitable for diagnosis.
- Enhanced multiframe and invalid geometry are rejected.
- The previous validator must approve the series.
- HTTP authentication is implemented separately.

Python 3.10+
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
import threading
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from enum import Enum
from typing import Callable

import numpy as np

from ..core.config import AppSettings, get_settings
from ..dicom.decoder import (
    DicomDecoderError,
    decode_dicom_file,
)
from ..dicom.metadata import (
    DicomMetadata,
    read_dicom_metadata,
)
from ..dicom.validator import (
    validate_series,
)
from .dataset_service import (
    DatasetService,
    DatasetServiceError,
    validate_handle,
    validate_owner_id,
)


# ============================================================
# 01 — CONSTANTS
# ============================================================

DEFAULT_PREVIEW_DIMENSION = 256
DEFAULT_MAX_SAMPLED_SLICES = 96

MAX_PREVIEW_DIMENSION = 512
MAX_SAMPLED_SLICES = 384

ProgressCallback = Callable[[int, int], None]


# ============================================================
# 02 — PREVIEW TYPES
# ============================================================

class PreviewPlane(str, Enum):
    AXIAL = "axial"
    CORONAL = "coronal"
    SAGITTAL = "sagittal"


class PreviewErrorCode(str, Enum):
    INVALID_REQUEST = "INVALID_REQUEST"
    SERIES_NOT_FOUND = "SERIES_NOT_FOUND"
    SERIES_INVALID = "SERIES_INVALID"
    SOURCE_CHANGED = "SOURCE_CHANGED"
    DECODING_FAILED = "DECODER_ERROR"
    CANCELLED = "OPERATION_CANCELLED"
    BUSY = "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE"
    MEMORY_LIMIT = "OUT_OF_MEMORY"


class PreviewServiceError(RuntimeError):
    """
    Controlled error without source file paths or PHI.
    """

    def __init__(self, code: PreviewErrorCode) -> None:
        self.code = code
        super().__init__(code.value)


# ============================================================
# 03 — OUTPUT MODELS
# ============================================================

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PreviewImage:
    plane: PreviewPlane
    pixels: np.ndarray = field(repr=False)

    source_shape_zyx: tuple[int, int, int]
    displayed_shape_yx: tuple[int, int]

    sampled_slice_count: int
    representative_slice_index: int

    window_low: float
    window_high: float

    @property
    def byte_size(self) -> int:
        return int(self.pixels.nbytes)

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "plane": self.plane.value,
            "shape_yx": list(self.displayed_shape_yx),
            "dtype": "uint8",
            "byte_size": self.byte_size,
            "source_shape_zyx": list(
                self.source_shape_zyx
            ),
            "sampled_slice_count": (
                self.sampled_slice_count
            ),
            "representative_slice_index": (
                self.representative_slice_index
            ),
            "window_low": self.window_low,
            "window_high": self.window_high,
            "diagnostic_quality": False,
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PreviewPackage:
    axial: PreviewImage
    coronal: PreviewImage
    sagittal: PreviewImage

    def get_plane(
        self,
        plane: PreviewPlane,
    ) -> PreviewImage:
        if plane == PreviewPlane.AXIAL:
            return self.axial

        if plane == PreviewPlane.CORONAL:
            return self.coronal

        if plane == PreviewPlane.SAGITTAL:
            return self.sagittal

        raise PreviewServiceError(
            PreviewErrorCode.INVALID_REQUEST
        )

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "axial": self.axial.safe_summary(),
            "coronal": self.coronal.safe_summary(),
            "sagittal": self.sagittal.safe_summary(),
            "diagnostic_quality": False,
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class PreviewServiceStatistics:
    requests: int
    completed: int
    failed: int
    active: int
    closed: bool

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict:
        return {
            "requests": self.requests,
            "completed": self.completed,
            "failed": self.failed,
            "active": self.active,
            "closed": self.closed,
        }


# ============================================================
# 04 — VALIDATION HELPERS
# ============================================================

def _positive_integer(
    value: int,
    *,
    maximum: int,
) -> int:
    if (
        not isinstance(value, int)
        or isinstance(value, bool)
        or value < 1
        or value > maximum
    ):
        raise PreviewServiceError(
            PreviewErrorCode.INVALID_REQUEST
        )

    return value


def check_preview_cancelled(
    event: threading.Event | None,
) -> None:
    if event is not None and event.is_set():
        raise PreviewServiceError(
            PreviewErrorCode.CANCELLED
        )


def sample_indices(
    length: int,
    maximum_samples: int,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Select evenly distributed integer voxel indices.

    Returns an ordered 1D array. Does not allocate a
    volume or access DICOM files.
    """

    _positive_integer(
        length,
        maximum=2**31 - 1,
    )

    _positive_integer(
        maximum_samples,
        maximum=MAX_PREVIEW_DIMENSION,
    )

    count = min(length, maximum_samples)

    if count == 1:
        indices = np.array(
            [(length - 1) // 2],
            dtype=np.int64,
        )
    else:
        indices = np.rint(
            np.linspace(
                0,
                length - 1,
                count,
                dtype=np.float64,
            )
        ).astype(np.int64)

    indices.setflags(write=False)

    return indices


# ============================================================
# 05 — SOURCE METADATA RECHECK
# ============================================================

def verify_preview_source(
    original: DicomMetadata,
    current: DicomMetadata,
) -> None:
    """
    Check important identity, geometry and pixel fields.

    This detects common changes since indexing, but is
    not a cryptographic snapshot or race-free file lock.
    """

    if (
        original.study_uid != current.study_uid
        or original.series_uid != current.series_uid
        or original.sop_instance_uid != current.sop_instance_uid
        or original.geometry != current.geometry
        or original.pixels != current.pixels
        or original.file_size_bytes != current.file_size_bytes
    ):
        raise PreviewServiceError(
            PreviewErrorCode.SOURCE_CHANGED
        )


# ============================================================
# 06 — READ ONE SOURCE SLICE
# ============================================================

def read_preview_slice(
    metadata: DicomMetadata,
    settings: AppSettings,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Read a single source image without building a 3D volume.

    Uses the dedicated decoder's path and pixel checks.
    """

    try:
        current = read_dicom_metadata(
            metadata.source_path,
            settings,
        )

        verify_preview_source(
            metadata,
            current,
        )

        decoded = decode_dicom_file(
            metadata.source_path,
            settings,
            expected_metadata=metadata,
            apply_modality_rescale=True,
        )

        if decoded.pixels.ndim != 2:
            raise PreviewServiceError(
                PreviewErrorCode.DECODING_FAILED
            )

        return decoded.pixels

    except PreviewServiceError:
        raise

    except DicomDecoderError:
        raise PreviewServiceError(
            PreviewErrorCode.DECODING_FAILED
        ) from None

    except (OSError, ValueError, RuntimeError):
        raise PreviewServiceError(
            PreviewErrorCode.SOURCE_CHANGED
        ) from None


# ============================================================
# 07 — DISPLAY WINDOWING
# ============================================================

def calculate_preview_window(
    sampled_pixels: np.ndarray,
) -> tuple[float, float]:
    """
    Estimate an intensity display window from sampled data.

    No diagnostic window preset is assumed for CBCT.
    """

    values = np.asarray(
        sampled_pixels,
        dtype=np.float32,
    )

    if values.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(values)):
        raise PreviewServiceError(
            PreviewErrorCode.DECODING_FAILED
        )

    try:
        low, high = np.percentile(
            values,
            [1.0, 99.0],
        )

        low = float(low)
        high = float(high)

    except (MemoryError, ValueError):
        raise PreviewServiceError(
            PreviewErrorCode.MEMORY_LIMIT
        ) from None

    if not (math.isfinite(low) and math.isfinite(high)):
        raise PreviewServiceError(
            PreviewErrorCode.DECODING_FAILED
        )

    if high <= low:
        low = float(values.min())
        high = float(values.max())

    if high <= low:
        high = low + 1.0

    return low, high


def apply_preview_window(
    pixels: np.ndarray,
    *,
    low: float,
    high: float,
    invert: bool = False,
) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Convert a small floating-point image into uint8.

    The operation affects RAM only, never source DICOM.
    """

    if not (
        math.isfinite(low)
        and math.isfinite(high)
        and high > low
    ):
        raise PreviewServiceError(
            PreviewErrorCode.INVALID_REQUEST
        )

    scaled = (
        np.asarray(pixels, dtype=np.float32) - low
    ) / (high - low)

    scaled = np.clip(
        scaled,
        0.0,
        1.0,
    )

    output = np.rint(
        scaled * 255.0
    ).astype(np.uint8)

    if invert:
        output = np.subtract(
            255,
            output,
            dtype=np.uint8,
        )

    output.setflags(write=False)

    return output


# ============================================================
# 08 — METADATA-VALIDATED PREVIEW BUILD
# ============================================================

def build_previews_from_series(
    instances: tuple[DicomMetadata, ...],
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
    *,
    max_dimension: int = DEFAULT_PREVIEW_DIMENSION,
    max_sampled_slices: int = DEFAULT_MAX_SAMPLED_SLICES,
    cancel_event: threading.Event | None = None,
    progress_callback: ProgressCallback | None = None,
) -> PreviewPackage:
    """
    Build three navigation previews using selected slices.

    Peak working memory is driven primarily by a single
    decoded source slice and small preview arrays, not
    the full volume's voxel count.

    Assumes conventional single-frame, regular geometry
    approved by validate_series().
    """

    active = settings if settings is not None else get_settings()

    dimension = _positive_integer(
        max_dimension,
        maximum=MAX_PREVIEW_DIMENSION,
    )

    sampled_limit = _positive_integer(
        max_sampled_slices,
        maximum=MAX_SAMPLED_SLICES,
    )

    check_preview_cancelled(cancel_event)

    report = validate_series(
        instances,
        active,
    )

    if not report.volume_ready or report.geometry is None:
        raise PreviewServiceError(
            PreviewErrorCode.SERIES_INVALID
        )

    geometry = report.geometry

    ordered = tuple(
        instances[index]
        for index in geometry.sorted_indices
    )

    depth = len(ordered)
    height = geometry.rows
    width = geometry.columns

    # Evenly sample source slices and horizontal/vertical
    # voxel indices. Only one original slice is decoded
    # at a time.
    z_indices = sample_indices(
        depth,
        min(sampled_limit, dimension),
    )

    y_indices = sample_indices(
        height,
        dimension,
    )

    x_indices = sample_indices(
        width,
        dimension,
    )

    center_z = depth // 2
    center_y = height // 2
    center_x = width // 2

    axial_float: np.ndarray | None = None

    try:
        coronal_float = np.empty(
            (len(z_indices), len(x_indices)),
            dtype=np.float32,
        )

        sagittal_float = np.empty(
            (len(z_indices), len(y_indices)),
            dtype=np.float32,
        )

        # Include exact central axial source slice even
        # when it is absent from the distributed samples.
        selected_slices = sorted(
            set(int(z) for z in z_indices)
            | {center_z}
        )

        lookup = {
            int(z): position
            for position, z in enumerate(z_indices)
        }

        invert_display = (
            ordered[center_z]
            .pixels.photometric_interpretation
            == "MONOCHROME1"
        )

        for completed, z in enumerate(
            selected_slices,
            start=1,
        ):
            check_preview_cancelled(cancel_event)

            image = read_preview_slice(
                ordered[z],
                active,
            )

            if image.shape != (height, width):
                raise PreviewServiceError(
                    PreviewErrorCode.SOURCE_CHANGED
                )

            if z in lookup:
                row = lookup[z]

                coronal_float[row] = image[
                    center_y, x_indices
                ]

                sagittal_float[row] = image[
                    y_indices, center_x
                ]

            if z == center_z:
                axial_float = image[
                    np.ix_(
                        y_indices,
                        x_indices,
                    )
                ].copy()

            del image

            if progress_callback is not None:
                progress_callback(
                    completed,
                    len(selected_slices),
                )

        if axial_float is None:
            raise PreviewServiceError(
                PreviewErrorCode.DECODING_FAILED
            )

        check_preview_cancelled(cancel_event)

        # A shared window helps the three planes represent
        # comparable intensity ranges for navigation.
        window_samples = np.concatenate([
            axial_float.ravel(),
            coronal_float.ravel(),
            sagittal_float.ravel(),
        ])

        low, high = calculate_preview_window(
            window_samples
        )

        def make_image(
            plane: PreviewPlane,
            pixels: np.ndarray,
        ) -> PreviewImage:
            display = apply_preview_window(
                pixels,
                low=low,
                high=high,
                invert=invert_display,
            )

            return PreviewImage(
                plane=plane,
                pixels=display,
                source_shape_zyx=(
                    depth,
                    height,
                    width,
                ),
                displayed_shape_yx=tuple(
                    int(v) for v in display.shape
                ),
                sampled_slice_count=len(z_indices),
                representative_slice_index=center_z,
                window_low=low,
                window_high=high,
            )

        return PreviewPackage(
            axial=make_image(
                PreviewPlane.AXIAL,
                axial_float,
            ),
            coronal=make_image(
                PreviewPlane.CORONAL,
                coronal_float,
            ),
            sagittal=make_image(
                PreviewPlane.SAGITTAL,
                sagittal_float,
            ),
        )

    except MemoryError:
        raise PreviewServiceError(
            PreviewErrorCode.MEMORY_LIMIT
        ) from None


# ============================================================
# 09 — AUTHORIZED PREVIEW SERVICE
# ============================================================

class PreviewService:
    """
    Process-local preview service.

    Uses the existing DatasetService for ownership and
    series lookup. One preview build runs at a time by
    default to limit simultaneous decoder memory usage.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        dataset_service: DatasetService,
        settings: AppSettings | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        if not isinstance(dataset_service, DatasetService):
            raise TypeError(
                "DatasetService instance required."
            )

        self._datasets = dataset_service

        self._settings = (
            settings if settings is not None
            else get_settings()
        )

        self._lock = threading.RLock()

        self._build_gate = threading.BoundedSemaphore(1)

        self._closed = False
        self._requests = 0
        self._completed = 0
        self._failed = 0
        self._active = 0

    def _require_open(self) -> None:
        if self._closed:
            raise PreviewServiceError(
                PreviewErrorCode.BUSY
            )

    def build_preview(
        self,
        dataset_session_id: str,
        series_handle: str,
        *,
        owner_id: str,
        max_dimension: int = DEFAULT_PREVIEW_DIMENSION,
        max_sampled_slices: int = DEFAULT_MAX_SAMPLED_SLICES,
        cancel_event: threading.Event | None = None,
        progress_callback: ProgressCallback | None = None,
    ) -> PreviewPackage:
        """
        Build authorized navigation previews.

        The owner_id must come from verified server-side
        authentication context, never a browser-supplied
        username or arbitrary request parameter.
        """

        validate_owner_id(owner_id)
        validate_handle(dataset_session_id)
        validate_handle(series_handle)

        _positive_integer(
            max_dimension,
            maximum=MAX_PREVIEW_DIMENSION,
        )

        _positive_integer(
            max_sampled_slices,
            maximum=MAX_SAMPLED_SLICES,
        )

        with self._lock:
            self._require_open()
            self._requests += 1

        if not self._build_gate.acquire(blocking=False):
            with self._lock:
                self._failed += 1

            raise PreviewServiceError(
                PreviewErrorCode.BUSY
            )

        with self._lock:
            self._active += 1

        try:
            try:
                session = self._datasets.get_dataset(
                    dataset_session_id,
                    owner_id=owner_id,
                )

                series = self._datasets.get_series(
                    dataset_session_id,
                    series_handle,
                    owner_id=owner_id,
                )

            except DatasetServiceError:
                raise PreviewServiceError(
                    PreviewErrorCode.SERIES_NOT_FOUND
                ) from None

            if session.get("state") != "ready":
                raise PreviewServiceError(
                    PreviewErrorCode.BUSY
                )

            generation = session["generation"]

            result = build_previews_from_series(
                series.instances,
                self._settings,
                max_dimension=max_dimension,
                max_sampled_slices=max_sampled_slices,
                cancel_event=cancel_event,
                progress_callback=progress_callback,
            )

            # Recheck authorization after potentially long
            # image decoding, before returning patient data.
            try:
                current = self._datasets.get_dataset(
                    dataset_session_id,
                    owner_id=owner_id,
                )

                self._datasets.get_series(
                    dataset_session_id,
                    series_handle,
                    owner_id=owner_id,
                )

            except DatasetServiceError:
                raise PreviewServiceError(
                    PreviewErrorCode.SOURCE_CHANGED
                ) from None

            if (
                current.get("state") != "ready"
                or current.get("generation") != generation
            ):
                raise PreviewServiceError(
                    PreviewErrorCode.SOURCE_CHANGED
                )

            with self._lock:
                self._require_open()
                self._completed += 1

            return result

        except Exception:
            with self._lock:
                self._failed += 1
            raise

        finally:
            with self._lock:
                self._active -= 1

            self._build_gate.release()

    def statistics(self) -> PreviewServiceStatistics:
        with self._lock:
            return PreviewServiceStatistics(
                requests=self._requests,
                completed=self._completed,
                failed=self._failed,
                active=self._active,
                closed=self._closed,
            )

    def close(self) -> None:
        """
        Prevent new preview requests.

        Active worker tasks finish or observe their
        supplied cancellation event.
        """

        with self._lock:
            self._closed = True


# ============================================================
# 10 — SERVICE FACTORY AND SAFE SUMMARY
# ============================================================

def create_preview_service(
    dataset_service: DatasetService,
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
) -> PreviewService:
    return PreviewService(
        dataset_service=dataset_service,
        settings=settings,
    )


def get_preview_service_summary(
    service: PreviewService,
) -> dict:
    return service.statistics().safe_summary()


# ============================================================
# 11 — CAPABILITY REPORT
# ============================================================

def get_preview_capabilities() -> dict:
    return {
        "planes": [
            "axial",
            "coronal",
            "sagittal",
        ],
        "output_dtype": "uint8",
        "preview_only": True,
        "full_volume_required": False,
        "source_slice_decode_required": True,
        "multiframe_supported": False,
        "maximum_dimension": MAX_PREVIEW_DIMENSION,
        "maximum_sampled_slices": MAX_SAMPLED_SLICES,
        "read_only_source": True,
        "output_format": "raw_grayscale_array",
        "http_endpoint_implemented": False,
    }


# ============================================================
# END OF LOW-MEMORY PREVIEW SERVICE
# ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/backend/app/services/series_service.py


### sinus-viewer/backend/app/services/streaming_service.py
```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — SECURE VOLUME STREAMING SERVICE
===============================================

Purpose:
- Plan bounded byte-range requests for 3D volumes.
- Support HTTP single-range byte syntax.
- Stream binary volume data in controlled chunks.
- Recheck series authorization while yielding chunks.
- Support cancellation and clean shutdown.
- Create safe HTTP response headers.
- Avoid copying the entire volume into one response.

Conventions:
    Volume dtype:       float32
    Array order:        Z, Y, X
    Byte order:         little-endian (verified by API layer)
    Range offsets:      byte offsets, not voxel offsets

Security:
- Owner identity must come from trusted authentication.
- Dataset session and series handles are not credentials.
- Every streamed chunk is obtained through VolumeService.
- Do not log Range headers, patient IDs or file paths.
- No raw exception messages are returned.
- Response caching is disabled.
- No original DICOM file is written or modified.

Limitations:
- VolumeService loads/caches a volume before streaming.
- This is not direct disk-to-browser DICOM streaming.
- One HTTP response cannot exceed its configured limit.
- This module prepares streams; FastAPI routes come later.

Python 3.10+
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import threading
from dataclasses import dataclass
from enum import Enum
from typing import Any, Iterator

from ..core.config import AppSettings, get_settings
from .volume_service import (
    VolumeService,
    VolumeServiceError,
)


# ============================================================
# 01 — CONSTANTS
# ============================================================

MIB = 1024 ** 2

DEFAULT_CHUNK_SIZE_KB = 1024
DEFAULT_MAX_RESPONSE_MB = 64
DEFAULT_MAX_CHUNK_MB = 4

MAX_RANGE_HEADER_LENGTH = 128

RANGE_PATTERN = re.compile(
    r"^bytes=(\d*)-(\d*)$",
    re.ASCII,
)

BINARY_MEDIA_TYPE = "application/octet-stream"

VOLUME_FORMAT = "sinus-viewer-f32-zyx-v1"


# ============================================================
# 02 — ERROR CODES
# ============================================================

class StreamingErrorCode(str, Enum):
    INVALID_RANGE = "INVALID_REQUEST"
    RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE = "RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE"
    VOLUME_NOT_FOUND = "SERIES_NOT_FOUND"
    STREAM_CANCELLED = "OPERATION_CANCELLED"
    SERVICE_CLOSED = "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE"
    STREAM_FAILED = "PROCESSING_FAILED"


class StreamingServiceError(RuntimeError):
    """
    Controlled streaming failure.

    Does not include patient-derived information.
    """

    def __init__(self, code: StreamingErrorCode) -> None:
        self.code = code
        super().__init__(code.value)


# ============================================================
# 03 — RANGE AND STREAM MODELS
# ============================================================

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ByteRange:
    """
    Inclusive start/end byte positions.

    Example:
        start=0, end=1023 -> length=1024
    """

    start: int
    end: int
    total_bytes: int

    def __post_init__(self) -> None:
        if (
            self.total_bytes < 1
            or self.start < 0
            or self.end < self.start
            or self.end >= self.total_bytes
        ):
            raise StreamingServiceError(
                StreamingErrorCode.RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE
            )

    @property
    def length(self) -> int:
        return self.end - self.start + 1

    @property
    def content_range(self) -> str:
        return (
            f"bytes {self.start}-{self.end}/"
            f"{self.total_bytes}"
        )


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class StreamPlan:
    dataset_session_id: str
    series_handle: str
    level: int

    byte_range: ByteRange
    chunk_size_bytes: int
    status_code: int
    is_partial: bool

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Excludes internal handles and patient identifiers.
        """

        return {
            "level": self.level,
            "content_length": self.byte_range.length,
            "total_bytes": self.byte_range.total_bytes,
            "chunk_size_bytes": self.chunk_size_bytes,
            "status_code": self.status_code,
            "is_partial": self.is_partial,
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class StreamStatistics:
    plans_created: int
    streams_started: int
    streams_completed: int
    streams_failed: int
    chunks_delivered: int
    bytes_delivered: int
    active_streams: int
    closed: bool

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "plans_created": self.plans_created,
            "streams_started": self.streams_started,
            "streams_completed": self.streams_completed,
            "streams_failed": self.streams_failed,
            "chunks_delivered": self.chunks_delivered,
            "bytes_delivered": self.bytes_delivered,
            "active_streams": self.active_streams,
            "closed": self.closed,
        }


# ============================================================
# 04 — HTTP RANGE PARSING
# ============================================================

def parse_byte_range(
    range_header: str | None,
    total_bytes: int,
    *,
    max_response_bytes: int,
) -> tuple[ByteRange, bool]:
    """
    Parse one HTTP bytes range.

    Supported:
        bytes=0-1023
        bytes=1024-
        bytes=-4096

    Unsupported:
        Multi-range requests
        Non-byte range units
        Malformed ranges

    A missing Range header returns the full response only
    when its size fits within max_response_bytes.
    Otherwise the caller must request a bounded range.

    No raw Range header is included in errors or logs.
    """

    if (
        not isinstance(total_bytes, int)
        or isinstance(total_bytes, bool)
        or total_bytes <= 0
        or not isinstance(max_response_bytes, int)
        or isinstance(max_response_bytes, bool)
        or max_response_bytes <= 0
    ):
        raise StreamingServiceError(
            StreamingErrorCode.INVALID_RANGE
        )

    if range_header is None:
        if total_bytes > max_response_bytes:
            raise StreamingServiceError(
                StreamingErrorCode.RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE
            )

        return (
            ByteRange(
                start=0,
                end=total_bytes - 1,
                total_bytes=total_bytes,
            ),
            False,
        )

    if (
        not isinstance(range_header, str)
        or len(range_header) > MAX_RANGE_HEADER_LENGTH
    ):
        raise StreamingServiceError(
            StreamingErrorCode.INVALID_RANGE
        )

    match = RANGE_PATTERN.fullmatch(
        range_header.strip()
    )

    if match is None:
        raise StreamingServiceError(
            StreamingErrorCode.INVALID_RANGE
        )

    start_text, end_text = match.groups()

    if not start_text and not end_text:
        raise StreamingServiceError(
            StreamingErrorCode.INVALID_RANGE
        )

    if start_text:
        start = int(start_text)

        if start >= total_bytes:
            raise StreamingServiceError(
                StreamingErrorCode.RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE
            )

        if end_text:
            requested_end = int(end_text)

            if requested_end < start:
                raise StreamingServiceError(
                    StreamingErrorCode.RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE
                )

            end = min(
                requested_end,
                total_bytes - 1,
            )

        else:
            end = total_bytes - 1

    else:
        # Suffix range: bytes=-N
        suffix_length = int(end_text)

        if suffix_length == 0:
            raise StreamingServiceError(
                StreamingErrorCode.RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE
            )

        start = max(
            0,
            total_bytes - suffix_length,
        )

        end = total_bytes - 1

    selected = ByteRange(
        start=start,
        end=end,
        total_bytes=total_bytes,
    )

    if selected.length > max_response_bytes:
        raise StreamingServiceError(
            StreamingErrorCode.RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE
        )

    return selected, True


# ============================================================
# 05 — CHUNK ITERATION PLAN
# ============================================================

def iter_chunk_ranges(
    selected: ByteRange,
    chunk_size_bytes: int,
) -> Iterator[tuple[int, int]]:
    """
    Yield (offset, length) for bounded chunk retrieval.

    Does not allocate or read any pixel bytes.
    """

    if (
        not isinstance(chunk_size_bytes, int)
        or isinstance(chunk_size_bytes, bool)
        or chunk_size_bytes < 1
    ):
        raise StreamingServiceError(
            StreamingErrorCode.INVALID_RANGE
        )

    position = selected.start
    last_exclusive = selected.end + 1

    while position < last_exclusive:
        length = min(
            chunk_size_bytes,
            last_exclusive - position,
        )

        yield position, length

        position += length


# ============================================================
# 06 — SAFE HTTP HEADERS
# ============================================================

def build_stream_headers(
    plan: StreamPlan,
) -> dict[str, str]:
    """
    HTTP headers for the eventual authenticated API.

    The API must use these with the same selected byte range
    and response status code.

    Content-Encoding must remain identity for these raw
    byte offsets to be meaningful.
    """

    headers = {
        "Content-Type": BINARY_MEDIA_TYPE,
        "Content-Length": str(
            plan.byte_range.length
        ),
        "Accept-Ranges": "bytes",
        "Cache-Control": "no-store, private",
        "Pragma": "no-cache",
        "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
        "Content-Disposition": "inline",
        "Content-Encoding": "identity",
        "X-Volume-Format": VOLUME_FORMAT,
    }

    if plan.is_partial:
        headers["Content-Range"] = (
            plan.byte_range.content_range
        )

    return headers


def build_unsatisfied_range_headers(
    total_bytes: int,
) -> dict[str, str]:
    """
    Optional headers for HTTP 416.

    The API controls when these may be returned.
    """

    if total_bytes < 0:
        raise StreamingServiceError(
            StreamingErrorCode.INVALID_RANGE
        )

    return {
        "Content-Range": f"bytes */{total_bytes}",
        "Cache-Control": "no-store, private",
        "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
    }


# ============================================================
# 07 — STREAMING SERVICE
# ============================================================

class VolumeStreamingService:
    """
    Process-local bounded volume streamer.

    The underlying VolumeService performs owner/session
    authorization on every read_volume_bytes() call.

    This class does not create HTTP responses directly.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        volume_service: VolumeService,
        settings: AppSettings | None = None,
        *,
        max_response_mb: int = DEFAULT_MAX_RESPONSE_MB,
        max_chunk_mb: int = DEFAULT_MAX_CHUNK_MB,
    ) -> None:
        if not isinstance(volume_service, VolumeService):
            raise TypeError(
                "VolumeService instance is required."
            )

        if (
            not isinstance(max_response_mb, int)
            or isinstance(max_response_mb, bool)
            or max_response_mb < 1
            or not isinstance(max_chunk_mb, int)
            or isinstance(max_chunk_mb, bool)
            or max_chunk_mb < 1
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Invalid streaming limits."
            )

        self._volumes = volume_service

        self._settings = (
            settings if settings is not None
            else get_settings()
        )

        configured_kb = (
            self._settings.performance.streaming.chunk_size_kb
        )

        self._chunk_bytes = min(
            max(1, int(configured_kb) * 1024),
            max_chunk_mb * MIB,
        )

        self._max_response_bytes = max_response_mb * MIB
        self._max_chunk_mb = max_chunk_mb

        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._closed = False

        self._plans_created = 0
        self._streams_started = 0
        self._streams_completed = 0
        self._streams_failed = 0
        self._chunks_delivered = 0
        self._bytes_delivered = 0
        self._active_streams = 0

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # SERVICE STATE
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    def _require_open(self) -> None:
        if self._closed:
            raise StreamingServiceError(
                StreamingErrorCode.SERVICE_CLOSED
            )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # PREPARE STREAM
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    def prepare_stream(
        self,
        dataset_session_id: str,
        series_handle: str,
        *,
        owner_id: str,
        level: int = 0,
        range_header: str | None = None,
    ) -> StreamPlan:
        """
        Verify selected level and prepare an HTTP byte range.

        The trusted owner_id must come from authenticated
        server-side request context.

        Full-volume loading may occur at this step.
        """

        with self._lock:
            self._require_open()

        if (
            not isinstance(level, int)
            or isinstance(level, bool)
            or level < 0
        ):
            raise StreamingServiceError(
                StreamingErrorCode.INVALID_RANGE
            )

        try:
            volume = self._volumes.get_level(
                dataset_session_id,
                series_handle,
                owner_id=owner_id,
                level=level,
            )

        except VolumeServiceError:
            raise StreamingServiceError(
                StreamingErrorCode.VOLUME_NOT_FOUND
            ) from None

        total_bytes = volume.byte_size

        selected, is_partial = parse_byte_range(
            range_header,
            total_bytes,
            max_response_bytes=self._max_response_bytes,
        )

        with self._lock:
            self._require_open()
            self._plans_created += 1

        return StreamPlan(
            dataset_session_id=dataset_session_id,
            series_handle=series_handle,
            level=level,
            byte_range=selected,
            chunk_size_bytes=self._chunk_bytes,
            status_code=206 if is_partial else 200,
            is_partial=is_partial,
        )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # ITERATE PIXEL BYTES
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    def iter_volume_bytes(
        self,
        plan: StreamPlan,
        *,
        owner_id: str,
        cancel_event: threading.Event | None = None,
    ) -> Iterator[bytes]:
        """
        Stream an already planned byte range.

        Each chunk is obtained through the authenticated
        VolumeService.read_volume_bytes() interface.

        No unbounded pixel data is copied to a response.

        If authorization is revoked or the underlying dataset
        changes, the stream fails rather than silently using
        a stale unauthorized volume.
        """

        with self._lock:
            self._require_open()

        completed = False

        with self._lock:
            self._streams_started += 1
            self._active_streams += 1

        try:
            for offset, length in iter_chunk_ranges(
                plan.byte_range,
                plan.chunk_size_bytes,
            ):
                if (
                    cancel_event is not None
                    and cancel_event.is_set()
                ):
                    raise StreamingServiceError(
                        StreamingErrorCode.STREAM_CANCELLED
                    )

                with self._lock:
                    self._require_open()

                try:
                    chunk = self._volumes.read_volume_bytes(
                        plan.dataset_session_id,
                        plan.series_handle,
                        owner_id=owner_id,
                        level=plan.level,
                        offset=offset,
                        length=length,
                        max_range_mb=self._max_chunk_mb,
                    )

                except VolumeServiceError:
                    raise StreamingServiceError(
                        StreamingErrorCode.STREAM_FAILED
                    ) from None

                if len(chunk) != length:
                    raise StreamingServiceError(
                        StreamingErrorCode.STREAM_FAILED
                    )

                with self._lock:
                    self._chunks_delivered += 1
                    self._bytes_delivered += len(chunk)

                yield chunk

            completed = True

        finally:
            with self._lock:
                self._active_streams -= 1

                if completed:
                    self._streams_completed += 1
                else:
                    self._streams_failed += 1

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # SAFE STREAM DESCRIPTOR
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    def describe_stream(
        self,
        plan: StreamPlan,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Non-identifying stream information.

        Intended for diagnostics within authenticated APIs.
        """

        return {
            **plan.safe_summary(),
            "content_type": BINARY_MEDIA_TYPE,
            "encoding": VOLUME_FORMAT,
        }

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # STATISTICS
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    def statistics(self) -> StreamStatistics:
        with self._lock:
            return StreamStatistics(
                plans_created=self._plans_created,
                streams_started=self._streams_started,
                streams_completed=self._streams_completed,
                streams_failed=self._streams_failed,
                chunks_delivered=self._chunks_delivered,
                bytes_delivered=self._bytes_delivered,
                active_streams=self._active_streams,
                closed=self._closed,
            )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # SHUTDOWN
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    def close(self) -> None:
        """
        Stop new stream work.

        Existing iterators observe shutdown at their next
        chunk boundary. No dataset files are touched.
        """

        with self._lock:
            self._closed = True


# ============================================================
# 08 — SERVICE FACTORY
# ============================================================

def create_streaming_service(
    volume_service: VolumeService,
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
) -> VolumeStreamingService:
    """
    Create one streaming service per FastAPI process.

    The application lifespan manager owns shutdown.
    """

    return VolumeStreamingService(
        volume_service=volume_service,
        settings=settings,
    )


# ============================================================
# 09 — SAFE OPERATIONAL SUMMARY
# ============================================================

def get_streaming_service_summary(
    service: VolumeStreamingService,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return service.statistics().safe_summary()


# ============================================================
# END OF VOLUME STREAMING SERVICE
# ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/backend/app/services/volume_service.py
```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — VOLUME MANAGEMENT SERVICE
========================================

Coordinates:
- Dataset session authorization and series lookup.
- Validated DICOM volume loading.
- Memory-aware cache integration.
- Single-flight loading through VolumeCacheService.
- Bounded concurrent full-volume reconstruction.
- Preview/full-resolution level selection.
- Safe technical volume descriptors.
- Bounded in-memory pixel byte-range access.
- Dataset generation checks against stale indexes.
- Graceful shutdown.

Security:
- The API must supply a trusted, authenticated owner_id.
- Client-supplied owner_id values are never trusted.
- Dataset/series handles are NOT authentication tokens.
- Every retrieval rechecks dataset ownership.
- Raw DICOM paths and identifiers are never logged.
- Original source data is strictly read-only.
- No disk writes are performed by this service.

This service is synchronous. FastAPI routes must call
blocking operations through a bounded thread pool.

Python 3.10+
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import threading
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Callable

from ..core.config import AppSettings, get_settings
from ..core.hardware import (
    HardwareSnapshot,
    detect_hardware,
)
from ..dicom.volume_builder import (
    PreparedVolume,
    VolumePackage,
    build_volume_package,
    describe_volume_level,
    describe_volume_package,
    get_volume_byte_view,
)
from .cache_service import (
    CacheServiceError,
    VolumeCacheService,
    create_volume_cache,
)
from .dataset_service import (
    DatasetService,
    DatasetServiceError,
    DatasetSeriesNotFound,
    validate_handle,
    validate_owner_id,
)


# ============================================================
# 01 — CONSTANTS AND TYPES
# ============================================================

MIB = 1024 ** 2

DEFAULT_MAX_CONCURRENT_LOADS = 1
DEFAULT_MAX_BYTE_RANGE_MB = 4

VolumeProgressCallback = Callable[[int, int], None]


# ============================================================
# 02 — CONTROLLED SERVICE EXCEPTIONS
# ============================================================

class VolumeServiceError(RuntimeError):
    """
    Safe error without patient identifiers or file paths.
    """

    public_code = "PROCESSING_FAILED"

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        super().__init__(self.public_code)


class VolumeServiceClosed(VolumeServiceError):
    public_code = "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE"


class VolumeServiceBusy(VolumeServiceError):
    public_code = "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE"


class VolumeNotFound(VolumeServiceError):
    public_code = "SERIES_NOT_FOUND"


class VolumeNotReady(VolumeServiceError):
    public_code = "SERIES_INVALID"


class VolumeLoadFailed(VolumeServiceError):
    public_code = "PROCESSING_FAILED"


class VolumeMemoryExceeded(VolumeServiceError):
    public_code = "OUT_OF_MEMORY"


class VolumeSourceChanged(VolumeServiceError):
    public_code = "SOURCE_CHANGED"


class VolumeInvalidRange(VolumeServiceError):
    public_code = "INVALID_REQUEST"


# ============================================================
# 03 — INTERNAL CONTEXT
# ============================================================

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VolumeContext:
    """
    Authorized lookup snapshot.

    An index generation identifies the dataset version
    from which the series was selected.

    No sensitive patient metadata is stored here.
    """

    dataset_session_id: str
    series_handle: str
    generation: int
    cache_key: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VolumeLevelInfo:
    level: int
    shape_zyx: tuple[int, int, int]
    byte_size: int
    is_preview: bool

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "level": self.level,
            "shape_zyx": list(self.shape_zyx),
            "byte_size": self.byte_size,
            "is_preview": self.is_preview,
        }


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class VolumeServiceStatistics:
    load_requests: int
    completed_loads: int
    failed_loads: int
    active_loads: int
    maximum_concurrent_loads: int
    closed: bool

    def safe_summary(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "load_requests": self.load_requests,
            "completed_loads": self.completed_loads,
            "failed_loads": self.failed_loads,
            "active_loads": self.active_loads,
            "maximum_concurrent_loads": (
                self.maximum_concurrent_loads
            ),
            "closed": self.closed,
        }


# ============================================================
# 04 — CACHE KEY GENERATION
# ============================================================

def make_volume_cache_key(
    dataset_session_id: str,
    series_handle: str,
    generation: int,
) -> str:
    """
    Include dataset generation to prevent using an older
    cached volume after a successful dataset refresh.

    This hash is an internal lookup key, not anonymization.
    """

    validate_handle(dataset_session_id)
    validate_handle(series_handle)

    if (
        not isinstance(generation, int)
        or isinstance(generation, bool)
        or generation < 1
    ):
        raise VolumeInvalidRange()

    value = (
        f"{dataset_session_id}:"
        f"{series_handle}:"
        f"{generation}"
    )

    return "volume_" + hashlib.sha256(
        value.encode("utf-8")
    ).hexdigest()


# ============================================================
# 05 — VOLUME SERVICE
# ============================================================

class VolumeService:
    """
    Process-local volume manager.

    The DatasetService controls dataset ownership.
    The VolumeCacheService manages cached volume packages.

    The semaphore bounds simultaneous full-volume loads.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        dataset_service: DatasetService,
        cache_service: VolumeCacheService | None = None,
        settings: AppSettings | None = None,
        *,
        max_concurrent_loads: int | None = None,
    ) -> None:

        if not isinstance(dataset_service, DatasetService):
            raise TypeError(
                "A DatasetService instance is required."
            )

        self._settings = (
            settings if settings is not None
            else get_settings()
        )

        configured_limit = (
            self._settings.server.limits
            .max_concurrent_volume_loads
        )

        limit = (
            configured_limit
            if max_concurrent_loads is None
            else max_concurrent_loads
        )

        if (
            not isinstance(limit, int)
            or isinstance(limit, bool)
            or limit < 1
        ):
            raise ValueError(
                "Concurrent load limit must be positive."
            )

        self._datasets = dataset_service

        self._owns_cache = cache_service is None

        self._cache = (
            cache_service
            if cache_service is not None
            else create_volume_cache(self._settings)
        )

        self._maximum_loads = limit

        self._load_semaphore = threading.BoundedSemaphore(
            value=limit
        )

        self._lock = threading.RLock()
        self._closed = False

        self._load_requests = 0
        self._completed_loads = 0
        self._failed_loads = 0
        self._active_loads = 0

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # SERVICE STATE
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    def _require_open(self) -> None:
        if self._closed:
            raise VolumeServiceClosed()

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # AUTHORIZED SERIES LOOKUP
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    def _resolve_context(
        self,
        dataset_session_id: str,
        series_handle: str,
        owner_id: str,
    ) -> VolumeContext:
        """
        Obtain a trusted dataset generation and verify that
        the series belongs to this owner's dataset session.

        The dataset may change concurrently, so generation
        is checked again after loading.
        """

        validate_owner_id(owner_id)
        validate_handle(dataset_session_id)
        validate_handle(series_handle)

        with self._lock:
            self._require_open()

        try:
            dataset_info = self._datasets.get_dataset(
                dataset_session_id,
                owner_id=owner_id,
            )

            self._datasets.get_series(
                dataset_session_id,
                series_handle,
                owner_id=owner_id,
            )

        except DatasetServiceError:
            raise VolumeNotFound() from None

        if dataset_info.get("state") != "ready":
            raise VolumeServiceBusy()

        generation = dataset_info["generation"]

        return VolumeContext(
            dataset_session_id=dataset_session_id,
            series_handle=series_handle,
            generation=generation,
            cache_key=make_volume_cache_key(
                dataset_session_id,
                series_handle,
                generation,
            ),
        )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # GENERATION / ACCESS REVALIDATION
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    def _verify_context(
        self,
        context: VolumeContext,
        owner_id: str,
    ) -> None:
        """
        Reject volumes whose dataset session is no longer
        authorized or whose index generation changed.
        """

        with self._lock:
            self._require_open()

        try:
            current = self._datasets.get_dataset(
                context.dataset_session_id,
                owner_id=owner_id,
            )

            self._datasets.get_series(
                context.dataset_session_id,
                context.series_handle,
                owner_id=owner_id,
            )

        except DatasetServiceError:
            raise VolumeNotFound() from None

        if current.get("state") != "ready":
            raise VolumeServiceBusy()

        if current.get("generation") != context.generation:
            raise VolumeSourceChanged()

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # LOAD ONE SERIES
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    def load_volume(
        self,
        dataset_session_id: str,
        series_handle: str,
        *,
        owner_id: str,
        hardware_snapshot: HardwareSnapshot | None = None,
        cancel_event: threading.Event | None = None,
        progress_callback: VolumeProgressCallback | None = None,
    ) -> VolumePackage:
        """
        Retrieve or build a volume for an authorized series.

        No raw DICOM paths or identifiers are returned.

        A full-volume reconstruction runs only after a
        bounded-load semaphore has been acquired.
        """

        context = self._resolve_context(
            dataset_session_id,
            series_handle,
            owner_id,
        )

        with self._lock:
            self._require_open()
            self._load_requests += 1

        def loader() -> VolumePackage:
            acquired = self._load_semaphore.acquire(
                timeout=self._settings.server.request_timeout_seconds
            )

            if not acquired:
                raise VolumeServiceBusy()

            with self._lock:
                self._active_loads += 1

            try:
                self._verify_context(context, owner_id)

                series = self._datasets.get_series(
                    dataset_session_id,
                    series_handle,
                    owner_id=owner_id,
                )

                if (
                    series.validation is None
                    or not series.validation.volume_ready
                ):
                    raise VolumeNotReady()

                snapshot = (
                    hardware_snapshot
                    if hardware_snapshot is not None
                    else detect_hardware(self._settings)
                )

                package = build_volume_package(
                    series.instances,
                    self._settings,
                    hardware_snapshot=snapshot,
                    cancel_event=cancel_event,
                    progress_callback=progress_callback,
                )

                self._verify_context(context, owner_id)

                return package

            finally:
                with self._lock:
                    self._active_loads -= 1

                self._load_semaphore.release()

        try:
            package = self._cache.get_or_load(
                context.cache_key,
                loader,
            )

            # Cached results must also pass authorization
            # and generation checks before being returned.
            self._verify_context(context, owner_id)

            with self._lock:
                self._completed_loads += 1

            return package

        except VolumeServiceError:
            with self._lock:
                self._failed_loads += 1
            raise

        except CacheServiceError:
            with self._lock:
                self._failed_loads += 1
            raise VolumeLoadFailed() from None

        except DatasetServiceError:
            with self._lock:
                self._failed_loads += 1
            raise VolumeNotFound() from None

        except Exception:
            with self._lock:
                self._failed_loads += 1
            raise VolumeLoadFailed() from None

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # GET ONE PREPARED LEVEL
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    def get_level(
        self,
        dataset_session_id: str,
        series_handle: str,
        *,
        owner_id: str,
        level: int = 0,
    ) -> PreparedVolume:
        """
        Get one full-resolution or preview level.

        Level 0 = full-resolution volume.
        Positive levels = preview levels, if generated.
        """

        if (
            not isinstance(level, int)
            or isinstance(level, bool)
            or level < 0
        ):
            raise VolumeInvalidRange()

        package = self.load_volume(
            dataset_session_id,
            series_handle,
            owner_id=owner_id,
        )

        result = package.get_level(level)

        if result is None:
            raise VolumeNotFound()

        return result

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # SAFE VOLUME DESCRIPTORS
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    def describe_volume(
        self,
        dataset_session_id: str,
        series_handle: str,
        *,
        owner_id: str,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """
        Return volume metadata without transmitting pixels,
        source paths, patient names or DICOM identifiers.
        """

        package = self.load_volume(
            dataset_session_id,
            series_handle,
            owner_id=owner_id,
        )

        return describe_volume_package(package)

    def describe_level(
        self,
        dataset_session_id: str,
        series_handle: str,
        *,
        owner_id: str,
        level: int = 0,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:

        volume = self.get_level(
            dataset_session_id,
            series_handle,
            owner_id=owner_id,
            level=level,
        )

        return describe_volume_level(volume)

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # BOUNDED PIXEL BYTE-RANGE ACCESS
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    def read_volume_bytes(
        self,
        dataset_session_id: str,
        series_handle: str,
        *,
        owner_id: str,
        level: int,
        offset: int,
        length: int,
        max_range_mb: int = DEFAULT_MAX_BYTE_RANGE_MB,
    ) -> bytes:
        """
        Return a bounded slice of a prepared volume's bytes.

        This is NOT an HTTP endpoint. The eventual API must:
        - Enforce authentication, authorization and CSRF
          protections where applicable.
        - Apply request-rate and concurrency limits.
        - Disable response caching.
        - Avoid logging raw URLs or query parameters.

        Byte offset/length operate on contiguous float32
        volume data in Z,Y,X order.

        No DICOM file writing or temporary-file creation.
        """

        for value in (offset, length, max_range_mb):
            if (
                not isinstance(value, int)
                or isinstance(value, bool)
            ):
                raise VolumeInvalidRange()

        if (
            offset < 0
            or length < 1
            or max_range_mb < 1
            or length > max_range_mb * MIB
        ):
            raise VolumeInvalidRange()

        volume = self.get_level(
            dataset_session_id,
            series_handle,
            owner_id=owner_id,
            level=level,
        )

        byte_view = get_volume_byte_view(volume)

        total = len(byte_view)

        if offset > total or length > total - offset:
            raise VolumeInvalidRange()

        # Independent bounded bytes copy; it does not expose
        # a mutable NumPy array to the API layer.
        result = bytes(
            byte_view[offset:offset + length]
        )

        return result

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # CACHE INVALIDATION
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    def invalidate_series(
        self,
        dataset_session_id: str,
        series_handle: str,
        *,
        owner_id: str,
    ) -> bool:
        """
        Invalidate the current generation's cached volume.

        Does not modify DICOM files or filesystem storage.
        """

        context = self._resolve_context(
            dataset_session_id,
            series_handle,
            owner_id,
        )

        try:
            return self._cache.invalidate(
                context.cache_key
            )

        except CacheServiceError:
            raise VolumeLoadFailed() from None

    def clear_cached_volumes(self) -> int:
        """
        Internal administrative operation.

        Do not expose without server-side authorization.
        """

        with self._lock:
            self._require_open()

        return self._cache.clear()

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # SERVICE STATISTICS
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    def statistics(self) -> VolumeServiceStatistics:
        with self._lock:
            return VolumeServiceStatistics(
                load_requests=self._load_requests,
                completed_loads=self._completed_loads,
                failed_loads=self._failed_loads,
                active_loads=self._active_loads,
                maximum_concurrent_loads=self._maximum_loads,
                closed=self._closed,
            )

    # --------------------------------------------------------
    # SHUTDOWN
    # --------------------------------------------------------

    def close(self) -> None:
        """
        Stop accepting new service operations.

        The application lifespan manager must also drain its
        worker pool before terminating the process.
        """

        with self._lock:
            if self._closed:
                return

            self._closed = True

        if self._owns_cache:
            self._cache.close()


# ============================================================
# 06 — SERVICE FACTORY
# ============================================================

def create_volume_service(
    dataset_service: DatasetService,
    cache_service: VolumeCacheService | None = None,
    settings: AppSettings | None = None,
) -> VolumeService:
    """
    Create one process-local volume service.

    Intended for FastAPI lifespan initialization.
    """

    return VolumeService(
        dataset_service=dataset_service,
        cache_service=cache_service,
        settings=settings,
    )


# ============================================================
# 07 — SAFE SERVICE SUMMARY
# ============================================================

def get_volume_service_summary(
    service: VolumeService,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Non-identifying operational service statistics.
    """

    return service.statistics().safe_summary()


# ============================================================
# END OF VOLUME MANAGEMENT SERVICE
# ============================================================
```
