### sinus-viewer/frontend/src/hooks/useDataset.ts
```
/**
 * SINUS VIEWER — REACT DATASET HOOK
 * =================================
 *
 * React 18 integration for FrontendDatasetService.
 *
 * Features:
 * - useSyncExternalStore for consistent subscriptions.
 * - One stable service instance per hook owner.
 * - Dataset selection and refresh actions.
 * - Patient and series browser actions.
 * - Series validation and viewer-ready selection.
 * - Safe state reset and logical cancellation.
 * - Cleanup on component unmount.
 * - Explicit authentication-session reset support.
 *
 * SECURITY:
 * - No original DICOM file operations.
 * - No browser persistent storage.
 * - No logging of patient names, paths or UIDs.
 * - Backend remains authoritative for authorization.
 * - Only opaque handles are retained in service state.
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 * File 46 currently implements logical cancellation.
 * Pending HTTP requests may complete, but their
 * responses cannot update state after invalidation.
 *
 * The returned actions are intended for user-driven
 * controls; do not call them during React render.
 */

import {
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useState,
  useSyncExternalStore,
} from "react";

import {
  createDatasetService,
  DatasetBrowserError,
  FrontendDatasetService,
  type DatasetBrowserState,
} from "../services/datasetService";


// ============================================================
// 01 — PUBLIC TYPES
// ============================================================

export interface DatasetViewerSelection {
  readonly datasetSessionId: string;
  readonly seriesHandle: string;
}

export interface DatasetActions {
  readonly listDatasets: () => Promise<void>;

  readonly selectDataset: (
    datasetPath: string,
  ) => Promise<void>;

  readonly selectExistingDataset: (
    datasetSessionId: string,
  ) => void;

  readonly reloadSelectedDataset: () => Promise<void>;

  readonly refreshDataset: () => Promise<void>;

  readonly closeDataset: (
    datasetSessionId?: string,
  ) => Promise<void>;

  readonly loadPatients: () => Promise<void>;

  readonly selectPatient: (
    patientHandle: string,
  ) => Promise<void>;

  readonly selectSeries: (
    seriesHandle: string,
  ) => Promise<void>;

  readonly cancelPending: () => void;

  readonly reset: () => void;

  readonly getViewerSelection: (
  ) => DatasetViewerSelection | null;
}

export interface UseDatasetResult {
  readonly state: DatasetBrowserState;
  readonly actions: DatasetActions;
  readonly service: FrontendDatasetService;

  readonly isLoading: boolean;
  readonly isReady: boolean;
  readonly requiresAuthentication: boolean;

  readonly hasSelectedDataset: boolean;
  readonly hasSelectedPatient: boolean;
  readonly hasSelectedSeries: boolean;

  readonly viewerSelection: DatasetViewerSelection | null;
}


// ============================================================
// 02 — SERVICE OWNERSHIP
// ============================================================

/**
 * Create a stable dataset service for this hook instance.
 *
 * useState initializer is evaluated once for a committed
 * React hook instance. React StrictMode may perform
 * development checks; constructing a service has no
 * network or filesystem side effects.
 */
function useOwnedDatasetService(): FrontendDatasetService {
  const [service] = useState<FrontendDatasetService>(
    () => createDatasetService(),
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      /**
       * Invalidate pending responses and clear dataset
       * state when the owner unmounts.
       *
       * We intentionally avoid dispose() in this cleanup:
       * React 18 StrictMode replays effect setup/cleanup
       * during development. Disposing here would leave
       * the same service permanently unusable after replay.
       *
       * Reset is idempotent, so StrictMode replay is safe.
       *
       * When this hook owner is actually removed, React
       * releases its reference to the service. Any still
       * running request may retain it temporarily until
       * that request settles.
       */
      service.reset();
    };
  }, [service]);

  return service;
}


// ============================================================
// 03 — EXTERNAL STORE SUBSCRIPTION
// ============================================================

/**
 * Subscribe to an existing service.
 *
 * Use this when a parent component or context owns the
 * service lifetime, so multiple child components can
 * observe exactly the same dataset browser state.
 *
 * This hook does not reset or dispose external services.
 */
export function useDatasetSnapshot(
  service: FrontendDatasetService,
): DatasetBrowserState {
  return useSyncExternalStore(
    service.subscribe,
    service.getSnapshot,
    service.getSnapshot,
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 04 — SERVICE ACTION ADAPTER
// ============================================================

/**
 * Stable React action wrappers around the service methods.
 *
 * Promise rejections remain observable to the caller for
 * action-specific UI feedback. Synchronous invalid input
 * errors also propagate to the caller.
 */
export function useDatasetActions(
  service: FrontendDatasetService,
): DatasetActions {
  const listDatasets = useCallback(
    (): Promise<void> => service.listDatasets(),
    [service],
  );

  const selectDataset = useCallback(
    (datasetPath: string): Promise<void> =>
      service.selectDataset(datasetPath),
    [service],
  );

  const selectExistingDataset = useCallback(
    (datasetSessionId: string): void => {
      service.selectExistingDataset(datasetSessionId);
    },
    [service],
  );

  const reloadSelectedDataset = useCallback(
    (): Promise<void> =>
      service.reloadSelectedDataset(),
    [service],
  );

  const refreshDataset = useCallback(
    (): Promise<void> => service.refreshDataset(),
    [service],
  );

  const closeDataset = useCallback(
    (datasetSessionId?: string): Promise<void> =>
      service.closeDataset(datasetSessionId),
    [service],
  );

  const loadPatients = useCallback(
    (): Promise<void> => service.loadPatients(),
    [service],
  );

  const selectPatient = useCallback(
    (patientHandle: string): Promise<void> =>
      service.selectPatient(patientHandle),
    [service],
  );

  const selectSeries = useCallback(
    (seriesHandle: string): Promise<void> =>
      service.selectSeries(seriesHandle),
    [service],
  );

  const cancelPending = useCallback(
    (): void => service.cancelPending(),
    [service],
  );

  const reset = useCallback(
    (): void => service.reset(),
    [service],
  );

  const getViewerSelection = useCallback(
    (): DatasetViewerSelection | null =>
      service.getViewerSelection(),
    [service],
  );

  return {
    listDatasets,
    selectDataset,
    selectExistingDataset,
    reloadSelectedDataset,
    refreshDataset,
    closeDataset,
    loadPatients,
    selectPatient,
    selectSeries,
    cancelPending,
    reset,
    getViewerSelection,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 05 — MAIN REACT DATASET HOOK
// ============================================================

/**
 * Self-contained dataset browser hook.
 *
 * Each invocation owns a separate service.
 * Use it once at the dataset-browser container level,
 * not independently in every row or child component.
 *
 * If multiple components must share dataset state,
 * create the service in their common owner and pass
 * it to useDatasetSnapshot() and useDatasetActions().
 */
export function useDataset(): UseDatasetResult {
  const service = useOwnedDatasetService();

  const state = useDatasetSnapshot(service);
  const actions = useDatasetActions(service);

  const isLoading = state.status === "loading";

  const isReady = state.status === "ready";

  const requiresAuthentication =
    state.status === "authentication-required";

  const hasSelectedDataset =
    state.selectedDataset !== null;

  const hasSelectedPatient =
    state.selectedPatientHandle !== null;

  const hasSelectedSeries =
    state.selectedSeriesHandle !== null;

  /**
   * Determine viewer readiness from the current
   * subscribed snapshot, not from a cached stale
   * selection object.
   */
  const selectedSeries = state.selectedSeriesHandle;
  const selectedDataset = state.selectedDataset;
  const validation = state.validation;

  const viewerSelection: DatasetViewerSelection | null =
    selectedDataset &&
    selectedSeries &&
    validation &&
    validation.series_handle === selectedSeries &&
    validation.volume_ready
      ? {
          datasetSessionId:
            selectedDataset.dataset_session_id,
          seriesHandle: selectedSeries,
        }
      : null;

  return {
    state,
    actions,
    service,
    isLoading,
    isReady,
    requiresAuthentication,
    hasSelectedDataset,
    hasSelectedPatient,
    hasSelectedSeries,
    viewerSelection,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 06 — SAFE ACTION ERROR CLASSIFICATION
// ============================================================

/**
 * Optional helper for UI handlers.
 *
 * Does not display raw API errors or internal file paths.
 * Errors already sanitized by DatasetBrowserError
 * retain their safe human-readable message.
 */
export function getDatasetActionError(
  error: unknown,
): string {
  if (error instanceof DatasetBrowserError) {
    return error.message;
  }

  return "The dataset operation could not be completed.";
}


// ============================================================
// 07 — HOOK CAPABILITIES
// ============================================================

export function getDatasetHookCapabilities() {
  return {
    reactExternalStore: true,
    stablePerOwnerService: true,
    snapshotSubscriptions: true,
    datasetSelection: true,
    patientBrowsing: true,
    seriesBrowsing: true,
    seriesValidation: true,
    staleResponseProtection: true,
    logicalCancellation: true,
    transportCancellation: false,
    persistentBrowserStorage: false,
    modifiesSourceDataset: false,
  } as const;
}


// ============================================================
// END OF SINUS VIEWER REACT DATASET HOOK
// ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/frontend/src/hooks/useMPR.ts
```
/**
 * SINUS VIEWER — REACT MPR CONTROLLER
 * ===================================
 *
 * React state management for three patient-aligned
 * multiplanar reconstruction viewports.
 *
 * Features:
 * - Axial, coronal and sagittal viewport state.
 * - Shared crosshair in DICOM LPS millimetres.
 * - Zoom around a screen-space anchor.
 * - Physical panning.
 * - Slice navigation and scrolling.
 * - Screen-to-world crosshair placement.
 * - Synchronized slice positions.
 * - Viewport resizing and fit-to-volume.
 * - Sampling-plane generation for File 51.
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 * The current functions manage geometry and state.
 * Actual image resampling and canvas rendering
 * are performed by later components.
 *
 * SECURITY:
 * - No patient metadata or source paths are stored.
 * - No network requests or persistent storage.
 * - No original DICOM files are accessed or modified.
 *
 * CLINICAL LIMITATION:
 * Rendering orientation and image measurement accuracy
 * require separate validation using reference datasets.
 */

import {
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useState,
} from "react";

import type {
  PreviewPlane,
  VolumeGeometry,
} from "../types/api";

import {
  assertVolumeGeometry,
  clampVoxelToVolume,
  voxelToWorldLPS,
  worldLPSToVoxel,
  type VoxelPoint,
  type WorldPointLPS,
} from "../utils/geometry";

import {
  alignMPRSliceToWorld,
  buildMPRSamplingPlane,
  centreMPRAtWorld,
  createMPRViewport,
  getMPRSliceStepMm,
  mprScreenToWorldLPS,
  moveMPRSliceMm,
  panMPRViewport,
  projectMPRCrosshair,
  resizeMPRViewport,
  resetMPRViewport,
  zoomMPRViewport,
  type MPRCrosshairProjection,
  type MPRScreenPoint,
  type MPRViewportSize,
  type MPRViewportState,
} from "../utils/mpr";

import type {
  MPRPlane,
} from "../utils/volumeSampling";


// ============================================================
// 01 — CONSTANTS
// ============================================================

export const MPR_PLANES = [
  "axial",
  "coronal",
  "sagittal",
] as const;

export const DEFAULT_MPR_VIEWPORT_SIZE = {
  width: 512,
  height: 512,
} as const satisfies MPRViewportSize;

export const DEFAULT_MPR_ZOOM_FACTOR = 1.2;


// ============================================================
// 02 — PUBLIC TYPES
// ============================================================

export type MPRViewportMap = Readonly<
  Record<PreviewPlane, MPRViewportState>
>;

export type MPRSamplingPlaneMap = Readonly<
  Record<PreviewPlane, MPRPlane>
>;

export type MPRCrosshairProjectionMap = Readonly<
  Record<PreviewPlane, MPRCrosshairProjection>
>;

export interface MPRInitialSizes {
  readonly axial?: MPRViewportSize;
  readonly coronal?: MPRViewportSize;
  readonly sagittal?: MPRViewportSize;
}

export interface MPRControllerState {
  readonly viewports: MPRViewportMap;
  readonly crosshairWorld: WorldPointLPS;
  readonly activePlane: PreviewPlane;
  readonly revision: number;
}

export interface MPRControllerActions {
  readonly setActivePlane: (
    plane: PreviewPlane,
  ) => void;

  readonly setCrosshairWorld: (
    world: WorldPointLPS,
  ) => void;

  readonly setCrosshairVoxel: (
    voxel: VoxelPoint,
  ) => void;

  readonly setCrosshairFromScreen: (
    plane: PreviewPlane,
    point: MPRScreenPoint,
  ) => void;

  readonly scrollSlice: (
    plane: PreviewPlane,
    steps: number,
  ) => void;

  readonly moveSliceMm: (
    plane: PreviewPlane,
    distanceMm: number,
  ) => void;

  readonly pan: (
    plane: PreviewPlane,
    deltaX: number,
    deltaY: number,
  ) => void;

  readonly zoom: (
    plane: PreviewPlane,
    factor: number,
    anchor?: MPRScreenPoint,
  ) => void;

  readonly resize: (
    plane: PreviewPlane,
    size: MPRViewportSize,
  ) => void;

  readonly fit: (
    plane?: PreviewPlane,
  ) => void;

  readonly centerOnCrosshair: (
    plane?: PreviewPlane,
  ) => void;

  readonly reset: () => void;
}

export interface UseMPRResult {
  readonly state: MPRControllerState;
  readonly actions: MPRControllerActions;

  readonly axial: MPRViewportState;
  readonly coronal: MPRViewportState;
  readonly sagittal: MPRViewportState;

  readonly samplingPlanes: MPRSamplingPlaneMap;
  readonly crosshairProjections: MPRCrosshairProjectionMap;

  readonly crosshairWorld: WorldPointLPS;
  readonly crosshairVoxel: VoxelPoint;
  readonly activePlane: PreviewPlane;

  readonly ready: true;
}


// ============================================================
// 03 — VALIDATION
// ============================================================

function assertPlane(
  plane: PreviewPlane,
): void {
  if (
    plane !== "axial" &&
    plane !== "coronal" &&
    plane !== "sagittal"
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Invalid MPR plane selection.",
    );
  }
}

function assertWorld(
  world: WorldPointLPS,
): void {
  if (
    !world ||
    !Number.isFinite(world.x) ||
    !Number.isFinite(world.y) ||
    !Number.isFinite(world.z)
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Invalid physical crosshair position.",
    );
  }
}

function assertFiniteNumber(
  value: number,
): void {
  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {
    throw new Error(
      "Invalid MPR navigation value.",
    );
  }
}

function isViewportSize(
  value: MPRViewportSize,
): boolean {
  return (
    Number.isSafeInteger(value.width) &&
    Number.isSafeInteger(value.height) &&
    value.width > 0 &&
    value.height > 0 &&
    value.width <= 2048 &&
    value.height <= 2048 &&
    value.width * value.height <= 4194304
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 04 — INITIALIZATION
// ============================================================

function resolveSize(
  sizes: MPRInitialSizes,
  plane: PreviewPlane,
): MPRViewportSize {
  const size =
    sizes[plane] ?? DEFAULT_MPR_VIEWPORT_SIZE;

  if (!isViewportSize(size)) {
    throw new Error(
      "Invalid initial MPR viewport size.",
    );
  }

  return {
    width: size.width,
    height: size.height,
  };
}

function createInitialState(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
  sizes: MPRInitialSizes,
): MPRControllerState {
  assertVolumeGeometry(geometry);

  const axial = createMPRViewport(
    geometry,
    "axial",
    resolveSize(sizes, "axial"),
  );

  const coronal = createMPRViewport(
    geometry,
    "coronal",
    resolveSize(sizes, "coronal"),
  );

  const sagittal = createMPRViewport(
    geometry,
    "sagittal",
    resolveSize(sizes, "sagittal"),
  );

  return {
    viewports: {
      axial,
      coronal,
      sagittal,
    },
    crosshairWorld: {
      ...axial.centerLPS,
    },
    activePlane: "axial",
    revision: 0,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 05 — CROSSHAIR BOUNDS
// ============================================================

function boundWorldToVolume(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
  world: WorldPointLPS,
): WorldPointLPS {
  assertWorld(world);

  const voxel = worldLPSToVoxel(
    geometry,
    world,
  );

  const bounded = clampVoxelToVolume(
    geometry,
    voxel,
  );

  return voxelToWorldLPS(
    geometry,
    bounded,
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 06 — SYNCHRONIZE THREE SLICES
// ============================================================

/**
 * Shift each viewport only along its anatomical normal,
 * retaining its in-plane pan and zoom.
 *
 * This preserves independent camera navigation while
 * synchronizing slice depth to one 3D crosshair.
 */
function synchronizeSlices(
  viewports: MPRViewportMap,
  crosshairWorld: WorldPointLPS,
): MPRViewportMap {
  return {
    axial: alignMPRSliceToWorld(
      viewports.axial,
      crosshairWorld,
    ),
    coronal: alignMPRSliceToWorld(
      viewports.coronal,
      crosshairWorld,
    ),
    sagittal: alignMPRSliceToWorld(
      viewports.sagittal,
      crosshairWorld,
    ),
  };
}

function updateCrosshair(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
  previous: MPRControllerState,
  world: WorldPointLPS,
  activePlane?: PreviewPlane,
): MPRControllerState {
  const crosshairWorld = boundWorldToVolume(
    geometry,
    world,
  );

  return {
    ...previous,
    crosshairWorld,
    viewports: synchronizeSlices(
      previous.viewports,
      crosshairWorld,
    ),
    activePlane:
      activePlane ?? previous.activePlane,
    revision: previous.revision + 1,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 07 — HOOK
// ============================================================

/**
 * Use one instance for all three MPR views.
 *
 * Pass the corresponding viewport and sampling plane
 * to child rendering components.
 *
 * A changed geometry reference reinitializes the
 * controller. Keep the geometry object stable while
 * navigating a single loaded volume.
 */
export function useMPR(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
  initialSizes: MPRInitialSizes = {},
): UseMPRResult {
  const [state, setState] =
    useState<MPRControllerState>(
      () => createInitialState(
        geometry,
        initialSizes,
      ),
    );

  /**
   * Preserve latest viewport dimensions when changing
   * volumes, but initialize the new volume's physical
   * centre, scale and crosshair.
   *
   * The effect intentionally does not depend on
   * initialSizes: sizes are initial defaults only.
   */
  useEffect(() => {
    setState((previous) => {
      const sizes: MPRInitialSizes = {
        axial: previous.viewports.axial.size,
        coronal: previous.viewports.coronal.size,
        sagittal: previous.viewports.sagittal.size,
      };

      const fresh = createInitialState(
        geometry,
        sizes,
      );

      return {
        ...fresh,
        revision: previous.revision + 1,
      };
    });
  }, [geometry]);

  // ==========================================================
  // 08 — ACTIVE VIEW
  // ==========================================================

  const setActivePlane = useCallback(
    (plane: PreviewPlane): void => {
      assertPlane(plane);

      setState((previous) => (
        previous.activePlane === plane
          ? previous
          : {
              ...previous,
              activePlane: plane,
              revision: previous.revision + 1,
            }
      ));
    },
    [],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 09 — CROSSHAIR WORLD POSITION
  // ==========================================================

  const setCrosshairWorld = useCallback(
    (world: WorldPointLPS): void => {
      const bounded = boundWorldToVolume(
        geometry,
        world,
      );

      setState((previous) =>
        updateCrosshair(
          geometry,
          previous,
          bounded,
        ),
      );
    },
    [geometry],
  );

  const setCrosshairVoxel = useCallback(
    (voxel: VoxelPoint): void => {
      const bounded = clampVoxelToVolume(
        geometry,
        voxel,
      );

      setCrosshairWorld(
        voxelToWorldLPS(
          geometry,
          bounded,
        ),
      );
    },
    [geometry, setCrosshairWorld],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 10 — CROSSHAIR FROM MOUSE POSITION
  // ==========================================================

  const setCrosshairFromScreen = useCallback(
    (
      plane: PreviewPlane,
      point: MPRScreenPoint,
    ): void => {
      assertPlane(plane);

      setState((previous) => {
        const viewport =
          previous.viewports[plane];

        const world = mprScreenToWorldLPS(
          viewport,
          point,
        );

        return updateCrosshair(
          geometry,
          previous,
          world,
          plane,
        );
      });
    },
    [geometry],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 11 — SLICE SCROLLING
  // ==========================================================

  const moveSliceMm = useCallback(
    (
      plane: PreviewPlane,
      distanceMm: number,
    ): void => {
      assertPlane(plane);
      assertFiniteNumber(distanceMm);

      setState((previous) => {
        const moved = moveMPRSliceMm(
          previous.viewports[plane],
          distanceMm,
        );

        /**
         * Move the 3D crosshair along the same plane
         * normal by the requested physical distance.
         * The final point is bounded by volume geometry.
         */
        const currentCrosshair =
          previous.crosshairWorld;

        const original = mprScreenToWorldLPS(
          previous.viewports[plane],
          {
            x: (
              previous.viewports[plane].size.width - 1
            ) / 2,
            y: (
              previous.viewports[plane].size.height - 1
            ) / 2,
          },
        );

        const shifted = mprScreenToWorldLPS(
          moved,
          {
            x: (
              moved.size.width - 1
            ) / 2,
            y: (
              moved.size.height - 1
            ) / 2,
          },
        );

        const world: WorldPointLPS = {
          x: currentCrosshair.x +
            shifted.x - original.x,
          y: currentCrosshair.y +
            shifted.y - original.y,
          z: currentCrosshair.z +
            shifted.z - original.z,
        };

        return updateCrosshair(
          geometry,
          previous,
          world,
          plane,
        );
      });
    },
    [geometry],
  );

  const scrollSlice = useCallback(
    (
      plane: PreviewPlane,
      steps: number,
    ): void => {
      assertPlane(plane);

      if (!Number.isSafeInteger(steps)) {
        throw new Error(
          "Slice steps must be integers.",
        );
      }

      const spacing = getMPRSliceStepMm(
        geometry,
        plane,
      );

      moveSliceMm(
        plane,
        steps * spacing,
      );
    },
    [geometry, moveSliceMm],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 12 — PAN
  // ==========================================================

  const pan = useCallback(
    (
      plane: PreviewPlane,
      deltaX: number,
      deltaY: number,
    ): void => {
      assertPlane(plane);
      assertFiniteNumber(deltaX);
      assertFiniteNumber(deltaY);

      setState((previous) => ({
        ...previous,
        viewports: {
          ...previous.viewports,
          [plane]: panMPRViewport(
            previous.viewports[plane],
            deltaX,
            deltaY,
          ),
        },
        activePlane: plane,
        revision: previous.revision + 1,
      }));
    },
    [],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 13 — ZOOM
  // ==========================================================

  const zoom = useCallback(
    (
      plane: PreviewPlane,
      factor: number,
      anchor?: MPRScreenPoint,
    ): void => {
      assertPlane(plane);
      assertFiniteNumber(factor);

      setState((previous) => ({
        ...previous,
        viewports: {
          ...previous.viewports,
          [plane]: zoomMPRViewport(
            previous.viewports[plane],
            factor,
            anchor,
          ),
        },
        activePlane: plane,
        revision: previous.revision + 1,
      }));
    },
    [],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 14 — RESIZE
  // ==========================================================

  const resize = useCallback(
    (
      plane: PreviewPlane,
      size: MPRViewportSize,
    ): void => {
      assertPlane(plane);

      if (!isViewportSize(size)) {
        throw new Error(
          "Invalid MPR viewport size.",
        );
      }

      setState((previous) => {
        const current =
          previous.viewports[plane];

        if (
          current.size.width === size.width &&
          current.size.height === size.height
        ) {
          return previous;
        }

        return {
          ...previous,
          viewports: {
            ...previous.viewports,
            [plane]: resizeMPRViewport(
              current,
              size,
            ),
          },
          revision: previous.revision + 1,
        };
      });
    },
    [],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 15 — FIT TO VOLUME
  // ==========================================================

  const fit = useCallback(
    (plane?: PreviewPlane): void => {
      if (plane !== undefined) {
        assertPlane(plane);
      }

      setState((previous) => {
        const next: Record<
          PreviewPlane,
          MPRViewportState
        > = {
          ...previous.viewports,
        };

        for (const target of MPR_PLANES) {
          if (
            plane !== undefined &&
            target !== plane
          ) {
            continue;
          }

          const reset = resetMPRViewport(
            geometry,
            previous.viewports[target],
          );

          next[target] = alignMPRSliceToWorld(
            reset,
            previous.crosshairWorld,
          );
        }

        return {
          ...previous,
          viewports: next,
          revision: previous.revision + 1,
        };
      });
    },
    [geometry],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 16 — CENTRE ON CROSSHAIR
  // ==========================================================

  const centerOnCrosshair = useCallback(
    (plane?: PreviewPlane): void => {
      if (plane !== undefined) {
        assertPlane(plane);
      }

      setState((previous) => {
        const next: Record<
          PreviewPlane,
          MPRViewportState
        > = {
          ...previous.viewports,
        };

        for (const target of MPR_PLANES) {
          if (
            plane !== undefined &&
            target !== plane
          ) {
            continue;
          }

          next[target] = centreMPRAtWorld(
            previous.viewports[target],
            previous.crosshairWorld,
          );
        }

        return {
          ...previous,
          viewports: next,
          revision: previous.revision + 1,
        };
      });
    },
    [],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 17 — RESET ALL MPR VIEWS
  // ==========================================================

  const reset = useCallback(
    (): void => {
      setState((previous) => {
        const sizes: MPRInitialSizes = {
          axial: previous.viewports.axial.size,
          coronal: previous.viewports.coronal.size,
          sagittal: previous.viewports.sagittal.size,
        };

        const fresh = createInitialState(
          geometry,
          sizes,
        );

        return {
          ...fresh,
          revision: previous.revision + 1,
        };
      });
    },
    [geometry],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 18 — RESAMPLING PLANE DESCRIPTORS
  // ==========================================================

  const samplingPlanes = useMemo(
    (): MPRSamplingPlaneMap => ({
      axial: buildMPRSamplingPlane(
        state.viewports.axial,
      ),
      coronal: buildMPRSamplingPlane(
        state.viewports.coronal,
      ),
      sagittal: buildMPRSamplingPlane(
        state.viewports.sagittal,
      ),
    }),
    [state.viewports],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 19 — CROSSHAIR PROJECTIONS
  // ==========================================================

  const crosshairProjections = useMemo(
    (): MPRCrosshairProjectionMap => ({
      axial: projectMPRCrosshair(
        state.viewports.axial,
        state.crosshairWorld,
      ),
      coronal: projectMPRCrosshair(
        state.viewports.coronal,
        state.crosshairWorld,
      ),
      sagittal: projectMPRCrosshair(
        state.viewports.sagittal,
        state.crosshairWorld,
      ),
    }),
    [state.viewports, state.crosshairWorld],
  );

  const crosshairVoxel = useMemo(
    (): VoxelPoint => worldLPSToVoxel(
      geometry,
      state.crosshairWorld,
    ),
    [geometry, state.crosshairWorld],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 20 — ACTION BUNDLE
  // ==========================================================

  const actions = useMemo(
    (): MPRControllerActions => ({
      setActivePlane,
      setCrosshairWorld,
      setCrosshairVoxel,
      setCrosshairFromScreen,
      scrollSlice,
      moveSliceMm,
      pan,
      zoom,
      resize,
      fit,
      centerOnCrosshair,
      reset,
    }),
    [
      setActivePlane,
      setCrosshairWorld,
      setCrosshairVoxel,
      setCrosshairFromScreen,
      scrollSlice,
      moveSliceMm,
      pan,
      zoom,
      resize,
      fit,
      centerOnCrosshair,
      reset,
    ],
  );

  return {
    state,
    actions,

    axial: state.viewports.axial,
    coronal: state.viewports.coronal,
    sagittal: state.viewports.sagittal,

    samplingPlanes,
    crosshairProjections,

    crosshairWorld: state.crosshairWorld,
    crosshairVoxel,
    activePlane: state.activePlane,

    ready: true,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 21 — HOOK CAPABILITIES
// ============================================================

export function getMPRHookCapabilities() {
  return {
    synchronizedThreePlaneNavigation: true,
    dicomLPSCoordinates: true,
    obliqueVolumeCompatibility: true,
    physicalSliceNavigation: true,
    independentPanAndZoom: true,
    anchoredZoom: true,
    crosshairWorldSynchronization: true,
    resamplingPlaneDescriptors: true,
    actualImageRendering: false,
    automaticVolumeLoading: false,
    modifiesSourceData: false,
    diagnosticValidationComplete: false,
  } as const;
}


// ============================================================
// END OF SINUS VIEWER REACT MPR CONTROLLER
// ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/frontend/src/hooks/useMeasurements.ts
```
/**
 * SINUS VIEWER — REACT MEASUREMENT CONTROLLER
 * ==========================================
 *
 * React 18 measurement state for CT / CBCT MPR:
 * - Physical distance (2 points)
 * - Three-point angle (3 points)
 * - Polyline length (2+ points)
 * - Planar polygon area (3+ points)
 * - Screen-pixel to DICOM LPS point placement
 * - Draft undo, finish, cancel and clear
 * - Measurement visibility and selection
 * - In-memory measurement history
 *
 * Measurement mathematics: File 53
 * MPR coordinate transforms: File 52
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 * - All measurement points use DICOM LPS millimetres.
 * - Polygon boundaries must be simple and planar.
 * - ROI intensity measurement is handled by File 53;
 *   this hook manages geometric drawing annotations.
 * - Measurements are NOT persisted automatically.
 * - No original DICOM files are accessed or modified.
 *
 * Measurements require numerical and clinical validation.
 */

import {
  useCallback,
  useMemo,
  useRef,
  useState,
} from "react";

import type { PreviewPlane } from "../types/api";

import type { WorldPointLPS } from "../utils/geometry";

import {
  mprScreenToWorldLPS,
  type MPRScreenPoint,
  type MPRViewportState,
} from "../utils/mpr";

import {
  measureAngleDegrees,
  measureDistanceMm,
  measurePolygonAreaMm2,
  measurePolylineMm,
} from "../utils/measurements";


// ============================================================
// 01 — PUBLIC CONSTANTS
// ============================================================

export const MAX_STORED_MEASUREMENTS = 1000;
export const MAX_MEASUREMENT_DRAWING_POINTS = 10000;

export const MEASUREMENT_TOOLS = [
  "distance",
  "angle",
  "polyline",
  "polygon",
] as const;


// ============================================================
// 02 — PUBLIC TYPES
// ============================================================

export type MeasurementTool =
  (typeof MEASUREMENT_TOOLS)[number];

export type MeasurementMode =
  | MeasurementTool
  | "none";

export interface MeasurementDraft {
  readonly tool: MeasurementTool;
  readonly plane: PreviewPlane;
  readonly points: readonly WorldPointLPS[];
}

export interface MeasurementBase {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly plane: PreviewPlane;
  readonly points: readonly WorldPointLPS[];
  readonly visible: boolean;
}

export interface StoredDistanceMeasurement
  extends MeasurementBase {
  readonly kind: "distance";
  readonly distanceMm: number;
}

export interface StoredAngleMeasurement
  extends MeasurementBase {
  readonly kind: "angle";
  readonly angleDegrees: number;
}

export interface StoredPolylineMeasurement
  extends MeasurementBase {
  readonly kind: "polyline";
  readonly lengthMm: number;
}

export interface StoredPolygonMeasurement
  extends MeasurementBase {
  readonly kind: "polygon";
  readonly areaMm2: number;
  readonly perimeterMm: number;
}

export type StoredMeasurement =
  | StoredDistanceMeasurement
  | StoredAngleMeasurement
  | StoredPolylineMeasurement
  | StoredPolygonMeasurement;

export interface MeasurementControllerState {
  readonly activeTool: MeasurementMode;
  readonly draft: MeasurementDraft | null;
  readonly measurements: readonly StoredMeasurement[];
  readonly selectedId: string | null;
  readonly revision: number;
}

export interface MeasurementActions {
  readonly setTool: (tool: MeasurementMode) => void;

  readonly addWorldPoint: (
    plane: PreviewPlane,
    point: WorldPointLPS,
  ) => void;

  readonly addScreenPoint: (
    viewport: MPRViewportState,
    screen: MPRScreenPoint,
  ) => void;

  readonly finishDraft: () => void;
  readonly undoDraftPoint: () => void;
  readonly cancelDraft: () => void;

  readonly removeMeasurement: (id: string) => void;
  readonly undoLastMeasurement: () => void;
  readonly clearMeasurements: () => void;

  readonly selectMeasurement: (
    id: string | null,
  ) => void;

  readonly setMeasurementVisible: (
    id: string,
    visible: boolean,
  ) => void;

  readonly reset: () => void;
}

export interface UseMeasurementsResult {
  readonly state: MeasurementControllerState;
  readonly actions: MeasurementActions;
  readonly activeTool: MeasurementMode;
  readonly draft: MeasurementDraft | null;
  readonly measurements: readonly StoredMeasurement[];
  readonly selectedMeasurement: StoredMeasurement | null;
  readonly canFinishDraft: boolean;
  readonly canUndoDraft: boolean;
  readonly hasMeasurements: boolean;
  readonly measurementCount: number;
}


// ============================================================
// 03 — CONTROLLED ERRORS
// ============================================================

export class MeasurementControllerError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "MeasurementControllerError";
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 04 — VALIDATION
// ============================================================

function assertTool(tool: MeasurementMode): void {
  if (
    tool !== "none" &&
    !MEASUREMENT_TOOLS.includes(
      tool as MeasurementTool,
    )
  ) {
    throw new MeasurementControllerError(
      "Unsupported measurement tool.",
    );
  }
}

function assertPlane(plane: PreviewPlane): void {
  if (
    plane !== "axial" &&
    plane !== "coronal" &&
    plane !== "sagittal"
  ) {
    throw new MeasurementControllerError(
      "Invalid measurement plane.",
    );
  }
}

function copyWorldPoint(
  point: WorldPointLPS,
): WorldPointLPS {
  if (
    !point ||
    !Number.isFinite(point.x) ||
    !Number.isFinite(point.y) ||
    !Number.isFinite(point.z)
  ) {
    throw new MeasurementControllerError(
      "Invalid physical measurement position.",
    );
  }

  return {
    x: point.x,
    y: point.y,
    z: point.z,
  };
}

function assertMeasurementId(id: string): void {
  if (
    typeof id !== "string" ||
    id.length === 0 ||
    id.length > 128
  ) {
    throw new MeasurementControllerError(
      "Invalid measurement identifier.",
    );
  }
}

function minimumPointCount(
  tool: MeasurementTool,
): number {
  switch (tool) {
    case "distance":
      return 2;
    case "angle":
      return 3;
    case "polyline":
      return 2;
    case "polygon":
      return 3;
  }
}

function maximumPointCount(
  tool: MeasurementTool,
): number {
  switch (tool) {
    case "distance":
      return 2;
    case "angle":
      return 3;
    case "polyline":
    case "polygon":
      return MAX_MEASUREMENT_DRAWING_POINTS;
  }
}

function canFinish(
  draft: MeasurementDraft | null,
): boolean {
  return (
    draft !== null &&
    draft.points.length >=
      minimumPointCount(draft.tool)
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 05 — INITIAL STATE
// ============================================================

function initialState(): MeasurementControllerState {
  return {
    activeTool: "none",
    draft: null,
    measurements: [],
    selectedId: null,
    revision: 0,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 06 — MEASUREMENT CONSTRUCTION
// ============================================================

/**
 * All geometry measurements use the original
 * unrounded world-space positions.
 *
 * Display formatting is left to the UI; no
 * rounding is performed in stored results.
 */
function buildMeasurement(
  id: string,
  draft: MeasurementDraft,
): StoredMeasurement {
  const points = draft.points.map(copyWorldPoint);

  const base: MeasurementBase = {
    id,
    plane: draft.plane,
    points,
    visible: true,
  };

  switch (draft.tool) {
    case "distance":
      return {
        ...base,
        kind: "distance",
        distanceMm: measureDistanceMm(
          points[0],
          points[1],
        ).distanceMm,
      };

    case "angle":
      return {
        ...base,
        kind: "angle",
        angleDegrees: measureAngleDegrees(
          points[0],
          points[1],
          points[2],
        ).angleDegrees,
      };

    case "polyline":
      return {
        ...base,
        kind: "polyline",
        lengthMm: measurePolylineMm(
          points,
        ).lengthMm,
      };

    case "polygon": {
      const result = measurePolygonAreaMm2(
        points,
      );

      return {
        ...base,
        kind: "polygon",
        areaMm2: result.areaMm2,
        perimeterMm: result.perimeterMm,
      };
    }
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 07 — MAIN REACT HOOK
// ============================================================

/**
 * Create one hook instance for one loaded volume.
 *
 * Mount this controller in the viewer container,
 * not independently in all three MPR panels.
 *
 * Remount the owning component with a new key or
 * invoke actions.reset() when the selected volume
 * changes or the authenticated session ends.
 */
export function useMeasurements():
  UseMeasurementsResult {
  const [state, setState] =
    useState<MeasurementControllerState>(
      initialState,
    );

  /**
   * Local monotonically increasing identifiers.
   * These are UI IDs, not DICOM identifiers.
   */
  const nextId = useRef(0);

  // ==========================================================
  // 08 — SELECT TOOL
  // ==========================================================

  const setTool = useCallback(
    (tool: MeasurementMode): void => {
      assertTool(tool);

      setState((previous) => {
        if (
          previous.activeTool === tool &&
          previous.draft === null
        ) {
          return previous;
        }

        return {
          ...previous,
          activeTool: tool,
          draft: null,
          revision: previous.revision + 1,
        };
      });
    },
    [],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 09 — COMMIT COMPLETED DRAFT
  // ==========================================================

  const commitDraft = useCallback(
    (
      previous: MeasurementControllerState,
      draft: MeasurementDraft,
    ): MeasurementControllerState => {
      if (
        previous.measurements.length >=
        MAX_STORED_MEASUREMENTS
      ) {
        throw new MeasurementControllerError(
          "Measurement history limit reached.",
        );
      }

      const id = `measurement-${++nextId.current}`;

      const measurement = buildMeasurement(
        id,
        draft,
      );

      return {
        ...previous,
        draft: null,
        measurements: [
          ...previous.measurements,
          measurement,
        ],
        selectedId: id,
        revision: previous.revision + 1,
      };
    },
    [],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 10 — ADD WORLD POINT
  // ==========================================================

  const addWorldPoint = useCallback(
    (
      plane: PreviewPlane,
      point: WorldPointLPS,
    ): void => {
      assertPlane(plane);
      const safePoint = copyWorldPoint(point);

      setState((previous) => {
        const tool = previous.activeTool;

        if (tool === "none") {
          return previous;
        }

        /**
         * Keep every geometric drawing associated
         * with a single MPR plane.
         *
         * Switching planes starts a new draft.
         * This prevents accidental mixing of points
         * captured in unrelated anatomical slices.
         */
        const existing =
          previous.draft?.tool === tool &&
          previous.draft.plane === plane
            ? previous.draft.points
            : [];

        if (
          existing.length >=
          maximumPointCount(tool)
        ) {
          throw new MeasurementControllerError(
            "Too many measurement points.",
          );
        }

        const draft: MeasurementDraft = {
          tool,
          plane,
          points: [...existing, safePoint],
        };

        /**
         * Distance and angle automatically complete
         * at their required fixed number of points.
         */
        if (
          (tool === "distance" || tool === "angle") &&
          draft.points.length ===
            maximumPointCount(tool)
        ) {
          return commitDraft(previous, draft);
        }

        return {
          ...previous,
          draft,
          selectedId: null,
          revision: previous.revision + 1,
        };
      });
    },
    [commitDraft],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 11 — ADD POINT FROM AN MPR SCREEN
  // ==========================================================

  const addScreenPoint = useCallback(
    (
      viewport: MPRViewportState,
      screen: MPRScreenPoint,
    ): void => {
      const world = mprScreenToWorldLPS(
        viewport,
        screen,
      );

      addWorldPoint(
        viewport.plane,
        world,
      );
    },
    [addWorldPoint],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 12 — FINISH POLYLINE OR POLYGON
  // ==========================================================

  const finishDraft = useCallback(
    (): void => {
      setState((previous) => {
        const draft = previous.draft;

        if (!draft) {
          return previous;
        }

        if (!canFinish(draft)) {
          throw new MeasurementControllerError(
            "More points are required.",
          );
        }

        return commitDraft(
          previous,
          draft,
        );
      });
    },
    [commitDraft],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 13 — UNDO ONE DRAFT POINT
  // ==========================================================

  const undoDraftPoint = useCallback(
    (): void => {
      setState((previous) => {
        if (!previous.draft) {
          return previous;
        }

        const remaining = previous.draft.points.slice(
          0,
          -1,
        );

        return {
          ...previous,
          draft:
            remaining.length > 0
              ? {
                  ...previous.draft,
                  points: remaining,
                }
              : null,
          revision: previous.revision + 1,
        };
      });
    },
    [],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 14 — CANCEL UNFINISHED DRAWING
  // ==========================================================

  const cancelDraft = useCallback(
    (): void => {
      setState((previous) => (
        previous.draft === null
          ? previous
          : {
              ...previous,
              draft: null,
              revision: previous.revision + 1,
            }
      ));
    },
    [],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 15 — REMOVE ONE MEASUREMENT
  // ==========================================================

  const removeMeasurement = useCallback(
    (id: string): void => {
      assertMeasurementId(id);

      setState((previous) => {
        if (
          !previous.measurements.some(
            (item) => item.id === id,
          )
        ) {
          return previous;
        }

        return {
          ...previous,
          measurements:
            previous.measurements.filter(
              (item) => item.id !== id,
            ),
          selectedId:
            previous.selectedId === id
              ? null
              : previous.selectedId,
          revision: previous.revision + 1,
        };
      });
    },
    [],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 16 — UNDO MOST RECENT COMPLETED MEASUREMENT
  // ==========================================================

  const undoLastMeasurement = useCallback(
    (): void => {
      setState((previous) => {
        if (previous.measurements.length === 0) {
          return previous;
        }

        const remaining =
          previous.measurements.slice(0, -1);

        const selectionStillExists =
          remaining.some(
            (item) => item.id === previous.selectedId,
          );

        return {
          ...previous,
          measurements: remaining,
          selectedId: selectionStillExists
            ? previous.selectedId
            : null,
          revision: previous.revision + 1,
        };
      });
    },
    [],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 17 — CLEAR MEASUREMENT HISTORY
  // ==========================================================

  const clearMeasurements = useCallback(
    (): void => {
      setState((previous) => {
        if (
          previous.measurements.length === 0 &&
          previous.selectedId === null &&
          previous.draft === null
        ) {
          return previous;
        }

        return {
          ...previous,
          measurements: [],
          selectedId: null,
          draft: null,
          revision: previous.revision + 1,
        };
      });
    },
    [],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 18 — SELECT EXISTING MEASUREMENT
  // ==========================================================

  const selectMeasurement = useCallback(
    (id: string | null): void => {
      if (id !== null) {
        assertMeasurementId(id);
      }

      setState((previous) => {
        if (
          id !== null &&
          !previous.measurements.some(
            (item) => item.id === id,
          )
        ) {
          return previous;
        }

        if (previous.selectedId === id) {
          return previous;
        }

        return {
          ...previous,
          selectedId: id,
          revision: previous.revision + 1,
        };
      });
    },
    [],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 19 — MEASUREMENT VISIBILITY
  // ==========================================================

  const setMeasurementVisible = useCallback(
    (
      id: string,
      visible: boolean,
    ): void => {
      assertMeasurementId(id);

      if (typeof visible !== "boolean") {
        throw new MeasurementControllerError(
          "Invalid visibility setting.",
        );
      }

      setState((previous) => {
        const target = previous.measurements.find(
          (item) => item.id === id,
        );

        if (!target || target.visible === visible) {
          return previous;
        }

        return {
          ...previous,
          measurements: previous.measurements.map(
            (item) =>
              item.id === id
                ? { ...item, visible }
                : item,
          ),
          revision: previous.revision + 1,
        };
      });
    },
    [],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 20 — RESET
  // ==========================================================

  const reset = useCallback(
    (): void => {
      setState((previous) => ({
        ...initialState(),
        revision: previous.revision + 1,
      }));
    },
    [],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 21 — DERIVED STATE
  // ==========================================================

  const selectedMeasurement = useMemo(
    (): StoredMeasurement | null =>
      state.measurements.find(
        (item) => item.id === state.selectedId,
      ) ?? null,
    [state.measurements, state.selectedId],
  );

  const canFinishDraft = canFinish(
    state.draft,
  );

  const canUndoDraft =
    state.draft !== null &&
    state.draft.points.length > 0;

  // ==========================================================
  // 22 — STABLE ACTION BUNDLE
  // ==========================================================

  const actions = useMemo<MeasurementActions>(
    () => ({
      setTool,
      addWorldPoint,
      addScreenPoint,
      finishDraft,
      undoDraftPoint,
      cancelDraft,
      removeMeasurement,
      undoLastMeasurement,
      clearMeasurements,
      selectMeasurement,
      setMeasurementVisible,
      reset,
    }),
    [
      setTool,
      addWorldPoint,
      addScreenPoint,
      finishDraft,
      undoDraftPoint,
      cancelDraft,
      removeMeasurement,
      undoLastMeasurement,
      clearMeasurements,
      selectMeasurement,
      setMeasurementVisible,
      reset,
    ],
  );

  return {
    state,
    actions,
    activeTool: state.activeTool,
    draft: state.draft,
    measurements: state.measurements,
    selectedMeasurement,
    canFinishDraft,
    canUndoDraft,
    hasMeasurements: state.measurements.length > 0,
    measurementCount: state.measurements.length,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 23 — USER-FACING ERROR ADAPTER
// ============================================================

export function getMeasurementControllerError(
  error: unknown,
): string {
  if (
    error instanceof MeasurementControllerError ||
    (error instanceof Error &&
      error.name === "MeasurementError") ||
    (error instanceof Error &&
      error.name === "MPRError")
  ) {
    return error.message;
  }

  return "The measurement could not be completed.";
}


// ============================================================
// 24 — CAPABILITIES
// ============================================================

export function getMeasurementsHookCapabilities() {
  return {
    coordinateSystem: "DICOM_LPS",
    physicalDistance: true,
    threePointAngle: true,
    polylineLength: true,
    polygonArea: true,
    screenPointPlacement: true,
    draftUndo: true,
    historyRemoval: true,
    measurementVisibility: true,
    measurementSelection: true,
    automaticPersistence: false,
    originalDatasetModification: false,
    clinicalValidationComplete: false,
  } as const;
}


// ============================================================
// END OF SINUS VIEWER REACT MEASUREMENT CONTROLLER
// ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/frontend/src/hooks/usePatients.ts

### sinus-viewer/frontend/src/hooks/usePreviews.ts
```
/**
 * SINUS VIEWER — REACT PREVIEWS HOOK
 * ==================================
 *
 * React 18 integration for FrontendPreviewService.
 *
 * Features:
 * - useSyncExternalStore subscriptions.
 * - Stable preview service per hook owner.
 * - Authorized axial/coronal/sagittal PNG loading.
 * - Progress and authentication state handling.
 * - AbortSignal forwarding to preview loading.
 * - Selection comparison for dataset changes.
 * - Object URL lifecycle delegated to File 48.
 * - StrictMode-compatible cleanup.
 *
 * SECURITY:
 * - No patient names, original paths or DICOM UIDs.
 * - No image persistence in browser storage.
 * - No console logging of image data or URLs.
 * - Original DICOM dataset remains read-only.
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 * - The service owns every generated Blob URL.
 * - UI components must not revoke service-owned URLs.
 * - Reset on logout and dataset/series change.
 * - URL references become invalid after reset/dispose.
 * - Previews are navigation aids, not diagnostic images.
 */

import {
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useState,
  useSyncExternalStore,
} from "react";

import {
  createPreviewService,
  FrontendPreviewError,
  type FrontendPreviewService,
  type PreviewLoadOptions,
  type PreviewLoadProgress,
  type PreviewResource,
  type PreviewResources,
  type PreviewServiceSnapshot,
} from "../services/previewService";

import type {
  PreviewDescriptor,
  PreviewPlane,
} from "../types/api";


// ============================================================
// 01 — PUBLIC TYPES
// ============================================================

export interface PreviewSelection {
  readonly datasetSessionId: string;
  readonly seriesHandle: string;
}

export interface PreviewActions {
  readonly load: (
    selection: PreviewSelection,
    options?: PreviewLoadOptions,
  ) => Promise<PreviewResources>;

  readonly cancel: () => void;

  readonly reset: () => void;

  readonly getPreview: (
    plane: PreviewPlane,
  ) => PreviewResource | null;
}

export interface UsePreviewsResult {
  readonly state: PreviewServiceSnapshot;
  readonly progress: PreviewLoadProgress;
  readonly images: PreviewResources | null;
  readonly descriptor: PreviewDescriptor | null;

  readonly axial: PreviewResource | null;
  readonly coronal: PreviewResource | null;
  readonly sagittal: PreviewResource | null;

  readonly actions: PreviewActions;
  readonly service: FrontendPreviewService;

  readonly isLoading: boolean;
  readonly isReady: boolean;
  readonly isCancelled: boolean;
  readonly hasError: boolean;
  readonly requiresAuthentication: boolean;
  readonly hasPreviews: boolean;

  readonly completedPlanes: number;
  readonly percentage: number;
  readonly error: string | null;
}


// ============================================================
// 02 — OWNED SERVICE
// ============================================================

/**
 * One service instance belongs to one hook owner.
 *
 * Do not call usePreviews() independently for every
 * image card. The three planes must share one loader.
 *
 * Construction does not perform HTTP requests.
 */
function useOwnedPreviewService():
  FrontendPreviewService {
  const [service] = useState<FrontendPreviewService>(
    () => createPreviewService(),
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      /**
       * Reset cancels pending PNG requests and revokes
       * all committed service-owned Blob URLs.
       *
       * React StrictMode may replay effect cleanup
       * in development. Reset is reusable while
       * dispose permanently disables the service.
       *
       * Therefore dispose() is not invoked from this
       * effect cleanup.
       */
      service.reset();
    };
  }, [service]);

  return service;
}


// ============================================================
// 03 — EXTERNAL STORE SUBSCRIPTION
// ============================================================

/**
 * Observe an existing service owned by a parent or
 * React context.
 *
 * This hook never resets or disposes external services.
 */
export function usePreviewsSnapshot(
  service: FrontendPreviewService,
): PreviewServiceSnapshot {
  return useSyncExternalStore(
    service.subscribe,
    service.getSnapshot,
    service.getSnapshot,
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 04 — STABLE ACTION WRAPPERS
// ============================================================

export function usePreviewsActions(
  service: FrontendPreviewService,
): PreviewActions {
  const load = useCallback(
    (
      selection: PreviewSelection,
      options: PreviewLoadOptions = {},
    ): Promise<PreviewResources> => {
      return service.load(
        selection.datasetSessionId,
        selection.seriesHandle,
        options,
      );
    },
    [service],
  );

  const cancel = useCallback(
    (): void => {
      service.cancel();
    },
    [service],
  );

  const reset = useCallback(
    (): void => {
      service.reset();
    },
    [service],
  );

  const getPreview = useCallback(
    (plane: PreviewPlane): PreviewResource | null => {
      return service.getPreview(plane);
    },
    [service],
  );

  return {
    load,
    cancel,
    reset,
    getPreview,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 05 — MAIN USE PREVIEWS HOOK
// ============================================================

/**
 * Self-contained previews hook.
 *
 * Use once inside the dataset browser or viewer owner.
 * Pass axial/coronal/sagittal resources to child views.
 *
 * Loading is intentionally explicit rather than
 * automatically triggered during render or mount.
 *
 * This prevents unintended medical-image requests
 * before an authorized series has been selected.
 */
export function usePreviews(): UsePreviewsResult {
  const service = useOwnedPreviewService();

  const state = usePreviewsSnapshot(service);
  const actions = usePreviewsActions(service);

  const progress = state.progress;
  const images = state.images;
  const descriptor = state.descriptor;

  const isLoading =
    progress.status === "loading";

  const isReady =
    progress.status === "ready" &&
    images !== null;

  const isCancelled =
    progress.status === "cancelled";

  const hasError =
    progress.status === "error";

  const requiresAuthentication =
    progress.status === "authentication-required";

  const hasPreviews = isReady;

  const axial = images?.axial ?? null;
  const coronal = images?.coronal ?? null;
  const sagittal = images?.sagittal ?? null;

  return {
    state,
    progress,
    images,
    descriptor,

    axial,
    coronal,
    sagittal,

    actions,
    service,

    isLoading,
    isReady,
    isCancelled,
    hasError,
    requiresAuthentication,
    hasPreviews,

    completedPlanes: progress.completedPlanes,
    percentage: progress.percentage,
    error: progress.error,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 06 — SAFE ERROR ADAPTER
// ============================================================

/**
 * Returns a controlled UI-facing error.
 *
 * Do not expose unknown exception details, image
 * bytes, raw response objects or source locations.
 */
export function getPreviewActionError(
  error: unknown,
): string {
  if (error instanceof FrontendPreviewError) {
    return error.message;
  }

  return "The preview operation could not be completed.";
}


// ============================================================
// 07 — SELECTION EQUALITY
// ============================================================

/**
 * Compare opaque dataset/series handles.
 *
 * Used by the future viewer integration to determine
 * when old preview resources must be reset.
 */
export function isSamePreviewSelection(
  first: PreviewSelection | null,
  second: PreviewSelection | null,
): boolean {
  if (first === null || second === null) {
    return first === second;
  }

  return (
    first.datasetSessionId === second.datasetSessionId &&
    first.seriesHandle === second.seriesHandle
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 08 — SERVICE RESOURCE VALIDITY
// ============================================================

/**
 * Check whether the current snapshot belongs to the
 * active dataset/series selection.
 *
 * This is useful before passing URLs to an image
 * component after asynchronous selection changes.
 *
 * This does not perform an authentication check;
 * the API server enforces session authorization.
 */
export function previewsMatchSelection(
  snapshot: PreviewServiceSnapshot,
  selection: PreviewSelection | null,
): boolean {
  if (
    selection === null ||
    snapshot.progress.status !== "ready" ||
    snapshot.images === null
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  return (
    snapshot.datasetSessionId ===
      selection.datasetSessionId &&
    snapshot.seriesHandle ===
      selection.seriesHandle
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 09 — SAFE IMAGE ACCESS FROM SNAPSHOT
// ============================================================

/**
 * Select a preview directly from the subscribed
 * snapshot instead of using a possibly stale
 * asynchronous result from load().
 */
export function getSnapshotPreview(
  snapshot: PreviewServiceSnapshot,
  plane: PreviewPlane,
): PreviewResource | null {
  if (
    snapshot.progress.status !== "ready" ||
    snapshot.images === null
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  switch (plane) {
    case "axial":
      return snapshot.images.axial;

    case "coronal":
      return snapshot.images.coronal;

    case "sagittal":
      return snapshot.images.sagittal;

    default:
      return null;
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 10 — HOOK CAPABILITIES
// ============================================================

export function getPreviewsHookCapabilities() {
  return {
    reactExternalStore: true,
    stablePerOwnerService: true,
    threePlanePreviews: true,
    explicitAuthorizedLoading: true,
    progressSubscriptions: true,
    cancellablePNGRequests: true,
    staleResponseProtection: true,
    blobURLCleanup: true,
    automaticLoadOnMount: false,
    persistentBrowserStorage: false,
    diagnosticQuality: false,
    modifiesSourceDataset: false,
  } as const;
}


// ============================================================
// END OF SINUS VIEWER REACT PREVIEWS HOOK
// ============================================================
```
