### sinus-viewer/frontend/src/hooks/useVolume.ts
```
/**
 * SINUS VIEWER — REACT VOLUME HOOK
 * =================================
 *
 * React 18 integration for FrontendVolumeService.
 *
 * Features:
 * - useSyncExternalStore subscriptions.
 * - Stable service ownership per hook instance.
 * - Authorized volume loading through File 47.
 * - Progress, error and authentication-expiry states.
 * - Explicit load cancellation.
 * - Volume reset on selection change or logout.
 * - Cleanup compatible with React StrictMode replay.
 * - Access to decoded Float32 voxel data and geometry.
 *
 * SECURITY:
 * - No patient identifiers, UIDs or source paths in logs.
 * - No persistent browser storage.
 * - No original DICOM file modifications.
 * - Backend remains responsible for authorization.
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 * Loaded voxel buffers can contain sensitive medical data.
 * Never persist or transmit them to unrelated services.
 */

import {
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useState,
  useSyncExternalStore,
} from "react";

import {
  createVolumeService,
  FrontendVolumeError,
  type FrontendVolumeService,
  type LoadedVolume,
  type VolumeLoadOptions,
  type VolumeLoadProgress,
  type VolumeServiceOptions,
  type VolumeServiceSnapshot,
} from "../services/volumeService";


// ============================================================
// 01 — PUBLIC TYPES
// ============================================================

export interface VolumeSelection {
  readonly datasetSessionId: string;
  readonly seriesHandle: string;
}

export interface VolumeActions {
  readonly load: (
    selection: VolumeSelection,
    options?: VolumeLoadOptions,
  ) => Promise<LoadedVolume>;

  readonly cancel: () => void;
  readonly reset: () => void;

  readonly getVoxel: (
    z: number,
    y: number,
    x: number,
  ) => number | null;
}

export interface UseVolumeResult {
  readonly state: VolumeServiceSnapshot;
  readonly progress: VolumeLoadProgress;
  readonly volume: LoadedVolume | null;
  readonly actions: VolumeActions;
  readonly service: FrontendVolumeService;

  readonly isLoading: boolean;
  readonly isReady: boolean;
  readonly isCancelled: boolean;
  readonly hasError: boolean;
  readonly requiresAuthentication: boolean;
  readonly hasVolume: boolean;
}


// ============================================================
// 02 — STABLE SERVICE OWNERSHIP
// ============================================================

/**
 * Creates one service for the committed hook owner.
 *
 * Configuration is captured at initialization.
 * To change the memory limit or chunk size, remount
 * the owning component with a new React key.
 *
 * No network request occurs during construction.
 */
function useOwnedVolumeService(
  options?: VolumeServiceOptions,
): FrontendVolumeService {
  const [service] = useState<FrontendVolumeService>(
    () => createVolumeService(options),
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      /**
       * React StrictMode can replay effect cleanup and
       * setup during development.
       *
       * reset() cancels active requests and releases
       * the current volume reference without permanently
       * disposing the service.
       *
       * dispose() is intentionally not used here because
       * that would make a replayed service unusable.
       */
      service.reset();
    };
  }, [service]);

  return service;
}


// ============================================================
// 03 — EXTERNAL STORE SUBSCRIPTION
// ============================================================

/**
 * Observe an existing FrontendVolumeService.
 *
 * Use this function when a parent/context owns the
 * service and multiple viewers share its state.
 *
 * The external service is not reset on unmount.
 */
export function useVolumeSnapshot(
  service: FrontendVolumeService,
): VolumeServiceSnapshot {
  return useSyncExternalStore(
    service.subscribe,
    service.getSnapshot,
    service.getSnapshot,
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 04 — STABLE ACTION ADAPTER
// ============================================================

export function useVolumeActions(
  service: FrontendVolumeService,
): VolumeActions {
  const load = useCallback(
    (
      selection: VolumeSelection,
      options: VolumeLoadOptions = {},
    ): Promise<LoadedVolume> => {
      return service.load(
        selection.datasetSessionId,
        selection.seriesHandle,
        options,
      );
    },
    [service],
  );

  const cancel = useCallback(
    (): void => {
      service.cancel();
    },
    [service],
  );

  const reset = useCallback(
    (): void => {
      service.reset();
    },
    [service],
  );

  const getVoxel = useCallback(
    (
      z: number,
      y: number,
      x: number,
    ): number | null => {
      return service.getVoxel(z, y, x);
    },
    [service],
  );

  return {
    load,
    cancel,
    reset,
    getVoxel,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 05 — MAIN REACT VOLUME HOOK
// ============================================================

/**
 * Self-contained volume hook for one viewer owner.
 *
 * Call once in the viewer container and pass state/actions
 * down to axial, coronal, sagittal and 3D child views.
 *
 * Calling useVolume() independently in all four views
 * would allocate separate services and may download
 * the same medical volume multiple times.
 */
export function useVolume(
  options?: VolumeServiceOptions,
): UseVolumeResult {
  const service = useOwnedVolumeService(options);

  const state = useVolumeSnapshot(service);
  const actions = useVolumeActions(service);

  const progress = state.progress;
  const volume = state.volume;

  const isLoading =
    progress.status === "preparing" ||
    progress.status === "downloading";

  const isReady =
    progress.status === "ready" &&
    volume !== null;

  const isCancelled =
    progress.status === "cancelled";

  const hasError =
    progress.status === "error";

  const requiresAuthentication =
    progress.status === "authentication-required";

  const hasVolume = volume !== null;

  return {
    state,
    progress,
    volume,
    actions,
    service,
    isLoading,
    isReady,
    isCancelled,
    hasError,
    requiresAuthentication,
    hasVolume,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 06 — ERROR MESSAGE ADAPTER
// ============================================================

/**
 * Returns controlled errors appropriate for the UI.
 *
 * Do not pass raw unknown error objects to React error
 * boundaries, browser analytics or application logs.
 */
export function getVolumeActionError(
  error: unknown,
): string {
  if (error instanceof FrontendVolumeError) {
    return error.message;
  }

  return "The volume operation could not be completed.";
}


// ============================================================
// 07 — SAFE VOLUME SELECTION COMPARISON
// ============================================================

/**
 * Compare server-issued opaque handles without exposing
 * original dataset paths or DICOM identifiers.
 *
 * Useful for detecting whether a React viewer selection
 * has changed and whether old loaded voxels must be reset.
 */
export function isSameVolumeSelection(
  first: VolumeSelection | null,
  second: VolumeSelection | null,
): boolean {
  if (first === null || second === null) {
    return first === second;
  }

  return (
    first.datasetSessionId === second.datasetSessionId &&
    first.seriesHandle === second.seriesHandle
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 08 — HOOK CAPABILITIES
// ============================================================

export function getVolumeHookCapabilities() {
  return {
    reactExternalStore: true,
    stablePerOwnerService: true,
    progressSubscriptions: true,
    voxelBufferAccess: true,
    geometryAccess: true,
    cancellableRangeDownloads: true,
    staleResponseProtection: true,
    memoryLimitEnforcement: true,
    automaticLoadOnMount: false,
    persistentBrowserStorage: false,
    modifiesSourceDataset: false,
    clinicalValidationComplete: false,
  } as const;
}


// ============================================================
// END OF SINUS VIEWER REACT VOLUME HOOK
// ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/frontend/src/hooks/useWindowLevel.ts
```
/**
 * SINUS VIEWER — REACT WINDOW / LEVEL HOOK
 * ========================================
 *
 * React 18 state management for CT/CBCT display:
 * - Window centre and width.
 * - Brightness and contrast.
 * - Grayscale inversion.
 * - Built-in and custom display presets.
 * - Percentile-based automatic window estimation.
 * - Mouse-drag window adjustment.
 * - Display reset.
 * - Rendering configuration for File 50.
 *
 * Important:
 * - Original voxel intensities are never modified.
 * - CBCT intensities are not assumed to be calibrated HU.
 * - This is a display controller, not HU calibration.
 * - No API, storage, or filesystem operations.
 */

import {
  useCallback,
  useMemo,
  useState,
} from "react";

import {
  DEFAULT_WINDOW_CENTER,
  DEFAULT_WINDOW_WIDTH,
  WINDOW_PRESETS,
  adjustBrightness,
  adjustContrast,
  createWindowLevel,
  estimateAutoWindow,
  normalizeAdjustments,
  scaleWindowWidth,
  shiftWindowCenter,
  validateWindowLevel,
  type AutoWindowOptions,
  type DisplayAdjustments,
  type RenderOptions,
  type WindowLevel,
} from "../utils/windowLevel";


// ============================================================
// 01 — PUBLIC CONSTANTS
// ============================================================

export const DEFAULT_WINDOW_DRAG_SENSITIVITY = 1;

export const DEFAULT_WIDTH_DRAG_SENSITIVITY = 1;

export const DEFAULT_WINDOW_LEVEL_SETTINGS = {
  window: {
    center: DEFAULT_WINDOW_CENTER,
    width: DEFAULT_WINDOW_WIDTH,
  },
  adjustments: {
    brightness: 0,
    contrast: 1,
    invert: false,
  },
} as const;


// ============================================================
// 02 — PUBLIC TYPES
// ============================================================

export type WindowPresetName =
  keyof typeof WINDOW_PRESETS;

export interface WindowLevelState {
  readonly window: WindowLevel;
  readonly adjustments: DisplayAdjustments;
  readonly selectedPreset: WindowPresetName | null;
  readonly revision: number;
}

export interface WindowLevelInitialOptions {
  readonly window?: WindowLevel;
  readonly adjustments?: Partial<DisplayAdjustments>;
}

export interface WindowDragOptions {
  readonly centerSensitivity?: number;
  readonly widthSensitivity?: number;
}

export interface WindowLevelActions {
  readonly setWindow: (
    window: WindowLevel,
  ) => void;

  readonly setCenter: (
    center: number,
  ) => void;

  readonly setWidth: (
    width: number,
  ) => void;

  readonly shiftCenter: (
    delta: number,
  ) => void;

  readonly scaleWidth: (
    factor: number,
  ) => void;

  readonly dragWindow: (
    deltaX: number,
    deltaY: number,
    options?: WindowDragOptions,
  ) => void;

  readonly setBrightness: (
    brightness: number,
  ) => void;

  readonly setContrast: (
    contrast: number,
  ) => void;

  readonly setInverted: (
    inverted: boolean,
  ) => void;

  readonly toggleInvert: () => void;

  readonly setAdjustments: (
    adjustments: Partial<DisplayAdjustments>,
  ) => void;

  readonly applyPreset: (
    name: WindowPresetName,
  ) => void;

  readonly autoWindow: (
    intensities: Float32Array | Float64Array,
    options?: AutoWindowOptions,
  ) => void;

  readonly reset: () => void;
}

export interface UseWindowLevelResult {
  readonly state: WindowLevelState;
  readonly actions: WindowLevelActions;

  readonly window: WindowLevel;
  readonly center: number;
  readonly width: number;

  readonly brightness: number;
  readonly contrast: number;
  readonly inverted: boolean;

  readonly selectedPreset: WindowPresetName | null;
  readonly renderOptions: RenderOptions;
  readonly revision: number;
}


// ============================================================
// 03 — INTERNAL HELPERS
// ============================================================

function assertFinite(
  value: number,
): void {
  if (!Number.isFinite(value)) {
    throw new Error(
      "Invalid display adjustment value.",
    );
  }
}

function assertPreset(
  name: WindowPresetName,
): void {
  if (
    !Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(
      WINDOW_PRESETS,
      name,
    )
  ) {
    throw new Error(
      "Unknown window/level preset.",
    );
  }
}

function makeInitialState(
  options: WindowLevelInitialOptions,
): WindowLevelState {
  const window = options.window
    ? createWindowLevel(
        options.window.center,
        options.window.width,
      )
    : createWindowLevel(
        DEFAULT_WINDOW_CENTER,
        DEFAULT_WINDOW_WIDTH,
      );

  const adjustments = normalizeAdjustments(
    options.adjustments,
  );

  return {
    window,
    adjustments,
    selectedPreset: null,
    revision: 0,
  };
}

function withWindow(
  previous: WindowLevelState,
  window: WindowLevel,
  preset: WindowPresetName | null = null,
): WindowLevelState {
  validateWindowLevel(window);

  if (
    previous.window.center === window.center &&
    previous.window.width === window.width &&
    previous.selectedPreset === preset
  ) {
    return previous;
  }

  return {
    ...previous,
    window: {
      center: window.center,
      width: window.width,
    },
    selectedPreset: preset,
    revision: previous.revision + 1,
  };
}

function withAdjustments(
  previous: WindowLevelState,
  adjustments: DisplayAdjustments,
): WindowLevelState {
  if (
    previous.adjustments.brightness ===
      adjustments.brightness &&
    previous.adjustments.contrast ===
      adjustments.contrast &&
    previous.adjustments.invert ===
      adjustments.invert
  ) {
    return previous;
  }

  return {
    ...previous,
    adjustments,
    revision: previous.revision + 1,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 04 — MAIN REACT HOOK
// ============================================================

/**
 * Each hook invocation owns independent display settings.
 *
 * Call once at the viewer container level when three MPR
 * planes should use one shared window/level configuration.
 *
 * Initial options are captured on mount. To restore new
 * volume-specific defaults, remount with a new React key
 * or invoke reset().
 */
export function useWindowLevel(
  initialOptions: WindowLevelInitialOptions = {},
): UseWindowLevelResult {
  const [state, setState] = useState<WindowLevelState>(
    () => makeInitialState(initialOptions),
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 05 — WINDOW CENTRE / WIDTH
  // ==========================================================

  const setWindow = useCallback(
    (window: WindowLevel): void => {
      const checked = createWindowLevel(
        window.center,
        window.width,
      );

      setState((previous) =>
        withWindow(previous, checked),
      );
    },
    [],
  );

  const setCenter = useCallback(
    (center: number): void => {
      assertFinite(center);

      setState((previous) =>
        withWindow(
          previous,
          createWindowLevel(
            center,
            previous.window.width,
          ),
        ),
      );
    },
    [],
  );

  const setWidth = useCallback(
    (width: number): void => {
      assertFinite(width);

      setState((previous) =>
        withWindow(
          previous,
          createWindowLevel(
            previous.window.center,
            width,
          ),
        ),
      );
    },
    [],
  );

  const shiftCenter = useCallback(
    (delta: number): void => {
      assertFinite(delta);

      setState((previous) =>
        withWindow(
          previous,
          shiftWindowCenter(
            previous.window,
            delta,
          ),
        ),
      );
    },
    [],
  );

  const scaleWidth = useCallback(
    (factor: number): void => {
      assertFinite(factor);

      setState((previous) =>
        withWindow(
          previous,
          scaleWindowWidth(
            previous.window,
            factor,
          ),
        ),
      );
    },
    [],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 06 — MOUSE DRAG WINDOW ADJUSTMENT
  // ==========================================================

  /**
   * Horizontal drag changes width.
   * Vertical drag changes centre.
   *
   * Sensitivities are source-intensity units per
   * screen pixel, not necessarily HU per pixel.
   */
  const dragWindow = useCallback(
    (
      deltaX: number,
      deltaY: number,
      options: WindowDragOptions = {},
    ): void => {
      assertFinite(deltaX);
      assertFinite(deltaY);

      const centerSensitivity =
        options.centerSensitivity ??
        DEFAULT_WINDOW_DRAG_SENSITIVITY;

      const widthSensitivity =
        options.widthSensitivity ??
        DEFAULT_WIDTH_DRAG_SENSITIVITY;

      if (
        !Number.isFinite(centerSensitivity) ||
        !Number.isFinite(widthSensitivity) ||
        centerSensitivity <= 0 ||
        widthSensitivity <= 0
      ) {
        throw new Error(
          "Invalid window drag sensitivity.",
        );
      }

      setState((previous) => {
        const nextCenter =
          previous.window.center +
          deltaY * centerSensitivity;

        const nextWidth = Math.max(
          1,
          previous.window.width +
          deltaX * widthSensitivity,
        );

        return withWindow(
          previous,
          createWindowLevel(
            nextCenter,
            nextWidth,
          ),
        );
      });
    },
    [],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 07 — BRIGHTNESS / CONTRAST
  // ==========================================================

  const setBrightness = useCallback(
    (brightness: number): void => {
      setState((previous) =>
        withAdjustments(
          previous,
          adjustBrightness(
            previous.adjustments,
            brightness,
          ),
        ),
      );
    },
    [],
  );

  const setContrast = useCallback(
    (contrast: number): void => {
      setState((previous) =>
        withAdjustments(
          previous,
          adjustContrast(
            previous.adjustments,
            contrast,
          ),
        ),
      );
    },
    [],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 08 — GRAYSCALE INVERSION
  // ==========================================================

  const setInverted = useCallback(
    (inverted: boolean): void => {
      const checked = normalizeAdjustments({
        invert: inverted,
      });

      setState((previous) =>
        withAdjustments(
          previous,
          {
            ...previous.adjustments,
            invert: checked.invert,
          },
        ),
      );
    },
    [],
  );

  const toggleInvert = useCallback(
    (): void => {
      setState((previous) =>
        withAdjustments(
          previous,
          normalizeAdjustments({
            ...previous.adjustments,
            invert: !previous.adjustments.invert,
          }),
        ),
      );
    },
    [],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 09 — MULTIPLE DISPLAY ADJUSTMENTS
  // ==========================================================

  const setAdjustments = useCallback(
    (
      adjustments: Partial<DisplayAdjustments>,
    ): void => {
      setState((previous) =>
        withAdjustments(
          previous,
          normalizeAdjustments({
            ...previous.adjustments,
            ...adjustments,
          }),
        ),
      );
    },
    [],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 10 — BUILT-IN PRESETS
  // ==========================================================

  const applyPreset = useCallback(
    (name: WindowPresetName): void => {
      assertPreset(name);

      const preset = WINDOW_PRESETS[name];

      setState((previous) =>
        withWindow(
          previous,
          createWindowLevel(
            preset.center,
            preset.width,
          ),
          name,
        ),
      );
    },
    [],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 11 — AUTOMATIC INTENSITY WINDOW
  // ==========================================================

  /**
   * This operation samples bounded source intensities
   * and estimates a useful visual display range.
   *
   * No source buffer mutation occurs.
   * This does NOT determine clinically calibrated HU.
   */
  const autoWindow = useCallback(
    (
      intensities: Float32Array | Float64Array,
      options: AutoWindowOptions = {},
    ): void => {
      const estimated = estimateAutoWindow(
        intensities,
        options,
      );

      setState((previous) =>
        withWindow(previous, estimated),
      );
    },
    [],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 12 — RESET
  // ==========================================================

  const reset = useCallback(
    (): void => {
      const initial = makeInitialState(initialOptions);

      setState((previous) => ({
        ...initial,
        revision: previous.revision + 1,
      }));
    },
    // Initial options are mount-time configuration.
    // Prefer a stable options object in the owning component.
    [initialOptions],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 13 — CANVAS RENDERING OPTIONS
  // ==========================================================

  const renderOptions = useMemo<RenderOptions>(
    () => ({
      window: state.window,
      adjustments: state.adjustments,
      invalidPixelValue: 0,
    }),
    [state.window, state.adjustments],
  );

  // ==========================================================
  // 14 — STABLE ACTION BUNDLE
  // ==========================================================

  const actions = useMemo<WindowLevelActions>(
    () => ({
      setWindow,
      setCenter,
      setWidth,
      shiftCenter,
      scaleWidth,
      dragWindow,
      setBrightness,
      setContrast,
      setInverted,
      toggleInvert,
      setAdjustments,
      applyPreset,
      autoWindow,
      reset,
    }),
    [
      setWindow,
      setCenter,
      setWidth,
      shiftCenter,
      scaleWidth,
      dragWindow,
      setBrightness,
      setContrast,
      setInverted,
      toggleInvert,
      setAdjustments,
      applyPreset,
      autoWindow,
      reset,
    ],
  );

  return {
    state,
    actions,

    window: state.window,
    center: state.window.center,
    width: state.window.width,

    brightness: state.adjustments.brightness,
    contrast: state.adjustments.contrast,
    inverted: state.adjustments.invert,

    selectedPreset: state.selectedPreset,
    renderOptions,
    revision: state.revision,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 15 — DISPLAY PRESET DISCOVERY
// ============================================================

export function getWindowPresetNames():
  readonly WindowPresetName[] {
  return Object.keys(
    WINDOW_PRESETS,
  ) as WindowPresetName[];
}


// ============================================================
// 16 — HOOK CAPABILITIES
// ============================================================

export function getWindowLevelHookCapabilities() {
  return {
    reactStateController: true,
    windowCenterAndWidth: true,
    brightnessAndContrast: true,
    grayscaleInversion: true,
    builtinPresets: true,
    percentileAutoWindow: true,
    mouseDragAdjustment: true,
    canvasRenderOptions: true,
    assumesCalibratedHU: false,
    modifiesSourceVoxels: false,
    persistentBrowserStorage: false,
    diagnosticValidationComplete: false,
  } as const;
}


// ============================================================
// END OF SINUS VIEWER REACT WINDOW / LEVEL HOOK
// ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/frontend/src/services/api.ts

### sinus-viewer/frontend/src/services/apiClient.ts
```
/**
 * SINUS VIEWER — SECURE FRONTEND API CLIENT
 * =========================================
 *
 * Central browser communication with the FastAPI backend.
 *
 * Features:
 * - Same-origin API request restriction.
 * - Browser-managed HttpOnly session cookies.
 * - JSON size limits and runtime validators.
 * - Request timeouts and optional cancellation.
 * - Controlled API errors without sensitive details.
 * - Dataset, patient, series and volume request methods.
 * - Authenticated PNG preview fetching.
 * - Bounded HTTP Range binary transfers.
 *
 * Security:
 * - No localStorage or sessionStorage credentials.
 * - No document.cookie access.
 * - No raw request/response logging.
 * - Never expose credentials or medical identifiers
 *   inside error messages.
 * - Server remains responsible for authorization.
 *
 * Python/Node dependencies are NOT installed by this file.
 * No direct DICOM filesystem operations occur here.
 */

import {
  getSafeAPIErrorMessage,
  isAPIErrorResponse,
  isDatasetSummary,
  isHealthResponse,
  isLoginResponse,
  isOpaqueHandle,
  isPatientSummary,
  isSeriesSummary,
  isSessionStatus,
  isVolumeLevel,
  isVolumeResponse,
  type DatasetCloseResponse,
  type DatasetListResponse,
  type DatasetResponse,
  type DatasetSelectRequest,
  type HealthResponse,
  type LoginRequest,
  type LoginResponse,
  type LogoutResponse,
  type PatientListResponse,
  type PreviewResponse,
  type PublicErrorCode,
  type SeriesListResponse,
  type SeriesValidationResponse,
  type SessionStatus,
  type StreamResponseHeaders,
  type VolumeLevel,
  type VolumeResponse,
} from "../types/api";


// ============================================================
// 01 — CONSTANTS
// ============================================================

const API_PREFIX = "/api/";

const DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS = 15000;
const VOLUME_TIMEOUT_MS = 120000;

const MAX_JSON_BYTES = 2 * 1024 * 1024;
const MAX_PNG_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024;
const MAX_BINARY_BYTES = 64 * 1024 * 1024;

const JSON_MIME = "application/json";
const PNG_MIME = "image/png";
const BINARY_MIME = "application/octet-stream";

const REQUEST_HEADERS = {
  Accept: JSON_MIME,
} as const;


// ============================================================
// 02 — SAFE ERROR CLASS
// ============================================================

export type ClientErrorCode =
  | PublicErrorCode
  | "NETWORK_ERROR"
  | "REQUEST_ABORTED"
  | "INVALID_RESPONSE";

export class APIClientError extends Error {
  readonly code: ClientErrorCode;
  readonly status: number | null;
  readonly retryable: boolean;

  constructor(
    code: ClientErrorCode,
    message: string,
    status: number | null = null,
    retryable = false,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "APIClientError";
    this.code = code;
    this.status = status;
    this.retryable = retryable;
  }
}

function invalidResponse(): APIClientError {
  return new APIClientError(
    "INVALID_RESPONSE",
    "The server returned an invalid response.",
  );
}

function safeError(
  status: number,
  payload: unknown,
): APIClientError {
  if (isAPIErrorResponse(payload)) {
    return new APIClientError(
      payload.error.code,
      getSafeAPIErrorMessage(payload.error.code),
      status,
      payload.error.retryable,
    );
  }

  const fallback: PublicErrorCode =
    status === 401 ? "AUTH_REQUIRED" :
    status === 403 ? "ACCESS_DENIED" :
    status === 404 ? "INVALID_REQUEST" :
    status === 413 ? "REQUEST_TOO_LARGE" :
    status === 416 ? "RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE" :
    status === 429 ? "RATE_LIMITED" :
    status === 503 ? "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE" :
    "PROCESSING_FAILED";

  return new APIClientError(
    fallback,
    getSafeAPIErrorMessage(fallback),
    status,
    status === 429 || status === 503,
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 03 — LIGHTWEIGHT VALIDATION
// ============================================================

type Validator<T> = (value: unknown) => value is T;

function isRecord(
  value: unknown,
): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return (
    typeof value === "object" &&
    value !== null &&
    !Array.isArray(value)
  );
}

function isSuccess(
  value: unknown,
): value is Record<string, unknown> & { success: true } {
  return isRecord(value) && value.success === true;
}

function isDatasetResponse(
  value: unknown,
): value is DatasetResponse {
  return (
    isSuccess(value) &&
    isDatasetSummary(value.dataset)
  );
}

function isDatasetListResponse(
  value: unknown,
): value is DatasetListResponse {
  return (
    isSuccess(value) &&
    Array.isArray(value.datasets) &&
    value.datasets.length <= 100 &&
    value.datasets.every(isDatasetSummary)
  );
}

function isDatasetCloseResponse(
  value: unknown,
): value is DatasetCloseResponse {
  return isSuccess(value) && value.closed === true;
}

function isPatientListResponse(
  value: unknown,
): value is PatientListResponse {
  return (
    isSuccess(value) &&
    isOpaqueHandle(value.dataset_session_id) &&
    Array.isArray(value.patients) &&
    value.patients.length <= 100000 &&
    value.patients.every(isPatientSummary)
  );
}

function isSeriesListResponse(
  value: unknown,
): value is SeriesListResponse {
  return (
    isSuccess(value) &&
    isOpaqueHandle(value.dataset_session_id) &&
    isOpaqueHandle(value.patient_handle) &&
    Array.isArray(value.series) &&
    value.series.length <= 100000 &&
    value.series.every(isSeriesSummary)
  );
}

function isSeriesValidationResponse(
  value: unknown,
): value is SeriesValidationResponse {
  return (
    isSuccess(value) &&
    isOpaqueHandle(value.series_handle) &&
    typeof value.valid === "boolean" &&
    typeof value.volume_ready === "boolean" &&
    Number.isSafeInteger(value.file_count) &&
    Number(value.file_count) >= 0 &&
    Number.isSafeInteger(value.frame_count) &&
    Number(value.frame_count) >= 0 &&
    Array.isArray(value.issues) &&
    value.issues.length <= 256 &&
    value.issues.every(
      (issue: unknown) =>
        isRecord(issue) &&
        typeof issue.code === "string" &&
        /^[A-Z0-9_]{1,64}$/.test(issue.code) &&
        (
          issue.severity === "info" ||
          issue.severity === "warning" ||
          issue.severity === "error"
        ),
    )
  );
}

function isPreviewResponse(
  value: unknown,
): value is PreviewResponse {
  if (
    !isSuccess(value) ||
    !isOpaqueHandle(value.series_handle) ||
    !isRecord(value.previews)
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  const previews = value.previews;

  for (const plane of [
    "axial",
    "coronal",
    "sagittal",
  ] as const) {
    const image = previews[plane];

    if (
      !isRecord(image) ||
      image.plane !== plane ||
      image.dtype !== "uint8" ||
      !Array.isArray(image.shape_yx) ||
      image.shape_yx.length !== 2 ||
      !image.shape_yx.every(
        (dimension: unknown) =>
          Number.isSafeInteger(dimension) &&
          Number(dimension) > 0,
      ) ||
      image.diagnostic_quality !== false
    ) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  return previews.diagnostic_quality === false;
}

function isLogoutResponse(
  value: unknown,
): value is LogoutResponse {
  return isSuccess(value) && value.authenticated === false;
}


// ============================================================
// 04 — SAFE PATH BUILDING
// ============================================================

function apiPath(
  path: string,
): string {
  if (
    !path.startsWith(API_PREFIX) ||
    path.includes("://") ||
    path.includes("\\") ||
    path.includes("?") ||
    path.includes("#") ||
    path.split("/").some(
      (segment) => segment === "." || segment === "..",
    ) ||
    /[\u0000-\u001f\u007f]/.test(path)
  ) {
    throw new APIClientError(
      "INVALID_RESPONSE",
      "An invalid API endpoint was requested.",
    );
  }

  return path;
}

function handlePath(value: string): string {
  if (!isOpaqueHandle(value)) {
    throw new APIClientError(
      "INVALID_RESPONSE",
      "An invalid resource identifier was provided.",
    );
  }

  return encodeURIComponent(value);
}


// ============================================================
// 05 — RESPONSE SIZE HANDLING
// ============================================================

async function readBoundedBytes(
  response: Response,
  maximumBytes: number,
): Promise<Uint8Array> {
  const declared = response.headers.get("Content-Length");

  if (declared !== null) {
    const size = Number(declared);

    if (
      !Number.isSafeInteger(size) ||
      size < 0 ||
      size > maximumBytes
    ) {
      throw invalidResponse();
    }
  }

  const reader = response.body?.getReader();

  if (!reader) {
    throw invalidResponse();
  }

  const chunks: Uint8Array[] = [];
  let total = 0;

  try {
    while (true) {
      const result = await reader.read();

      if (result.done) {
        break;
      }

      total += result.value.byteLength;

      if (total > maximumBytes) {
        await reader.cancel();
        throw invalidResponse();
      }

      chunks.push(result.value);
    }
  } finally {
    reader.releaseLock();
  }

  if (
    declared !== null &&
    total !== Number(declared)
  ) {
    throw invalidResponse();
  }

  const output = new Uint8Array(total);
  let offset = 0;

  for (const chunk of chunks) {
    output.set(chunk, offset);
    offset += chunk.byteLength;
  }

  return output;
}

async function parseBoundedJSON(
  response: Response,
): Promise<unknown> {
  const contentType =
    response.headers.get("Content-Type") ?? "";

  if (
    !contentType.toLowerCase().startsWith(JSON_MIME)
  ) {
    throw invalidResponse();
  }

  const bytes = await readBoundedBytes(
    response,
    MAX_JSON_BYTES,
  );

  try {
    return JSON.parse(
      new TextDecoder("utf-8", {
        fatal: true,
      }).decode(bytes),
    ) as unknown;
  } catch {
    throw invalidResponse();
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 06 — SECURE FETCH ENGINE
// ============================================================

interface RequestOptions {
  readonly method?: "GET" | "POST" | "DELETE";
  readonly body?: unknown;
  readonly timeoutMs?: number;
  readonly signal?: AbortSignal;
  readonly headers?: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
}

async function performRequest(
  endpoint: string,
  options: RequestOptions = {},
): Promise<Response> {
  const method = options.method ?? "GET";
  const timeoutMs = options.timeoutMs ?? DEFAULT_TIMEOUT_MS;

  if (
    !Number.isSafeInteger(timeoutMs) ||
    timeoutMs < 1000 ||
    timeoutMs > 300000
  ) {
    throw new APIClientError(
      "INVALID_RESPONSE",
      "Invalid request timeout.",
    );
  }

  const controller = new AbortController();

  const abortFromCaller = () => controller.abort();

  options.signal?.addEventListener(
    "abort",
    abortFromCaller,
    { once: true },
  );

  if (options.signal?.aborted) {
    controller.abort();
  }

  const timeout = globalThis.setTimeout(
    () => controller.abort(),
    timeoutMs,
  );

  try {
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {
      ...REQUEST_HEADERS,
      ...options.headers,
    };

    let body: string | undefined;

    if (options.body !== undefined) {
      headers["Content-Type"] = JSON_MIME;
      body = JSON.stringify(options.body);
    }

    const response = await fetch(
      apiPath(endpoint),
      {
        method,
        credentials: "include",
        cache: "no-store",
        redirect: "error",
        mode: "same-origin",
        referrerPolicy: "no-referrer",
        headers,
        body,
        signal: controller.signal,
      },
    );

    return response;
  } catch {
    if (controller.signal.aborted) {
      throw new APIClientError(
        "REQUEST_ABORTED",
        "The request was cancelled or timed out.",
      );
    }

    throw new APIClientError(
      "NETWORK_ERROR",
      "Unable to communicate with the server.",
      null,
      true,
    );
  } finally {
    globalThis.clearTimeout(timeout);

    options.signal?.removeEventListener(
      "abort",
      abortFromCaller,
    );
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 07 — JSON API REQUEST ENGINE
// ============================================================

async function requestJSON<T>(
  endpoint: string,
  validator: Validator<T>,
  options: RequestOptions = {},
): Promise<T> {
  const response = await performRequest(
    endpoint,
    options,
  );

  const payload = await parseBoundedJSON(response);

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw safeError(
      response.status,
      payload,
    );
  }

  if (!validator(payload)) {
    throw invalidResponse();
  }

  return payload;
}


// ============================================================
// 08 — AUTHENTICATION
// ============================================================

export async function apiLogin(
  credentials: LoginRequest,
): Promise<LoginResponse> {
  return requestJSON(
    "/api/auth/login",
    isLoginResponse,
    {
      method: "POST",
      body: credentials,
    },
  );
}

export async function apiSession(): Promise<SessionStatus> {
  return requestJSON(
    "/api/auth/session",
    isSessionStatus,
  );
}

export async function apiLogout(): Promise<LogoutResponse> {
  return requestJSON(
    "/api/auth/logout",
    isLogoutResponse,
    {
      method: "POST",
    },
  );
}

): Promise<VolumeLevel> {

  ) {
    throw invalidResponse();

  return requestJSON(
    `/api/volumes/${handlePath(datasetSessionId)}` +
      `/${handlePath(seriesHandle)}/levels/${level}`,
    isVolumeLevel,
  );
}

// ============================================================
// 13 — BINARY AND PNG HELPERS
// ============================================================

async function requestBytes(
  endpoint: string,
  {
  mimeType,
  maximumBytes,
  headers,
  signal,
  timeoutMs,
}: {
  mimeType: string;
  maximumBytes: number;
  headers?: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
  timeoutMs?: number;
}): Promise<{
  bytes: Uint8Array;
  response: Response;
}> {
  const response = await performRequest(
    endpoint,
    {
      headers: {
        Accept: mimeType,
        ...headers,
      },
      signal,
      timeoutMs,
    },
  );

  if (!response.ok) {
    let payload: unknown = null;

    try {
      payload = await parseBoundedJSON(response);
    } catch {
      // No raw server response is exposed to the caller.
    }

    throw safeError(response.status, payload);
  }

  const receivedType =
    response.headers.get("Content-Type") ?? "";

  if (
    !receivedType.toLowerCase().startsWith(mimeType)
  ) {
    throw invalidResponse();
  }

  const bytes = await readBoundedBytes(
    response,
    maximumBytes,
  );

  return {
    bytes,
    response,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 14 — AUTHENTICATED PNG PREVIEW
// ============================================================

export async function apiGetPreviewPNG(
  datasetSessionId: string,
  seriesHandle: string,
  plane: "axial" | "coronal" | "sagittal",
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<Blob> {
  if (
    plane !== "axial" &&
    plane !== "coronal" &&
    plane !== "sagittal"
  ) {
    throw invalidResponse();
  }

  const endpoint =
    `/api/previews/${handlePath(datasetSessionId)}` +
    `/${handlePath(seriesHandle)}/${plane}.png`;

  const { bytes } = await requestBytes(
    endpoint,
    {
      mimeType: PNG_MIME,
      maximumBytes: MAX_PNG_BYTES,
      signal,
      timeoutMs: VOLUME_TIMEOUT_MS,
    },
  );

  const pngSignature = [
    137, 80, 78, 71, 13, 10, 26, 10,
  ];

  if (
    bytes.length < pngSignature.length ||
    !pngSignature.every(
      (value, index) => bytes[index] === value,
    )
  ) {
    throw invalidResponse();
  }

  const copy = new Uint8Array(bytes.byteLength);
  copy.set(bytes);

  return new Blob(
    [copy],
    {
      type: PNG_MIME,
    },
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 15 — HTTP BYTE RANGE FETCH
// ============================================================

export interface BinaryRangeResult {
  readonly bytes: Uint8Array;
  readonly headers: StreamResponseHeaders;
  readonly status: 206;
}

export async function apiGetVolumeRange(
  datasetSessionId: string,
  seriesHandle: string,
  level: number,
  offset: number,
  length: number,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<BinaryRangeResult> {
  if (
    !Number.isSafeInteger(level) ||
    level < 0 ||
    level > 64 ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(offset) ||
    offset < 0 ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(length) ||
    length < 1 ||
    length > MAX_BINARY_BYTES
  ) {
    throw invalidResponse();
  }

  const end = offset + length - 1;

  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(end)) {
    throw invalidResponse();
  }

  const endpoint =
    `/api/streaming/${handlePath(datasetSessionId)}` +
    `/${handlePath(seriesHandle)}` +
    `/levels/${level}/bytes`;

  const { bytes, response } = await requestBytes(
    endpoint,
    {
      mimeType: BINARY_MIME,
      maximumBytes: MAX_BINARY_BYTES,
      signal,
      timeoutMs: VOLUME_TIMEOUT_MS,
      headers: {
        Range: `bytes=${offset}-${end}`,
      },
    },
  );

  if (
    response.status !== 206 ||
    bytes.length !== length
  ) {
    throw invalidResponse();
  }

  const contentRange =
    response.headers.get("Content-Range");

  if (contentRange === null) {
    throw invalidResponse();
  }

  const rangeMatch =
    /^bytes ([0-9]+)-([0-9]+)\/([0-9]+)$/.exec(
      contentRange,
    );

  if (!rangeMatch) {
    throw invalidResponse();
  }

  const responseStart = Number(rangeMatch[1]);
  const responseEnd = Number(rangeMatch[2]);
  const totalBytes = Number(rangeMatch[3]);

  if (
    !Number.isSafeInteger(totalBytes) ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(responseStart) ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(responseEnd) ||
    responseStart !== offset ||
    responseEnd !== end ||
    totalBytes <= end
  ) {
    throw invalidResponse();
  }

  return {
    bytes,
    status: 206,
    headers: {
      contentLength: bytes.byteLength,
      contentRange,
      acceptRanges: response.headers.get("Accept-Ranges"),
      contentType: BINARY_MIME,
    },
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 16 — PUBLIC HEALTH CHECK
// ============================================================

export async function apiHealth(): Promise<HealthResponse> {
  const response = await performRequest("/api/health");

  const payload = await parseBoundedJSON(response);

  // A degraded backend may legitimately return HTTP 503.
  if (
    (response.status === 200 || response.status === 503) &&
    isHealthResponse(payload)
  ) {
    return payload;
  }

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw safeError(response.status, payload);
  }

  throw invalidResponse();
}


// ============================================================
// 17 — ERROR HELPERS
// ============================================================

export function isAuthenticationFailure(
  error: unknown,
): boolean {
  return (
    error instanceof APIClientError &&
    (
      error.status === 401 ||
      error.code === "AUTH_REQUIRED" ||
      error.code === "SESSION_EXPIRED"
    )
  );
}

export function getPublicErrorText(
  error: unknown,
): string {
  if (error instanceof APIClientError) {
    return error.message;
  }

  return "The operation could not be completed.";
}


// ============================================================
// END OF SINUS VIEWER API CLIENT
// ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/frontend/src/services/datasetService.ts
```
/**
 * SINUS VIEWER — FRONTEND DATASET SERVICE
 * =======================================
 *
 * Coordinates:
 * - Dataset selection, listing, refresh and close.
 * - Authorized patient and series discovery.
 * - Series validation.
 * - React-compatible state subscriptions.
 * - Stale response suppression using request versions.
 * - Safe errors and authentication-expiry handling.
 *
 * Security:
 * - Only opaque handles are retained in browser state.
 * - The selected filesystem path is never retained.
 * - Authentication remains enforced by FastAPI.
 * - No credential, patient metadata or image bytes are logged.
 * - No localStorage/sessionStorage persistence.
 * - No original DICOM dataset operations.
 *
 * Note:
 * File 45's JSON API functions do not yet accept AbortSignal.
 * Therefore cancellation here is logical cancellation:
 * outdated results are ignored, but HTTP requests may finish.
 */

import {
  apiCloseDataset,
  apiGetDataset,
  apiListDatasets,
  apiListPatients,
  apiListSeries,
  apiRefreshDataset,
  apiSelectDataset,
  apiValidateSeries,
  getPublicErrorText,
  isAuthenticationFailure,
} from "./apiClient";

import {
  isOpaqueHandle,
  type DatasetSummary,
  type PatientSummary,
  type SeriesSummary,
  type SeriesValidationResponse,
} from "../types/api";


// ============================================================
// 01 — PUBLIC STATE TYPES
// ============================================================

export type DatasetOperation =
  | "idle"
  | "listing"
  | "selecting"
  | "refreshing"
  | "closing"
  | "loading-patients"
  | "loading-series"
  | "validating-series";

export type DatasetServiceStatus =
  | "idle"
  | "loading"
  | "ready"
  | "error"
  | "authentication-required";

export interface DatasetBrowserState {
  readonly status: DatasetServiceStatus;
  readonly operation: DatasetOperation;
  readonly error: string | null;

  readonly datasets: readonly DatasetSummary[];
  readonly selectedDataset: DatasetSummary | null;

  readonly patients: readonly PatientSummary[];
  readonly selectedPatientHandle: string | null;

  readonly series: readonly SeriesSummary[];
  readonly selectedSeriesHandle: string | null;

  readonly validation: SeriesValidationResponse | null;
}

export type DatasetStateListener = (
  state: DatasetBrowserState,
) => void;


// ============================================================
// 02 — INITIAL STATE
// ============================================================

function initialState(): DatasetBrowserState {
  return {
    status: "idle",
    operation: "idle",
    error: null,

    datasets: [],
    selectedDataset: null,

    patients: [],
    selectedPatientHandle: null,

    series: [],
    selectedSeriesHandle: null,

    validation: null,
  };
}

function clearSelection(): Pick<
  DatasetBrowserState,
  | "selectedDataset"
  | "patients"
  | "selectedPatientHandle"
  | "series"
  | "selectedSeriesHandle"
  | "validation"
> {
  return {
    selectedDataset: null,
    patients: [],
    selectedPatientHandle: null,
    series: [],
    selectedSeriesHandle: null,
    validation: null,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 03 — CONTROLLED SERVICE ERRORS
// ============================================================

export class DatasetBrowserError extends Error {
  readonly authenticationRequired: boolean;

  constructor(
    message: string,
    authenticationRequired = false,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "DatasetBrowserError";
    this.authenticationRequired = authenticationRequired;
  }
}

function publicError(error: unknown): DatasetBrowserError {
  const authFailure = isAuthenticationFailure(error);

  return new DatasetBrowserError(
    authFailure
      ? "Your session has expired. Sign in again."
      : getPublicErrorText(error),
    authFailure,
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 04 — STATE MANAGEMENT
// ============================================================

export class FrontendDatasetService {
  private currentState: DatasetBrowserState = initialState();

  private readonly listeners =
    new Set<DatasetStateListener>();

  /**
   * Each new operation invalidates previous operations.
   * A response may update state only if its request ID
   * still matches the current request generation.
   */
  private requestVersion = 0;

  /**
   * Changing dataset selection also invalidates dependent
   * patient / series / validation response data.
   */
  private selectionVersion = 0;

  private disposed = false;

  getSnapshot = (): DatasetBrowserState => {
    return this.currentState;
  };

  subscribe = (
    listener: DatasetStateListener,
  ): (() => void) => {
    if (this.disposed) {
      return () => undefined;
    }

    this.listeners.add(listener);

    return () => {
      this.listeners.delete(listener);
    };
  };

  private publish(
    next: DatasetBrowserState,
  ): void {
    if (this.disposed) {
      return;
    }

    this.currentState = next;

    for (const listener of this.listeners) {
      try {
        listener(next);
      } catch {
        // A listener cannot prevent other subscribers
        // from receiving state updates.
      }
    }
  }

  private patch(
    change: Partial<DatasetBrowserState>,
  ): void {
    this.publish({
      ...this.currentState,
      ...change,
    });
  }

  private start(
    operation: DatasetOperation,
  ): number {
    const version = ++this.requestVersion;

    this.patch({
      status: "loading",
      operation,
      error: null,
    });

    return version;
  }

  private current(version: number): boolean {
    return (
      !this.disposed &&
      version === this.requestVersion
    );
  }

  private finishError(
    error: unknown,
    version: number,
  ): void {
    if (!this.current(version)) {
      return;
    }

    const failure = publicError(error);

    if (failure.authenticationRequired) {
      this.selectionVersion++;

      this.patch({
        ...clearSelection(),
        datasets: [],
        status: "authentication-required",
        operation: "idle",
        error: failure.message,
      });

      return;
    }

    this.patch({
      status: "error",
      operation: "idle",
      error: failure.message,
    });
  }

  private requireHandle(value: string): void {
    if (!isOpaqueHandle(value)) {
      throw new DatasetBrowserError(
        "The selected resource is invalid.",
      );
    }
  }

  private ensureActive(): void {
    if (this.disposed) {
      throw new DatasetBrowserError(
        "The dataset browser is unavailable.",
      );
    }
  }

  /**
   * Explicitly invalidate pending updates.
   *
   * This does not abort network requests in File 45.
   * It prevents completed stale responses from being used.
   */
  cancelPending(): void {
    if (this.disposed) {
      return;
    }

    this.requestVersion++;
    this.selectionVersion++;

    this.patch({
      status: this.currentState.selectedDataset
        ? "ready"
        : "idle",
      operation: "idle",
      error: null,
    });
  }

  /**
   * Called during sign-out or session invalidation.
   * Clears patient-linked browser state immediately.
   */
  reset(): void {
    if (this.disposed) {
      return;
    }

    this.requestVersion++;
    this.selectionVersion++;

    this.publish(initialState());
  }

  /**
   * Dispose per-component service instances.
   * No request, file or backend session deletion occurs.
   */
  dispose(): void {
    if (this.disposed) {
      return;
    }

    this.requestVersion++;
    this.selectionVersion++;
    this.disposed = true;

    this.listeners.clear();
    this.currentState = initialState();
  }

  // ==========================================================
  // 05 — LIST DATASETS
  // ==========================================================

  async listDatasets(): Promise<void> {
    this.ensureActive();

    const version = this.start("listing");

    try {
      const response = await apiListDatasets();

      if (!this.current(version)) {
        return;
      }

      const selectedId =
        this.currentState.selectedDataset
          ?.dataset_session_id;

      const selected = selectedId
        ? response.datasets.find(
            (item) => item.dataset_session_id === selectedId,
          ) ?? null
        : null;

      const selectionChanged =
        Boolean(selectedId) && selected === null;

      if (selectionChanged) {
        this.selectionVersion++;
      }

      this.patch({
        ...(selectionChanged ? clearSelection() : {}),
        datasets: [...response.datasets],
        selectedDataset: selected,
        status: "ready",
        operation: "idle",
        error: null,
      });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.finishError(error, version);
    }
  }

  // ==========================================================
  // 06 — SELECT DATASET
  // ==========================================================

  async selectDataset(datasetPath: string): Promise<void> {
    this.ensureActive();

    if (
      typeof datasetPath !== "string" ||
      datasetPath.length < 1 ||
      datasetPath.length > 2048
    ) {
      throw new DatasetBrowserError(
        "Enter a valid dataset location.",
      );
    }

    const version = this.start("selecting");

    // Never retain the original selection path in state.
    this.selectionVersion++;

    this.patch(clearSelection());

    try {
      const response = await apiSelectDataset({
        dataset_path: datasetPath,
      });

      if (!this.current(version)) {
        return;
      }

      const dataset = response.dataset;

      const others = this.currentState.datasets.filter(
        (item) =>
          item.dataset_session_id !==
          dataset.dataset_session_id,
      );

      this.patch({
        ...clearSelection(),
        datasets: [...others, dataset],
        selectedDataset: dataset,
        status: "ready",
        operation: "idle",
        error: null,
      });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.finishError(error, version);
    }
  }

  // ==========================================================
  // 07 — SWITCH SELECTED DATASET
  // ==========================================================

  selectExistingDataset(
    datasetSessionId: string,
  ): void {
    this.ensureActive();
    this.requireHandle(datasetSessionId);

    const dataset = this.currentState.datasets.find(
      (item) =>
        item.dataset_session_id === datasetSessionId,
    );

    if (!dataset) {
      throw new DatasetBrowserError(
        "The dataset is not available.",
      );
    }

    this.requestVersion++;
    this.selectionVersion++;

    this.patch({
      ...clearSelection(),
      selectedDataset: dataset,
      status: "ready",
      operation: "idle",
      error: null,
    });
  }

  // ==========================================================
  // 08 — RELOAD SELECTED DATASET STATUS
  // ==========================================================

  async reloadSelectedDataset(): Promise<void> {
    this.ensureActive();

    const selected =
      this.currentState.selectedDataset;

    if (!selected) {
      throw new DatasetBrowserError(
        "Select a dataset first.",
      );
    }

    const id = selected.dataset_session_id;
    const version = this.start("listing");
    const selectionVersion = this.selectionVersion;

    try {
      const response = await apiGetDataset(id);

      if (
        !this.current(version) ||
        selectionVersion !== this.selectionVersion
      ) {
        return;
      }

      const updated = response.dataset;

      const generationChanged =
        updated.generation !== selected.generation;

      if (generationChanged) {
        this.selectionVersion++;
      }

      this.patch({
        ...(generationChanged
          ? {
              patients: [],
              selectedPatientHandle: null,
              series: [],
              selectedSeriesHandle: null,
              validation: null,
            }
          : {}),
        datasets: this.currentState.datasets.map(
          (item) =>
            item.dataset_session_id === id
              ? updated
              : item,
        ),
        selectedDataset: updated,
        status: "ready",
        operation: "idle",
        error: null,
      });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.finishError(error, version);
    }
  }

  // ==========================================================
  // 09 — REFRESH DATASET
  // ==========================================================

  async refreshDataset(): Promise<void> {
    this.ensureActive();

    const selected =
      this.currentState.selectedDataset;

    if (!selected) {
      throw new DatasetBrowserError(
        "Select a dataset first.",
      );
    }

    const version = this.start("refreshing");
    const id = selected.dataset_session_id;

    this.selectionVersion++;

    this.patch({
      patients: [],
      selectedPatientHandle: null,
      series: [],
      selectedSeriesHandle: null,
      validation: null,
    });

    try {
      const response = await apiRefreshDataset(id);

      if (!this.current(version)) {
        return;
      }

      const updated = response.dataset;

      this.patch({
        datasets: this.currentState.datasets.map(
          (item) =>
            item.dataset_session_id === id
              ? updated
              : item,
        ),
        selectedDataset: updated,
        status: "ready",
        operation: "idle",
        error: null,
      });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.finishError(error, version);
    }
  }

  // ==========================================================
  // 10 — CLOSE DATASET
  // ==========================================================

  async closeDataset(
    datasetSessionId?: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    this.ensureActive();

    const id = datasetSessionId ??
      this.currentState.selectedDataset
        ?.dataset_session_id;

    if (!id) {
      throw new DatasetBrowserError(
        "Select a dataset first.",
      );
    }

    this.requireHandle(id);

    const version = this.start("closing");

    try {
      await apiCloseDataset(id);

      if (!this.current(version)) {
        return;
      }

      const selectedClosed =
        this.currentState.selectedDataset
          ?.dataset_session_id === id;

      if (selectedClosed) {
        this.selectionVersion++;
      }

      this.patch({
        ...(selectedClosed ? clearSelection() : {}),
        datasets: this.currentState.datasets.filter(
          (item) =>
            item.dataset_session_id !== id,
        ),
        status: "ready",
        operation: "idle",
        error: null,
      });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.finishError(error, version);
    }
  }

  // ==========================================================
  // 11 — LOAD PATIENTS
  // ==========================================================

  async loadPatients(): Promise<void> {
    this.ensureActive();

    const dataset =
      this.currentState.selectedDataset;

    if (!dataset) {
      throw new DatasetBrowserError(
        "Select a dataset first.",
      );
    }

    const datasetId = dataset.dataset_session_id;
    const version = this.start("loading-patients");
    const selectionVersion = this.selectionVersion;

    this.patch({
      patients: [],
      selectedPatientHandle: null,
      series: [],
      selectedSeriesHandle: null,
      validation: null,
    });

    try {
      const response = await apiListPatients(datasetId);

      if (
        !this.current(version) ||
        selectionVersion !== this.selectionVersion ||
        response.dataset_session_id !== datasetId
      ) {
        return;
      }

      this.patch({
        patients: [...response.patients],
        status: "ready",
        operation: "idle",
        error: null,
      });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.finishError(error, version);
    }
  }

  // ==========================================================
  // 12 — SELECT PATIENT AND LOAD SERIES
  // ==========================================================

  async selectPatient(
    patientHandle: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    this.ensureActive();
    this.requireHandle(patientHandle);

    const dataset =
      this.currentState.selectedDataset;

    const patient = this.currentState.patients.find(
      (item) =>
        item.patient_handle === patientHandle,
    );

    if (!dataset || !patient) {
      throw new DatasetBrowserError(
        "The patient selection is unavailable.",
      );
    }

    const version = this.start("loading-series");
    const selectionVersion = ++this.selectionVersion;
    const datasetId = dataset.dataset_session_id;

    this.patch({
      selectedPatientHandle: patientHandle,
      series: [],
      selectedSeriesHandle: null,
      validation: null,
    });

    try {
      const response = await apiListSeries(
        datasetId,
        patientHandle,
      );

      if (
        !this.current(version) ||
        selectionVersion !== this.selectionVersion ||
        response.dataset_session_id !== datasetId ||
        response.patient_handle !== patientHandle
      ) {
        return;
      }

      this.patch({
        series: [...response.series],
        status: "ready",
        operation: "idle",
        error: null,
      });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.finishError(error, version);
    }
  }

  // ==========================================================
  // 13 — SELECT AND VALIDATE SERIES
  // ==========================================================

  async selectSeries(
    seriesHandle: string,
  ): Promise<void> {
    this.ensureActive();
    this.requireHandle(seriesHandle);

    const dataset =
      this.currentState.selectedDataset;

    const series = this.currentState.series.find(
      (item) =>
        item.series_handle === seriesHandle,
    );

    if (!dataset || !series) {
      throw new DatasetBrowserError(
        "The series selection is unavailable.",
      );
    }

    const version = this.start("validating-series");
    const selectionVersion = ++this.selectionVersion;
    const datasetId = dataset.dataset_session_id;

    this.patch({
      selectedSeriesHandle: seriesHandle,
      validation: null,
    });

    try {
      const result = await apiValidateSeries(
        datasetId,
        seriesHandle,
      );

      if (
        !this.current(version) ||
        selectionVersion !== this.selectionVersion ||
        result.series_handle !== seriesHandle
      ) {
        return;
      }

      this.patch({
        validation: result,
        status: "ready",
        operation: "idle",
        error: null,
      });
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      this.finishError(error, version);
    }
  }

  // ==========================================================
  // 14 — VIEWER SELECTION
  // ==========================================================

  getViewerSelection(): {
    datasetSessionId: string;
    seriesHandle: string;
  } | null {
    const dataset = this.currentState.selectedDataset;
    const seriesHandle =
      this.currentState.selectedSeriesHandle;
    const validation = this.currentState.validation;

    if (
      !dataset ||
      !seriesHandle ||
      !validation ||
      validation.series_handle !== seriesHandle ||
      !validation.volume_ready
    ) {
      return null;
    }

    return {
      datasetSessionId: dataset.dataset_session_id,
      seriesHandle,
    };
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 15 — FACTORY
// ============================================================

export function createDatasetService():
  FrontendDatasetService {
  return new FrontendDatasetService();
}


// ============================================================
// END OF FRONTEND DATASET SERVICE
// ============================================================
```
