### sinus-viewer/frontend/src/services/previewService.ts
```
/**
 * SINUS VIEWER — FRONTEND PREVIEW SERVICE
 * =======================================
 *
 * Features:
 * - Authorized axial / coronal / sagittal previews.
 * - Preview metadata and image loading.
 * - Sequential PNG downloads to limit server contention.
 * - Browser object URL creation and lifecycle management.
 * - Cancellation and stale-response protection.
 * - React useSyncExternalStore-compatible subscriptions.
 * - Cleanup on dataset change, logout or unmount.
 *
 * Security:
 * - No patient identifiers or raw DICOM metadata in state.
 * - Only opaque, server-generated handles are accepted.
 * - PNGs are held in memory; never written to browser storage.
 * - No raw HTTP responses, paths or errors are logged.
 * - Blob URLs must not be persisted or shared.
 * - Backend authentication and ownership remain mandatory.
 *
 * Preview images are navigation aids, NOT diagnostic images.
 *
 * No filesystem operations.
 */

import {
  apiGetPreviewPNG,
  apiGetPreviews,
  getPublicErrorText,
  isAuthenticationFailure,
} from "./apiClient";

import {
  isOpaqueHandle,
  type PreviewDescriptor,
  type PreviewImage,
  type PreviewPlane,
} from "../types/api";


// ============================================================
// 01 — TYPES AND CONSTANTS
// ============================================================

export const PREVIEW_PLANES = [
  "axial",
  "coronal",
  "sagittal",
] as const;

export type PreviewLoadStatus =
  | "idle"
  | "loading"
  | "ready"
  | "cancelled"
  | "error"
  | "authentication-required";

export interface PreviewResource {
  readonly plane: PreviewPlane;
  readonly url: string;
  readonly metadata: PreviewImage;
}

export interface PreviewResources {
  readonly axial: PreviewResource;
  readonly coronal: PreviewResource;
  readonly sagittal: PreviewResource;
}

export interface PreviewLoadProgress {
  readonly status: PreviewLoadStatus;
  readonly completedPlanes: number;
  readonly totalPlanes: 3;
  readonly currentPlane: PreviewPlane | null;
  readonly percentage: number;
  readonly error: string | null;
}

export interface PreviewServiceSnapshot {
  readonly datasetSessionId: string | null;
  readonly seriesHandle: string | null;
  readonly descriptor: PreviewDescriptor | null;
  readonly images: PreviewResources | null;
  readonly progress: PreviewLoadProgress;
}

export interface PreviewLoadOptions {
  readonly signal?: AbortSignal;
}

export type PreviewStateListener = (
  snapshot: PreviewServiceSnapshot,
) => void;

const TOTAL_PLANES = 3 as const;


// ============================================================
// 02 — CONTROLLED PUBLIC ERRORS
// ============================================================

export type FrontendPreviewErrorCode =
  | "INVALID_SELECTION"
  | "INVALID_PREVIEW"
  | "CANCELLED"
  | "AUTH_REQUIRED"
  | "NETWORK_FAILURE";

export class FrontendPreviewError extends Error {
  readonly code: FrontendPreviewErrorCode;

  constructor(
    code: FrontendPreviewErrorCode,
    message: string,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "FrontendPreviewError";
    this.code = code;
  }
}

function cancellationError(): FrontendPreviewError {
  return new FrontendPreviewError(
    "CANCELLED",
    "Preview loading was cancelled.",
  );
}

function publicError(error: unknown): FrontendPreviewError {
  if (error instanceof FrontendPreviewError) {
    return error;
  }

  if (isAuthenticationFailure(error)) {
    return new FrontendPreviewError(
      "AUTH_REQUIRED",
      "Your session has expired. Sign in again.",
    );
  }

  return new FrontendPreviewError(
    "NETWORK_FAILURE",
    getPublicErrorText(error),
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 03 — INITIAL SNAPSHOT
// ============================================================

function makeProgress(
  status: PreviewLoadStatus = "idle",
  completedPlanes = 0,
  currentPlane: PreviewPlane | null = null,
  error: string | null = null,
): PreviewLoadProgress {
  return {
    status,
    completedPlanes,
    totalPlanes: TOTAL_PLANES,
    currentPlane,
    percentage: Math.round(
      (completedPlanes / TOTAL_PLANES) * 100,
    ),
    error,
  };
}

function initialSnapshot(): PreviewServiceSnapshot {
  return {
    datasetSessionId: null,
    seriesHandle: null,
    descriptor: null,
    images: null,
    progress: makeProgress(),
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 04 — INPUT AND METADATA VALIDATION
// ============================================================

function validateSelection(
  datasetSessionId: string,
  seriesHandle: string,
): void {
  if (
    !isOpaqueHandle(datasetSessionId) ||
    !isOpaqueHandle(seriesHandle)
  ) {
    throw new FrontendPreviewError(
      "INVALID_SELECTION",
      "Select a valid dataset and series.",
    );
  }
}

function validateImageMetadata(
  image: PreviewImage,
  plane: PreviewPlane,
): void {
  const [height, width] = image.shape_yx;

  if (
    image.plane !== plane ||
    image.dtype !== "uint8" ||
    image.diagnostic_quality !== false ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(height) ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(width) ||
    height < 1 ||
    width < 1 ||
    height > 512 ||
    width > 512 ||
    image.byte_size !== height * width ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(image.sampled_slice_count) ||
    image.sampled_slice_count < 1 ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(image.representative_slice_index) ||
    image.representative_slice_index < 0 ||
    !Number.isFinite(image.window_low) ||
    !Number.isFinite(image.window_high) ||
    image.window_high <= image.window_low
  ) {
    throw new FrontendPreviewError(
      "INVALID_PREVIEW",
      "Invalid preview metadata.",
    );
  }
}

function validateDescriptor(
  descriptor: PreviewDescriptor,
): void {
  if (descriptor.diagnostic_quality !== false) {
    throw new FrontendPreviewError(
      "INVALID_PREVIEW",
      "Invalid preview quality declaration.",
    );
  }

  for (const plane of PREVIEW_PLANES) {
    validateImageMetadata(descriptor[plane], plane);
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 05 — OBJECT URL CLEANUP
// ============================================================

function revokePreviewURLs(
  resources: PreviewResources | null,
): void {
  if (!resources) {
    return;
  }

  for (const plane of PREVIEW_PLANES) {
    URL.revokeObjectURL(resources[plane].url);
  }
}

function revokePendingURLs(
  urls: readonly string[],
): void {
  for (const url of urls) {
    URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 06 — FRONTEND PREVIEW SERVICE
// ============================================================

export class FrontendPreviewService {
  private snapshot: PreviewServiceSnapshot =
    initialSnapshot();

  private readonly listeners =
    new Set<PreviewStateListener>();

  private requestVersion = 0;

  private controller: AbortController | null = null;

  private disposed = false;

  // ==========================================================
  // 07 — REACT STATE SUBSCRIPTIONS
  // ==========================================================

  getSnapshot = (): PreviewServiceSnapshot => {
    return this.snapshot;
  };

  subscribe = (
    listener: PreviewStateListener,
  ): (() => void) => {
    if (this.disposed) {
      return () => undefined;
    }

    this.listeners.add(listener);

    return () => {
      this.listeners.delete(listener);
    };
  };

  private publish(
    next: PreviewServiceSnapshot,
  ): void {
    if (this.disposed) {
      return;
    }

    this.snapshot = next;

    for (const listener of this.listeners) {
      try {
        listener(next);
      } catch {
        // UI listener failures cannot stop other listeners.
      }
    }
  }

  private isCurrent(
    version: number,
    controller: AbortController,
  ): boolean {
    return (
      !this.disposed &&
      this.requestVersion === version &&
      this.controller === controller &&
      !controller.signal.aborted
    );
  }

  private assertCurrent(
    version: number,
    controller: AbortController,
  ): void {
    if (!this.isCurrent(version, controller)) {
      throw cancellationError();
    }
  }

  private publishProgress(
    progress: PreviewLoadProgress,
  ): void {
    this.publish({
      ...this.snapshot,
      progress,
    });
  }

  // ==========================================================
  // 08 — CANCEL CURRENT LOADING
  // ==========================================================

  cancel(): void {
    if (this.disposed) {
      return;
    }

    this.requestVersion++;
    this.controller?.abort();
    this.controller = null;

    if (this.snapshot.progress.status === "loading") {
      this.publishProgress(
        makeProgress("cancelled"),
      );
    }
  }

  // ==========================================================
  // 09 — CLEAR ALL PREVIEW DATA
  // ==========================================================

  reset(): void {
    if (this.disposed) {
      return;
    }

    this.requestVersion++;
    this.controller?.abort();
    this.controller = null;

    const oldImages = this.snapshot.images;

    this.publish(initialSnapshot());

    revokePreviewURLs(oldImages);
  }

  // ==========================================================
  // 10 — DISPOSE
  // ==========================================================

  dispose(): void {
    if (this.disposed) {
      return;
    }

    this.requestVersion++;
    this.controller?.abort();
    this.controller = null;
    this.disposed = true;

    const oldImages = this.snapshot.images;

    this.listeners.clear();
    this.snapshot = initialSnapshot();

    revokePreviewURLs(oldImages);
  }

  // ==========================================================
  // 11 — LOAD THREE PREVIEW IMAGES
  // ==========================================================

  async load(
    datasetSessionId: string,
    seriesHandle: string,
    options: PreviewLoadOptions = {},
  ): Promise<PreviewResources> {
    if (this.disposed) {
      throw new FrontendPreviewError(
        "INVALID_SELECTION",
        "Preview service is unavailable.",
      );
    }

    validateSelection(datasetSessionId, seriesHandle);

    // Supersede the previous request.
    this.requestVersion++;
    const version = this.requestVersion;

    this.controller?.abort();

    const controller = new AbortController();
    this.controller = controller;

    const abortFromCaller = (): void => {
      controller.abort();
    };

    options.signal?.addEventListener(
      "abort",
      abortFromCaller,
      { once: true },
    );

    if (options.signal?.aborted) {
      controller.abort();
    }

    const oldImages = this.snapshot.images;

    // Remove old image references before loading the next
    // selection. Existing UI consumers must stop using URLs
    // from previous snapshots.
    this.publish({
      datasetSessionId,
      seriesHandle,
      descriptor: null,
      images: null,
      progress: makeProgress(
        "loading",
        0,
        "axial",
      ),
    });

    revokePreviewURLs(oldImages);

    // URLs created before completion remain owned by this
    // operation and must always be revoked on failure.
    const pendingURLs: string[] = [];

    let resourcesCommitted = false;

    try {
      this.assertCurrent(version, controller);

      // Metadata calls currently cannot accept AbortSignal,
      // so stale results are explicitly rejected afterward.
      const response = await apiGetPreviews(
        datasetSessionId,
        seriesHandle,
      );

      this.assertCurrent(version, controller);

      if (response.series_handle !== seriesHandle) {
        throw new FrontendPreviewError(
          "INVALID_PREVIEW",
          "Preview series mismatch.",
        );
      }

      const descriptor = response.previews;

      validateDescriptor(descriptor);

      const loaded = {} as Record<
        PreviewPlane,
        PreviewResource
      >;

      let completed = 0;

      // Sequential download prevents three concurrent
      // builds from competing for the backend semaphore.
      for (const plane of PREVIEW_PLANES) {
        this.assertCurrent(version, controller);

        this.publishProgress(
          makeProgress(
            "loading",
            completed,
            plane,
          ),
        );

        const blob = await apiGetPreviewPNG(
          datasetSessionId,
          seriesHandle,
          plane,
          controller.signal,
        );

        this.assertCurrent(version, controller);

        if (
          blob.type !== "image/png" ||
          blob.size < 8 ||
          blob.size > 8 * 1024 * 1024
        ) {
          throw new FrontendPreviewError(
            "INVALID_PREVIEW",
            "Invalid preview image.",
          );
        }

        const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);

        pendingURLs.push(url);

        loaded[plane] = {
          plane,
          url,
          metadata: descriptor[plane],
        };

        completed++;

        this.publishProgress(
          makeProgress(
            "loading",
            completed,
            completed < TOTAL_PLANES
              ? PREVIEW_PLANES[completed]
              : null,
          ),
        );
      }

      this.assertCurrent(version, controller);

      const images: PreviewResources = {
        axial: loaded.axial,
        coronal: loaded.coronal,
        sagittal: loaded.sagittal,
      };

      this.publish({
        datasetSessionId,
        seriesHandle,
        descriptor,
        images,
        progress: makeProgress(
          "ready",
          TOTAL_PLANES,
        ),
      });

      // The service snapshot now owns these URLs.
      resourcesCommitted = true;

      return images;
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      if (!this.isCurrent(version, controller)) {
        throw cancellationError();
      }

      const failure = publicError(error);

      this.publish({
        datasetSessionId,
        seriesHandle,
        descriptor: null,
        images: null,
        progress: makeProgress(
          failure.code === "AUTH_REQUIRED"
            ? "authentication-required"
            : failure.code === "CANCELLED"
              ? "cancelled"
              : "error",
          0,
          null,
          failure.message,
        ),
      });

      throw failure;
    } finally {
      options.signal?.removeEventListener(
        "abort",
        abortFromCaller,
      );

      if (!resourcesCommitted) {
        revokePendingURLs(pendingURLs);
      }

      if (this.controller === controller) {
        this.controller = null;
      }
    }
  }

  // ==========================================================
  // 12 — SAFE PREVIEW ACCESSORS
  // ==========================================================

  getPreview(
    plane: PreviewPlane,
  ): PreviewResource | null {
    if (this.disposed) {
      return null;
    }

    return this.snapshot.images?.[plane] ?? null;
  }

  hasPreviews(): boolean {
    return (
      !this.disposed &&
      this.snapshot.progress.status === "ready" &&
      this.snapshot.images !== null
    );
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 13 — SERVICE FACTORY
// ============================================================

export function createPreviewService():
  FrontendPreviewService {
  return new FrontendPreviewService();
}


// ============================================================
// END OF FRONTEND PREVIEW SERVICE
// ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/frontend/src/services/volumeService.ts
```
/**
 * SINUS VIEWER — FRONTEND VOLUME SERVICE
 * ======================================
 *
 * Responsibilities:
 * - Obtain authorized 3D volume descriptors.
 * - Select full-resolution or preview levels.
 * - Download bounded HTTP byte ranges.
 * - Assemble little-endian float32 voxel arrays.
 * - Publish React-compatible loading progress.
 * - Cancel outdated downloads.
 * - Enforce per-volume browser memory limits.
 * - Clear image data after logout/unmount.
 *
 * SECURITY:
 * - Only server-issued opaque resource handles are used.
 * - The backend authenticates and authorizes every request.
 * - No pixel data, identifiers or raw errors are logged.
 * - No localStorage, IndexedDB or filesystem persistence.
 * - No original DICOM dataset modifications.
 *
 * GEOMETRY:
 * - Array storage is C-contiguous Z,Y,X.
 * - Voxel data is IEEE float32 little-endian.
 * - Physical coordinates are DICOM LPS.
 * - Geometry must be retained with the selected level.
 *
 * LIMITATION:
 * - Complete selected levels are assembled in browser RAM.
 * - This is not out-of-core or GPU streaming.
 * - For oversized volumes, use smaller preview levels.
 */

import {
  apiGetVolumeRange,
  apiLoadVolume,
  getPublicErrorText,
  isAuthenticationFailure,
} from "./apiClient";

import {
  isOpaqueHandle,
  isVolumeLevel,
  type VolumeDescriptor,
  type VolumeGeometry,
  type VolumeLevel,
} from "../types/api";


// ============================================================
// 01 — LIMITS AND CONSTANTS
// ============================================================

export const DEFAULT_VOLUME_MEMORY_LIMIT_BYTES =
  256 * 1024 * 1024;

export const DEFAULT_VOLUME_CHUNK_BYTES =
  4 * 1024 * 1024;

export const MAX_VOLUME_MEMORY_LIMIT_BYTES =
  512 * 1024 * 1024;

export const MAX_VOLUME_CHUNK_BYTES =
  16 * 1024 * 1024;

const BYTES_PER_FLOAT32 = 4;

const LITTLE_ENDIAN = true;


// ============================================================
// 02 — PUBLIC TYPES
// ============================================================

export type VolumeLoadStatus =
  | "idle"
  | "preparing"
  | "downloading"
  | "ready"
  | "cancelled"
  | "error"
  | "authentication-required";

export interface LoadedVolume {
  readonly datasetSessionId: string;
  readonly seriesHandle: string;
  readonly level: number;

  readonly shapeZYX: readonly [
    number,
    number,
    number,
  ];

  readonly geometry: VolumeGeometry;

  readonly voxelCount: number;
  readonly byteSize: number;

  /**
   * Float32Array is mutable.
   * Treat it as owned by the viewer and do not modify it
   * outside explicitly controlled rendering operations.
   */
  readonly voxels: Float32Array;
}

export interface VolumeLoadProgress {
  readonly status: VolumeLoadStatus;
  readonly downloadedBytes: number;
  readonly totalBytes: number;
  readonly percentage: number;
  readonly selectedLevel: number | null;
  readonly error: string | null;
}

export interface VolumeServiceSnapshot {
  readonly progress: VolumeLoadProgress;
  readonly volume: LoadedVolume | null;
  readonly descriptor: VolumeDescriptor | null;
}

export interface VolumeLoadOptions {
  readonly level?: number;
  readonly preferPreview?: boolean;
  readonly signal?: AbortSignal;
}

export interface VolumeServiceOptions {
  readonly memoryLimitBytes?: number;
  readonly chunkBytes?: number;
}

export type VolumeStateListener = (
  snapshot: VolumeServiceSnapshot,
) => void;


// ============================================================
// 03 — CONTROLLED ERRORS
// ============================================================

export class FrontendVolumeError extends Error {
  readonly code:
    | "INVALID_SELECTION"
    | "INVALID_VOLUME"
    | "MEMORY_LIMIT"
    | "CANCELLED"
    | "AUTH_REQUIRED"
    | "NETWORK_FAILURE";

  constructor(
    code: FrontendVolumeError["code"],
    message: string,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "FrontendVolumeError";
    this.code = code;
  }
}

function cancelledError(): FrontendVolumeError {
  return new FrontendVolumeError(
    "CANCELLED",
    "Volume loading was cancelled.",
  );
}

function convertPublicError(
  error: unknown,
): FrontendVolumeError {
  if (error instanceof FrontendVolumeError) {
    return error;
  }

  if (isAuthenticationFailure(error)) {
    return new FrontendVolumeError(
      "AUTH_REQUIRED",
      "Your session has expired. Sign in again.",
    );
  }

  return new FrontendVolumeError(
    "NETWORK_FAILURE",
    getPublicErrorText(error),
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 04 — SAFE NUMERIC VALIDATION
// ============================================================

function isPositiveSafeInteger(
  value: unknown,
): value is number {
  return (
    typeof value === "number" &&
    Number.isSafeInteger(value) &&
    value > 0
  );
}

function validateConfiguration(
  memoryLimitBytes: number,
  chunkBytes: number,
): void {
  if (
    !isPositiveSafeInteger(memoryLimitBytes) ||
    memoryLimitBytes > MAX_VOLUME_MEMORY_LIMIT_BYTES ||
    memoryLimitBytes < BYTES_PER_FLOAT32 ||
    !isPositiveSafeInteger(chunkBytes) ||
    chunkBytes > MAX_VOLUME_CHUNK_BYTES ||
    chunkBytes < BYTES_PER_FLOAT32 ||
    chunkBytes % BYTES_PER_FLOAT32 !== 0
  ) {
    throw new FrontendVolumeError(
      "INVALID_VOLUME",
      "Invalid volume loading configuration.",
    );
  }
}

function validateSelection(
  datasetSessionId: string,
  seriesHandle: string,
): void {
  if (
    !isOpaqueHandle(datasetSessionId) ||
    !isOpaqueHandle(seriesHandle)
  ) {
    throw new FrontendVolumeError(
      "INVALID_SELECTION",
      "Select a valid dataset and series.",
    );
  }
}

function verifyLevel(
  level: VolumeLevel,
  memoryLimitBytes: number,
): void {
  if (!isVolumeLevel(level)) {
    throw new FrontendVolumeError(
      "INVALID_VOLUME",
      "Invalid volume geometry or data format.",
    );
  }

  if (
    level.byte_size > memoryLimitBytes ||
    level.byte_size > MAX_VOLUME_MEMORY_LIMIT_BYTES
  ) {
    throw new FrontendVolumeError(
      "MEMORY_LIMIT",
      "This volume exceeds the browser memory limit.",
    );
  }

  if (
    level.byte_size % BYTES_PER_FLOAT32 !== 0 ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(level.byte_size)
  ) {
    throw new FrontendVolumeError(
      "INVALID_VOLUME",
      "The volume byte layout is invalid.",
    );
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 05 — LEVEL SELECTION
// ============================================================

export function chooseVolumeLevel(
  descriptor: VolumeDescriptor,
  memoryLimitBytes: number,
  requestedLevel?: number,
  preferPreview = true,
): VolumeLevel {
  if (
    !Array.isArray(descriptor.levels) ||
    descriptor.levels.length === 0
  ) {
    throw new FrontendVolumeError(
      "INVALID_VOLUME",
      "No volume levels are available.",
    );
  }

  if (requestedLevel !== undefined) {
    if (
      !Number.isSafeInteger(requestedLevel) ||
      requestedLevel < 0 ||
      requestedLevel > 64
    ) {
      throw new FrontendVolumeError(
        "INVALID_SELECTION",
        "Invalid volume resolution level.",
      );
    }

    const selected = descriptor.levels.find(
      (item) => item.level === requestedLevel,
    );

    if (!selected) {
      throw new FrontendVolumeError(
        "INVALID_SELECTION",
        "The selected volume level is unavailable.",
      );
    }

    verifyLevel(selected, memoryLimitBytes);
    return selected;
  }

  const fitting = descriptor.levels.filter(
    (item) => (
      isVolumeLevel(item) &&
      item.byte_size <= memoryLimitBytes
    ),
  );

  if (fitting.length === 0) {
    throw new FrontendVolumeError(
      "MEMORY_LIMIT",
      "No available volume level fits the memory limit.",
    );
  }

  const ordered = [...fitting].sort(
    (a, b) => a.byte_size - b.byte_size,
  );

  if (preferPreview) {
    const preview = ordered.find(
      (item) => item.is_preview,
    );

    if (preview) {
      verifyLevel(preview, memoryLimitBytes);
      return preview;
    }
  }

  const highestResolution = ordered[
    ordered.length - 1
  ];

  verifyLevel(highestResolution, memoryLimitBytes);
  return highestResolution;
}


// ============================================================
// 06 — SNAPSHOT FACTORY
// ============================================================

function makeProgress(
  status: VolumeLoadStatus = "idle",
  downloadedBytes = 0,
  totalBytes = 0,
  selectedLevel: number | null = null,
  error: string | null = null,
): VolumeLoadProgress {
  const percentage = totalBytes > 0
    ? Math.min(
        100,
        Math.max(
          0,
          (downloadedBytes / totalBytes) * 100,
        ),
      )
    : 0;

  return {
    status,
    downloadedBytes,
    totalBytes,
    percentage,
    selectedLevel,
    error,
  };
}

function makeInitialSnapshot(): VolumeServiceSnapshot {
  return {
    progress: makeProgress(),
    volume: null,
    descriptor: null,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 07 — FRONTEND VOLUME SERVICE
// ============================================================

export class FrontendVolumeService {
  private readonly memoryLimitBytes: number;
  private readonly chunkBytes: number;

  private readonly listeners =
    new Set<VolumeStateListener>();

  private snapshot: VolumeServiceSnapshot =
    makeInitialSnapshot();

  private requestVersion = 0;
  private controller: AbortController | null = null;
  private disposed = false;

  constructor(options: VolumeServiceOptions = {}) {
    const memoryLimit =
      options.memoryLimitBytes ??
      DEFAULT_VOLUME_MEMORY_LIMIT_BYTES;

    const chunkSize =
      options.chunkBytes ??
      DEFAULT_VOLUME_CHUNK_BYTES;

    validateConfiguration(memoryLimit, chunkSize);

    this.memoryLimitBytes = memoryLimit;
    this.chunkBytes = Math.min(
      chunkSize,
      memoryLimit,
    );
  }

  // ==========================================================
  // 08 — STATE SUBSCRIPTIONS
  // ==========================================================

  getSnapshot = (): VolumeServiceSnapshot => {
    return this.snapshot;
  };

  subscribe = (
    listener: VolumeStateListener,
  ): (() => void) => {
    if (this.disposed) {
      return () => undefined;
    }

    this.listeners.add(listener);

    return () => {
      this.listeners.delete(listener);
    };
  };

  private publish(
    value: VolumeServiceSnapshot,
  ): void {
    if (this.disposed) {
      return;
    }

    this.snapshot = value;

    for (const listener of this.listeners) {
      try {
        listener(value);
      } catch {
        // An unrelated UI subscriber cannot interrupt
        // volume loading or other subscribers.
      }
    }
  }

  private updateProgress(
    progress: VolumeLoadProgress,
  ): void {
    this.publish({
      ...this.snapshot,
      progress,
    });
  }

  private isCurrent(
    version: number,
    controller: AbortController,
  ): boolean {
    return (
      !this.disposed &&
      this.requestVersion === version &&
      this.controller === controller &&
      !controller.signal.aborted
    );
  }

  private assertCurrent(
    version: number,
    controller: AbortController,
  ): void {
    if (!this.isCurrent(version, controller)) {
      throw cancelledError();
    }
  }

  // ==========================================================
  // 09 — CANCEL AND RESET
  // ==========================================================

  cancel(): void {
    if (this.disposed) {
      return;
    }

    this.requestVersion++;
    this.controller?.abort();
    this.controller = null;

    if (
      this.snapshot.progress.status === "preparing" ||
      this.snapshot.progress.status === "downloading"
    ) {
      this.updateProgress(
        makeProgress("cancelled"),
      );
    }
  }

  /**
   * Clear all loaded image data references.
   *
   * Call on logout, dataset change, or viewer unmount.
   * JavaScript GC determines when memory is reclaimed.
   */
  reset(): void {
    if (this.disposed) {
      return;
    }

    this.requestVersion++;
    this.controller?.abort();
    this.controller = null;

    this.publish(makeInitialSnapshot());
  }

  dispose(): void {
    if (this.disposed) {
      return;
    }

    this.requestVersion++;
    this.controller?.abort();
    this.controller = null;
    this.disposed = true;

    this.listeners.clear();
    this.snapshot = makeInitialSnapshot();
  }

  // ==========================================================
  // 10 — LOAD SELECTED VOLUME
  // ==========================================================

  async load(
    datasetSessionId: string,
    seriesHandle: string,
    options: VolumeLoadOptions = {},
  ): Promise<LoadedVolume> {
    if (this.disposed) {
      throw new FrontendVolumeError(
        "INVALID_SELECTION",
        "The volume loader is unavailable.",
      );
    }

    validateSelection(datasetSessionId, seriesHandle);

    // A newer request must supersede older downloads.
    this.requestVersion++;
    const version = this.requestVersion;

    this.controller?.abort();

    const controller = new AbortController();
    this.controller = controller;

    const externalSignal = options.signal;

    const abortFromCaller = () => {
      controller.abort();
    };

    externalSignal?.addEventListener(
      "abort",
      abortFromCaller,
      { once: true },
    );

    if (externalSignal?.aborted) {
      controller.abort();
    }

    // Drop old image references before allocating new data.
    this.publish({
      progress: makeProgress("preparing"),
      volume: null,
      descriptor: null,
    });

    try {
      this.assertCurrent(version, controller);

      const response = await apiLoadVolume(
        datasetSessionId,
        seriesHandle,
      );

      this.assertCurrent(version, controller);

      const descriptor = response.volume;

      const level = chooseVolumeLevel(
        descriptor,
        this.memoryLimitBytes,
        options.level,
        options.preferPreview ?? true,
      );

      const totalBytes = level.byte_size;

      // Allocate once; avoid collecting all binary chunks
      // in a separate list before assembling the volume.
      const buffer = new ArrayBuffer(totalBytes);
      const destination = new Uint8Array(buffer);

      this.publish({
        descriptor,
        volume: null,
        progress: makeProgress(
          "downloading",
          0,
          totalBytes,
          level.level,
        ),
      });

      let offset = 0;

      while (offset < totalBytes) {
        this.assertCurrent(version, controller);

        const length = Math.min(
          this.chunkBytes,
          totalBytes - offset,
        );

        if (
          length <= 0 ||
          length % BYTES_PER_FLOAT32 !== 0
        ) {
          throw new FrontendVolumeError(
            "INVALID_VOLUME",
            "An invalid binary chunk was requested.",
          );
        }

        const result = await apiGetVolumeRange(
          datasetSessionId,
          seriesHandle,
          level.level,
          offset,
          length,
          controller.signal,
        );

        this.assertCurrent(version, controller);

        if (
          result.status !== 206 ||
          result.bytes.byteLength !== length
        ) {
          throw new FrontendVolumeError(
            "INVALID_VOLUME",
            "Incomplete volume data received.",
          );
        }

        destination.set(result.bytes, offset);
        offset += length;

        this.updateProgress(
          makeProgress(
            "downloading",
            offset,
            totalBytes,
            level.level,
          ),
        );
      }

      this.assertCurrent(version, controller);

      if (
        offset !== totalBytes ||
        buffer.byteLength !== totalBytes
      ) {
        throw new FrontendVolumeError(
          "INVALID_VOLUME",
          "The downloaded volume is incomplete.",
        );
      }

      // DataView performs an explicit little-endian
      // decode, including on non-little-endian hosts.
      const view = new DataView(buffer);
      const voxelCount =
        totalBytes / BYTES_PER_FLOAT32;

      const voxels = new Float32Array(voxelCount);

      for (let index = 0; index < voxelCount; index++) {
        if (
          index % 262144 === 0 &&
          !this.isCurrent(version, controller)
        ) {
          throw cancelledError();
        }

        voxels[index] = view.getFloat32(
          index * BYTES_PER_FLOAT32,
          LITTLE_ENDIAN,
        );
      }

      this.assertCurrent(version, controller);

      const volume: LoadedVolume = {
        datasetSessionId,
        seriesHandle,
        level: level.level,
        shapeZYX: level.shape_zyx,
        geometry: level.geometry,
        voxelCount,
        byteSize: totalBytes,
        voxels,
      };

      this.publish({
        descriptor,
        volume,
        progress: makeProgress(
          "ready",
          totalBytes,
          totalBytes,
          level.level,
        ),
      });

      return volume;
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      if (
        !this.isCurrent(version, controller) ||
        controller.signal.aborted
      ) {
        throw cancelledError();
      }

      const failure = convertPublicError(error);

      this.publish({
        descriptor: null,
        volume: null,
        progress: makeProgress(
          failure.code === "AUTH_REQUIRED"
            ? "authentication-required"
            : failure.code === "CANCELLED"
              ? "cancelled"
              : "error",
          0,
          0,
          null,
          failure.message,
        ),
      });

      throw failure;
    } finally {
      externalSignal?.removeEventListener(
        "abort",
        abortFromCaller,
      );

      if (this.controller === controller) {
        this.controller = null;
      }
    }
  }

  // ==========================================================
  // 11 — LOADED VOXEL ACCESS
  // ==========================================================

  getVoxel(
    z: number,
    y: number,
    x: number,
  ): number | null {
    const volume = this.snapshot.volume;

    if (!volume) {
      return null;
    }

    const [depth, height, width] = volume.shapeZYX;

    if (
      !Number.isSafeInteger(z) ||
      !Number.isSafeInteger(y) ||
      !Number.isSafeInteger(x) ||
      z < 0 ||
      y < 0 ||
      x < 0 ||
      z >= depth ||
      y >= height ||
      x >= width
    ) {
      return null;
    }

    const index = (
      z * height * width +
      y * width +
      x
    );

    return volume.voxels[index] ?? null;
  }

  // ==========================================================
  // 12 — RESOURCE INFORMATION
  // ==========================================================

  getMemoryLimitBytes(): number {
    return this.memoryLimitBytes;
  }

  getChunkSizeBytes(): number {
    return this.chunkBytes;
  }

  hasLoadedVolume(): boolean {
    return (
      this.snapshot.progress.status === "ready" &&
      this.snapshot.volume !== null
    );
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 13 — FACTORY
// ============================================================

export function createVolumeService(
  options: VolumeServiceOptions = {},
): FrontendVolumeService {
  return new FrontendVolumeService(options);
}


// ============================================================
// END OF FRONTEND VOLUME SERVICE
// ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/frontend/src/types/api.ts
```
/**
 * SINUS VIEWER — FRONTEND API CONTRACTS
 * =====================================
 *
 * Mirrors the public API contracts defined in:
 *     backend/app/api/schemas.py
 *
 * Responsibilities:
 * - Strongly typed authentication responses.
 * - Dataset / patient / series browser contracts.
 * - Technical validation issue summaries.
 * - DICOM volume geometry in LPS coordinates.
 * - Full and preview volume level descriptors.
 * - Grayscale navigation preview metadata.
 * - Binary streaming metadata and range requests.
 * - Health responses and standardized API errors.
 * - Safe, lightweight runtime contract validation.
 *
 * SECURITY:
 * - No patient names, real PatientID values, DICOM UIDs,
 *   original file paths, or authentication tokens.
 * - Browser-visible identifiers are opaque handles.
 * - Do not print raw API responses or credentials.
 * - TypeScript interfaces do not validate network data;
 *   use runtime guards where appropriate.
 *
 * This module performs no network or filesystem operations.
 */


// ============================================================
// 01 — SHARED PRIMITIVE TYPES
// ============================================================

export type OpaqueHandle = string;

export type RequestId = string;

export type UnixTimestampSeconds = number;

export type CoordinateSystem = "LPS";

export type VolumeAxisOrder = "zyx";

export type VolumeDataType = "float32";

export type VolumeByteOrder = "little";

export type PreviewDataType = "uint8";

export type VolumeFormat = "sinus-viewer-volume-v1";

export type StreamEncoding = "sinus-viewer-f32-zyx-v1";

export type PreviewPlane =
  | "axial"
  | "coronal"
  | "sagittal";

export type VolumePlane =
  | PreviewPlane
  | "volume";

export type Vector3 = readonly [
  number,
  number,
  number,
];

export type Shape2 = readonly [
  number,
  number,
];

export type Shape3 = readonly [
  number,
  number,
  number,
];

export type Direction9 = readonly [
  number,
  number,
  number,
  number,
  number,
  number,
  number,
  number,
  number,
];

  readonly request_id: RequestId | null;
}

  readonly success: false;
  readonly error: APIError;
}

  "REQUEST_TOO_LARGE",

  "HTTPS_REQUIRED",
] as const;

  (typeof PUBLIC_ERROR_CODES)[number];

  Record<PublicErrorCode, string>
> = {
  AUTH_REQUIRED: "Authentication is required.",
  AUTH_FAILED: "Authentication failed.",
  SESSION_EXPIRED: "Session expired.",
  ACCESS_DENIED: "Access denied.",
  RATE_LIMITED: "Too many requests.",
  INVALID_REQUEST: "Invalid request.",
  REQUEST_TOO_LARGE: "Request too large.",
  PATH_REJECTED: "Dataset path rejected.",
  DATASET_NOT_FOUND: "Dataset not found.",
  PATIENT_NOT_FOUND: "Patient not found.",
  SERIES_NOT_FOUND: "Series not found.",
  SERIES_INVALID: "Series is not volume-ready.",
  SOURCE_CHANGED: "Source data changed.",
  DECODER_ERROR: "Image decoding failed.",
  OUT_OF_MEMORY: "Memory limit exceeded.",
  RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE: "Invalid byte range.",
  OPERATION_CANCELLED: "Operation cancelled.",
  SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE: "Service unavailable.",
  PROCESSING_FAILED: "Processing failed.",
  CONFIG_ERROR: "Service configuration error.",
  HTTPS_REQUIRED: "Secure transport required.",
};

// 08 — MEDICAL IMAGING GEOMETRY

/**
 * Geometry contract:
 *
 * shape_zyx:
 *     Array dimensions in Z, Y, X order.
 *
 * spacing_xyz_mm:
 *     Physical spacing in X, Y, Z order (millimetres).
 *
 * origin_lps:
 *     World origin in DICOM LPS coordinates.
 *
 * direction_lps:
 *     Row-major 3x3 direction matrix.
 *
 * Important:
 * A displayed axial/coronal/sagittal slice needs proper
 * orientation and spacing calculations; do not assume
 * the image axes align with the screen axes.
 */
export interface VolumeGeometry {
  readonly shape_zyx: Shape3;
  readonly spacing_xyz_mm: Vector3;
  readonly origin_lps: Vector3;
  readonly direction_lps: Direction9;
  readonly coordinate_system: "LPS";
}

  readonly byte_order: VolumeByteOrder;

// ============================================================
// 13 — SAFE RUNTIME TYPE HELPERS
// ============================================================

function isRecord(
  value: unknown,
): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return (
    typeof value === "object" &&
    value !== null &&
    !Array.isArray(value)
  );
}

export function isOpaqueHandle(
  value: unknown,
): value is OpaqueHandle {
  if (
    typeof value !== "string" ||
    value.length < 1 ||
    value.length > 128
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  for (const character of value) {
    const code = character.charCodeAt(0);

    const alphaUpper = code >= 65 && code <= 90;
    const alphaLower = code >= 97 && code <= 122;
    const digit = code >= 48 && code <= 57;

    if (
      !alphaUpper &&
      !alphaLower &&
      !digit &&
      character !== "_" &&
      character !== "-"
    ) {
      return false;
    }
  }

  return true;
}

export function isPublicErrorCode(
  value: unknown,
): value is PublicErrorCode {
  return (
    typeof value === "string" &&
    Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(
      SAFE_ERROR_MESSAGES,
      value,
    )
  );
}

export function isAPIErrorResponse(
  value: unknown,
): value is APIErrorResponse {
  if (!isRecord(value) || value.success !== false) {
    return false;
  }

  const detail = value.error;

  if (!isRecord(detail)) {
    return false;
  }

  return (
    isPublicErrorCode(detail.code) &&
    detail.message === SAFE_ERROR_MESSAGES[detail.code] &&
    typeof detail.retryable === "boolean" &&
    (
      detail.request_id === null ||
      (
        typeof detail.request_id === "string" &&
        /^[a-f0-9]{32}$/.test(detail.request_id)
      )
    )
  );
}

export function isSessionStatus(
  value: unknown,
): value is SessionStatus {
  if (!isRecord(value)) {
    return false;
  }

  return (
    value.authenticated === true &&
    typeof value.expires_at === "number" &&
    Number.isFinite(value.expires_at) &&
    value.expires_at > 0
  );
}

export function isLoginResponse(
  value: unknown,
): value is LoginResponse {
  return (
    isRecord(value) &&
    value.success === true &&
    isSessionStatus(value.session)
  );
}

export function isDatasetSummary(
  value: unknown,
): value is DatasetSummary {
  if (!isRecord(value)) {
    return false;
  }

  const stateIsValid =
    value.state === "ready" ||
    value.state === "scanning" ||
    value.state === "closed";

  return (
    isOpaqueHandle(value.dataset_session_id) &&
    stateIsValid &&
    isPositiveInteger(value.generation) &&
    isNonNegativeInteger(value.patient_count) &&
    isNonNegativeInteger(value.series_count) &&
    isNonNegativeInteger(value.dicom_files)
  );
}

export function isPatientSummary(
  value: unknown,
): value is PatientSummary {
  if (!isRecord(value)) {
    return false;
  }

  return (
    isOpaqueHandle(value.patient_handle) &&
    isNonNegativeInteger(value.series_count) &&
    isNonNegativeInteger(value.volume_ready_series) &&
    value.volume_ready_series <= value.series_count
  );
}

export function isSeriesSummary(
  value: unknown,
): value is SeriesSummary {
  if (!isRecord(value)) {
    return false;
  }

  const validModality =
    value.modality === null ||
    (
      typeof value.modality === "string" &&
      /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{0,16}$/.test(value.modality)
    );

  return (
    isOpaqueHandle(value.series_handle) &&
    isOpaqueHandle(value.patient_handle) &&
    validModality &&
    isNonNegativeInteger(value.slice_count) &&
    isNonNegativeInteger(value.rows) &&
    isNonNegativeInteger(value.columns) &&
    typeof value.volume_ready === "boolean"
  );
}

export function isHealthResponse(
  value: unknown,
): value is HealthResponse {
  if (!isRecord(value)) {
    return false;
  }

  return (
    value.service === "sinus-viewer" &&
    (
      value.status === "ok" ||
      value.status === "degraded"
    )
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 14 — NUMERIC VALIDATION HELPERS
// ============================================================

export function isNonNegativeInteger(
  value: unknown,
): value is number {
  return (
    typeof value === "number" &&
    Number.isSafeInteger(value) &&
    value >= 0
  );
}

export function isPositiveInteger(
  value: unknown,
): value is number {
  return (
    typeof value === "number" &&
    Number.isSafeInteger(value) &&
    value > 0
  );
}

export function isFinitePositiveNumber(
  value: unknown,
): value is number {
  return (
    typeof value === "number" &&
    Number.isFinite(value) &&
    value > 0
  );
}

export function isShape3(
  value: unknown,
): value is Shape3 {
  return (
    Array.isArray(value) &&
    value.length === 3 &&
    value.every(isPositiveInteger)
  );
}

export function isVector3(
  value: unknown,
): value is Vector3 {
  return (
    Array.isArray(value) &&
    value.length === 3 &&
    value.every(
      (component: unknown) =>
        typeof component === "number" &&
        Number.isFinite(component),
    )
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 15 — VOLUME GEOMETRY RUNTIME GUARD
// ============================================================

export function isVolumeGeometry(
  value: unknown,
): value is VolumeGeometry {
  if (!isRecord(value)) {
    return false;
  }

  const spacing = value.spacing_xyz_mm;
  const direction = value.direction_lps;

  if (
    !isShape3(value.shape_zyx) ||
    !isVector3(value.origin_lps) ||
    !isVector3(spacing) ||
    value.coordinate_system !== "LPS"
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  if (!spacing.every(isFinitePositiveNumber)) {
    return false;
  }

  if (
    !Array.isArray(direction) ||
    direction.length !== 9 ||
    !direction.every(
      (component: unknown) =>
        typeof component === "number" &&
        Number.isFinite(component),
    )
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  // Orthogonality and unit-length are checked by backend
  // Pydantic and must be checked again in imaging math.
  return true;
}


// ============================================================
// 16 — VOLUME CONTRACT RUNTIME GUARD
// ============================================================

export function isVolumeLevel(
  value: unknown,
): value is VolumeLevel {
  if (!isRecord(value)) {
    return false;
  }

  if (
    !isNonNegativeInteger(value.level) ||
    value.level > 64 ||
    typeof value.is_preview !== "boolean" ||
    value.is_preview !== (value.level > 0) ||
    !isShape3(value.shape_zyx) ||
    !isPositiveInteger(value.byte_size) ||
    value.dtype !== "float32" ||
    value.axis_order !== "zyx" ||
    value.byte_order !== "little" ||
    !isVolumeGeometry(value.geometry)
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  const shape = value.shape_zyx;
  const expectedBytes = shape[0] * shape[1] * shape[2] * 4;

  if (
    !Number.isSafeInteger(expectedBytes) ||
    expectedBytes !== value.byte_size
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  const geometry = value.geometry;

  return (
    shape[0] === geometry.shape_zyx[0] &&
    shape[1] === geometry.shape_zyx[1] &&
    shape[2] === geometry.shape_zyx[2]
  );
}

export function isVolumeResponse(
  value: unknown,
): value is VolumeResponse {
  if (
    !isRecord(value) ||
    value.success !== true ||
    !isOpaqueHandle(value.series_handle)
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  const volume = value.volume;

  if (
    !isRecord(volume) ||
    volume.format !== "sinus-viewer-volume-v1" ||
    volume.coordinate_system !== "LPS" ||
    !Array.isArray(volume.levels) ||
    volume.levels.length < 1 ||
    volume.levels.length > 65 ||
    !volume.levels.every(isVolumeLevel)
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  const levels = volume.levels as VolumeLevel[];
  const indices = levels.map((level) => level.level);

  return (
    indices.includes(0) &&
    new Set(indices).size === indices.length
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 17 — SAFE API ERROR MESSAGE
// ============================================================

export function getSafeAPIErrorMessage(
  code: PublicErrorCode,
): string {
  return SAFE_ERROR_MESSAGES[code];
}


// ============================================================
// 18 — STATIC API CONTRACT CAPABILITIES
// ============================================================

export function getAPIContractCapabilities() {
  return {
    schemaVersion: "1",
    authenticatedSession: true,
    opaqueHandles: true,
    patientIdentifiersIncluded: false,
    sourcePathsIncluded: false,
    coordinateSystem: "LPS",
    volumeAxisOrder: "zyx",
    volumeDataType: "float32",
    volumeByteOrder: "little",
    runtimeGuards: true,
  } as const;
}
```

```
/**
 * SINUS VIEWER — DICOM GEOMETRY UTILITIES
 * ======================================
 *
 * Pure TypeScript mathematical operations for:
 * - Voxel XYZ <-> DICOM LPS world coordinates.
 * - Affine transforms with physical voxel spacing.
 * - Direction matrix validation and inversion.
 * - Volume indexing in ZYX memory order.
 * - Orthogonal image-plane navigation.
 * - Crosshair positioning and synchronization.
 * - Physical bounds and slice spacing.
 *
 * COORDINATE CONVENTIONS
 * ----------------------
 *
 * Voxel:
 *   x = column index
 *   y = row index
 *   z = slice index
 *
 * Array:
 *   shape_zyx = [depth, height, width]
 *   index = z * height * width + y * width + x
 *
 * World:
 *   DICOM LPS coordinates, measured in millimetres.
 *   +X = Left, +Y = Posterior, +Z = Superior.
 *
 * Direction matrix:
 *   Row-major 3x3 matrix D, applied as:
 *
 *   world = origin + D * diag(spacingXYZ) * voxelXYZ
 *
 * Matrix row-major storage:
 *   [d00, d01, d02,
 *    d10, d11, d12,
 *    d20, d21, d22]
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 * Oblique acquisitions are supported by the affine math.
 * Constant-index planes are NOT necessarily true anatomical
 * axial, coronal or sagittal planes in oblique datasets.
 *
 * No filesystem access, HTTP calls or dataset modification.
 */

import {
  isVolumeGeometry,
  type PreviewPlane,
  type Shape3,
  type Vector3,
  type VolumeGeometry,
} from "../types/api";


// ============================================================
// 01 — PUBLIC TYPES
// ============================================================

export type Vec3 = readonly [
  number,
  number,
  number,
];

export type Mat3 = readonly [
  number, number, number,
  number, number, number,
  number, number, number,
];

export interface VoxelPoint {
  readonly x: number;
  readonly y: number;
  readonly z: number;
}

export interface WorldPointLPS {
  readonly x: number;
  readonly y: number;
  readonly z: number;
}

export interface VoxelBounds {
  readonly min: Vec3;
  readonly max: Vec3;
}

export interface WorldBoundsLPS {
  readonly min: Vec3;
  readonly max: Vec3;
}

export interface SlicePlaneInfo {
  readonly axis: 0 | 1 | 2;
  readonly count: number;
  readonly spacingMm: number;
}

export interface CrosshairState {
  readonly voxel: VoxelPoint;
  readonly worldLPS: WorldPointLPS;
  readonly axialIndex: number;
  readonly coronalIndex: number;
  readonly sagittalIndex: number;
}

export interface PlaneVoxelCoordinates {
  readonly u: number;
  readonly v: number;
  readonly slice: number;
}

export class GeometryError extends Error {
  constructor(message = "Invalid image geometry.") {
    super(message);
    this.name = "GeometryError";
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 02 — NUMERIC HELPERS
// ============================================================

const EPSILON = 1e-8;
const ORTHOGONAL_TOLERANCE = 1e-3;

function isFiniteNumber(value: unknown): value is number {
  return (
    typeof value === "number" &&
    Number.isFinite(value)
  );
}

function assertFiniteVec3(value: Vec3): void {
  if (
    value.length !== 3 ||
    !value.every(isFiniteNumber)
  ) {
    throw new GeometryError();
  }
}

function assertFiniteMat3(value: Mat3): void {
  if (
    value.length !== 9 ||
    !value.every(isFiniteNumber)
  ) {
    throw new GeometryError();
  }
}

export function clamp(
  value: number,
  minimum: number,
  maximum: number,
): number {
  if (
    !isFiniteNumber(value) ||
    !isFiniteNumber(minimum) ||
    !isFiniteNumber(maximum) ||
    minimum > maximum
  ) {
    throw new GeometryError("Invalid numeric bounds.");
  }

  return Math.min(maximum, Math.max(minimum, value));
}

export function dot3(a: Vec3, b: Vec3): number {
  assertFiniteVec3(a);
  assertFiniteVec3(b);

  return a[0] * b[0] + a[1] * b[1] + a[2] * b[2];
}

export function length3(value: Vec3): number {
  return Math.hypot(value[0], value[1], value[2]);
}

export function subtract3(a: Vec3, b: Vec3): Vec3 {
  assertFiniteVec3(a);
  assertFiniteVec3(b);

  return [
    a[0] - b[0],
    a[1] - b[1],
    a[2] - b[2],
  ];
}

export function add3(a: Vec3, b: Vec3): Vec3 {
  assertFiniteVec3(a);
  assertFiniteVec3(b);

  return [
    a[0] + b[0],
    a[1] + b[1],
    a[2] + b[2],
  ];
}


// ============================================================
// 03 — MATRIX OPERATIONS
// ============================================================

export function multiplyMat3Vec3(
  matrix: Mat3,
  vector: Vec3,
): Vec3 {
  assertFiniteMat3(matrix);
  assertFiniteVec3(vector);

  return [
    matrix[0] * vector[0] +
      matrix[1] * vector[1] +
      matrix[2] * vector[2],

    matrix[3] * vector[0] +
      matrix[4] * vector[1] +
      matrix[5] * vector[2],

    matrix[6] * vector[0] +
      matrix[7] * vector[1] +
      matrix[8] * vector[2],
  ];
}

export function determinantMat3(
  matrix: Mat3,
): number {
  assertFiniteMat3(matrix);

  const [
    a, b, c,
    d, e, f,
    g, h, i,
  ] = matrix;

  return (
    a * (e * i - f * h) -
    b * (d * i - f * g) +
    c * (d * h - e * g)
  );
}

export function invertMat3(matrix: Mat3): Mat3 {
  assertFiniteMat3(matrix);

  const [
    a, b, c,
    d, e, f,
    g, h, i,
  ] = matrix;

  const det = determinantMat3(matrix);

  if (
    !Number.isFinite(det) ||
    Math.abs(det) < EPSILON
  ) {
    throw new GeometryError(
      "The image direction matrix is singular.",
    );
  }

  const inverse: Mat3 = [
    (e * i - f * h) / det,
    (c * h - b * i) / det,
    (b * f - c * e) / det,

    (f * g - d * i) / det,
    (a * i - c * g) / det,
    (c * d - a * f) / det,

    (d * h - e * g) / det,
    (b * g - a * h) / det,
    (a * e - b * d) / det,
  ];

  if (!inverse.every(Number.isFinite)) {
    throw new GeometryError(
      "The inverse direction matrix is invalid.",
    );
  }

  return inverse;
}


// ============================================================
// 04 — DIRECTION VALIDATION
// ============================================================

function matrixColumn(
  matrix: Mat3,
  index: 0 | 1 | 2,
): Vec3 {
  return [
    matrix[index],
    matrix[3 + index],
    matrix[6 + index],
  ];
}

export function isOrthonormalDirection(
  matrix: Mat3,
  tolerance = ORTHOGONAL_TOLERANCE,
): boolean {
  if (
    !Number.isFinite(tolerance) ||
    tolerance <= 0 ||
    tolerance > 0.1 ||
    !Array.isArray(matrix) ||
    matrix.length !== 9 ||
    !matrix.every(isFiniteNumber)
  ) {
    return false;
  }

  const x = matrixColumn(matrix, 0);
  const y = matrixColumn(matrix, 1);
  const z = matrixColumn(matrix, 2);

  const unitLengths = [x, y, z].every(
    (axis) => Math.abs(length3(axis) - 1) <= tolerance,
  );

  const perpendicular =
    Math.abs(dot3(x, y)) <= tolerance &&
    Math.abs(dot3(x, z)) <= tolerance &&
    Math.abs(dot3(y, z)) <= tolerance;

  const determinant = Math.abs(
    determinantMat3(matrix),
  );

  return (
    unitLengths &&
    perpendicular &&
    Math.abs(determinant - 1) <=
      3 * tolerance
  );
}

export function assertVolumeGeometry(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
): void {
  if (!isVolumeGeometry(geometry)) {
    throw new GeometryError();
  }

  if (
    !isOrthonormalDirection(
      geometry.direction_lps as Mat3,
    )
  ) {
    throw new GeometryError(
      "Invalid volume orientation matrix.",
    );
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 05 — COORDINATE CONVERSIONS
// ============================================================

function asVec3(point: VoxelPoint | WorldPointLPS): Vec3 {
  const vector: Vec3 = [
    point.x,
    point.y,
    point.z,
  ];

  assertFiniteVec3(vector);
  return vector;
}

/**
 * Convert continuous voxel XYZ coordinates to world LPS.
 *
 * Indices refer to voxel centres, not voxel boundaries.
 */
export function voxelToWorldLPS(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
  voxel: VoxelPoint,
): WorldPointLPS {
  assertVolumeGeometry(geometry);

  const point = asVec3(voxel);
  const spacing = geometry.spacing_xyz_mm;

  const scaled: Vec3 = [
    point[0] * spacing[0],
    point[1] * spacing[1],
    point[2] * spacing[2],
  ];

  const rotated = multiplyMat3Vec3(
    geometry.direction_lps as Mat3,
    scaled,
  );

  const world = add3(
    geometry.origin_lps as Vec3,
    rotated,
  );

  return {
    x: world[0],
    y: world[1],
    z: world[2],
  };
}

/**
 * Convert DICOM LPS millimetres to continuous voxel XYZ.
 *
 * No automatic clamping is performed, so callers can
 * detect world positions outside the volume.
 */
export function worldLPSToVoxel(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
  world: WorldPointLPS,
): VoxelPoint {
  assertVolumeGeometry(geometry);

  const delta = subtract3(
    asVec3(world),
    geometry.origin_lps as Vec3,
  );

  const inverse = invertMat3(
    geometry.direction_lps as Mat3,
  );

  const local = multiplyMat3Vec3(
    inverse,
    delta,
  );

  const spacing = geometry.spacing_xyz_mm;

  return {
    x: local[0] / spacing[0],
    y: local[1] / spacing[1],
    z: local[2] / spacing[2],
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 06 — VOLUME BOUNDS
// ============================================================

export function getVoxelBounds(
  shapeZYX: Shape3,
): VoxelBounds {
  const [depth, height, width] = shapeZYX;

  if (
    !Number.isSafeInteger(depth) ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(height) ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(width) ||
    depth < 1 ||
    height < 1 ||
    width < 1
  ) {
    throw new GeometryError("Invalid volume shape.");
  }

  return {
    min: [0, 0, 0],
    max: [
      width - 1,
      height - 1,
      depth - 1,
    ],
  };
}

export function isVoxelInsideVolume(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
  voxel: VoxelPoint,
): boolean {
  assertVolumeGeometry(geometry);

  const bounds = getVoxelBounds(
    geometry.shape_zyx,
  );

  const point = asVec3(voxel);

  return point.every(
    (value, index) =>
      value >= bounds.min[index] &&
      value <= bounds.max[index],
  );
}

export function clampVoxelToVolume(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
  voxel: VoxelPoint,
): VoxelPoint {
  assertVolumeGeometry(geometry);

  const bounds = getVoxelBounds(
    geometry.shape_zyx,
  );

  return {
    x: clamp(voxel.x, bounds.min[0], bounds.max[0]),
    y: clamp(voxel.y, bounds.min[1], bounds.max[1]),
    z: clamp(voxel.z, bounds.min[2], bounds.max[2]),
  };
}

export function getWorldBoundsLPS(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
): WorldBoundsLPS {
  assertVolumeGeometry(geometry);

  const { max } = getVoxelBounds(
    geometry.shape_zyx,
  );

  const corners: Vec3[] = [];

  for (const z of [0, max[2]]) {
    for (const y of [0, max[1]]) {
      for (const x of [0, max[0]]) {
        const world = voxelToWorldLPS(
          geometry,
          { x, y, z },
        );

        corners.push([
          world.x,
          world.y,
          world.z,
        ]);
      }
    }
  }

  return {
    min: [
      Math.min(...corners.map((p) => p[0])),
      Math.min(...corners.map((p) => p[1])),
      Math.min(...corners.map((p) => p[2])),
    ],
    max: [
      Math.max(...corners.map((p) => p[0])),
      Math.max(...corners.map((p) => p[1])),
      Math.max(...corners.map((p) => p[2])),
    ],
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 07 — VOXEL BUFFER INDEXING
// ============================================================

export function voxelLinearIndex(
  shapeZYX: Shape3,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  z: number,
): number | null {
  const [depth, height, width] = shapeZYX;

  if (
    !Number.isSafeInteger(depth) ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(height) ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(width) ||
    depth <= 0 ||
    height <= 0 ||
    width <= 0 ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(x) ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(y) ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(z) ||
    x < 0 ||
    y < 0 ||
    z < 0 ||
    x >= width ||
    y >= height ||
    z >= depth
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const index =
    z * height * width +
    y * width +
    x;

  return Number.isSafeInteger(index)
    ? index
    : null;
}

export function linearIndexToVoxel(
  shapeZYX: Shape3,
  index: number,
): VoxelPoint | null {
  const [depth, height, width] = shapeZYX;
  const voxelCount = depth * height * width;

  if (
    !Number.isSafeInteger(depth) ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(height) ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(width) ||
    depth <= 0 ||
    height <= 0 ||
    width <= 0 ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(voxelCount) ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(index) ||
    index < 0 ||
    index >= voxelCount
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const planeSize = height * width;

  const z = Math.floor(index / planeSize);
  const remainder = index - z * planeSize;
  const y = Math.floor(remainder / width);
  const x = remainder - y * width;

  return { x, y, z };
}


// ============================================================
// 08 — CONSTANT-INDEX SLICE NAVIGATION
// ============================================================

/**
 * These names identify voxel-axis slices.
 *
 * For oblique acquisitions, a constant Z slice is an
 * acquisition-plane slice, not necessarily a true axial
 * anatomical plane. True patient-aligned MPR requires
 * world-space resampling.
 */
export function getSlicePlaneInfo(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
  plane: PreviewPlane,
): SlicePlaneInfo {
  assertVolumeGeometry(geometry);

  const [depth, height, width] =
    geometry.shape_zyx;

  const [sx, sy, sz] =
    geometry.spacing_xyz_mm;

  switch (plane) {
    case "axial":
      return {
        axis: 2,
        count: depth,
        spacingMm: sz,
      };

    case "coronal":
      return {
        axis: 1,
        count: height,
        spacingMm: sy,
      };

    case "sagittal":
      return {
        axis: 0,
        count: width,
        spacingMm: sx,
      };
  }
}

export function clampSliceIndex(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
  plane: PreviewPlane,
  index: number,
): number {
  const info = getSlicePlaneInfo(
    geometry,
    plane,
  );

  return Math.round(
    clamp(index, 0, info.count - 1),
  );
}

/**
 * Pixel axes are defined in voxel space:
 *
 * axial:    u=X, v=Y, slice=Z
 * coronal:  u=X, v=Z, slice=Y
 * sagittal: u=Y, v=Z, slice=X
 *
 * Screen flips and radiological orientation are separate
 * presentation transforms, not performed here.
 */
export function planeToVoxel(
  plane: PreviewPlane,
  point: PlaneVoxelCoordinates,
): VoxelPoint {
  const { u, v, slice } = point;

  if (![u, v, slice].every(isFiniteNumber)) {
    throw new GeometryError();
  }

  switch (plane) {
    case "axial":
      return {
        x: u,
        y: v,
        z: slice,
      };

    case "coronal":
      return {
        x: u,
        y: slice,
        z: v,
      };

    case "sagittal":
      return {
        x: slice,
        y: u,
        z: v,
      };
  }
}

export function voxelToPlane(
  plane: PreviewPlane,
  voxel: VoxelPoint,
): PlaneVoxelCoordinates {
  const { x, y, z } = voxel;

  if (![x, y, z].every(isFiniteNumber)) {
    throw new GeometryError();
  }

  switch (plane) {
    case "axial":
      return {
        u: x,
        v: y,
        slice: z,
      };

    case "coronal":
      return {
        u: x,
        v: z,
        slice: y,
      };

    case "sagittal":
      return {
        u: y,
        v: z,
        slice: x,
      };
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 09 — CROSSHAIR SYNCHRONIZATION
// ============================================================

export function makeCrosshairFromVoxel(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
  voxel: VoxelPoint,
): CrosshairState {
  const bounded = clampVoxelToVolume(
    geometry,
    voxel,
  );

  const rounded: VoxelPoint = {
    x: Math.round(bounded.x),
    y: Math.round(bounded.y),
    z: Math.round(bounded.z),
  };

  return {
    voxel: rounded,
    worldLPS: voxelToWorldLPS(
      geometry,
      rounded,
    ),
    axialIndex: rounded.z,
    coronalIndex: rounded.y,
    sagittalIndex: rounded.x,
  };
}

export function makeCrosshairFromWorldLPS(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
  world: WorldPointLPS,
): CrosshairState {
  const voxel = worldLPSToVoxel(
    geometry,
    world,
  );

  return makeCrosshairFromVoxel(
    geometry,
    voxel,
  );
}

export function makeCenteredCrosshair(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
): CrosshairState {
  assertVolumeGeometry(geometry);

  const [depth, height, width] =
    geometry.shape_zyx;

  return makeCrosshairFromVoxel(
    geometry,
    {
      x: (width - 1) / 2,
      y: (height - 1) / 2,
      z: (depth - 1) / 2,
    },
  );
}

export function moveCrosshairSlice(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
  crosshair: CrosshairState,
  plane: PreviewPlane,
  newIndex: number,
): CrosshairState {
  const index = clampSliceIndex(
    geometry,
    plane,
    newIndex,
  );

  const previous = crosshair.voxel;

  switch (plane) {
    case "axial":
      return makeCrosshairFromVoxel(
        geometry,
        {
          ...previous,
          z: index,
        },
      );

    case "coronal":
      return makeCrosshairFromVoxel(
        geometry,
        {
          ...previous,
          y: index,
        },
      );

    case "sagittal":
      return makeCrosshairFromVoxel(
        geometry,
        {
          ...previous,
          x: index,
        },
      );
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 10 — PHYSICAL DISTANCE
// ============================================================

export function distanceWorldMm(
  a: WorldPointLPS,
  b: WorldPointLPS,
): number {
  const delta = subtract3(
    asVec3(a),
    asVec3(b),
  );

  return length3(delta);
}

export function distanceVoxelMm(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
  a: VoxelPoint,
  b: VoxelPoint,
): number {
  const first = voxelToWorldLPS(
    geometry,
    a,
  );

  const second = voxelToWorldLPS(
    geometry,
    b,
  );

  return distanceWorldMm(first, second);
}


// ============================================================
// 11 — LPS / RAS CONVERSION
// ============================================================

/**
 * DICOM LPS and RAS differ by X and Y axis signs.
 * These functions do not change voxel indices.
 */
export function lpsToRAS(
  lps: Vector3,
): Vec3 {
  assertFiniteVec3(lps);

  return [
    -lps[0],
    -lps[1],
    lps[2],
  ];
}

export function rasToLPS(
  ras: Vector3,
): Vec3 {
  assertFiniteVec3(ras);

  return [
    -ras[0],
    -ras[1],
    ras[2],
  ];
}


// ============================================================
// END OF SINUS VIEWER GEOMETRY UTILITIES
// ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/frontend/src/utils/measurements.ts
```
/**
 * SINUS VIEWER — MEASUREMENT UTILITIES
 * ====================================
 *
 * Pure mathematical measurements for CT / CBCT:
 * - 3D physical distance in millimetres.
 * - Voxel-to-world physical distance.
 * - Three-point angle in degrees.
 * - Polyline perimeter / length.
 * - Planar polygon area in square millimetres.
 * - Circle / ellipse / polygon ROI sampling.
 * - ROI intensity statistics on MPR images.
 * - Finite value and boundary validation.
 *
 * COORDINATES:
 *   DICOM LPS world positions are in millimetres.
 *   MPR pixel coordinates refer to pixel centres.
 *
 * IMPORTANT:
 *   These functions do not establish calibrated HU.
 *   Input intensities retain their original units.
 *   2D areas are geometric projected plane areas,
 *   not anatomical volumes.
 *
 * SECURITY:
 *   No HTTP, filesystem, browser storage or DICOM writes.
 *   No patient identifiers or image content are logged.
 *
 * Clinical measurement accuracy must be validated
 * against known-geometry reference datasets.
 */

import type {
  VolumeGeometry,
} from "../types/api";

import {
  voxelToWorldLPS,
  type VoxelPoint,
  type WorldPointLPS,
} from "./geometry";

import {
  mprPixelToWorldLPS,
  validateMPRPlane,
  type MPRPlane,
  type SampledMPRImage,
} from "./volumeSampling";


// ============================================================
// 01 — CONSTANTS
// ============================================================

export const MAX_MEASUREMENT_POINTS = 10000;
export const MAX_ROI_PIXELS = 4194304;
export const MAX_ROI_VERTICES = 10000;

const MIN_GEOMETRIC_TOLERANCE = 1e-9;
const MAX_GEOMETRIC_TOLERANCE = 1;


// ============================================================
// 02 — PUBLIC TYPES
// ============================================================

export interface Point2D {
  readonly x: number;
  readonly y: number;
}

export interface DistanceMeasurement {
  readonly distanceMm: number;
}

export interface AngleMeasurement {
  readonly angleDegrees: number;
}

export interface PolylineMeasurement {
  readonly lengthMm: number;
  readonly segmentCount: number;
}

export interface PolygonAreaMeasurement {
  readonly areaMm2: number;
  readonly perimeterMm: number;
  readonly vertexCount: number;
}

export interface IntensityStatistics {
  readonly count: number;
  readonly minimum: number | null;
  readonly maximum: number | null;
  readonly mean: number | null;
  readonly standardDeviation: number | null;
  readonly sum: number;
}

export interface ROIMeasurement {
  readonly areaMm2: number;
  readonly includedPixelCount: number;
  readonly invalidPixelCount: number;
  readonly statistics: IntensityStatistics;
}

export interface CircleROI {
  readonly center: Point2D;
  readonly radiusPixels: number;
}

export interface EllipseROI {
  readonly center: Point2D;
  readonly radiusXPixels: number;
  readonly radiusYPixels: number;
}

export interface PolygonROI {
  readonly points: readonly Point2D[];
}

export type ROIShape =
  | { readonly kind: "circle"; readonly roi: CircleROI }
  | { readonly kind: "ellipse"; readonly roi: EllipseROI }
  | { readonly kind: "polygon"; readonly roi: PolygonROI };

export class MeasurementError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message = "Invalid medical imaging measurement.",
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "MeasurementError";
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 03 — VALIDATION
// ============================================================

function finite(value: unknown): value is number {
  return typeof value === "number" &&
    Number.isFinite(value);
}

function assertWorld(point: WorldPointLPS): void {
  if (
    !point ||
    !finite(point.x) ||
    !finite(point.y) ||
    !finite(point.z)
  ) {
    throw new MeasurementError(
      "Invalid physical coordinate.",
    );
  }
}

function assertPoint2D(point: Point2D): void {
  if (
    !point ||
    !finite(point.x) ||
    !finite(point.y)
  ) {
    throw new MeasurementError(
      "Invalid ROI pixel coordinate.",
    );
  }
}

function assertPointCollection<T>(
  points: readonly T[],
  minimum: number,
): void {
  if (
    !Array.isArray(points) ||
    points.length < minimum ||
    points.length > MAX_MEASUREMENT_POINTS
  ) {
    throw new MeasurementError(
      "Invalid number of measurement points.",
    );
  }
}

function assertImage(image: SampledMPRImage): void {
  if (!image) {
    throw new MeasurementError("Image is unavailable.");
  }

  validateMPRPlane(image.plane);

  const { width, height, intensities } = image;
  const count = width * height;

  if (
    width !== image.plane.width ||
    height !== image.plane.height ||
    count > MAX_ROI_PIXELS ||
    !(intensities instanceof Float32Array) ||
    intensities.length !== count
  ) {
    throw new MeasurementError(
      "Invalid image dimensions or intensity buffer.",
    );
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 04 — VECTOR MATHEMATICS
// ============================================================

function difference(
  first: WorldPointLPS,
  second: WorldPointLPS,
): readonly [number, number, number] {
  return [
    first.x - second.x,
    first.y - second.y,
    first.z - second.z,
  ];
}

function dot(
  first: readonly number[],
  second: readonly number[],
): number {
  return (
    first[0] * second[0] +
    first[1] * second[1] +
    first[2] * second[2]
  );
}

function norm(vector: readonly number[]): number {
  return Math.hypot(
    vector[0],
    vector[1],
    vector[2],
  );
}

function cross(
  a: readonly number[],
  b: readonly number[],
): readonly [number, number, number] {
  return [
    a[1] * b[2] - a[2] * b[1],
    a[2] * b[0] - a[0] * b[2],
    a[0] * b[1] - a[1] * b[0],
  ];
}

function clamp(value: number): number {
  return Math.min(1, Math.max(-1, value));
}


// ============================================================
// 05 — PHYSICAL DISTANCE
// ============================================================

export function measureDistanceMm(
  first: WorldPointLPS,
  second: WorldPointLPS,
): DistanceMeasurement {
  assertWorld(first);
  assertWorld(second);

  const distanceMm = norm(
    difference(first, second),
  );

  if (!finite(distanceMm)) {
    throw new MeasurementError(
      "Physical distance exceeds numeric limits.",
    );
  }

  return { distanceMm };
}

export function measureVoxelDistanceMm(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
  first: VoxelPoint,
  second: VoxelPoint,
): DistanceMeasurement {
  const worldA = voxelToWorldLPS(
    geometry,
    first,
  );

  const worldB = voxelToWorldLPS(
    geometry,
    second,
  );

  return measureDistanceMm(worldA, worldB);
}


// ============================================================
// 06 — THREE-POINT ANGLE
// ============================================================

/**
 * Angle A-B-C, with the vertex at B.
 * Returns a value between 0 and 180 degrees.
 */
export function measureAngleDegrees(
  a: WorldPointLPS,
  vertex: WorldPointLPS,
  c: WorldPointLPS,
): AngleMeasurement {
  assertWorld(a);
  assertWorld(vertex);
  assertWorld(c);

  const first = difference(a, vertex);
  const second = difference(c, vertex);

  const lengthA = norm(first);
  const lengthB = norm(second);

  if (
    lengthA <= 0 ||
    lengthB <= 0 ||
    !finite(lengthA) ||
    !finite(lengthB)
  ) {
    throw new MeasurementError(
      "Angle arms must have nonzero length.",
    );
  }

  const cosine = dot(
    first.map((value) => value / lengthA),
    second.map((value) => value / lengthB),
  );

  const angleDegrees =
    Math.acos(clamp(cosine)) * 180 / Math.PI;

  if (!finite(angleDegrees)) {
    throw new MeasurementError(
      "Unable to calculate angle.",
    );
  }

  return { angleDegrees };
}


// ============================================================
// 07 — POLYLINE LENGTH
// ============================================================

export function measurePolylineMm(
  points: readonly WorldPointLPS[],
  closed = false,
): PolylineMeasurement {
  assertPointCollection(points, 2);

  for (const point of points) {
    assertWorld(point);
  }

  let lengthMm = 0;

  for (let index = 1; index < points.length; index++) {
    lengthMm += measureDistanceMm(
      points[index - 1],
      points[index],
    ).distanceMm;
  }

  if (closed && points.length > 2) {
    lengthMm += measureDistanceMm(
      points[points.length - 1],
      points[0],
    ).distanceMm;
  }

  if (!finite(lengthMm)) {
    throw new MeasurementError(
      "Polyline length exceeds numeric limits.",
    );
  }

  return {
    lengthMm,
    segmentCount:
      points.length - 1 +
      (closed && points.length > 2 ? 1 : 0),
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 08 — PLANAR WORLD-SPACE POLYGON AREA
// ============================================================

/**
 * Computes 3D planar polygon area using an area vector.
 *
 * Vertices must describe one simple, planar polygon
 * in their boundary order.
 *
 * Self-intersections are not supported.
 */
export function measurePolygonAreaMm2(
  points: readonly WorldPointLPS[],
  toleranceMm = 0.01,
): PolygonAreaMeasurement {
  assertPointCollection(points, 3);

  if (
    !finite(toleranceMm) ||
    toleranceMm < MIN_GEOMETRIC_TOLERANCE ||
    toleranceMm > MAX_GEOMETRIC_TOLERANCE
  ) {
    throw new MeasurementError(
      "Invalid polygon planarity tolerance.",
    );
  }

  for (const point of points) {
    assertWorld(point);
  }

  const origin = points[0];
  let areaX = 0;
  let areaY = 0;
  let areaZ = 0;

  for (let index = 1; index < points.length - 1; index++) {
    const a = difference(points[index], origin);
    const b = difference(points[index + 1], origin);
    const triangle = cross(a, b);

    areaX += triangle[0];
    areaY += triangle[1];
    areaZ += triangle[2];
  }

  const magnitude = Math.hypot(
    areaX,
    areaY,
    areaZ,
  );

  if (!finite(magnitude) || magnitude <= 0) {
    throw new MeasurementError(
      "Polygon has zero or invalid area.",
    );
  }

  const normal = [
    areaX / magnitude,
    areaY / magnitude,
    areaZ / magnitude,
  ];

  for (const point of points) {
    const displacement = difference(point, origin);
    const signedDistance = dot(
      normal,
      displacement,
    );

    if (Math.abs(signedDistance) > toleranceMm) {
      throw new MeasurementError(
        "Polygon points are not coplanar.",
      );
    }
  }

  return {
    areaMm2: magnitude / 2,
    perimeterMm: measurePolylineMm(
      points,
      true,
    ).lengthMm,
    vertexCount: points.length,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 09 — PIXEL-TO-WORLD MEASUREMENTS
// ============================================================

export function measureMPRDistanceMm(
  plane: MPRPlane,
  a: Point2D,
  b: Point2D,
): DistanceMeasurement {
  assertPoint2D(a);
  assertPoint2D(b);

  const worldA = mprPixelToWorldLPS(
    plane,
    a.x,
    a.y,
  );

  const worldB = mprPixelToWorldLPS(
    plane,
    b.x,
    b.y,
  );

  return measureDistanceMm(worldA, worldB);
}

export function measureMPRPolygonAreaMm2(
  plane: MPRPlane,
  points: readonly Point2D[],
): PolygonAreaMeasurement {
  assertPointCollection(points, 3);
  validateMPRPlane(plane);

  const worldPoints = points.map((point) => {
    assertPoint2D(point);

    return mprPixelToWorldLPS(
      plane,
      point.x,
      point.y,
    );
  });

  return measurePolygonAreaMm2(
    worldPoints,
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 10 — POLYGON PIXEL CONTAINMENT
// ============================================================

/**
 * Even-odd ray casting at pixel-centre coordinates.
 * Polygon vertices must form a simple boundary.
 */
export function pointInsidePolygon(
  point: Point2D,
  vertices: readonly Point2D[],
): boolean {
  assertPoint2D(point);
  assertPointCollection(vertices, 3);

  if (vertices.length > MAX_ROI_VERTICES) {
    throw new MeasurementError(
      "ROI polygon contains too many vertices.",
    );
  }

  let inside = false;

  for (
    let i = 0, j = vertices.length - 1;
    i < vertices.length;
    j = i++
  ) {
    const a = vertices[i];
    const b = vertices[j];

    assertPoint2D(a);
    assertPoint2D(b);

    if (
      (a.y > point.y) !== (b.y > point.y)
    ) {
      const intersectionX =
        (b.x - a.x) *
        (point.y - a.y) /
        (b.y - a.y) +
        a.x;

      if (point.x < intersectionX) {
        inside = !inside;
      }
    }
  }

  return inside;
}


// ============================================================
// 11 — ROI SHAPE VALIDATION
// ============================================================

export function validateROIShape(
  shape: ROIShape,
): void {
  if (!shape || typeof shape !== "object") {
    throw new MeasurementError("Invalid ROI shape.");
  }

  switch (shape.kind) {
    case "circle": {
      assertPoint2D(shape.roi.center);

      if (
        !finite(shape.roi.radiusPixels) ||
        shape.roi.radiusPixels <= 0
      ) {
        throw new MeasurementError(
```
