```
          "Invalid circular ROI radius.",
        );
      }

      return;
    }

    case "ellipse": {
      assertPoint2D(shape.roi.center);

      if (
        !finite(shape.roi.radiusXPixels) ||
        !finite(shape.roi.radiusYPixels) ||
        shape.roi.radiusXPixels <= 0 ||
        shape.roi.radiusYPixels <= 0
      ) {
        throw new MeasurementError(
          "Invalid elliptical ROI radii.",
        );
      }

      return;
    }

    case "polygon": {
      assertPointCollection(shape.roi.points, 3);

      if (shape.roi.points.length > MAX_ROI_VERTICES) {
        throw new MeasurementError(
          "ROI polygon contains too many vertices.",
        );
      }

      for (const vertex of shape.roi.points) {
        assertPoint2D(vertex);
      }

      return;
    }

    default:
      throw new MeasurementError(
        "Unsupported ROI shape.",
      );
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 12 — PIXEL MEMBERSHIP
// ============================================================

function roiContains(
  shape: ROIShape,
  x: number,
  y: number,
): boolean {
  switch (shape.kind) {
    case "circle": {
      const dx = x - shape.roi.center.x;
      const dy = y - shape.roi.center.y;

      return (
        dx * dx + dy * dy <=
        shape.roi.radiusPixels ** 2
      );
    }

    case "ellipse": {
      const dx = (
        x - shape.roi.center.x
      ) / shape.roi.radiusXPixels;

      const dy = (
        y - shape.roi.center.y
      ) / shape.roi.radiusYPixels;

      return dx * dx + dy * dy <= 1;
    }

    case "polygon":
      return pointInsidePolygon(
        { x, y },
        shape.roi.points,
      );
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 13 — INTENSITY STATISTICS
// ============================================================

/**
 * Uses Welford's online algorithm.
 *
 * Standard deviation is population SD (division by N).
 * Non-finite input values are excluded.
 */
export function calculateIntensityStatistics(
  values: readonly number[] | Float32Array | Float64Array,
): IntensityStatistics {
  let count = 0;
  let mean = 0;
  let m2 = 0;
  let minimum = Infinity;
  let maximum = -Infinity;
  let sum = 0;

  for (const value of values) {
    if (!finite(value)) {
      continue;
    }

    count++;
    sum += value;

    const delta = value - mean;
    mean += delta / count;

    const delta2 = value - mean;
    m2 += delta * delta2;

    minimum = Math.min(minimum, value);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, value);
  }

  if (count === 0) {
    return {
      count: 0,
      minimum: null,
      maximum: null,
      mean: null,
      standardDeviation: null,
      sum: 0,
    };
  }

  if (
    !finite(sum) ||
    !finite(mean) ||
    !finite(m2)
  ) {
    throw new MeasurementError(
      "Intensity statistics exceed numeric limits.",
    );
  }

  return {
    count,
    minimum,
    maximum,
    mean,
    standardDeviation: Math.sqrt(
      Math.max(0, m2 / count),
    ),
    sum,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 14 — ROI INTENSITY MEASUREMENTS
// ============================================================

/**
 * Pixel-centre inclusion mask.
 *
 * Physical area is included pixel count multiplied by
 * pixelSpacingU * pixelSpacingV. This is a rasterized
 * area approximation, not exact analytic boundary area.
 *
 * Invalid voxels contribute to geometry / area but are
 * excluded from intensity statistics.
 */
export function measureROI(
  image: SampledMPRImage,
  shape: ROIShape,
): ROIMeasurement {
  assertImage(image);
  validateROIShape(shape);

  const { width, height, intensities, plane } = image;
  const selected: number[] = [];

  let includedPixelCount = 0;
  let invalidPixelCount = 0;

  for (let y = 0; y < height; y++) {
    for (let x = 0; x < width; x++) {
      if (!roiContains(shape, x, y)) {
        continue;
      }

      includedPixelCount++;

      const value = intensities[
        y * width + x
      ];

      if (finite(value)) {
        selected.push(value);
      } else {
        invalidPixelCount++;
      }
    }
  }

  const areaMm2 = (
    includedPixelCount *
    plane.pixelSpacingUMm *
    plane.pixelSpacingVMm
  );

  if (!finite(areaMm2)) {
    throw new MeasurementError(
      "ROI area exceeds numeric limits.",
    );
  }

  return {
    areaMm2,
    includedPixelCount,
    invalidPixelCount,
    statistics: calculateIntensityStatistics(selected),
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 15 — MEASUREMENT CAPABILITIES
// ============================================================

export function getMeasurementCapabilities() {
  return {
    coordinateSystem: "LPS",
    distanceUnit: "mm",
    areaUnit: "mm2",
    angleUnit: "degrees",
    distance3D: true,
    angle3D: true,
    polyline3D: true,
    planarPolygonArea: true,
    circularROI: true,
    ellipticalROI: true,
    polygonROI: true,
    intensityStatistics: true,
    assumesCalibratedHU: false,
    modifiesSourceData: false,
    diagnosticValidationComplete: false,
  } as const;
}


// ============================================================
// END OF SINUS VIEWER MEASUREMENT UTILITIES
// ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/frontend/src/utils/mpr.ts
```
/**
 * SINUS VIEWER — MPR VIEWPORT MATHEMATICS
 * ======================================
 *
 * Pure TypeScript utilities for:
 * - Anatomical axial / coronal / sagittal views.
 * - Viewport sizing and fit-to-volume.
 * - Zoom around a screen anchor.
 * - Physical-millimetre panning.
 * - Slice movement along anatomical plane normals.
 * - Screen pixel <-> DICOM LPS world coordinates.
 * - Synchronized crosshair projections.
 * - MPR resampling-plane construction.
 *
 * Conventions:
 *   DICOM world coordinates: LPS millimetres.
 *   Viewport coordinates: left-to-right X, top-to-bottom Y.
 *   Image pixel coordinates refer to pixel centres.
 *
 * This module does not render or access source DICOM data.
 * Patient-aligned MPR resampling is performed by
 * volumeSampling.ts using the planes built here.
 *
 * The resulting view orientation is mathematical;
 * clinical radiological screen conventions need explicit
 * validation during viewer integration.
 */

import type {
  PreviewPlane,
  VolumeGeometry,
} from "../types/api";

import {
  assertVolumeGeometry,
  voxelToWorldLPS,
  type Vec3,
  type VoxelPoint,
  type WorldPointLPS,
} from "./geometry";

import {
  makePatientAlignedMPRPlane,
  type MPRPlane,
} from "./volumeSampling";


// ============================================================
// 01 — CONSTANTS
// ============================================================

export const MIN_MPR_ZOOM = 0.1;
export const MAX_MPR_ZOOM = 20;

export const DEFAULT_MPR_ZOOM = 1;
export const DEFAULT_MPR_FIT_PADDING = 0.92;

export const MAX_MPR_VIEWPORT_DIMENSION = 2048;
export const MAX_MPR_VIEWPORT_PIXELS = 4194304;

const FLOAT_TOLERANCE = 1e-8;


// ============================================================
// 02 — PUBLIC TYPES
// ============================================================

export interface MPRViewportSize {
  readonly width: number;
  readonly height: number;
}

export interface MPRScreenPoint {
  readonly x: number;
  readonly y: number;
}

export interface MPRViewportState {
  readonly plane: PreviewPlane;
  readonly size: MPRViewportSize;

  /**
   * World coordinate at the viewport centre.
   * Panning and slice navigation update this point.
   */
  readonly centerLPS: WorldPointLPS;

  /**
   * Physical millimetres per display pixel at zoom = 1.
   */
  readonly baseMmPerPixel: number;

  /**
   * Larger zoom makes anatomy appear larger.
   */
  readonly zoom: number;
}

export interface MPRCrosshairProjection {
  readonly screen: MPRScreenPoint;
  readonly visible: boolean;
  readonly planeDistanceMm: number;
}

export interface MPRPlaneAxes {
  readonly u: Vec3;
  readonly v: Vec3;
  readonly normal: Vec3;
}

export interface MPRFitOptions {
  readonly padding?: number;
  readonly minimumMmPerPixel?: number;
}

export class MPRError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message = "Invalid MPR viewport geometry.",
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "MPRError";
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 03 — NUMERIC AND VECTOR HELPERS
// ============================================================

function finite(value: unknown): value is number {
  return (
    typeof value === "number" &&
    Number.isFinite(value)
  );
}

function positive(value: unknown): value is number {
  return finite(value) && value > 0;
}

function validDimension(value: unknown): value is number {
  return (
    typeof value === "number" &&
    Number.isSafeInteger(value) &&
    value > 0 &&
    value <= MAX_MPR_VIEWPORT_DIMENSION
  );
}

function assertWorldPoint(point: WorldPointLPS): void {
  if (
    !point ||
    !finite(point.x) ||
    !finite(point.y) ||
    !finite(point.z)
  ) {
    throw new MPRError(
      "Invalid DICOM LPS coordinate.",
    );
  }
}

function assertScreenPoint(point: MPRScreenPoint): void {
  if (
    !point ||
    !finite(point.x) ||
    !finite(point.y)
  ) {
    throw new MPRError(
      "Invalid viewport pixel coordinate.",
    );
  }
}

function dot(a: Vec3, b: Vec3): number {
  return (
    a[0] * b[0] +
    a[1] * b[1] +
    a[2] * b[2]
  );
}

function subtract(
  a: WorldPointLPS,
  b: WorldPointLPS,
): Vec3 {
  return [
    a.x - b.x,
    a.y - b.y,
    a.z - b.z,
  ];
}

function translate(
  point: WorldPointLPS,
  axis: Vec3,
  distanceMm: number,
): WorldPointLPS {
  return {
    x: point.x + axis[0] * distanceMm,
    y: point.y + axis[1] * distanceMm,
    z: point.z + axis[2] * distanceMm,
  };
}

function clamp(
  value: number,
  minimum: number,
  maximum: number,
): number {
  return Math.min(
    maximum,
    Math.max(minimum, value),
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 04 — ANATOMICAL PLANE AXES
// ============================================================

/**
 * Axis mapping is deliberately consistent with
 * makePatientAlignedMPRPlane() in volumeSampling.ts.
 *
 * Axial:
 *   U = +L, V = +P, normal = +S
 *
 * Coronal:
 *   U = +L, V = +S, normal = -P
 *
 * Sagittal:
 *   U = +P, V = +S, normal = +L
 *
 * Coronal normal is -P because U cross V = -P.
 * Anatomical slice steps use these signed normals.
 */
export function getMPRPlaneAxes(
  plane: PreviewPlane,
): MPRPlaneAxes {
  switch (plane) {
    case "axial":
      return {
        u: [1, 0, 0],
        v: [0, 1, 0],
        normal: [0, 0, 1],
      };

    case "coronal":
      return {
        u: [1, 0, 0],
        v: [0, 0, 1],
        normal: [0, -1, 0],
      };

    case "sagittal":
      return {
        u: [0, 1, 0],
        v: [0, 0, 1],
        normal: [1, 0, 0],
      };

    default:
      throw new MPRError("Unsupported MPR plane.");
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 05 — VIEWPORT VALIDATION
// ============================================================

export function validateMPRViewportSize(
  size: MPRViewportSize,
): void {
  if (
    !size ||
    !validDimension(size.width) ||
    !validDimension(size.height) ||
    size.width * size.height >
      MAX_MPR_VIEWPORT_PIXELS
  ) {
    throw new MPRError(
      "Invalid or oversized MPR viewport.",
    );
  }
}

export function validateMPRViewport(
  state: MPRViewportState,
): void {
  if (!state) {
    throw new MPRError();
  }

  getMPRPlaneAxes(state.plane);
  validateMPRViewportSize(state.size);
  assertWorldPoint(state.centerLPS);

  if (
    !positive(state.baseMmPerPixel) ||
    !positive(state.zoom) ||
    state.zoom < MIN_MPR_ZOOM ||
    state.zoom > MAX_MPR_ZOOM
  ) {
    throw new MPRError(
      "Invalid MPR scale or zoom.",
    );
  }

  const effective = (
    state.baseMmPerPixel / state.zoom
  );

  if (!positive(effective)) {
    throw new MPRError(
      "Invalid MPR effective pixel spacing.",
    );
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 06 — WORLD-SPACE VOLUME CORNERS
// ============================================================

/**
 * Bounding positions refer to voxel centres.
 * We inspect all eight corners because datasets may
 * have an oblique direction matrix.
 */
export function getMPRVolumeCorners(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
): readonly WorldPointLPS[] {
  assertVolumeGeometry(geometry);

  const [depth, height, width] =
    geometry.shape_zyx;

  const result: WorldPointLPS[] = [];

  for (const z of [0, depth - 1]) {
    for (const y of [0, height - 1]) {
      for (const x of [0, width - 1]) {
        result.push(
          voxelToWorldLPS(
            geometry,
            { x, y, z },
          ),
        );
      }
    }
  }

  return result;
}


// ============================================================
// 07 — PROJECTED ANATOMICAL EXTENTS
// ============================================================

export interface MPRProjectedBounds {
  readonly minimumU: number;
  readonly maximumU: number;
  readonly minimumV: number;
  readonly maximumV: number;
  readonly minimumNormal: number;
  readonly maximumNormal: number;
}

export function getMPRProjectedBounds(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
  plane: PreviewPlane,
): MPRProjectedBounds {
  const axes = getMPRPlaneAxes(plane);
  const corners = getMPRVolumeCorners(geometry);

  let minimumU = Infinity;
  let maximumU = -Infinity;
  let minimumV = Infinity;
  let maximumV = -Infinity;
  let minimumNormal = Infinity;
  let maximumNormal = -Infinity;

  for (const point of corners) {
    const world: Vec3 = [
      point.x,
      point.y,
      point.z,
    ];

    const u = dot(world, axes.u);
    const v = dot(world, axes.v);
    const normal = dot(world, axes.normal);

    minimumU = Math.min(minimumU, u);
    maximumU = Math.max(maximumU, u);

    minimumV = Math.min(minimumV, v);
    maximumV = Math.max(maximumV, v);

    minimumNormal = Math.min(
      minimumNormal,
      normal,
    );

    maximumNormal = Math.max(
      maximumNormal,
      normal,
    );
  }

  return {
    minimumU,
    maximumU,
    minimumV,
    maximumV,
    minimumNormal,
    maximumNormal,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 08 — FIT-TO-VOLUME SCALE
// ============================================================

/**
 * Returns physical millimetres per display pixel.
 * The entire projected voxel-centre bounding box
 * fits inside the viewport, with configurable padding.
 *
 * Degenerate single-slice dimensions get a fallback
 * physical extent from minimum source spacing.
 */
export function computeMPRFitScale(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
  plane: PreviewPlane,
  size: MPRViewportSize,
  options: MPRFitOptions = {},
): number {
  assertVolumeGeometry(geometry);
  validateMPRViewportSize(size);

  const padding = options.padding ??
    DEFAULT_MPR_FIT_PADDING;

  const minimumMmPerPixel =
    options.minimumMmPerPixel ??
    Math.min(...geometry.spacing_xyz_mm) / 8;

  if (
    !finite(padding) ||
    padding <= 0 ||
    padding > 1 ||
    !positive(minimumMmPerPixel)
  ) {
    throw new MPRError(
      "Invalid MPR fit configuration.",
    );
  }

  const bounds = getMPRProjectedBounds(
    geometry,
    plane,
  );

  const fallbackExtent =
    Math.min(...geometry.spacing_xyz_mm);

  const extentU = Math.max(
    bounds.maximumU - bounds.minimumU,
    fallbackExtent,
  );

  const extentV = Math.max(
    bounds.maximumV - bounds.minimumV,
    fallbackExtent,
  );

  const availableWidth = Math.max(
    1,
    (size.width - 1) * padding,
  );

  const availableHeight = Math.max(
    1,
    (size.height - 1) * padding,
  );

  const scale = Math.max(
    extentU / availableWidth,
    extentV / availableHeight,
    minimumMmPerPixel,
  );

  if (!positive(scale)) {
    throw new MPRError(
      "Unable to fit MPR volume.",
    );
  }

  return scale;
}


// ============================================================
// 09 — VOLUME WORLD CENTRE
// ============================================================

export function getMPRVolumeCenter(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
): WorldPointLPS {
  assertVolumeGeometry(geometry);

  const [depth, height, width] =
    geometry.shape_zyx;

  const voxel: VoxelPoint = {
    x: (width - 1) / 2,
    y: (height - 1) / 2,
    z: (depth - 1) / 2,
  };

  return voxelToWorldLPS(
    geometry,
    voxel,
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 10 — CREATE / RESET VIEWPORT
// ============================================================

export function createMPRViewport(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
  plane: PreviewPlane,
  size: MPRViewportSize,
  options: MPRFitOptions = {},
): MPRViewportState {
  const state: MPRViewportState = {
    plane,
    size,
    centerLPS: getMPRVolumeCenter(geometry),
    baseMmPerPixel: computeMPRFitScale(
      geometry,
      plane,
      size,
      options,
    ),
    zoom: DEFAULT_MPR_ZOOM,
  };

  validateMPRViewport(state);
  return state;
}

export function resetMPRViewport(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
  state: MPRViewportState,
): MPRViewportState {
  validateMPRViewport(state);

  return createMPRViewport(
    geometry,
    state.plane,
    state.size,
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 11 — EFFECTIVE DISPLAY SPACING
// ============================================================

export function getMPRMmPerPixel(
  state: MPRViewportState,
): number {
  validateMPRViewport(state);

  return state.baseMmPerPixel / state.zoom;
}


// ============================================================
// 12 — SCREEN TO WORLD
// ============================================================

/**
 * Pixel origin is top-left.
 * For odd viewport sizes, the centre lands on a pixel.
 * For even viewport sizes, the centre is between pixels.
 *
 * The returned world coordinate lies on the current
 * MPR plane, with no image-bound clamping.
 */
export function mprScreenToWorldLPS(
  state: MPRViewportState,
  screen: MPRScreenPoint,
): WorldPointLPS {
  validateMPRViewport(state);
  assertScreenPoint(screen);

  const axes = getMPRPlaneAxes(state.plane);
  const mmPerPixel = getMPRMmPerPixel(state);

  const centerX = (state.size.width - 1) / 2;
  const centerY = (state.size.height - 1) / 2;

  const du = (screen.x - centerX) * mmPerPixel;
  const dv = (screen.y - centerY) * mmPerPixel;

  const movedU = translate(
    state.centerLPS,
    axes.u,
    du,
  );

  return translate(movedU, axes.v, dv);
}


// ============================================================
// 13 — WORLD TO SCREEN
// ============================================================

export function mprWorldToScreen(
  state: MPRViewportState,
  world: WorldPointLPS,
): MPRScreenPoint {
  validateMPRViewport(state);
  assertWorldPoint(world);

  const axes = getMPRPlaneAxes(state.plane);
  const delta = subtract(world, state.centerLPS);
  const mmPerPixel = getMPRMmPerPixel(state);

  return {
    x: (state.size.width - 1) / 2 +
      dot(delta, axes.u) / mmPerPixel,

    y: (state.size.height - 1) / 2 +
      dot(delta, axes.v) / mmPerPixel,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 14 — PAN THE VIEW
// ============================================================

/**
 * Dragging the image towards positive screen X
 * moves the displayed anatomy rightward.
 *
 * Therefore the world-space viewport centre shifts
 * in the opposite direction.
 */
export function panMPRViewport(
  state: MPRViewportState,
  deltaScreenX: number,
  deltaScreenY: number,
): MPRViewportState {
  validateMPRViewport(state);

  if (
    !finite(deltaScreenX) ||
    !finite(deltaScreenY)
  ) {
    throw new MPRError(
      "Invalid MPR pan distance.",
    );
  }

  const axes = getMPRPlaneAxes(state.plane);
  const spacing = getMPRMmPerPixel(state);

  const movedU = translate(
    state.centerLPS,
    axes.u,
    -deltaScreenX * spacing,
  );

  const centerLPS = translate(
    movedU,
    axes.v,
    -deltaScreenY * spacing,
  );

  const next: MPRViewportState = {
    ...state,
    centerLPS,
  };

  validateMPRViewport(next);
  return next;
}


// ============================================================
// 15 — ZOOM AROUND AN ANCHOR
// ============================================================

/**
 * Preserve the DICOM world position beneath the
 * zoom anchor (usually the pointer).
 */
export function zoomMPRViewport(
  state: MPRViewportState,
  scaleFactor: number,
  anchor?: MPRScreenPoint,
): MPRViewportState {
  validateMPRViewport(state);

  if (
    !positive(scaleFactor)
  ) {
    throw new MPRError(
      "Invalid MPR zoom factor.",
    );
  }

  const actualAnchor = anchor ?? {
    x: (state.size.width - 1) / 2,
    y: (state.size.height - 1) / 2,
  };

  assertScreenPoint(actualAnchor);

  const worldBefore = mprScreenToWorldLPS(
    state,
    actualAnchor,
  );

  const desiredZoom = state.zoom * scaleFactor;

  const zoom = clamp(
    desiredZoom,
    MIN_MPR_ZOOM,
    MAX_MPR_ZOOM,
  );

  const axes = getMPRPlaneAxes(state.plane);
  const nextMmPerPixel =
    state.baseMmPerPixel / zoom;

  const du = (
    actualAnchor.x -
    (state.size.width - 1) / 2
  ) * nextMmPerPixel;

  const dv = (
    actualAnchor.y -
    (state.size.height - 1) / 2
  ) * nextMmPerPixel;

  const shifted = translate(
    worldBefore,
    axes.u,
    -du,
  );

  const centerLPS = translate(
    shifted,
    axes.v,
    -dv,
  );

  const next: MPRViewportState = {
    ...state,
    zoom,
    centerLPS,
  };

  validateMPRViewport(next);
  return next;
}


// ============================================================
// 16 — RESIZE VIEWPORT
// ============================================================

/**
 * Preserve the physical scale and world centre
 * when the viewport dimensions change.
 */
export function resizeMPRViewport(
  state: MPRViewportState,
  size: MPRViewportSize,
): MPRViewportState {
  validateMPRViewport(state);
  validateMPRViewportSize(size);

  const next: MPRViewportState = {
    ...state,
    size,
  };

  validateMPRViewport(next);
  return next;
}


// ============================================================
// 17 — ANATOMICAL SLICE NAVIGATION
// ============================================================

/**
 * Slice navigation is measured in physical millimetres,
 * along the plane's signed normal.
 */
export function moveMPRSliceMm(
  state: MPRViewportState,
  distanceMm: number,
): MPRViewportState {
  validateMPRViewport(state);

  if (!finite(distanceMm)) {
    throw new MPRError(
      "Invalid slice movement.",
    );
  }

  const normal = getMPRPlaneAxes(
    state.plane,
  ).normal;

  const next: MPRViewportState = {
    ...state,
    centerLPS: translate(
      state.centerLPS,
      normal,
      distanceMm,
    ),
  };

  validateMPRViewport(next);
  return next;
}


// ============================================================
// 18 — ANATOMICAL SLICE STEP ESTIMATION
// ============================================================

/**
 * Estimate source-grid sampling spacing in the
 * anatomical plane-normal direction.
 *
 * For a rotated affine volume, the physical step
 * along a patient axis is determined by the inverse
 * voxel-to-world affine mapping.
 *
 * This is a navigation increment, not a statement
 * that the acquisition contains slices at that step.
 */
export function getMPRSliceStepMm(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
  plane: PreviewPlane,
): number {
  assertVolumeGeometry(geometry);

  const normal = getMPRPlaneAxes(plane).normal;
  const direction = geometry.direction_lps;
  const spacing = geometry.spacing_xyz_mm;

  // Direction is orthonormal, so its inverse is its
  // transpose for this step-length calculation.
  const rx = (
    direction[0] * normal[0] +
    direction[3] * normal[1] +
    direction[6] * normal[2]
  ) / spacing[0];

  const ry = (
    direction[1] * normal[0] +
    direction[4] * normal[1] +
    direction[7] * normal[2]
  ) / spacing[1];

  const rz = (
    direction[2] * normal[0] +
    direction[5] * normal[1] +
    direction[8] * normal[2]
  ) / spacing[2];

  const voxelDisplacementPerMm = Math.hypot(
    rx,
    ry,
    rz,
  );

  if (
    !positive(voxelDisplacementPerMm) ||
    voxelDisplacementPerMm < FLOAT_TOLERANCE
  ) {
    throw new MPRError(
      "Unable to estimate MPR slice step.",
    );
  }

  return 1 / voxelDisplacementPerMm;
}

export function stepMPRSlice(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
  state: MPRViewportState,
  steps: number,
): MPRViewportState {
  if (
    !Number.isSafeInteger(steps)
  ) {
    throw new MPRError(
      "Slice step count must be an integer.",
    );
  }

  return moveMPRSliceMm(
    state,
    steps * getMPRSliceStepMm(
      geometry,
      state.plane,
    ),
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 19 — CROSSHAIR PROJECTION
// ============================================================

/**
 * Crosshair is visible only if:
 * - projected pixel lies within the viewport, and
 * - its world point is sufficiently close to the
 *   current anatomical slice plane.
 *
 * The plane-distance tolerance is caller-controlled.
 */
export function projectMPRCrosshair(
  state: MPRViewportState,
  crosshairWorld: WorldPointLPS,
  planeToleranceMm = 0.5,
): MPRCrosshairProjection {
  validateMPRViewport(state);
  assertWorldPoint(crosshairWorld);

  if (
    !finite(planeToleranceMm) ||
    planeToleranceMm < 0
  ) {
    throw new MPRError(
      "Invalid crosshair tolerance.",
    );
  }

  const axes = getMPRPlaneAxes(state.plane);

  const difference = subtract(
    crosshairWorld,
    state.centerLPS,
  );

  const screen = mprWorldToScreen(
    state,
    crosshairWorld,
  );

  const planeDistanceMm = dot(
    difference,
    axes.normal,
  );

  const insideViewport = (
    screen.x >= 0 &&
    screen.y >= 0 &&
    screen.x <= state.size.width - 1 &&
    screen.y <= state.size.height - 1
  );

  return {
    screen,
    visible:
      insideViewport &&
      Math.abs(planeDistanceMm) <=
        planeToleranceMm,
    planeDistanceMm,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 20 — MOVE VIEW TO CROSSHAIR
// ============================================================

/**
 * Synchronize the slice coordinate to a crosshair,
 * retaining current in-plane pan and zoom.
 *
 * To centre the crosshair fully, use centreMPRAtWorld().
 */
export function alignMPRSliceToWorld(
  state: MPRViewportState,
  crosshairWorld: WorldPointLPS,
): MPRViewportState {
  validateMPRViewport(state);
  assertWorldPoint(crosshairWorld);

  const normal = getMPRPlaneAxes(
    state.plane,
  ).normal;

  const distanceMm = dot(
    subtract(
      crosshairWorld,
      state.centerLPS,
    ),
    normal,
  );

  return moveMPRSliceMm(
    state,
    distanceMm,
  );
}

export function centreMPRAtWorld(
  state: MPRViewportState,
  world: WorldPointLPS,
): MPRViewportState {
  validateMPRViewport(state);
  assertWorldPoint(world);

  const next: MPRViewportState = {
    ...state,
    centerLPS: { ...world },
  };

  validateMPRViewport(next);
  return next;
}


// ============================================================
// 21 — CONSTRUCT RESAMPLING PLANE
// ============================================================

/**
 * Creates the exact plane passed to
 * sampleMPRPlane() in volumeSampling.ts.
 *
 * Every output pixel represents the same physical
 * position as mprScreenToWorldLPS().
 */
export function buildMPRSamplingPlane(
  state: MPRViewportState,
): MPRPlane {
  validateMPRViewport(state);

  const mmPerPixel = getMPRMmPerPixel(state);

  return makePatientAlignedMPRPlane(
    state.plane,
    state.centerLPS,
    state.size.width,
    state.size.height,
    mmPerPixel,
    mmPerPixel,
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 22 — VIEWPORT GEOMETRY CAPABILITIES
// ============================================================

export function getMPRCapabilities() {
  return {
    coordinateSystem: "LPS",
    patientAlignedResampling: true,
    obliqueVolumeSupport: true,
    zoomAroundAnchor: true,
    physicalMillimetrePan: true,
    sliceNavigation: true,
    crosshairProjection: true,
    modifiesSourceVolume: false,
    diagnosticValidationComplete: false,
  } as const;
}


// ============================================================
// END OF SINUS VIEWER MPR VIEWPORT UTILITIES
// ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/frontend/src/utils/volumeSampling.ts
```
/**
 * SINUS VIEWER — VOLUME SAMPLING UTILITIES
 * ========================================
 *
 * Pure TypeScript utilities for:
 * - Nearest-neighbor sampling.
 * - Trilinear interpolation.
 * - Fractional voxel coordinates.
 * - DICOM LPS world-coordinate sampling.
 * - True patient-aligned MPR sampling grids.
 * - Out-of-bounds fill or clamp behavior.
 * - Non-finite intensity handling.
 * - Bounded image-buffer allocation.
 *
 * ARRAY CONTRACT
 * --------------
 * shapeZYX = [depth, height, width]
 *
 * Linear index:
 *   z * height * width + y * width + x
 *
 * Coordinate contract:
 *   voxel = [x, y, z]
 *   world = DICOM LPS millimetres
 *
 * This code samples the already decoded float32 volume.
 * No original DICOM files are accessed or modified.
 *
 * Rendering and diagnostic validation remain separate.
 */

import {
  assertVolumeGeometry,
  invertMat3,
  type Mat3,
  type Vec3,
  type VoxelPoint,
  type WorldPointLPS,
} from "./geometry";

import type {
  PreviewPlane,
  Shape3,
  VolumeGeometry,
} from "../types/api";


// ============================================================
// 01 — CONSTANTS
// ============================================================

export const DEFAULT_OUTSIDE_VALUE = 0;

export const MAX_SAMPLING_IMAGE_WIDTH = 2048;

export const MAX_SAMPLING_IMAGE_HEIGHT = 2048;

export const MAX_SAMPLING_PIXELS = 4 * 1024 * 1024;

const AXIS_TOLERANCE = 1e-4;


// ============================================================
// 02 — PUBLIC TYPES
// ============================================================

export type InterpolationMode =
  | "nearest"
  | "linear";

export type BoundaryMode =
  | "fill"
  | "clamp";

export interface SamplingOptions {
  readonly interpolation?: InterpolationMode;
  readonly boundary?: BoundaryMode;
  readonly outsideValue?: number;
}

export interface SamplingVolume {
  readonly voxels: Float32Array;
  readonly shapeZYX: Shape3;
  readonly geometry: VolumeGeometry;
}

export interface MPRPlane {
  readonly originLPS: WorldPointLPS;
  readonly uDirectionLPS: Vec3;
  readonly vDirectionLPS: Vec3;
  readonly pixelSpacingUMm: number;
  readonly pixelSpacingVMm: number;
  readonly width: number;
  readonly height: number;
}

export interface SampledMPRImage {
  readonly width: number;
  readonly height: number;
  readonly intensities: Float32Array;
  readonly plane: MPRPlane;
}

export interface VolumeSampler {
  readonly volume: SamplingVolume;

  sampleVoxel(
    x: number,
    y: number,
    z: number,
    options?: SamplingOptions,
  ): number;

  sampleWorld(
    point: WorldPointLPS,
    options?: SamplingOptions,
  ): number;

  samplePlane(
    plane: MPRPlane,
    options?: SamplingOptions,
  ): SampledMPRImage;
}

export class VolumeSamplingError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message = "Invalid volume sampling request.",
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "VolumeSamplingError";
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 03 — NUMERIC VALIDATION
// ============================================================

function finite(value: unknown): value is number {
  return (
    typeof value === "number" &&
    Number.isFinite(value)
  );
}

function positiveSafeInteger(
  value: unknown,
): value is number {
  return (
    typeof value === "number" &&
    Number.isSafeInteger(value) &&
    value > 0
  );
}

function finiteVec3(
  value: unknown,
): value is Vec3 {
  return (
    Array.isArray(value) &&
    value.length === 3 &&
    value.every(finite)
  );
}

function clamp(
  value: number,
  minimum: number,
  maximum: number,
): number {
  return Math.min(
    maximum,
    Math.max(minimum, value),
  );
}

function vectorLength(vector: Vec3): number {
  return Math.hypot(
    vector[0],
    vector[1],
    vector[2],
  );
}

function vectorDot(a: Vec3, b: Vec3): number {
  return (
    a[0] * b[0] +
    a[1] * b[1] +
    a[2] * b[2]
  );
}

function validateOptions(
  options: SamplingOptions,
): Required<SamplingOptions> {
  const interpolation =
    options.interpolation ?? "linear";

  const boundary =
    options.boundary ?? "fill";

  const outsideValue =
    options.outsideValue ?? DEFAULT_OUTSIDE_VALUE;

  if (
    (interpolation !== "nearest" &&
      interpolation !== "linear") ||
    (boundary !== "fill" &&
      boundary !== "clamp") ||
    !finite(outsideValue)
  ) {
    throw new VolumeSamplingError(
      "Invalid interpolation or boundary settings.",
    );
  }

  return {
    interpolation,
    boundary,
    outsideValue,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 04 — VOLUME VALIDATION
// ============================================================

export function validateSamplingVolume(
  volume: SamplingVolume,
): void {
  if (
    !volume ||
    !(volume.voxels instanceof Float32Array) ||
    !Array.isArray(volume.shapeZYX) ||
    volume.shapeZYX.length !== 3 ||
    !volume.shapeZYX.every(positiveSafeInteger)
  ) {
    throw new VolumeSamplingError(
      "Invalid volume buffer or dimensions.",
    );
  }

  assertVolumeGeometry(volume.geometry);

  const [depth, height, width] = volume.shapeZYX;

  const count = depth * height * width;

  if (
    !Number.isSafeInteger(count) ||
    count !== volume.voxels.length
  ) {
    throw new VolumeSamplingError(
      "Volume dimensions do not match voxel data.",
    );
  }

  const geometryShape = volume.geometry.shape_zyx;

  if (
    geometryShape[0] !== depth ||
    geometryShape[1] !== height ||
    geometryShape[2] !== width
  ) {
    throw new VolumeSamplingError(
      "Volume geometry dimensions do not match.",
    );
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 05 — BOUNDARY COORDINATES
// ============================================================

function resolveCoordinates(
  shape: Shape3,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  z: number,
  boundary: BoundaryMode,
): VoxelPoint | null {
  if (
    !finite(x) ||
    !finite(y) ||
    !finite(z)
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  const [depth, height, width] = shape;

  if (boundary === "clamp") {
    return {
      x: clamp(x, 0, width - 1),
      y: clamp(y, 0, height - 1),
      z: clamp(z, 0, depth - 1),
    };
  }

  if (
    x < 0 ||
    y < 0 ||
    z < 0 ||
    x > width - 1 ||
    y > height - 1 ||
    z > depth - 1
  ) {
    return null;
  }

  return { x, y, z };
}


// ============================================================
// 06 — VOXEL ARRAY ACCESS
// ============================================================

function voxelValue(
  volume: SamplingVolume,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  z: number,
): number {
  const [, height, width] = volume.shapeZYX;

  return volume.voxels[
    (z * height + y) * width + x
  ];
}


// ============================================================
// 07 — NEAREST-NEIGHBOR SAMPLING
// ============================================================

function sampleNearestUnchecked(
  volume: SamplingVolume,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  z: number,
  options: Required<SamplingOptions>,
): number {
  const point = resolveCoordinates(
    volume.shapeZYX,
    x,
    y,
    z,
    options.boundary,
  );

  if (!point) {
    return options.outsideValue;
  }

  const [depth, height, width] =
    volume.shapeZYX;

  const ix = clamp(
    Math.round(point.x),
    0,
    width - 1,
  );

  const iy = clamp(
    Math.round(point.y),
    0,
    height - 1,
  );

  const iz = clamp(
    Math.round(point.z),
    0,
    depth - 1,
  );

  const value = voxelValue(
    volume,
    ix,
    iy,
    iz,
  );

  return finite(value)
    ? value
    : options.outsideValue;
}

export function sampleNearest(
  volume: SamplingVolume,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  z: number,
  options: SamplingOptions = {},
): number {
  validateSamplingVolume(volume);

  return sampleNearestUnchecked(
    volume,
    x,
    y,
    z,
    validateOptions(options),
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 08 — TRILINEAR INTERPOLATION
// ============================================================

function sampleTrilinearUnchecked(
  volume: SamplingVolume,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  z: number,
  options: Required<SamplingOptions>,
): number {
  const point = resolveCoordinates(
    volume.shapeZYX,
    x,
    y,
    z,
    options.boundary,
  );

  if (!point) {
    return options.outsideValue;
  }

  const [depth, height, width] =
    volume.shapeZYX;

  const x0 = Math.floor(point.x);
  const y0 = Math.floor(point.y);
  const z0 = Math.floor(point.z);

  const x1 = Math.min(x0 + 1, width - 1);
  const y1 = Math.min(y0 + 1, height - 1);
  const z1 = Math.min(z0 + 1, depth - 1);

  const fx = point.x - x0;
  const fy = point.y - y0;
  const fz = point.z - z0;

  const wx = [1 - fx, fx];
  const wy = [1 - fy, fy];
  const wz = [1 - fz, fz];

  const xs = [x0, x1];
  const ys = [y0, y1];
  const zs = [z0, z1];

  let result = 0;

  for (let iz = 0; iz < 2; iz++) {
    for (let iy = 0; iy < 2; iy++) {
      for (let ix = 0; ix < 2; ix++) {
        const weight =
          wx[ix] * wy[iy] * wz[iz];

        // A zero-weight neighbor must not contaminate
        // an exact voxel-center sample with NaN.
        if (weight === 0) {
          continue;
        }

        const value = voxelValue(
          volume,
          xs[ix],
          ys[iy],
          zs[iz],
        );

        if (!finite(value)) {
          return options.outsideValue;
        }

        result += weight * value;
      }
    }
  }

  return finite(result)
    ? result
    : options.outsideValue;
}

export function sampleTrilinear(
  volume: SamplingVolume,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  z: number,
  options: SamplingOptions = {},
): number {
  validateSamplingVolume(volume);

  return sampleTrilinearUnchecked(
    volume,
    x,
    y,
    z,
    validateOptions(options),
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 09 — GENERIC VOXEL SAMPLING
// ============================================================

function samplePreparedVoxel(
  volume: SamplingVolume,
  x: number,
  y: number,
  z: number,
  options: Required<SamplingOptions>,
): number {
  if (options.interpolation === "nearest") {
    return sampleNearestUnchecked(
      volume,
      x,
      y,
      z,
      options,
    );
  }

  return sampleTrilinearUnchecked(
    volume,
    x,
    y,
    z,
    options,
  );
}

export function sampleVoxel(
  volume: SamplingVolume,
  point: VoxelPoint,
  options: SamplingOptions = {},
): number {
  validateSamplingVolume(volume);

  return samplePreparedVoxel(
    volume,
    point.x,
    point.y,
    point.z,
    validateOptions(options),
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 10 — CACHED LPS TO VOXEL TRANSFORM
// ============================================================

interface WorldTransform {
  readonly origin: Vec3;
  readonly inverseDirection: Mat3;
  readonly spacing: Vec3;
}

function prepareWorldTransform(
  geometry: VolumeGeometry,
): WorldTransform {
  assertVolumeGeometry(geometry);

  return {
    origin: geometry.origin_lps,
    inverseDirection: invertMat3(
      geometry.direction_lps,
    ),
    spacing: geometry.spacing_xyz_mm,
  };
}

function worldToVoxelPrepared(
  transform: WorldTransform,
  world: WorldPointLPS,
): VoxelPoint {
  if (
    !finite(world.x) ||
    !finite(world.y) ||
    !finite(world.z)
  ) {
    throw new VolumeSamplingError(
      "Invalid world coordinate.",
    );
  }

  const dx = world.x - transform.origin[0];
  const dy = world.y - transform.origin[1];
  const dz = world.z - transform.origin[2];

  const m = transform.inverseDirection;
  const spacing = transform.spacing;

  return {
    x: (
      m[0] * dx +
      m[1] * dy +
      m[2] * dz
    ) / spacing[0],

    y: (
      m[3] * dx +
      m[4] * dy +
      m[5] * dz
    ) / spacing[1],

    z: (
      m[6] * dx +
      m[7] * dy +
      m[8] * dz
    ) / spacing[2],
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 11 — WORLD-COORDINATE SAMPLING
// ============================================================

export function sampleWorldLPS(
  volume: SamplingVolume,
  world: WorldPointLPS,
  options: SamplingOptions = {},
): number {
  validateSamplingVolume(volume);

  const transform = prepareWorldTransform(
    volume.geometry,
  );

  const voxel = worldToVoxelPrepared(
    transform,
    world,
  );

  return samplePreparedVoxel(
    volume,
    voxel.x,
    voxel.y,
    voxel.z,
    validateOptions(options),
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 12 — MPR PLANE VALIDATION
// ============================================================

export function validateMPRPlane(
  plane: MPRPlane,
): void {
  if (
    !plane ||
    !finiteVec3(plane.uDirectionLPS) ||
    !finiteVec3(plane.vDirectionLPS) ||
    !plane.originLPS ||
    !finite(plane.originLPS.x) ||
    !finite(plane.originLPS.y) ||
    !finite(plane.originLPS.z) ||
    !positiveSafeInteger(plane.width) ||
    !positiveSafeInteger(plane.height) ||
    plane.width > MAX_SAMPLING_IMAGE_WIDTH ||
    plane.height > MAX_SAMPLING_IMAGE_HEIGHT ||
    plane.width * plane.height > MAX_SAMPLING_PIXELS ||
    !finite(plane.pixelSpacingUMm) ||
    !finite(plane.pixelSpacingVMm) ||
    plane.pixelSpacingUMm <= 0 ||
    plane.pixelSpacingVMm <= 0
  ) {
    throw new VolumeSamplingError(
      "Invalid MPR plane dimensions or geometry.",
    );
  }

  const uLength = vectorLength(
    plane.uDirectionLPS,
  );

  const vLength = vectorLength(
    plane.vDirectionLPS,
  );

  const dot = vectorDot(
    plane.uDirectionLPS,
    plane.vDirectionLPS,
  );

  if (
    Math.abs(uLength - 1) > AXIS_TOLERANCE ||
    Math.abs(vLength - 1) > AXIS_TOLERANCE ||
    Math.abs(dot) > AXIS_TOLERANCE
  ) {
    throw new VolumeSamplingError(
      "MPR plane axes must be orthonormal.",
    );
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 13 — PATIENT-ALIGNED MPR PLANES
// ============================================================

/**
 * Patient-aligned LPS plane axes.
 *
 * These are mathematical sampling directions.
 * Radiological / neurological screen flips are
 * presentation concerns and handled separately.
 *
 * axial:    LPS X / Y
 * coronal:  LPS X / Z
 * sagittal: LPS Y / Z
 */
function anatomicalAxes(
  plane: PreviewPlane,
): {
  u: Vec3;
  v: Vec3;
} {
  switch (plane) {
    case "axial":
      return {
        u: [1, 0, 0],
        v: [0, 1, 0],
      };

    case "coronal":
      return {
        u: [1, 0, 0],
        v: [0, 0, 1],
      };

    case "sagittal":
      return {
        u: [0, 1, 0],
        v: [0, 0, 1],
      };
  }
}

export function makePatientAlignedMPRPlane(
  plane: PreviewPlane,
  centerLPS: WorldPointLPS,
  width: number,
  height: number,
  pixelSpacingUMm: number,
  pixelSpacingVMm: number,
): MPRPlane {
  const axes = anatomicalAxes(plane);

  const halfU =
    (width - 1) * pixelSpacingUMm / 2;

  const halfV =
    (height - 1) * pixelSpacingVMm / 2;

  const result: MPRPlane = {
    originLPS: {
      x: centerLPS.x -
        axes.u[0] * halfU -
        axes.v[0] * halfV,

      y: centerLPS.y -
        axes.u[1] * halfU -
        axes.v[1] * halfV,

      z: centerLPS.z -
        axes.u[2] * halfU -
        axes.v[2] * halfV,
    },

    uDirectionLPS: axes.u,
    vDirectionLPS: axes.v,

    pixelSpacingUMm,
    pixelSpacingVMm,

    width,
    height,
  };

  validateMPRPlane(result);
  return result;
}


// ============================================================
// 14 — MPR PIXEL TO WORLD POSITION
// ============================================================

export function mprPixelToWorldLPS(
  plane: MPRPlane,
  u: number,
  v: number,
): WorldPointLPS {
  validateMPRPlane(plane);

  if (!finite(u) || !finite(v)) {
    throw new VolumeSamplingError(
      "Invalid MPR pixel coordinates.",
    );
  }

  const du = u * plane.pixelSpacingUMm;
  const dv = v * plane.pixelSpacingVMm;

  return {
    x: plane.originLPS.x +
      plane.uDirectionLPS[0] * du +
      plane.vDirectionLPS[0] * dv,

    y: plane.originLPS.y +
      plane.uDirectionLPS[1] * du +
      plane.vDirectionLPS[1] * dv,

    z: plane.originLPS.z +
      plane.uDirectionLPS[2] * du +
      plane.vDirectionLPS[2] * dv,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 15 — TRUE WORLD-SPACE MPR SAMPLING
// ============================================================

function sampleMPRPrepared(
  volume: SamplingVolume,
  transform: WorldTransform,
  plane: MPRPlane,
  options: Required<SamplingOptions>,
): SampledMPRImage {
  validateMPRPlane(plane);

  const intensities = new Float32Array(
    plane.width * plane.height,
  );

  const origin = plane.originLPS;

  const ux =
    plane.uDirectionLPS[0] *
    plane.pixelSpacingUMm;

  const uy =
    plane.uDirectionLPS[1] *
    plane.pixelSpacingUMm;

  const uz =
    plane.uDirectionLPS[2] *
    plane.pixelSpacingUMm;

  const vx =
    plane.vDirectionLPS[0] *
    plane.pixelSpacingVMm;

  const vy =
    plane.vDirectionLPS[1] *
    plane.pixelSpacingVMm;

  const vz =
    plane.vDirectionLPS[2] *
    plane.pixelSpacingVMm;

  let index = 0;

  for (let row = 0; row < plane.height; row++) {
    for (let col = 0; col < plane.width; col++) {
      const world: WorldPointLPS = {
        x: origin.x + col * ux + row * vx,
        y: origin.y + col * uy + row * vy,
        z: origin.z + col * uz + row * vz,
      };

      const voxel = worldToVoxelPrepared(
        transform,
        world,
      );

      intensities[index++] = samplePreparedVoxel(
        volume,
        voxel.x,
        voxel.y,
        voxel.z,
        options,
      );
    }
  }

  return {
    width: plane.width,
    height: plane.height,
    intensities,
    plane,
  };
}

export function sampleMPRPlane(
  volume: SamplingVolume,
  plane: MPRPlane,
  options: SamplingOptions = {},
): SampledMPRImage {
  validateSamplingVolume(volume);

  const transform = prepareWorldTransform(
    volume.geometry,
  );

  return sampleMPRPrepared(
    volume,
    transform,
    plane,
    validateOptions(options),
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 16 — PREPARED VOLUME SAMPLER
// ============================================================

/**
 * Reuse the validated geometry and inverse direction
 * matrix across many sampling requests.
 *
 * The caller must treat the input voxel buffer and
 * geometry as immutable while the sampler is active.
 */
export function createVolumeSampler(
  volume: SamplingVolume,
): VolumeSampler {
  validateSamplingVolume(volume);

  const transform = prepareWorldTransform(
    volume.geometry,
  );

  return {
    volume,

    sampleVoxel(
      x: number,
      y: number,
      z: number,
      options: SamplingOptions = {},
    ): number {
      return samplePreparedVoxel(
        volume,
        x,
        y,
        z,
        validateOptions(options),
      );
    },

    sampleWorld(
      world: WorldPointLPS,
      options: SamplingOptions = {},
    ): number {
      const voxel = worldToVoxelPrepared(
        transform,
        world,
      );

      return samplePreparedVoxel(
        volume,
        voxel.x,
        voxel.y,
        voxel.z,
        validateOptions(options),
      );
    },

    samplePlane(
      plane: MPRPlane,
      options: SamplingOptions = {},
    ): SampledMPRImage {
      return sampleMPRPrepared(
        volume,
        transform,
        plane,
        validateOptions(options),
      );
    },
  };
}


// ============================================================
// END OF SINUS VIEWER VOLUME SAMPLING
// ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/frontend/src/utils/windowLevel.ts
```
/**
 * SINUS VIEWER — WINDOW / LEVEL UTILITIES
 * ======================================
 *
 * Pure TypeScript intensity mapping for CT / CBCT:
 * - Window center and window width.
 * - DICOM-style linear grayscale mapping.
 * - Brightness and contrast adjustments.
 * - Automatic display range estimation.
 * - Percentile-based window selection.
 * - Uint8 grayscale and RGBA conversion.
 * - NaN / Infinity / degenerate range handling.
 * - Inverted grayscale presentation.
 *
 * Important:
 * CBCT values are not assumed to be calibrated HU.
 * These functions do not perform modality rescaling.
 * Voxel data must already contain correctly decoded
 * physical / source intensity values.
 *
 * These are display utilities, NOT diagnostic
 * calibration or image-quality certification.
 *
 * This module performs no filesystem, HTTP,
 * storage, or original DICOM modification.
 */


// ============================================================
// 01 — CONSTANTS
// ============================================================

export const DEFAULT_WINDOW_CENTER = 0;
export const DEFAULT_WINDOW_WIDTH = 400;

export const DEFAULT_AUTO_LOW_PERCENTILE = 1;
export const DEFAULT_AUTO_HIGH_PERCENTILE = 99;

export const DEFAULT_AUTO_SAMPLE_LIMIT = 100000;

export const MAX_AUTO_SAMPLE_LIMIT = 1000000;

export const MIN_WINDOW_WIDTH = 1;
export const MAX_WINDOW_WIDTH = 1000000000;

export const MIN_BRIGHTNESS = -1;
export const MAX_BRIGHTNESS = 1;

export const MIN_CONTRAST = 0.1;
export const MAX_CONTRAST = 5;

const UINT8_MAX = 255;


// ============================================================
// 02 — PUBLIC TYPES
// ============================================================

export interface WindowLevel {
  readonly center: number;
  readonly width: number;
}

export interface DisplayAdjustments {
  /**
   * Brightness offset in normalized intensity units:
   * -1 = darkest offset
   *  0 = unchanged
   * +1 = brightest offset
   */
  readonly brightness: number;

  /**
   * Contrast multiplier applied around 0.5:
   * 1 = unchanged
   */
  readonly contrast: number;

  readonly invert: boolean;
}

export interface IntensityStatistics {
  readonly minimum: number;
  readonly maximum: number;
  readonly mean: number;
  readonly finiteCount: number;
  readonly sampledCount: number;
}

export interface AutoWindowOptions {
  readonly lowPercentile?: number;
  readonly highPercentile?: number;
  readonly sampleLimit?: number;
  readonly minimumWidth?: number;
}

export interface RenderOptions {
  readonly window: WindowLevel;
  readonly adjustments?: Partial<DisplayAdjustments>;
  readonly invalidPixelValue?: number;
}

export interface GrayscaleImage {
  readonly width: number;
  readonly height: number;
  readonly pixels: Uint8ClampedArray;
}

export interface RGBAImage {
  readonly width: number;
  readonly height: number;
  readonly pixels: Uint8ClampedArray;
}

export class WindowLevelError extends Error {
  constructor(
    message = "Invalid window/level configuration.",
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "WindowLevelError";
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 03 — SHARED NUMERIC HELPERS
// ============================================================

function isFiniteNumber(
  value: unknown,
): value is number {
  return (
    typeof value === "number" &&
    Number.isFinite(value)
  );
}

function isPositiveInteger(
  value: unknown,
): value is number {
  return (
    typeof value === "number" &&
    Number.isSafeInteger(value) &&
    value > 0
  );
}

function clampNumber(
  value: number,
  minimum: number,
  maximum: number,
): number {
  return Math.min(
    maximum,
    Math.max(minimum, value),
  );
}

function clamp01(value: number): number {
  return clampNumber(value, 0, 1);
}

function byteValue(value: number): number {
  if (!isFiniteNumber(value)) {
    throw new WindowLevelError(
      "Invalid grayscale fill value.",
    );
  }

  return Math.round(
    clampNumber(value, 0, UINT8_MAX),
  );
}

function validateImageSize(
  width: number,
  height: number,
  inputLength: number,
  channels: 1 | 4,
): void {
  if (
    !isPositiveInteger(width) ||
    !isPositiveInteger(height) ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(inputLength) ||
    inputLength < 0
  ) {
    throw new WindowLevelError(
      "Invalid image dimensions.",
    );
  }

  const count = width * height;
  const outputLength = count * channels;

  if (
    !Number.isSafeInteger(count) ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(outputLength) ||
    count !== inputLength
  ) {
    throw new WindowLevelError(
      "Image dimensions do not match voxel count.",
    );
  }
}

function ensureIntensities(
  values: Float32Array | Float64Array,
): void {
  if (
    !(values instanceof Float32Array) &&
    !(values instanceof Float64Array)
  ) {
    throw new WindowLevelError(
      "Expected a floating-point intensity array.",
    );
  }
}


// ============================================================
// 04 — WINDOW VALIDATION
// ============================================================

export function validateWindowLevel(
  window: WindowLevel,
): void {
  if (
    !window ||
    !isFiniteNumber(window.center) ||
    !isFiniteNumber(window.width) ||
    window.width < MIN_WINDOW_WIDTH ||
    window.width > MAX_WINDOW_WIDTH
  ) {
    throw new WindowLevelError();
  }
}

export function createWindowLevel(
  center: number,
  width: number,
): WindowLevel {
  const result: WindowLevel = {
    center,
    width,
  };

  validateWindowLevel(result);
  return result;
}

export function windowLevelFromRange(
  minimum: number,
  maximum: number,
): WindowLevel {
  if (
    !isFiniteNumber(minimum) ||
    !isFiniteNumber(maximum) ||
    maximum < minimum
  ) {
    throw new WindowLevelError(
      "Invalid display intensity range.",
    );
  }

  const center = minimum / 2 + maximum / 2;

  if (!isFiniteNumber(center)) {
    throw new WindowLevelError(
      "Display center exceeds numeric limits.",
    );
  }

  const width = Math.max(
    MIN_WINDOW_WIDTH,
    maximum - minimum,
  );

  return createWindowLevel(center, width);
}


// ============================================================
// 05 — DISPLAY ADJUSTMENTS
// ============================================================

export function normalizeAdjustments(
  options: Partial<DisplayAdjustments> = {},
): DisplayAdjustments {
  const brightness = options.brightness ?? 0;
  const contrast = options.contrast ?? 1;
  const invert = options.invert ?? false;

  if (
    !isFiniteNumber(brightness) ||
    brightness < MIN_BRIGHTNESS ||
    brightness > MAX_BRIGHTNESS ||
    !isFiniteNumber(contrast) ||
    contrast < MIN_CONTRAST ||
    contrast > MAX_CONTRAST ||
    typeof invert !== "boolean"
  ) {
    throw new WindowLevelError(
      "Invalid brightness or contrast settings.",
    );
  }

  return {
    brightness,
    contrast,
    invert,
  };
}

export function adjustBrightness(
  adjustments: DisplayAdjustments,
  brightness: number,
): DisplayAdjustments {
  return normalizeAdjustments({
    ...adjustments,
    brightness,
  });
}

export function adjustContrast(
  adjustments: DisplayAdjustments,
  contrast: number,
): DisplayAdjustments {
  return normalizeAdjustments({
    ...adjustments,
    contrast,
  });
}


// ============================================================
// 06 — DICOM-STYLE LINEAR WINDOW MAPPING
// ============================================================

/**
 * Implements the DICOM LINEAR VOI window convention.
 *
 * For width = 1:
 *   x <= center - 0.5 -> black
 *   x >  center - 0.5 -> white
 *
 * For width > 1:
 *   lower = center - 0.5 - (width - 1)/2
 *   upper = center - 0.5 + (width - 1)/2
 *
 * The output is normalized to [0, 1].
 *
 * Does not implement LINEAR_EXACT, SIGMOID VOI,
 * presentation LUTs or modality rescale transforms.
 */
export function intensityToNormalized(
  intensity: number,
  window: WindowLevel,
): number {
  validateWindowLevel(window);

  if (!isFiniteNumber(intensity)) {
    return 0;
  }

  const center = window.center;
  const width = window.width;
  const adjustedCenter = center - 0.5;

  if (width === 1) {
    return intensity <= adjustedCenter ? 0 : 1;
  }

  const halfRange = (width - 1) / 2;
  const lower = adjustedCenter - halfRange;
  const upper = adjustedCenter + halfRange;

  if (intensity <= lower) {
    return 0;
  }

  if (intensity > upper) {
    return 1;
  }

  return clamp01(
    (intensity - adjustedCenter) /
      (width - 1) + 0.5,
  );
}

function applyAdjustments(
  normalized: number,
  adjustments: DisplayAdjustments,
): number {
  let value = (
    (normalized - 0.5) * adjustments.contrast +
    0.5 +
    adjustments.brightness
  );

  value = clamp01(value);

  return adjustments.invert
    ? 1 - value
    : value;
}

function mapPreparedIntensity(
  intensity: number,
  window: WindowLevel,
  adjustments: DisplayAdjustments,
  invalidPixel: number,
): number {
  if (!isFiniteNumber(intensity)) {
    return invalidPixel;
  }

  const normalized = intensityToNormalized(
    intensity,
    window,
  );

  return Math.round(
    applyAdjustments(
      normalized,
      adjustments,
    ) * UINT8_MAX,
  );
}

export function intensityToUint8(
  intensity: number,
  window: WindowLevel,
  options: Partial<DisplayAdjustments> = {},
): number {
  validateWindowLevel(window);

  const adjustments = normalizeAdjustments(options);

  return mapPreparedIntensity(
    intensity,
    window,
    adjustments,
    0,
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 07 — WINDOW POSITION AND WIDTH CONTROLS
// ============================================================

export function shiftWindowCenter(
  window: WindowLevel,
  delta: number,
): WindowLevel {
  validateWindowLevel(window);

  if (!isFiniteNumber(delta)) {
    throw new WindowLevelError(
      "Invalid window center adjustment.",
    );
  }

  return createWindowLevel(
    window.center + delta,
    window.width,
  );
}

export function scaleWindowWidth(
  window: WindowLevel,
  scale: number,
): WindowLevel {
  validateWindowLevel(window);

  if (
    !isFiniteNumber(scale) ||
    scale <= 0
  ) {
    throw new WindowLevelError(
      "Invalid window width adjustment.",
    );
  }

  const scaled = window.width * scale;

  if (!isFiniteNumber(scaled)) {
    throw new WindowLevelError(
      "Window width exceeds numeric limits.",
    );
  }

  return createWindowLevel(
    window.center,
    clampNumber(
      scaled,
      MIN_WINDOW_WIDTH,
      MAX_WINDOW_WIDTH,
    ),
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 08 — DETERMINISTIC INTENSITY SAMPLING
// ============================================================

/**
 * Bounded sampling to avoid sorting an entire volume.
 *
 * Sampling is deterministic and never mutates input.
 * Sampling a regular stride may underrepresent small
 * findings; this is for display auto-windowing only.
 */
export function sampleFiniteIntensities(
  values: Float32Array | Float64Array,
  sampleLimit = DEFAULT_AUTO_SAMPLE_LIMIT,
): Float64Array {
  ensureIntensities(values);

  if (
    !isPositiveInteger(sampleLimit) ||
    sampleLimit > MAX_AUTO_SAMPLE_LIMIT
  ) {
    throw new WindowLevelError(
      "Invalid sampling limit.",
    );
  }

  if (values.length === 0) {
    return new Float64Array(0);
  }

  const count = Math.min(
    sampleLimit,
    values.length,
  );

  const sample: number[] = [];

  for (let index = 0; index < count; index++) {
    const sourceIndex = count === 1
      ? Math.floor((values.length - 1) / 2)
      : Math.floor(
          index * (values.length - 1) / (count - 1),
        );

    const value = values[sourceIndex];

    if (Number.isFinite(value)) {
      sample.push(value);
    }
  }

  return Float64Array.from(sample);
}


// ============================================================
// 09 — INTENSITY STATISTICS
// ============================================================

export function getIntensityStatistics(
  values: Float32Array | Float64Array,
  sampleLimit = DEFAULT_AUTO_SAMPLE_LIMIT,
): IntensityStatistics | null {
  const sampled = sampleFiniteIntensities(
    values,
    sampleLimit,
  );

  if (sampled.length === 0) {
    return null;
  }

  let minimum = Infinity;
  let maximum = -Infinity;
  let mean = 0;

  // Incremental mean avoids a large sum overflow.
  for (let index = 0; index < sampled.length; index++) {
    const value = sampled[index];

    minimum = Math.min(minimum, value);
    maximum = Math.max(maximum, value);

    mean += (
      value - mean
    ) / (index + 1);
  }

  return {
    minimum,
    maximum,
    mean,
    finiteCount: sampled.length,
    sampledCount: sampled.length,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 10 — PERCENTILE ESTIMATION
// ============================================================

export function estimatePercentile(
  values: Float64Array,
  percentile: number,
): number {
  if (
    !(values instanceof Float64Array) ||
    values.length === 0 ||
    !isFiniteNumber(percentile) ||
    percentile < 0 ||
    percentile > 100
  ) {
    throw new WindowLevelError(
      "Invalid percentile request.",
    );
  }

  if (!values.every(Number.isFinite)) {
    throw new WindowLevelError(
      "Percentile values must be finite.",
    );
  }

  const ordered = Array.from(values).sort(
    (a, b) => a - b,
  );

  const rank = (
    percentile / 100
  ) * (ordered.length - 1);

  const lowerIndex = Math.floor(rank);
  const upperIndex = Math.ceil(rank);
  const fraction = rank - lowerIndex;

  const lower = ordered[lowerIndex];
  const upper = ordered[upperIndex];

  return lower + (
    upper - lower
  ) * fraction;
}


// ============================================================
// 11 — AUTOMATIC WINDOW ESTIMATION
// ============================================================

export function estimateAutoWindow(
  values: Float32Array | Float64Array,
  options: AutoWindowOptions = {},
): WindowLevel {
  const lowPercentile =
    options.lowPercentile ??
    DEFAULT_AUTO_LOW_PERCENTILE;

  const highPercentile =
    options.highPercentile ??
    DEFAULT_AUTO_HIGH_PERCENTILE;

  const sampleLimit =
    options.sampleLimit ??
    DEFAULT_AUTO_SAMPLE_LIMIT;

  const minimumWidth =
    options.minimumWidth ??
    MIN_WINDOW_WIDTH;

  if (
    !isFiniteNumber(lowPercentile) ||
    !isFiniteNumber(highPercentile) ||
    lowPercentile < 0 ||
    highPercentile > 100 ||
    lowPercentile >= highPercentile ||
    !isFiniteNumber(minimumWidth) ||
    minimumWidth < MIN_WINDOW_WIDTH ||
    minimumWidth > MAX_WINDOW_WIDTH
  ) {
    throw new WindowLevelError(
      "Invalid automatic window options.",
    );
  }

  const sampled = sampleFiniteIntensities(
    values,
    sampleLimit,
  );

  if (sampled.length === 0) {
    return createWindowLevel(
      DEFAULT_WINDOW_CENTER,
      DEFAULT_WINDOW_WIDTH,
    );
  }

  const low = estimatePercentile(
    sampled,
    lowPercentile,
  );

  const high = estimatePercentile(
    sampled,
    highPercentile,
  );

  const estimated = windowLevelFromRange(
    low,
    high,
  );

  return createWindowLevel(
    estimated.center,
    Math.min(
      MAX_WINDOW_WIDTH,
      Math.max(
        estimated.width,
        minimumWidth,
      ),
    ),
  );
}


// ============================================================
// 12 — GRAYSCALE IMAGE RENDERING
// ============================================================

export function renderGrayscale(
  values: Float32Array | Float64Array,
  width: number,
  height: number,
  options: RenderOptions,
): GrayscaleImage {
  ensureIntensities(values);

  validateImageSize(
    width,
    height,
    values.length,
    1,
  );

  validateWindowLevel(options.window);

  const adjustments = normalizeAdjustments(
    options.adjustments,
  );

  const invalidPixel = byteValue(
    options.invalidPixelValue ?? 0,
  );

  const pixels = new Uint8ClampedArray(
    values.length,
  );

  for (let index = 0; index < values.length; index++) {
    pixels[index] = mapPreparedIntensity(
      values[index],
      options.window,
      adjustments,
      invalidPixel,
    );
  }

  return {
    width,
    height,
    pixels,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 13 — RGBA IMAGE RENDERING
// ============================================================

/**
 * Creates RGBA pixels suitable for ImageData.
 *
 * Every rendered pixel is opaque. Invalid source
 * intensities use the configured grayscale fill.
 */
export function renderRGBA(
  values: Float32Array | Float64Array,
  width: number,
  height: number,
  options: RenderOptions,
): RGBAImage {
  ensureIntensities(values);

  validateImageSize(
    width,
    height,
    values.length,
    4,
  );

  validateWindowLevel(options.window);

  const adjustments = normalizeAdjustments(
    options.adjustments,
  );

  const invalidPixel = byteValue(
    options.invalidPixelValue ?? 0,
  );

  const pixels = new Uint8ClampedArray(
    values.length * 4,
  );

  for (let index = 0; index < values.length; index++) {
    const gray = mapPreparedIntensity(
      values[index],
      options.window,
      adjustments,
      invalidPixel,
    );

    const offset = index * 4;

    pixels[offset] = gray;
    pixels[offset + 1] = gray;
    pixels[offset + 2] = gray;
    pixels[offset + 3] = UINT8_MAX;
  }

  return {
    width,
    height,
    pixels,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 14 — GRAYSCALE TO RGBA
// ============================================================

export function grayscaleToRGBA(
  image: GrayscaleImage,
): RGBAImage {
  if (
    !isPositiveInteger(image.width) ||
    !isPositiveInteger(image.height) ||
    !(image.pixels instanceof Uint8ClampedArray)
  ) {
    throw new WindowLevelError(
      "Invalid grayscale image.",
    );
  }

  validateImageSize(
    image.width,
    image.height,
    image.pixels.length,
    4,
  );

  const rgba = new Uint8ClampedArray(
    image.pixels.length * 4,
  );

  for (
    let index = 0;
    index < image.pixels.length;
    index++
  ) {
    const value = image.pixels[index];
    const offset = index * 4;

    rgba[offset] = value;
    rgba[offset + 1] = value;
    rgba[offset + 2] = value;
    rgba[offset + 3] = UINT8_MAX;
  }

  return {
    width: image.width,
    height: image.height,
    pixels: rgba,
  };
}


// ============================================================
// 15 — SIMPLE GRAYSCALE INVERSION
// ============================================================

export function invertGrayscale(
  pixels: Uint8ClampedArray,
): Uint8ClampedArray {
  if (!(pixels instanceof Uint8ClampedArray)) {
    throw new WindowLevelError(
      "Expected an 8-bit grayscale array.",
    );
  }

  const output = new Uint8ClampedArray(
    pixels.length,
  );

  for (let index = 0; index < pixels.length; index++) {
    output[index] = UINT8_MAX - pixels[index];
  }

  return output;
}


// ============================================================
// 16 — DEFAULT DISPLAY PRESETS
// ============================================================

/**
 * Generic starting presets only.
 *
 * Their numeric values are meaningful as CT HU windows
 * only when the upstream pipeline has established that
 * the intensities are calibrated HU.
 *
 * For uncalibrated CBCT use estimateAutoWindow() or
 * an explicitly selected source-intensity window.
 */
export const WINDOW_PRESETS = {
  default: {
    center: DEFAULT_WINDOW_CENTER,
    width: DEFAULT_WINDOW_WIDTH,
  },
  narrow: {
    center: 0,
    width: 150,
  },
  wide: {
    center: 0,
    width: 1500,
  },
} as const satisfies Readonly<
  Record<string, WindowLevel>
>;


// ============================================================
// 17 — CAPABILITIES
// ============================================================

export function getWindowLevelCapabilities() {
  return {
    displayMapping: "DICOM_LINEAR",
    grayscaleOutput: "uint8",
    rgbaOutput: "uint8",
    automaticWindow: true,
    deterministicSampling: true,
    brightnessContrast: true,
    invert: true,
    assumesCalibratedHU: false,
    modifiesSourceVoxels: false,
  } as const;
}


// ============================================================
// END OF SINUS VIEWER WINDOW / LEVEL UTILITIES
// ============================================================
```
