### sinus-viewer/backend/app/api/routes/auth.py
```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — AUTHENTICATION API ROUTES
========================================

Endpoints:
    POST /api/auth/login
    POST /api/auth/logout
    GET  /api/auth/session

Integrates:
- AuthenticationService (security/auth.py)
- Origin and session dependencies (security/session.py)
- Strict API schemas (api/schemas.py)
- Dataset session ownership (services/dataset_service.py)

Security:
- No bearer tokens returned in JSON.
- Session token is placed in an HttpOnly cookie.
- Login route requires valid same-origin request.
- Invalid credentials receive generic responses.
- Logout revokes the server-side session.
- Logout closes datasets owned by that session.
- No patient identifiers or paths in responses or logs.
- No original DICOM files are modified.

Runtime:
    Routes are synchronous so FastAPI may execute them
    through its thread pool. The frontend must include
    credentials with browser API requests.

IMPORTANT:
    Login throttling and HTTPS checks are supplied by
    the central middleware. Register the middleware
    before serving requests.

Python 3.10+
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Annotated

from fastapi import (
    APIRouter,
    Depends,
    HTTPException,
    Request,
    Response,
    status,
)

from ..dependencies import (
    require_dataset_service,
)
from ..schemas import (
    LoginRequest,
    LoginResponse,
    LogoutResponse,
    SessionStatus,
)
from ...security.auth import (
    AuthConfigurationError,
    AuthError,
    AuthenticationService,
    InvalidCredentials,
    SessionCapacityExceeded,
    clear_session_cookie,
    set_session_cookie,
)
from ...security.session import (
    AuthenticatedContext,
    extract_session_token,
    get_authentication_service,
    require_authenticated_context,
    require_safe_login_request,
    require_safe_logout_request,
)
from ...services.dataset_service import (
    DatasetService,
    DatasetServiceError,
)


# ============================================================
# 01 — ROUTER
# ============================================================

router = APIRouter(
    prefix="/api/auth",
    tags=["Authentication"],
)


# ============================================================
# 02 — SAFE ERROR HELPER
# ============================================================

def _auth_http_error(
    status_code: int,
    code: str,
) -> HTTPException:
    """
    Return only a generic error code.

    No username, token, password hash, source filename
    or low-level authentication exception is disclosed.
    """

    return HTTPException(
        status_code=status_code,
        detail={
            "code": code,
        },
        headers={
            "Cache-Control": "no-store",
            "Pragma": "no-cache",
        },
    )


# ============================================================
# 03 — LOGIN
# ============================================================

@router.post(
    "/login",
    response_model=LoginResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
)
def login(
    credentials: LoginRequest,
    response: Response,
    request: Request,
    _: Annotated[
        None,
        Depends(require_safe_login_request),
    ],
) -> LoginResponse:
    """
    Authenticate configured operator credentials.

    On success:
    - Create a server-side session.
    - Set a secure HttpOnly cookie.
    - Return non-sensitive session metadata.

    Token is never included in the JSON response.
    """

    service: AuthenticationService = (
        get_authentication_service(request)
    )

    try:
        token = service.login(
            credentials.username,
            credentials.password,
        )

    except InvalidCredentials:
        raise _auth_http_error(
            status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            "AUTH_FAILED",
        ) from None

    except SessionCapacityExceeded:
        raise _auth_http_error(
            status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE",
        ) from None

    except AuthConfigurationError:
        raise _auth_http_error(
            status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            "CONFIG_ERROR",
        ) from None

    except AuthError:
        raise _auth_http_error(
            status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE",
        ) from None

    try:
        # Confirm that session registration succeeded
        # before attaching the cookie to the response.
        identity = service.authenticate(
            token,
            update_activity=False,
        )

        set_session_cookie(
            response,
            token,
        )

        response.headers["Cache-Control"] = "no-store"
        response.headers["Pragma"] = "no-cache"

        return LoginResponse(
            session=SessionStatus(
                authenticated=True,
                expires_at=identity.expires_at,
            )
        )

    except Exception:
        # If cookie creation or response validation fails,
        # revoke the newly issued server-side token.
        try:
            service.logout(token)
        except AuthError:
            pass
        raise


# ============================================================
# 04 — CURRENT SESSION STATUS
# ============================================================

@router.get(
    "/session",
    response_model=SessionStatus,
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
)
def current_session(
    context: Annotated[
        AuthenticatedContext,
        Depends(require_authenticated_context),
    ],
) -> SessionStatus:
    """
    Return current authenticated session information.

    Invalid or expired sessions are rejected by the
    dependency; no anonymous success response is returned.
    """

    return SessionStatus(
        authenticated=True,
        expires_at=context.expires_at,
    )


# ============================================================
# 05 — LOGOUT CLEANUP
# ============================================================

def close_owned_dataset_sessions(
    datasets: DatasetService,
    *,
    owner_id: str,
) -> int:
    """
    Close every dataset session belonging to this login.

    This does not modify DICOM files.

    An earlier in-flight image operation may still hold
    in-memory references; HTTP authorization must also
    be enforced for all later requests.
    """

    closed = 0

    sessions = datasets.list_datasets(
        owner_id=owner_id,
    )

    for session in sessions:
        session_id = session.get(
            "dataset_session_id"
        )

        if not isinstance(session_id, str):
            continue

        try:
            datasets.close_dataset(
                session_id,
                owner_id=owner_id,
            )
            closed += 1

        except DatasetServiceError:
            # A scan may be cancelled or a session may
            # already have closed concurrently.
            continue

    return closed


# ============================================================
# 06 — LOGOUT
# ============================================================

@router.post(
    "/logout",
    response_model=LogoutResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
)
def logout(
    request: Request,
    response: Response,
    context: Annotated[
        AuthenticatedContext,
        Depends(require_safe_logout_request),
    ],
    datasets: Annotated[
        DatasetService,
        Depends(require_dataset_service),
    ],
) -> LogoutResponse:
    """
    Revoke session and release owned dataset indexes.

    Session revocation takes priority. Dataset cleanup
    failures do not restore an authenticated session.

    Note:
        Cached volumes are managed by a separate cache
        service and are not globally cleared here because
        global eviction could interfere with other users.
    """

    service = get_authentication_service(request)

    token = extract_session_token(request)

    if token is None:
        raise _auth_http_error(
            status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
            "AUTH_REQUIRED",
        )

    try:
        revoked = service.logout(token)

        if not revoked:
            raise _auth_http_error(
                status.HTTP_401_UNAUTHORIZED,
                "AUTH_REQUIRED",
            )

        # Dataset ownership is tied to the authenticated
        # session's randomly generated owner ID.
        try:
            close_owned_dataset_sessions(
                datasets,
                owner_id=context.owner_id,
            )
        except DatasetServiceError:
            # Session is already revoked. The dataset
            # registry will need lifecycle cleanup.
            pass

    finally:
        clear_session_cookie(response)

    response.headers["Cache-Control"] = "no-store"
    response.headers["Pragma"] = "no-cache"

    return LogoutResponse(
        success=True,
        authenticated=False,
    )


# ============================================================
# 07 — ROUTE CAPABILITIES
# ============================================================

def get_auth_route_capabilities() -> dict:
    """
    Static, non-sensitive route contract declaration.
    """

    return {
        "login": "/api/auth/login",
        "logout": "/api/auth/logout",
        "session": "/api/auth/session",
        "cookie_authentication": True,
        "token_in_json": False,
        "login_origin_check": True,
        "logout_origin_check": True,
        "server_side_session_revocation": True,
        "logout_dataset_cleanup": True,
        "read_only_dataset": True,
    }


# ============================================================
# END OF AUTHENTICATION API ROUTES
# ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/backend/app/api/routes/datasets.py
```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — DATASET AND PATIENT API ROUTES
=============================================

Authenticated, read-only dataset browser API.

Endpoints:
    POST   /api/datasets
    GET    /api/datasets
    GET    /api/datasets/{dataset_session_id}
    POST   /api/datasets/{dataset_session_id}/refresh
    DELETE /api/datasets/{dataset_session_id}
    GET    /api/datasets/{dataset_session_id}/patients
    GET    /api/datasets/{dataset_session_id}/patients/{patient_handle}/series
    GET    /api/datasets/{dataset_session_id}/series/{series_handle}/validation

Security:
- Every endpoint requires an authenticated server session.
- owner_id comes only from trusted authentication context.
- Original patient identifiers and DICOM paths are excluded.
- Internal scanner objects are never serialized directly.
- Only approved output fields are copied into API schemas.
- No file or folder is created, renamed, modified or deleted.

Runtime:
- Dataset scanning is blocking and potentially expensive.
- FastAPI executes synchronous route functions in its
  worker-thread pool.
- Application-level scan concurrency is managed by
  DatasetService.
- Auth, Origin and HTTPS protections must be registered
  at application startup.

Python 3.10+ / FastAPI / Pydantic v2
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from typing import Annotated, Any

from fastapi import (
    APIRouter,
    Depends,
    HTTPException,
    status,
)

from ..dependencies import require_dataset_service
from ..schemas import (
    DatasetCloseResponse,
    DatasetListResponse,
    DatasetResponse,
    DatasetSelectRequest,
    DatasetSummary,
    PatientListResponse,
    PatientSummary,
    SeriesIssue,
    SeriesListResponse,
    SeriesSummary,
    SeriesValidationResponse,
    ValidationSeverity,
)
from ...security.session import (
    AuthenticatedContext,
    require_authenticated_context,
)
from ...services.dataset_service import (
    DatasetAccessError,
    DatasetBusyError,
    DatasetOperationCancelled,
    DatasetPatientNotFound,
    DatasetSelectionError,
    DatasetService,
    DatasetServiceError,
    DatasetSessionNotFound,
    DatasetSeriesNotFound,
)


# ============================================================
# 01 — ROUTER
# ============================================================

router = APIRouter(
    prefix="/api/datasets",
    tags=["Datasets"],
)

MAX_PUBLIC_ISSUES = 256
MAX_PUBLIC_PATIENTS = 100000
MAX_PUBLIC_SERIES = 100000

SAFE_ISSUE_CODE = re.compile(
    r"^[A-Z0-9_]{1,64}$"
)


# ============================================================
# 02 — AUTHENTICATED DEPENDENCY ALIASES
# ============================================================

TrustedUser = Annotated[
    AuthenticatedContext,
    Depends(require_authenticated_context),
]

TrustedDatasets = Annotated[
    DatasetService,
    Depends(require_dataset_service),
]


# ============================================================
# 03 — CONTROLLED HTTP ERRORS
# ============================================================

def _api_error(
    status_code: int,
    code: str,
) -> HTTPException:
    """
    No scanner exception text or raw source metadata.
    """

    return HTTPException(
        status_code=status_code,
        detail={"code": code},
        headers={
            "Cache-Control": "no-store",
            "Pragma": "no-cache",
        },
    )


def _translate_dataset_error(
    error: DatasetServiceError,
) -> HTTPException:
    """
    Centralized mapping from internal dataset failures.

    Never include error.args, filenames or source paths.
    """

    if isinstance(error, DatasetSessionNotFound):
        return _api_error(404, "DATASET_NOT_FOUND")

    if isinstance(error, DatasetPatientNotFound):
        return _api_error(404, "PATIENT_NOT_FOUND")

    if isinstance(error, DatasetSeriesNotFound):
        return _api_error(404, "SERIES_NOT_FOUND")

    if isinstance(error, DatasetSelectionError):
        return _api_error(403, "PATH_REJECTED")

    if isinstance(error, DatasetAccessError):
        return _api_error(403, "ACCESS_DENIED")

    if isinstance(error, DatasetOperationCancelled):
        return _api_error(409, "OPERATION_CANCELLED")

    if isinstance(error, DatasetBusyError):
        return _api_error(409, "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE")

    return _api_error(500, "PROCESSING_FAILED")


# ============================================================
# 04 — STRICT DATASET RESPONSE ADAPTER
# ============================================================

def _dataset_summary(
    value: dict[str, Any],
) -> DatasetSummary:
    """
    Explicit allowlist of dataset summary fields.
    """

    return DatasetSummary(
        dataset_session_id=value["dataset_session_id"],
        state=value["state"],
        generation=value["generation"],
        patient_count=value["patient_count"],
        series_count=value["series_count"],
        dicom_files=value["dicom_files"],
    )


# ============================================================
# 05 — STRICT PATIENT RESPONSE ADAPTER
# ============================================================

def _patient_summary(patient: Any) -> PatientSummary:
    """
    Construct browser-safe patient information.

    PatientID, PatientName, StudyDate and folder names
    are deliberately not exposed.
    """

    series_items = tuple(patient.series)

    ready_count = sum(
        1
        for series in series_items
        if (
            series.validation is not None
            and series.validation.volume_ready
        )
    )

    return PatientSummary(
        patient_handle=patient.handle,
        series_count=len(series_items),
        volume_ready_series=ready_count,
    )


# ============================================================
# 06 — STRICT SERIES RESPONSE ADAPTER
# ============================================================

def _series_summary(
    series: Any,
    patient_handle: str,
) -> SeriesSummary:
    """
    Construct series summary without DICOM identifiers.

    Original descriptions, UID values and source paths
    never become API response fields.
    """

    instances = tuple(series.instances)

    first = instances[0] if instances else None

    validation = series.validation

    geometry = (
        validation.geometry
        if validation is not None
        else None
    )

    rows = 0
    columns = 0

    if first is not None:
        rows = first.geometry.rows or 0
        columns = first.geometry.columns or 0

    if geometry is not None:
        rows = geometry.rows
        columns = geometry.columns

    modality: str | None = None

    if first is not None and first.modality is not None:
        candidate = str(first.modality)

        if (
            len(candidate) <= 16
            and re.fullmatch(
                r"[A-Za-z0-9_-]*",
                candidate,
            )
        ):
            modality = candidate

    slice_count = (
        validation.frame_count
        if validation is not None
        else len(instances)
    )

    return SeriesSummary(
        series_handle=series.handle,
        patient_handle=patient_handle,
        modality=modality,
        slice_count=slice_count,
        rows=rows,
        columns=columns,
        volume_ready=bool(
            validation is not None
            and validation.volume_ready
        ),
    )


# ============================================================
# 07 — STRICT VALIDATION ISSUE ADAPTER
# ============================================================

def _public_issue_code(issue: Any) -> str:
    """
    Accept only a valid generic machine-readable code.

    Unknown fields are not reflected into the response.
    """

    raw = getattr(issue, "code", None)

    if hasattr(raw, "value"):
        raw = raw.value

    if not isinstance(raw, str):
        return "VALIDATION_ISSUE"

    normalized = raw.upper()

    if SAFE_ISSUE_CODE.fullmatch(normalized) is None:
        return "VALIDATION_ISSUE"

    return normalized


def _public_issue_severity(issue: Any) -> ValidationSeverity:
    raw = getattr(issue, "severity", None)

    if hasattr(raw, "value"):
        raw = raw.value

    if not isinstance(raw, str):
        return ValidationSeverity.WARNING

    normalized = raw.lower()

    if normalized == "error":
        return ValidationSeverity.ERROR

    if normalized == "info":
        return ValidationSeverity.INFO

    return ValidationSeverity.WARNING


def _validation_response(
    series: Any,
) -> SeriesValidationResponse:
    """
    Build a safe validation summary.

    No original issue messages, filenames or metadata.
    """

    validation = series.validation

    if validation is None:
        raise _api_error(409, "SERIES_INVALID")

    issues = [
        SeriesIssue(
            code=_public_issue_code(item),
            severity=_public_issue_severity(item),
        )
        for item in validation.issues[:MAX_PUBLIC_ISSUES]
    ]

    return SeriesValidationResponse(
        series_handle=series.handle,
        valid=bool(validation.valid),
        volume_ready=bool(validation.volume_ready),
        file_count=int(validation.file_count),
        frame_count=int(validation.frame_count),
        issues=issues,
    )


# ============================================================
# 08 — SELECT DATASET
# ============================================================

@router.post(
    "",
    response_model=DatasetResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
)
def select_dataset(
    payload: DatasetSelectRequest,
    auth: TrustedUser,
    datasets: TrustedDatasets,
) -> DatasetResponse:
    """
    Select a read-only dataset beneath configured root.

    The path is never returned to the browser.
    """

    try:
        summary = datasets.select_dataset(
            payload.dataset_path,
            owner_id=auth.owner_id,
        )

        return DatasetResponse(
            dataset=_dataset_summary(summary),
        )

    except DatasetServiceError as error:
        raise _translate_dataset_error(error) from None


# ============================================================
# 09 — LIST DATASETS
# ============================================================

@router.get(
    "",
    response_model=DatasetListResponse,
)
def list_datasets(
    auth: TrustedUser,
    datasets: TrustedDatasets,
) -> DatasetListResponse:
    try:
        summaries = datasets.list_datasets(
            owner_id=auth.owner_id,
        )

        return DatasetListResponse(
            datasets=[
                _dataset_summary(item)
                for item in summaries
            ],
        )

    except DatasetServiceError as error:
        raise _translate_dataset_error(error) from None


# ============================================================
# 10 — GET DATASET STATUS
# ============================================================

@router.get(
    "/{dataset_session_id}",
    response_model=DatasetResponse,
)
def get_dataset(
    dataset_session_id: str,
    auth: TrustedUser,
    datasets: TrustedDatasets,
) -> DatasetResponse:
    try:
        summary = datasets.get_dataset(
            dataset_session_id,
            owner_id=auth.owner_id,
        )

        return DatasetResponse(
            dataset=_dataset_summary(summary),
        )

    except DatasetServiceError as error:
        raise _translate_dataset_error(error) from None


# ============================================================
# 11 — REFRESH DATASET
# ============================================================

@router.post(
    "/{dataset_session_id}/refresh",
    response_model=DatasetResponse,
)
def refresh_dataset(
    dataset_session_id: str,
    auth: TrustedUser,
    datasets: TrustedDatasets,
) -> DatasetResponse:
    """
    Rescan the selected dataset, preserving its original files.

    Dataset generation increments after successful refresh.

    Cache keys use the generation, so a refreshed dataset
    does not reuse an older generation's cached volume.
    """

    try:
        summary = datasets.refresh_dataset(
            dataset_session_id,
            owner_id=auth.owner_id,
        )

        return DatasetResponse(
            dataset=_dataset_summary(summary),
        )

    except DatasetServiceError as error:
        raise _translate_dataset_error(error) from None


# ============================================================
# 12 — CLOSE DATASET
# ============================================================

@router.delete(
    "/{dataset_session_id}",
    response_model=DatasetCloseResponse,
)
def close_dataset(
    dataset_session_id: str,
    auth: TrustedUser,
    datasets: TrustedDatasets,
) -> DatasetCloseResponse:
    """
    Close selected in-memory dataset session.

    This does not delete any original dataset directory.
    """

    try:
        datasets.close_dataset(
            dataset_session_id,
            owner_id=auth.owner_id,
        )

        return DatasetCloseResponse(
            success=True,
            closed=True,
        )

    except DatasetServiceError as error:
        raise _translate_dataset_error(error) from None


# ============================================================
# 13 — LIST PATIENTS
# ============================================================

@router.get(
    "/{dataset_session_id}/patients",
    response_model=PatientListResponse,
)
def list_patients(
    dataset_session_id: str,
    auth: TrustedUser,
    datasets: TrustedDatasets,
) -> PatientListResponse:
    """
    List opaque patient handles only.

    Uses the authorized scanner index; never serializes
    raw ScanPatient objects to HTTP.
    """

    try:
        # Verify ownership before accessing internal index.
        datasets.get_dataset(
            dataset_session_id,
            owner_id=auth.owner_id,
        )

        # DatasetService.list_patients() provides only
        # summaries. Patient handles are resolved through
        # its authorized get_patient() method.
        summaries = datasets.list_patients(
            dataset_session_id,
            owner_id=auth.owner_id,
        )

        if len(summaries) > MAX_PUBLIC_PATIENTS:
            raise _api_error(409, "PROCESSING_FAILED")

        patients: list[PatientSummary] = []

        for item in summaries:
            handle = item.get("patient_handle")

            if not isinstance(handle, str):
                raise _api_error(409, "PROCESSING_FAILED")

            patient = datasets.get_patient(
                dataset_session_id,
                handle,
                owner_id=auth.owner_id,
            )

            patients.append(
                _patient_summary(patient)
            )

        return PatientListResponse(
            dataset_session_id=dataset_session_id,
            patients=patients,
        )

    except DatasetServiceError as error:
        raise _translate_dataset_error(error) from None


# ============================================================
# 14 — LIST SERIES OF ONE PATIENT
# ============================================================

@router.get(
    "/{dataset_session_id}/patients/"
    "{patient_handle}/series",
    response_model=SeriesListResponse,
)
def list_patient_series(
    dataset_session_id: str,
    patient_handle: str,
    auth: TrustedUser,
    datasets: TrustedDatasets,
) -> SeriesListResponse:
    """
    Show series associated with a selected patient handle.
    """

    try:
        patient = datasets.get_patient(
            dataset_session_id,
            patient_handle,
            owner_id=auth.owner_id,
        )

        series_items = tuple(patient.series)

        if len(series_items) > MAX_PUBLIC_SERIES:
            raise _api_error(409, "PROCESSING_FAILED")

        return SeriesListResponse(
            dataset_session_id=dataset_session_id,
            patient_handle=patient_handle,
            series=[
                _series_summary(
                    series,
                    patient_handle,
                )
                for series in series_items
            ],
        )

    except DatasetServiceError as error:
        raise _translate_dataset_error(error) from None


# ============================================================
# 15 — SERIES VALIDATION
# ============================================================

@router.get(
    "/{dataset_session_id}/series/"
    "{series_handle}/validation",
    response_model=SeriesValidationResponse,
)
def get_series_validation(
    dataset_session_id: str,
    series_handle: str,
    auth: TrustedUser,
    datasets: TrustedDatasets,
) -> SeriesValidationResponse:
    """
    Return technical volume-readiness and safe issue codes.
    """

    try:
        series = datasets.get_series(
            dataset_session_id,
            series_handle,
            owner_id=auth.owner_id,
        )

        return _validation_response(series)

    except DatasetServiceError as error:
        raise _translate_dataset_error(error) from None


# ============================================================
# 16 — ROUTE CAPABILITIES
# ============================================================

def get_dataset_route_capabilities() -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "dataset_selection": True,
        "dataset_listing": True,
        "dataset_refresh": True,
        "dataset_close": True,
        "patient_listing": True,
        "series_listing": True,
        "series_validation": True,
        "authentication_required": True,
        "opaque_handles": True,
        "original_paths_in_responses": False,
        "patient_names_in_responses": False,
        "read_only_original_dataset": True,
    }


# ============================================================
# END OF DATASET API ROUTES
# ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/backend/app/api/routes/health.py
```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — HEALTH AND MONITORING API
=======================================

Endpoints:
    GET /api/health
    GET /api/health/details
    GET /api/health/capabilities

Purpose:
- Minimal public backend readiness check.
- Authenticated operational service status.
- Safe, aggregated runtime counters.
- Explicit, privacy-safe response schemas.
- No source file or DICOM dataset operations.

Security:
- Public health exposes only "ok" or "degraded".
- Detailed monitoring requires authentication.
- No usernames, passwords or bearer tokens.
- No patient names, IDs, handles or DICOM UIDs.
- No source file paths, dataset roots or environment values.
- No raw exception messages returned to clients.
- No source files or runtime files created or modified.

Limitations:
- Process-local monitoring only.
- "ok" means the core service registry is available;
  it does NOT guarantee successful DICOM decoding.
- This is not an external monitoring or alerting system.
- Detailed metrics should not be exposed publicly.

Python 3.10+
FastAPI / Pydantic v2
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Annotated, Literal

from fastapi import (
    APIRouter,
    Depends,
    Request,
    Response,
    status,
)
from pydantic import Field

from ..dependencies import (
    ApplicationServices,
    ServiceRegistryError,
    validate_service_registry,
)
from ..schemas import (
    HealthResponse,
    HealthStatus,
    StrictAPIModel,
)
from ...security.session import (
    AuthenticatedContext,
    require_authenticated_context,
)


# ============================================================
# 01 — ROUTER
# ============================================================

router = APIRouter(
    prefix="/api/health",
    tags=["Health"],
)

TrustedUser = Annotated[
    AuthenticatedContext,
    Depends(require_authenticated_context),
]

NO_STORE_HEADERS = {
    "Cache-Control": "no-store, private",
    "Pragma": "no-cache",
    "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
}


# ============================================================
# 02 — STRICT MONITORING SCHEMAS
# ============================================================

class ComponentStatus(StrictAPIModel):
    """
    Generic state only, without sensitive service details.
    """

    available: bool
    closed: bool


class OperationalCounters(StrictAPIModel):
    """
    Safe aggregate counters.

    These numbers are returned only to authenticated users.
    """

    active_dataset_sessions: int = Field(ge=0)
    active_volume_loads: int = Field(ge=0)
    active_preview_builds: int = Field(ge=0)
    active_binary_streams: int = Field(ge=0)

    cached_volumes: int = Field(ge=0)
    cached_memory_mib: float = Field(
        ge=0,
        allow_inf_nan=False,
    )

    active_login_sessions: int = Field(ge=0)

    volume_load_requests: int = Field(ge=0)
    completed_volume_loads: int = Field(ge=0)
    failed_volume_loads: int = Field(ge=0)

    completed_previews: int = Field(ge=0)
    failed_previews: int = Field(ge=0)

    completed_streams: int = Field(ge=0)
    failed_streams: int = Field(ge=0)


class OperationalHealthResponse(StrictAPIModel):
    """
    Authenticated operational summary.

    Component names are static, not derived from user data.
    """

    status: HealthStatus
    service: Literal["sinus-viewer"] = "sinus-viewer"

    components: dict[
        Literal[
            "authentication",
            "datasets",
            "cache",
            "volumes",
            "previews",
            "streaming",
        ],
        ComponentStatus,
    ]

    counters: OperationalCounters


class HealthCapabilitiesResponse(StrictAPIModel):
    """
    Static capabilities with no environment inventory.
    """

    service: Literal["sinus-viewer"] = "sinus-viewer"
    schema_version: Literal["1"] = "1"
    public_health: Literal[True] = True
    authenticated_details: Literal[True] = True

    volume_cache: Literal[True] = True
    volume_streaming: Literal[True] = True
    preview_service: Literal[True] = True
    read_only_dataset: Literal[True] = True

    runtime_integration_verified: Literal[False] = False


# ============================================================
# 03 — REGISTRY READINESS
# ============================================================

def _get_services_or_none(
    request: Request,
) -> ApplicationServices | None:
    """
    Retrieve service bundle without exposing exceptions.

    Registry existence is necessary but not sufficient for
    readiness; closed services are also checked.
    """

    try:
        return validate_service_registry(request.app)

    except (ServiceRegistryError, AttributeError, TypeError):
        return None


def _component_states(
    services: ApplicationServices,
) -> dict[str, ComponentStatus]:
    """
    Inspect service lifecycle flags through safe statistics.

    No original dataset scanning or volume loading.
    """

    states = {
        "authentication": services.authentication.statistics(),
        "datasets": services.datasets.statistics(),
        "cache": services.cache.statistics(),
        "volumes": services.volumes.statistics(),
        "previews": services.previews.statistics(),
        "streaming": services.streaming.statistics(),
    }

    return {
        name: ComponentStatus(
            available=True,
            closed=bool(statistics.closed),
        )
        for name, statistics in states.items()
    }


def _is_ready(
    services: ApplicationServices | None,
) -> bool:
    """
    Check service registry and process-local lifecycle state.

    Does not contact the filesystem or external tunnel.
    """

    if services is None:
        return False

    try:
        components = _component_states(services)

        return (
            len(components) == 6
            and all(
                component.available and not component.closed
                for component in components.values()
            )
        )

    except (AttributeError, RuntimeError, TypeError, ValueError):
        return False


# ============================================================
# 04 — PUBLIC HEALTH ENDPOINT
# ============================================================

@router.get(
    "",
    response_model=HealthResponse,
    responses={
        200: {
            "description": "Backend core services available",
        },
        503: {
            "description": "Backend not ready",
        },
    },
)
def public_health(
    request: Request,
    response: Response,
) -> HealthResponse:
    """
    Public endpoint.

    Only two values are exposed:
        service = sinus-viewer
        status  = ok | degraded

    No dataset counts, file paths, user sessions, GPU
    inventory or service statistics.
    """

    services = _get_services_or_none(request)

    ready = _is_ready(services)

    response.status_code = (
        status.HTTP_200_OK
        if ready
        else status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE
    )

    for name, value in NO_STORE_HEADERS.items():
        response.headers[name] = value

    return HealthResponse(
        status=(
            HealthStatus.OK
            if ready
            else HealthStatus.DEGRADED
        ),
        service="sinus-viewer",
    )


# ============================================================
# 05 — OPERATIONAL COUNTERS
# ============================================================

def _build_operational_counters(
    services: ApplicationServices,
) -> OperationalCounters:
    """
    Copy only approved aggregate counters.

    Internal statistics models may contain additional fields,
    but those fields will never enter this response.
    """

    authentication = services.authentication.statistics()
    datasets = services.datasets.statistics()
    cache = services.cache.statistics()
    volumes = services.volumes.statistics()
    previews = services.previews.statistics()
    streaming = services.streaming.statistics()

    return OperationalCounters(
        active_dataset_sessions=int(
            datasets.active_sessions
        ),
        active_volume_loads=int(
            volumes.active_loads
        ),
        active_preview_builds=int(
            previews.active
        ),
        active_binary_streams=int(
            streaming.active_streams
        ),
        cached_volumes=int(
            cache.entries
        ),
        cached_memory_mib=float(
            cache.total_mib
        ),
        active_login_sessions=int(
            authentication.active_sessions
        ),
        volume_load_requests=int(
            volumes.load_requests
        ),
        completed_volume_loads=int(
            volumes.completed_loads
        ),
        failed_volume_loads=int(
            volumes.failed_loads
        ),
        completed_previews=int(
            previews.completed
        ),
        failed_previews=int(
            previews.failed
        ),
        completed_streams=int(
            streaming.streams_completed
        ),
        failed_streams=int(
            streaming.streams_failed
        ),
    )


# ============================================================
# 06 — AUTHENTICATED OPERATIONAL HEALTH
# ============================================================

@router.get(
    "/details",
    response_model=OperationalHealthResponse,
    responses={
        200: {
            "description": "Authenticated operational status",
        },
        401: {
            "description": "Authentication required",
        },
        503: {
            "description": "Service not ready",
        },
    },
)
def operational_health(
    request: Request,
    response: Response,
    auth: TrustedUser,
) -> OperationalHealthResponse:
    """
    Detailed, authenticated monitoring endpoint.

    The authentication dependency verifies the cookie
    before this handler executes.

    Operational values are aggregate counters only.
    """

    services = _get_services_or_none(request)

    if services is None:
        response.status_code = status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE

        # Protected route cannot construct meaningful
        # statistics without a registered service bundle.
        # Fail with a controlled generic HTTP error.
        from fastapi import HTTPException

        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=503,
            detail={"code": "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE"},
        )

    try:
        components = _component_states(services)
        counters = _build_operational_counters(services)

    except (AttributeError, RuntimeError, TypeError, ValueError):
        from fastapi import HTTPException

        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=503,
            detail={"code": "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE"},
        ) from None

    ready = all(
        component.available and not component.closed
        for component in components.values()
    )

    response.status_code = (
        status.HTTP_200_OK
        if ready
        else status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE
    )

    for name, value in NO_STORE_HEADERS.items():
        response.headers[name] = value

    return OperationalHealthResponse(
        status=(
            HealthStatus.OK
            if ready
            else HealthStatus.DEGRADED
        ),
        service="sinus-viewer",
        components=components,
        counters=counters,
    )


# ============================================================
# 07 — AUTHENTICATED CAPABILITIES
# ============================================================

@router.get(
    "/capabilities",
    response_model=HealthCapabilitiesResponse,
)
def health_capabilities(
    response: Response,
    auth: TrustedUser,
) -> HealthCapabilitiesResponse:
    """
    Static capability declarations.

    These describe planned service contracts, not proven
    clinical correctness or successful integration tests.
    """

    for name, value in NO_STORE_HEADERS.items():
        response.headers[name] = value

    return HealthCapabilitiesResponse()


# ============================================================
# 08 — ROUTE CAPABILITY REPORT
# ============================================================

def get_health_route_capabilities() -> dict:
    """
    Safe static route summary.

    Do not expose internal application state.
    """

    return {
        "public_endpoint": "/api/health",
        "authenticated_details": "/api/health/details",
        "authenticated_capabilities": "/api/health/capabilities",
        "public_detail_level": "minimal",
        "private_counters": True,
        "patient_data_in_responses": False,
        "file_paths_in_responses": False,
        "credentials_in_responses": False,
        "read_only_original_dataset": True,
    }


# ============================================================
# END OF HEALTH AND MONITORING API
# ============================================================

### sinus-viewer/backend/app/api/routes/previews.py

"""
SINUS VIEWER — AUTHENTICATED DICOM PREVIEW API
==============================================

Endpoints:
    GET /api/previews/capabilities
    GET /api/previews/{dataset_session_id}/{series_handle}
    GET /api/previews/{dataset_session_id}/{series_handle}/{plane}.png

Features:
- Authentication and trusted dataset ownership.
- Axial, coronal and sagittal navigation previews.
- Strict Pydantic preview response schemas.
- In-memory, lossless 8-bit grayscale PNG encoding.
- Bounded source image dimensions.
- PreviewService concurrency safeguards.
- Controlled errors and no-store HTTP headers.
- No temporary image files or original DICOM writes.

Important:
- PreviewService currently rebuilds previews per request.
- These images are navigation aids, not diagnostic images.
- True physical-aspect-ratio reslicing is implemented later.
- HTTP authentication alone is not permission to publicly
  deploy identifiable medical images.

Python 3.10+
FastAPI, NumPy, Pydantic v2
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
import struct
import zlib
from typing import Annotated, Any

import numpy as np

from fastapi import (
    APIRouter,
    Depends,
    HTTPException,
    Path,
    Query,
    Response,
    status,
)

from ..dependencies import require_preview_service
from ..schemas import (
    PreviewDescriptor,
    PreviewImageSchema,
    PreviewResponse,
)
from ...security.session import (
    AuthenticatedContext,
    require_authenticated_context,
)
from ...services.preview_service import (
    MAX_PREVIEW_DIMENSION,
    MAX_SAMPLED_SLICES,
    DEFAULT_MAX_SAMPLED_SLICES,
    DEFAULT_PREVIEW_DIMENSION,
    PreviewErrorCode,
    PreviewImage,
    PreviewPackage,
    PreviewPlane,
    PreviewService,
    PreviewServiceError,
    get_preview_capabilities,
)


# ============================================================
# 01 — ROUTER AND CONSTANTS
# ============================================================

router = APIRouter(
    prefix="/api/previews",
    tags=["DICOM Previews"],
)

PNG_SIGNATURE = b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n"

PNG_MAX_PIXELS = MAX_PREVIEW_DIMENSION ** 2

HANDLE_PATTERN = re.compile(
    r"^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128}$",
    re.ASCII,
)

PREVIEW_HTTP_HEADERS = {
    "Cache-Control": "no-store, private",
    "Pragma": "no-cache",
    "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
    "Content-Disposition": "inline",
}


TrustedUser = Annotated[
    AuthenticatedContext,
    Depends(require_authenticated_context),
]

TrustedPreviews = Annotated[
    PreviewService,
    Depends(require_preview_service),
]


# ============================================================
# 02 — CONTROLLED HTTP ERRORS
# ============================================================

def _error(
    status_code: int,
    code: str,
) -> HTTPException:
    """
    Never include source paths, patient identifiers or
    low-level image decoder exception messages.
    """

    return HTTPException(
        status_code=status_code,
        detail={"code": code},
        headers={
            "Cache-Control": "no-store, private",
            "Pragma": "no-cache",
        },
    )


def _translate_preview_error(
    error: PreviewServiceError,
) -> HTTPException:
    code = error.code

    if code == PreviewErrorCode.INVALID_REQUEST:
        return _error(422, "INVALID_REQUEST")

    if code == PreviewErrorCode.SERIES_NOT_FOUND:
        return _error(404, "SERIES_NOT_FOUND")

    if code == PreviewErrorCode.SERIES_INVALID:
        return _error(409, "SERIES_INVALID")

    if code == PreviewErrorCode.SOURCE_CHANGED:
        return _error(409, "SOURCE_CHANGED")

    if code == PreviewErrorCode.CANCELLED:
        return _error(409, "OPERATION_CANCELLED")

    if code == PreviewErrorCode.BUSY:
        return _error(503, "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE")

    if code == PreviewErrorCode.MEMORY_LIMIT:
        return _error(507, "OUT_OF_MEMORY")

    return _error(422, "DECODER_ERROR")


# ============================================================
# 03 — HANDLE AND PLANE VALIDATION
# ============================================================

def _validate_handle(value: str) -> str:
    if (
        not isinstance(value, str)
        or HANDLE_PATTERN.fullmatch(value) is None
    ):
        raise _error(422, "INVALID_REQUEST")

    return value


def _parse_plane(value: str) -> PreviewPlane:
    try:
        return PreviewPlane(value.lower())

    except (ValueError, AttributeError):
        raise _error(422, "INVALID_REQUEST") from None


# ============================================================
# 04 — STRICT PREVIEW RESPONSE ADAPTERS
# ============================================================

def _image_schema(
    image: PreviewImage,
) -> PreviewImageSchema:
    """
    Explicit field allowlist.

    Raw pixel arrays and source geometry details are
    never serialized into preview metadata JSON.
    """

    pixels = image.pixels

    if (
        not isinstance(pixels, np.ndarray)
        or pixels.ndim != 2
        or pixels.dtype != np.dtype("uint8")
        or pixels.size < 1
        or pixels.size > PNG_MAX_PIXELS
        or tuple(pixels.shape) != image.displayed_shape_yx
        or pixels.flags.writeable
    ):
        raise _error(409, "PROCESSING_FAILED")

    return PreviewImageSchema(
        plane=image.plane.value,
        shape_yx=tuple(int(n) for n in pixels.shape),
        dtype="uint8",
        byte_size=int(pixels.nbytes),
        sampled_slice_count=int(image.sampled_slice_count),
        representative_slice_index=int(
            image.representative_slice_index
        ),
        window_low=float(image.window_low),
        window_high=float(image.window_high),
        diagnostic_quality=False,
    )


def _package_schema(
    package: PreviewPackage,
) -> PreviewDescriptor:
    return PreviewDescriptor(
        axial=_image_schema(package.axial),
        coronal=_image_schema(package.coronal),
        sagittal=_image_schema(package.sagittal),
        diagnostic_quality=False,
    )


# ============================================================
# 05 — STANDARD PNG CHUNK
# ============================================================

def _png_chunk(
    chunk_type: bytes,
    data: bytes,
) -> bytes:
    """
    Encode one PNG chunk with CRC32.

    All inputs are generated internally, not sourced
    directly from raw DICOM metadata.
    """

    if (
        not isinstance(chunk_type, bytes)
        or len(chunk_type) != 4
        or not isinstance(data, bytes)
    ):
        raise ValueError("Invalid PNG chunk.")

    checksum = zlib.crc32(
        chunk_type + data
    ) & 0xFFFFFFFF

    return (
        struct.pack(">I", len(data))
        + chunk_type
        + data
        + struct.pack(">I", checksum)
    )


# ============================================================
# 06 — LOSSLESS GRAYSCALE PNG ENCODER
# ============================================================

def encode_grayscale_png(
    pixels: np.ndarray,
) -> bytes:
    """
    Encode a 2D uint8 array as a valid PNG grayscale image.

    PNG:
        Color type: 0 = grayscale
        Bit depth: 8
        Filter:     0 = none

    No file is written.

    This function is intentionally limited to small,
    bounded preview arrays, not full DICOM slices.
    """

    if (
        not isinstance(pixels, np.ndarray)
        or pixels.ndim != 2
        or pixels.dtype != np.dtype("uint8")
    ):
        raise ValueError("Expected 2D uint8 pixels.")

    height, width = pixels.shape

    if (
        height < 1
        or width < 1
        or height > MAX_PREVIEW_DIMENSION
        or width > MAX_PREVIEW_DIMENSION
        or pixels.size > PNG_MAX_PIXELS
    ):
        raise ValueError("Preview dimensions out of range.")

    # Create a tightly packed array in RAM only.
    image = np.ascontiguousarray(pixels)

    # PNG requires one filter byte per scanline.
    # Filter 0 means each row stores raw grayscale bytes.
    raw_scanlines = b"".join(
        b"\x00" + image[row].tobytes()
        for row in range(height)
    )

    header = struct.pack(
        ">IIBBBBB",
        width,
        height,
        8,   # Bit depth
        0,   # Grayscale color type
        0,   # Compression method
        0,   # Filter method
        0,   # Interlace method
    )

    compressed = zlib.compress(
        raw_scanlines,
        level=6,
    )

    return (
        PNG_SIGNATURE
        + _png_chunk(b"IHDR", header)
        + _png_chunk(b"IDAT", compressed)
        + _png_chunk(b"IEND", b"")
    )


# ============================================================
# 07 — AUTHORIZED PREVIEW BUILDER
# ============================================================

def _build_authorized_package(
    dataset_session_id: str,
    series_handle: str,
    owner_id: str,
    previews: PreviewService,
    *,
    max_dimension: int,
    max_sampled_slices: int,
) -> PreviewPackage:
    """
    Perform authorized preview preparation.

    All owner checks are delegated to DatasetService
    through PreviewService.
    """

    _validate_handle(dataset_session_id)
    _validate_handle(series_handle)

    try:
        return previews.build_preview(
            dataset_session_id,
            series_handle,
            owner_id=owner_id,
            max_dimension=max_dimension,
            max_sampled_slices=max_sampled_slices,
        )

    except PreviewServiceError as error:
        raise _translate_preview_error(error) from None


# ============================================================
# 08 — GET STATIC CAPABILITIES
# ============================================================

@router.get(
    "/capabilities",
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
)
def preview_capabilities(
    auth: TrustedUser,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Non-sensitive feature declaration.

    Authentication remains required for consistency with
    the protected API route policy.
    """

    return get_preview_capabilities()


# ============================================================
# 09 — GET THREE-PLANE PREVIEW METADATA
# ============================================================

@router.get(
    "/{dataset_session_id}/{series_handle}",
    response_model=PreviewResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
)
def get_preview_metadata(
    dataset_session_id: str,
    series_handle: str,
    auth: TrustedUser,
    previews: TrustedPreviews,
    max_dimension: Annotated[
        int,
        Query(ge=1, le=MAX_PREVIEW_DIMENSION),
    ] = DEFAULT_PREVIEW_DIMENSION,
    max_sampled_slices: Annotated[
        int,
        Query(ge=1, le=MAX_SAMPLED_SLICES),
    ] = DEFAULT_MAX_SAMPLED_SLICES,
) -> PreviewResponse:
    """
    Return safe descriptions of axial/coronal/sagittal images.

    This currently builds the previews but does not return
    the actual PNG bytes. The PNG endpoint does that.
    """

    package = _build_authorized_package(
        dataset_session_id,
        series_handle,
        auth.owner_id,
        previews,
        max_dimension=max_dimension,
        max_sampled_slices=max_sampled_slices,
    )

    return PreviewResponse(
        success=True,
        series_handle=series_handle,
        previews=_package_schema(package),
    )


# ============================================================
# 10 — GET ONE PREVIEW PNG
# ============================================================

@router.get(
    "/{dataset_session_id}/{series_handle}/{plane}.png",
    response_class=Response,
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
    responses={
        200: {
            "content": {
                "image/png": {},
            },
            "description": "Authenticated grayscale preview PNG",
        },
        401: {
            "description": "Authentication required",
        },
        404: {
            "description": "Series not found",
        },
        409: {
            "description": "Series unsuitable for preview",
        },
    },
)
def get_preview_png(
    dataset_session_id: str,
    series_handle: str,
    plane: str,
    auth: TrustedUser,
    previews: TrustedPreviews,
    max_dimension: Annotated[
        int,
        Query(ge=1, le=MAX_PREVIEW_DIMENSION),
    ] = DEFAULT_PREVIEW_DIMENSION,
    max_sampled_slices: Annotated[
        int,
        Query(ge=1, le=MAX_SAMPLED_SLICES),
    ] = DEFAULT_MAX_SAMPLED_SLICES,
) -> Response:
    """
    Return one small lossless grayscale preview.

    No original source image, DICOM tags, filenames or
    patient identifiers are embedded into the PNG.

    The pixel appearance itself may still contain sensitive
    clinical information.
    """

    selected_plane = _parse_plane(plane)

    package = _build_authorized_package(
        dataset_session_id,
        series_handle,
        auth.owner_id,
        previews,
        max_dimension=max_dimension,
        max_sampled_slices=max_sampled_slices,
    )

    image = package.get_plane(selected_plane)

    # Validate output constraints before encoding.
    _image_schema(image)

    try:
        png_bytes = encode_grayscale_png(
            image.pixels
        )

    except (ValueError, MemoryError):
        raise _error(
            500,
            "PROCESSING_FAILED",
        ) from None

    return Response(
        content=png_bytes,
        media_type="image/png",
        status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
        headers=dict(PREVIEW_HTTP_HEADERS),
    )


# ============================================================
# 11 — SAFE CAPABILITY SUMMARY
# ============================================================

def get_preview_route_capabilities() -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "metadata_endpoint": True,
        "png_endpoint": True,
        "planes": [
            "axial",
            "coronal",
            "sagittal",
        ],
        "encoding": "png_grayscale_8bit",
        "lossless_png": True,
        "image_data_in_json": False,
        "authentication_required": True,
        "owner_authorization": True,
        "maximum_dimension": MAX_PREVIEW_DIMENSION,
        "preview_diagnostic_quality": False,
        "read_only_dataset": True,
        "temporary_files": False,
    }


# ============================================================
# END OF AUTHENTICATED PREVIEW API ROUTES
# ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/backend/app/api/routes/series.py
