### sinus-viewer/backend/app/api/routes/streaming.py
```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — AUTHENTICATED BINARY STREAMING ROUTES
====================================================

Endpoints:
    GET  /api/streaming/{dataset}/{series}/levels/{level}/bytes
    HEAD /api/streaming/{dataset}/{series}/levels/{level}/bytes

Features:
- Server-side cookie session authentication.
- Owner-authorized dataset and volume selection.
- Single HTTP byte-range support.
- HTTP 200, 206 and 416 handling.
- Bounded streaming with a synchronous generator.
- No-cache, nosniff response headers.
- No image pixels encoded as JSON.
- Controlled errors without patient identifiers.
- No filesystem writes.

Data contract:
    Pixel dtype: float32
    Array layout: Z, Y, X
    Byte order: little-endian
    Geometry: obtain separately from Volume API.

Important:
- The underlying VolumeService must verify the declared
  binary encoding before this API is enabled.
- Authentication and authorization must also be applied
  at the reverse proxy and application level.
- A range request does not bypass full-volume RAM loading.
- Streamed patient image pixels remain sensitive data.
- No actual anonymization occurs in this module.

Python 3.10+
FastAPI / Starlette
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import sys
import threading
from typing import Annotated, Iterator

from fastapi import (
    APIRouter,
    Depends,
    Header,
    HTTPException,
    Path,
    Request,
    status,
)
from fastapi.responses import Response, StreamingResponse

from ..dependencies import require_streaming_service
from ...security.session import (
    AuthenticatedContext,
    require_authenticated_context,
)
from ...services.streaming_service import (
    StreamingErrorCode,
    StreamingServiceError,
    StreamPlan,
    VolumeStreamingService,
    build_stream_headers,
    build_unsatisfied_range_headers,
)
from ...services.volume_service import (
    VolumeServiceError,
)


# ============================================================
# 01 — ROUTER AND LIMITS
# ============================================================

router = APIRouter(
    prefix="/api/streaming",
    tags=["Volume Streaming"],
)

MAX_LEVEL = 64

TrustedUser = Annotated[
    AuthenticatedContext,
    Depends(require_authenticated_context),
]

TrustedStreaming = Annotated[
    VolumeStreamingService,
    Depends(require_streaming_service),
]


# ============================================================
# 02 — SAFE ERRORS
# ============================================================

def _error(
    status_code: int,
    code: str,
    *,
    headers: dict[str, str] | None = None,
) -> HTTPException:
    """
    No patient metadata, token, path or raw exception text.
    """

    response_headers = {
        "Cache-Control": "no-store, private",
        "Pragma": "no-cache",
        "X-Content-Type-Options": "nosniff",
    }

    if headers:
        response_headers.update(headers)

    return HTTPException(
        status_code=status_code,
        detail={"code": code},
        headers=response_headers,
    )


def _translate_streaming_error(
    error: StreamingServiceError,
    *,
    total_bytes: int | None = None,
) -> HTTPException:
    """
    Convert controlled errors to HTTP status codes.

    For an unsatisfiable range, include Content-Range
    only when the authorized resource size is known.
    """

    code = error.code

    if code == StreamingErrorCode.INVALID_RANGE:
        return _error(400, "INVALID_REQUEST")

    if code == StreamingErrorCode.RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE:
        range_headers = (
            build_unsatisfied_range_headers(total_bytes)
            if total_bytes is not None
            else None
        )

        return _error(
            416,
            "RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE",
            headers=range_headers,
        )

    if code == StreamingErrorCode.VOLUME_NOT_FOUND:
        return _error(404, "SERIES_NOT_FOUND")

    if code == StreamingErrorCode.STREAM_CANCELLED:
        return _error(409, "OPERATION_CANCELLED")

    if code == StreamingErrorCode.SERVICE_CLOSED:
        return _error(503, "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE")

    return _error(500, "PROCESSING_FAILED")


# ============================================================
# 03 — INPUT VALIDATION
# ============================================================

def _validate_public_handle(value: str) -> str:
    if (
        not isinstance(value, str)
        or not 1 <= len(value) <= 128
        or not all(
            char.isascii()
            and (
                char.isalnum()
                or char in "_-"
            )
            for char in value
        )
    ):
        raise _error(422, "INVALID_REQUEST")

    return value


def _require_binary_compatibility() -> None:
    """
    Float32 volume bytes must match our advertised format.

    The current volume reader produces native float32 data.
    This API is restricted to little-endian platforms.
    """

    if sys.byteorder != "little":
        raise _error(503, "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE")


# ============================================================
# 04 — STREAM PLAN PREPARATION
# ============================================================

def _prepare_authorized_plan(
    dataset_session_id: str,
    series_handle: str,
    level: int,
    range_header: str | None,
    owner_id: str,
    streaming: VolumeStreamingService,
) -> StreamPlan:
    """
    Validate inputs and authorize volume before headers
    or medical pixel data are sent.
    """

    _require_binary_compatibility()

    _validate_public_handle(dataset_session_id)
    _validate_public_handle(series_handle)

    if not 0 <= level <= MAX_LEVEL:
        raise _error(422, "INVALID_REQUEST")

    try:
        return streaming.prepare_stream(
            dataset_session_id,
            series_handle,
            owner_id=owner_id,
            level=level,
            range_header=range_header,
        )

    except StreamingServiceError as error:
        if (
            error.code
            == StreamingErrorCode.RANGE_NOT_SATISFIABLE
        ):
            # Only retrieve the authorized resource size to
            # provide an RFC-compatible HTTP 416 response.
            #
            # The first prepare_stream call already performed
            # authorization. Reauthorize here to avoid
            # exposing a resource's size after session change.
            try:
                volume = streaming._volumes.get_level(
                    dataset_session_id,
                    series_handle,
                    owner_id=owner_id,
                    level=level,
                )
                total_bytes = int(volume.byte_size)
            except (VolumeServiceError, AttributeError):
                total_bytes = None

            raise _translate_streaming_error(
                error,
                total_bytes=total_bytes,
            ) from None

        raise _translate_streaming_error(error) from None


# ============================================================
# 05 — STREAM GENERATOR
# ============================================================

def _stream_authorized_chunks(
    plan: StreamPlan,
    streaming: VolumeStreamingService,
    owner_id: str,
    cancel_event: threading.Event,
) -> Iterator[bytes]:
    """
    Yield bounded pixel chunks.

    When a StreamingResponse is cancelled or its generator
    is closed, cleanup requests cancellation.

    Starlette handles a synchronous iterator through its
    thread-pool adapter. Disconnect behavior depends on
    ASGI server and Starlette runtime versions.

    No original DICOM file is opened by this function.
    """

    iterator = streaming.iter_volume_bytes(
        plan,
        owner_id=owner_id,
        cancel_event=cancel_event,
    )

    try:
        for chunk in iterator:
            if cancel_event.is_set():
                return

            yield chunk

    finally:
        cancel_event.set()

        close_iterator = getattr(
            iterator,
            "close",
            None,
        )

        if callable(close_iterator):
            close_iterator()


# ============================================================
# 06 — GET BINARY VOLUME CONTENT
# ============================================================

@router.get(
    "/{dataset_session_id}/{series_handle}"
    "/levels/{level}/bytes",
    response_class=StreamingResponse,
    responses={
        200: {
            "description": "Complete bounded volume level",
        },
        206: {
            "description": "Single partial byte range",
        },
        400: {
            "description": "Malformed byte range",
        },
        401: {
            "description": "Authentication required",
        },
        416: {
            "description": "Unsatisfiable byte range",
        },
    },
)
def get_volume_binary(
    request: Request,
    dataset_session_id: str,
    series_handle: str,
    level: Annotated[int, Path(ge=0, le=MAX_LEVEL)],
    auth: TrustedUser,
    streaming: TrustedStreaming,
    range_header: Annotated[
        str | None,
        Header(alias="Range"),
    ] = None,
) -> StreamingResponse:
    """
    Stream authorized binary volume bytes.

    Example Range header:
        Range: bytes=0-1048575

    Volume arrays are not included in JSON responses.
    """

    plan = _prepare_authorized_plan(
        dataset_session_id,
        series_handle,
        level,
        range_header,
        auth.owner_id,
        streaming,
    )

    headers = build_stream_headers(plan)

    cancel_event = threading.Event()

    generator = _stream_authorized_chunks(
        plan,
        streaming,
        auth.owner_id,
        cancel_event,
    )

    # Avoid leaking request URL, Range value or series IDs
    # through response headers.
    return StreamingResponse(
        content=generator,
        status_code=plan.status_code,
        headers=headers,
        media_type="application/octet-stream",
    )


# ============================================================
# 07 — HEAD BINARY VOLUME CONTENT
# ============================================================

@router.head(
    "/{dataset_session_id}/{series_handle}"
    "/levels/{level}/bytes",
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
    include_in_schema=True,
)
def head_volume_binary(
    dataset_session_id: str,
    series_handle: str,
    level: Annotated[int, Path(ge=0, le=MAX_LEVEL)],
    auth: TrustedUser,
    streaming: TrustedStreaming,
    range_header: Annotated[
        str | None,
        Header(alias="Range"),
    ] = None,
) -> Response:
    """
    Return volume byte-range headers without image pixels.

    HEAD may still cause the underlying full volume to load
    into backend memory if it is not already cached.
    """

    plan = _prepare_authorized_plan(
        dataset_session_id,
        series_handle,
        level,
        range_header,
        auth.owner_id,
        streaming,
    )

    return Response(
        content=b"",
        status_code=plan.status_code,
        headers=build_stream_headers(plan),
        media_type="application/octet-stream",
    )


# ============================================================
# 08 — SAFE CAPABILITY DECLARATION
# ============================================================

def get_streaming_route_capabilities() -> dict:
    return {
        "get_binary": True,
        "head_binary": True,
        "http_200": True,
        "http_206": True,
        "http_416": True,
        "single_byte_ranges": True,
        "multi_ranges": False,
        "bounded_response": True,
        "authentication_required": True,
        "owner_authorization": True,
        "pixel_encoding": "float32",
        "axis_order": "zyx",
        "byte_order": "little",
        "full_volume_load_required": True,
        "original_dataset_read_only": True,
    }


# ============================================================
# END OF BINARY STREAMING API ROUTES
# ============================================================
```

### sinus-viewer/backend/app/api/routes/volumes.py
```
"""
SINUS VIEWER — AUTHENTICATED VOLUME API ROUTES
==============================================

Routes:
    POST   /api/volumes/{dataset}/{series}/load
    GET    /api/volumes/{dataset}/{series}
    GET    /api/volumes/{dataset}/{series}/levels/{level}
    DELETE /api/volumes/{dataset}/{series}/cache

Responsibilities:
- Resolve server-authenticated owner identity.
- Authorize selected dataset and DICOM series.
- Load validated DICOM volume through VolumeService.
- Reuse the process-local volume cache.
- Expose strict, privacy-safe Pydantic descriptors.
- Preserve LPS geometry and Z,Y,X array ordering.
- Reject invalid levels and unsupported pixel layouts.
- Invalidate the selected series cache on request.

Security:
- Never trust owner IDs received from the browser.
- Never return raw patient data, file paths or DICOM UIDs.
- Never serialize internal ScanSeries or pydicom Dataset.
- Volume voxel data is NOT returned by these routes.
- No source filesystem writes are performed.

Note:
    All handlers are synchronous. FastAPI runs synchronous
    endpoints in its worker pool. Building a full volume
    may be expensive; VolumeService limits concurrency.

Python 3.10+ / FastAPI / Pydantic v2
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from typing import Annotated, Any

import numpy as np

from fastapi import (
    APIRouter,
    Depends,
    HTTPException,
    Path,
    status,
)

from ..dependencies import require_volume_service
from ..schemas import (
    VolumeDescriptor,
    VolumeGeometrySchema,
    VolumeLevelSchema,
    VolumeResponse,
)
from ...dicom.volume_builder import (
    PreparedVolume,
    VolumePackage,
)
from ...security.session import (
    AuthenticatedContext,
    require_authenticated_context,
)
from ...services.volume_service import (
    VolumeInvalidRange,
    VolumeLoadFailed,
    VolumeMemoryExceeded,
    VolumeNotFound,
    VolumeNotReady,
    VolumeService,
    VolumeServiceBusy,
    VolumeServiceClosed,
    VolumeServiceError,
    VolumeSourceChanged,
)


# ============================================================
# 01 — ROUTER
# ============================================================

router = APIRouter(
    prefix="/api/volumes",
    tags=["Volumes"],
)


# ============================================================
# 02 — DEPENDENCY ALIASES
# ============================================================

TrustedUser = Annotated[
    AuthenticatedContext,
    Depends(require_authenticated_context),
]

TrustedVolumes = Annotated[
    VolumeService,
    Depends(require_volume_service),
]


# ============================================================
# 03 — SAFE HTTP ERROR TRANSLATION
# ============================================================

def _api_error(
    status_code: int,
    code: str,
) -> HTTPException:
    """
    Return generic, allowlisted error identifiers.

    Do not expose internal exception text, source paths
    or DICOM patient identifiers.
    """

    return HTTPException(
        status_code=status_code,
        detail={"code": code},
        headers={
            "Cache-Control": "no-store",
            "Pragma": "no-cache",
        },
    )


def _translate_volume_error(
    error: VolumeServiceError,
) -> HTTPException:
    """
    Map known volume errors into safe HTTP responses.

    Never serialize error.args or underlying file errors.
    """

    if isinstance(error, VolumeInvalidRange):
        return _api_error(
            status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
            "INVALID_REQUEST",
        )

    if isinstance(error, VolumeNotFound):
        return _api_error(
            status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
            "SERIES_NOT_FOUND",
        )

    if isinstance(error, VolumeNotReady):
        return _api_error(
            status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
            "SERIES_INVALID",
        )

    if isinstance(error, VolumeSourceChanged):
        return _api_error(
            status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
            "SOURCE_CHANGED",
        )

    if isinstance(error, VolumeMemoryExceeded):
        return _api_error(
            status.HTTP_507_INSUFFICIENT_STORAGE,
            "OUT_OF_MEMORY",
        )

    if isinstance(
        error,
        (VolumeServiceBusy, VolumeServiceClosed),
    ):
        return _api_error(
            status.HTTP_503_SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE,
            "SERVICE_UNAVAILABLE",
        )

    if isinstance(error, VolumeLoadFailed):
        return _api_error(
            status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
            "PROCESSING_FAILED",
        )

    return _api_error(
        status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
        "PROCESSING_FAILED",
    )


# ============================================================
# 04 — HANDLE AND LEVEL VALIDATION
# ============================================================

def _validate_handle(value: str) -> str:
    """
    Enforce the public handle format before service access.

    Actual authorization remains the service's job.
    """

    if (
        not isinstance(value, str)
        or not (1 <= len(value) <= 128)
        or not all(
            char.isascii()
            and (char.isalnum() or char in "_-")
            for char in value
        )
    ):
        raise _api_error(
            status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
            "INVALID_REQUEST",
        )

    return value


def _validate_level(level: int) -> int:
    if (
        not isinstance(level, int)
        or isinstance(level, bool)
        or not (0 <= level <= 64)
    ):
        raise _api_error(
            status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
            "INVALID_REQUEST",
        )

    return level


# ============================================================
# 05 — GEOMETRY RESPONSE ADAPTER
# ============================================================

def _geometry_schema(
    volume: PreparedVolume,
) -> VolumeGeometrySchema:
    """
    Copy only approved spatial geometry fields.

    Direction is a row-major 3x3 matrix.

    This adapter deliberately does not expose scanner
    metadata, DICOM UIDs or original filesystem paths.
    """

    geometry = volume.geometry

    shape = tuple(
        int(value)
        for value in geometry.shape_zyx
    )

    spacing = tuple(
        float(value)
        for value in geometry.spacing_xyz_mm
    )

    origin = tuple(
        float(value)
        for value in geometry.origin_lps
    )

    direction = tuple(
        float(value)
        for value in geometry.direction_lps
    )

    return VolumeGeometrySchema(
        shape_zyx=shape,
        spacing_xyz_mm=spacing,
        origin_lps=origin,
        direction_lps=direction,
        coordinate_system="LPS",
    )


# ============================================================
# 06 — VOLUME LEVEL RESPONSE ADAPTER
# ============================================================

def _level_schema(
    volume: PreparedVolume,
) -> VolumeLevelSchema:
    """
    Convert a prepared level to a strict public descriptor.

    Check encoding assumptions before claiming the binary
    streaming format is little-endian float32.
    """

    pixels = volume.voxels

    if (
        not isinstance(pixels, np.ndarray)
        or pixels.ndim != 3
        or pixels.dtype.kind != "f"
        or pixels.dtype.itemsize != 4
        or pixels.dtype.byteorder not in ("<", "=", "|")
    ):
        raise _api_error(
            status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
            "SERIES_INVALID",
        )

    if not np.little_endian:
        raise _api_error(
            status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
            "SERIES_INVALID",
        )

    if (
        not pixels.flags.c_contiguous
        or pixels.flags.writeable
    ):
        raise _api_error(
            status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
            "SERIES_INVALID",
        )

    shape = tuple(
        int(value)
        for value in pixels.shape
    )

    expected_bytes = math.prod(shape) * 4

    if expected_bytes != int(pixels.nbytes):
        raise _api_error(
            status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
            "SERIES_INVALID",
        )

    return VolumeLevelSchema(
        level=int(volume.level),
        is_preview=bool(volume.is_preview),
        shape_zyx=shape,
        byte_size=int(pixels.nbytes),
        dtype="float32",
        axis_order="zyx",
        byte_order="little",
        geometry=_geometry_schema(volume),
    )


# ============================================================
# 07 — VOLUME PACKAGE RESPONSE ADAPTER
# ============================================================

def _volume_descriptor(
    package: VolumePackage,
) -> VolumeDescriptor:
    """
    Construct public metadata from validated volume levels.

    The internal package's other fields remain private.
    """

    if not isinstance(package, VolumePackage):
        raise _api_error(
            status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
            "PROCESSING_FAILED",
        )

    levels = [
        _level_schema(item)
        for item in package.all_levels()
    ]

    return VolumeDescriptor(
        format="sinus-viewer-volume-v1",
        coordinate_system="LPS",
        levels=levels,
    )


def _volume_response(
    series_handle: str,
    package: VolumePackage,
) -> VolumeResponse:
    return VolumeResponse(
        success=True,
        series_handle=series_handle,
        volume=_volume_descriptor(package),
    )


# ============================================================
# 08 — POST: LOAD VOLUME
# ============================================================

@router.post(
    "/{dataset_session_id}/{series_handle}/load",
    response_model=VolumeResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
)
def load_selected_volume(
    dataset_session_id: str,
    series_handle: str,
    auth: TrustedUser,
    volumes: TrustedVolumes,
) -> VolumeResponse:
    """
    Load or reuse an authorized medical imaging volume.

    Returns geometry and available levels, not pixel data.

    The same cache key may be reused by concurrent
    authenticated requests through VolumeCacheService.
    """

    dataset_session_id = _validate_handle(
        dataset_session_id
    )

    series_handle = _validate_handle(
        series_handle
    )

    try:
        package = volumes.load_volume(
            dataset_session_id,
            series_handle,
            owner_id=auth.owner_id,
        )

        return _volume_response(
            series_handle,
            package,
        )

    except VolumeServiceError as error:
        raise _translate_volume_error(error) from None


# ============================================================
# 09 — GET: VOLUME DESCRIPTOR
# ============================================================

@router.get(
    "/{dataset_session_id}/{series_handle}",
    response_model=VolumeResponse,
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
)
def get_volume_descriptor(
    dataset_session_id: str,
    series_handle: str,
    auth: TrustedUser,
    volumes: TrustedVolumes,
) -> VolumeResponse:
    """
    Return technical information for a selected series.

    If no cached volume exists, this request may trigger
    full-volume loading through VolumeService.

    Frontend should explicitly initiate load before
    requesting large-volume metadata when possible.
    """

    dataset_session_id = _validate_handle(
        dataset_session_id
    )

    series_handle = _validate_handle(
        series_handle
    )

    try:
        package = volumes.load_volume(
            dataset_session_id,
            series_handle,
            owner_id=auth.owner_id,
        )

        return _volume_response(
            series_handle,
            package,
        )

    except VolumeServiceError as error:
        raise _translate_volume_error(error) from None


# ============================================================
# 10 — GET: ONE VOLUME LEVEL
# ============================================================

@router.get(
    "/{dataset_session_id}/{series_handle}/levels/{level}",
    response_model=VolumeLevelSchema,
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
)
def get_volume_level_descriptor(
    dataset_session_id: str,
    series_handle: str,
    level: Annotated[int, Path(ge=0, le=64)],
    auth: TrustedUser,
    volumes: TrustedVolumes,
) -> VolumeLevelSchema:
    """
    Describe one volume level.

    Level 0: Full resolution.
    Level 1+: Available downsampled previews.

    Invalid/unavailable levels return controlled errors.
    """

    dataset_session_id = _validate_handle(
        dataset_session_id
    )

    series_handle = _validate_handle(
        series_handle
    )

    level = _validate_level(level)

    try:
        selected = volumes.get_level(
            dataset_session_id,
            series_handle,
            owner_id=auth.owner_id,
            level=level,
        )

        return _level_schema(selected)

    except VolumeServiceError as error:
        raise _translate_volume_error(error) from None


# ============================================================
# 11 — DELETE: INVALIDATE SELECTED VOLUME
# ============================================================

@router.delete(
    "/{dataset_session_id}/{series_handle}/cache",
    status_code=status.HTTP_200_OK,
)
def invalidate_selected_volume(
    dataset_session_id: str,
    series_handle: str,
    auth: TrustedUser,
    volumes: TrustedVolumes,
) -> dict[str, bool]:
    """
    Evict the selected authorized volume cache entry.

    Does not close the dataset session or modify any
    original DICOM file.

    Cached arrays may remain in RAM while referenced
    by active request handlers.
    """

    dataset_session_id = _validate_handle(
        dataset_session_id
    )

    series_handle = _validate_handle(
        series_handle
    )

    try:
        invalidated = volumes.invalidate_series(
            dataset_session_id,
            series_handle,
            owner_id=auth.owner_id,
        )

        return {
            "success": True,
            "invalidated": bool(invalidated),
        }

    except VolumeServiceError as error:
        raise _translate_volume_error(error) from None


# ============================================================
# 12 — SAFE ROUTE CAPABILITIES
# ============================================================

def get_volume_route_capabilities() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """
    Static route capability summary without patient data.
    """

    return {
        "volume_loading": True,
        "volume_descriptors": True,
        "per_level_descriptors": True,
        "selected_cache_invalidation": True,
        "authentication_required": True,
        "owner_authorization": True,
        "coordinate_system": "LPS",
        "axis_order": "zyx",
        "pixel_encoding": "float32",
        "pixel_byte_order": "little",
        "pixel_data_in_json": False,
        "binary_streaming_in_this_module": False,
        "original_dataset_read_only": True,
    }


# ============================================================
# END OF VOLUME API ROUTES
# ============================================================
```
